To bridge this gap, we propose TERGAD (Structure-aware Text-enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection), A novel data augmentation framework that enriches structural semantics for GAD via the semantic reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Specifically, TERGAD translates node-level topological properties into descriptive natural language narratives, which are subsequently processed by an LLM to derive high-level semantic embeddings. These embeddings are then adaptively fused with original node attributes through a gated dual-branch autoencoder to jointly reconstruct both graph structure and node features. The anomaly score is computed based on the integrated reconstruction error, effectively capturing deviations in both observable attributes and LLM-informed semantic expectations. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that TERGAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, our ablation studies validate the indispensable role of structural semantic guidance and the efficacy of the gated fusion mechanism. Code is available at https://github.com/Kantorakitty/TERGAD-main.

</details>


### [38] [Synthesis and Evaluation of Long-term History-aware Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19766)
*Hebin Hu,Renke Dai,Ah-Hwee Tan,Yilin Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 文章提出了MediLongChat数据集合成框架及首个针对医疗智能体纵向记忆能力的基准测试，旨在解决医疗领域缺乏长期对话评测资源的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗对话数据集多集中于单次交互，缺乏纵向的医疗历史情境，且真实医疗数据受隐私和伦理限制，难以支持医疗智能体在长期跟踪与跨会话逻辑推理能力方面的开发与系统性评估。

Method: 提出了一种基于知识引导的三阶段合成框架：1.构建具有多样化疾病与并发症轨迹的患者画像；2.生成单次就诊的多轮对话；3.整合为连贯的长期历史数据集（MediLongChat）。同时设计了包含对话内、跨对话、综合推理的三项评测任务及多维度的自动与LLM辅助评测体系。

Result: 构建了包含长序列医疗数据的MediLongChat基准测试，实验证实当前先进的LLM在处理跨会话医疗历史时存在明显的记忆和推理缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有的主流大语言模型（LLM）在跨会话医疗推理任务中表现不佳，证明了构建的长序列基准测试具有极高的挑战性，并强调了开发专门针对长期医疗记忆和推理技术的迫切需求。

Abstract: An effective healthcare agent must be able to recall and reason over a patient's longitudinal medical history. However, the absence of datasets with realistic long-term dialogue timelines limits systematic evaluation. Real clinical text is constrained by privacy and ethics, while existing benchmarks focus on isolated interactions, failing to capture cross-session reasoning. We introduce a framework for synthesizing high-quality, long-term medical dialogues with LLMs. Our approach entails a knowledge-guided decomposition into three stages: constructing synthetic patient profiles with diverse disease and complication trajectories, generating multi-turn dialogues per encounter, and integrating them into a coherent longitudinal history dataset, MediLongChat. We establish three benchmark tasks-In-dialogue Reasoning, Cross-dialogue Reasoning, and Synthesis Reasoning-to evaluate the memory capabilities of healthcare agents. To assess data quality, we introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining vector-based metrics with LLM-as-a-judge assessments. Specifically, we define automatic measures-Faithfulness, Coherence, and Diversity-together with two LLM-based evaluations: Correctness and Realism. Benchmark experiments show that even state-of-the-art LLMs struggle with MediLongChat. These findings highlight the benchmark's applicability and underscore the need for tailored methods to advance healthcare agents.

</details>


### [39] [Chunking German Legal Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19806)
*Max Prior,Natalia Milanova,Andreas Schultz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了德国民法典的各种文本切分策略，发现遵循法律固有结构（如章节）的简单切分在检索性能和计算效率上均优于复杂的语义和层级增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）在法律文档应用中，切分策略的选择对检索效果和系统效率有显著影响，本文旨在探讨什么样的切分方法在法律领域表现最优。

Method: 使用德国民法典作为基准，对比了结构化切分（章节、句子）、固定窗口、上下文切分、语义聚类、Lumber式切分及RAPTOR层级检索等多种策略，并评估了检索召回率、延迟、索引构建时间和存储成本。

Result: 基于法律固有结构（条款/章节）的切分方法召回率最高，且计算效率优于昂贵的语义模型（如RAPTOR、上下文切分），复杂的语义增强手段在法律语境下反而可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 在法律领域，保留特定领域的固有结构（如章节、条文）对于信息检索的有效性至关重要；简单的结构化切分方法在性能和计算效率上均优于复杂的语义增强策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates chunking strategies for retrieval-augmented generation on German statutory law, using the German Civil Code as a structured benchmark corpus. We implement and compare a range of segmentation approaches, including structural units (sections, subsections, sentences, propositions), fixed-size windows, contextual chunking, semantic clustering, Lumber-style chunking, and RAPTOR-based hierarchical retrieval. All methods are evaluated on a legal question-answering dataset with section-level gold labels, measuring recall, query latency, index build time, and storage requirements. Results show that chunking strategies aligned with the inherent legal structure - particularly section and subsection - based retrieval-achieve the highest recall, while more complex approaches that override this structure perform worse. These simpler methods also offer favorable computational efficiency compared to LLM-intensive techniques such as contextual chunking, RAPTOR, and Lumber. The findings highlight a key trade-off between semantic enrichment and operational cost, and demonstrate that preserving domain-specific structure is critical for effective legal information retrieval.

</details>


### [40] [LP-Eval: Rubric and Dataset for Measuring the Quality of Legal Proposition Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19815)
*Shanshan Xu,Johan Lindholm,Amogh Raina,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Daniel Hershcovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用大语言模型（LLM）自动生成法律命题的可行性，提出了一种名为LP-Eval的专家导向评估指标体系，发现在成熟法律案例中表现优异，但在复杂法律推理评估上与人类专家仍有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律命题生成是法律推理的核心，但在法律自然语言处理（Legal NLP）领域研究尚不足；亟需一套科学的自动生成与评估框架来提升法律人工智能的专业性和可靠性。

Method: 1. 利用大语言模型（LLMs）从欧盟法院（CJEU）的判决中自动生成法律命题；2. 引入由法律专家共同设计的“LP-Eval”三步评估指标体系，包含形式有效性和实质维度；3. 构建包含100条专家标注数据的评估数据集；4. 对比LLM自主评分与专家评估的相关性。

Result: LLMs生成的命题在形式和内容上表现出较高的专业水准；基于规则引导的LLM评估方法优于直接评分，但在处理高度专业且细致的法律逻辑差异时仍存在局限。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够生成高质量的法律命题，且对于判例法积累丰富的成熟案件效果更佳。尽管基于规则的LLM评估方法能提升与专家评估的一致性，但在捕捉细微的法律逻辑差异方面仍不及人类专家。

Abstract: Legal proposition generation is central to legal reasoning and doctrinal scholarship, yet remain under-examined in Legal NLP. This paper investigates the automatic generation and evaluation of legal propositions from decisions of the Court of Justice of the European Union using large language models (LLMs). We introduce LP-Eval, a three-step evaluation rubric co-designed with legal experts that decomposes legal proposition quality into formal validity and substantive dimensions. Using this rubric, we release a dataset of two experts' annotations for 100 LLM-generated legal propositions. Our results show that LLMs can generate predominantly well-formed and high-quality propositions, while expert evaluations reveal higher quality for propositions derived from well established cases than from recent ones. We further examine LLMs as evaluators and find that rubric-guided LLM judgments align more closely with expert assessments than direct overall scoring, but remain insensitive to finer-grained distinctions captured by human experts.

</details>


### [41] [Are Tools Always Beneficial? Learning to Invoke Tools Adaptively for Dual-Mode Multimodal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19852)
*Qinghe Ma,Zhen Zhao,Yiming Wu,Jian Zhang,Lei Bai,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有工具增强型 MLLM 过度调用的问题，AutoTool 通过强化学习实现自适应工具决策，在提升推理精度的同时显著改善了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）工具增强研究过于侧重“如何调用工具”，忽视了“是否需要调用工具”的问题；冗余或不恰当的工具调用会增加推理负担甚至干扰模型判断。

Method: 1. 提出基于强化学习的可自适应工具调用框架 AutoTool； 2. 设计包含“工具辅助”与“文本中心”的双模式推理策略； 3. 引入模式特定的奖励函数，并通过联合训练与探索机制优化决策过程。

Result: 在 V* 基准测试中精度提升了 21.8%；在 POPE 基准测试中，相比现有的工具增强方法，推理效率提升了 44.9%。

Conclusion: AutoTool 成功平衡了推理精度与效率，证明了在多模态大语言模型中引入“是否使用工具”的自适应决策机制比盲目调用工具更优。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has emerged as a promising direction for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing studies mainly focus on enabling models to perform tool invocation, while neglecting the necessity of invoking tools. We argue that tool usage is not always beneficial, as redundant or inappropriate invocations largely increase reasoning overhead and even mislead model predictions. To address this issue, we introduce AutoTool, a model that adaptively decides whether to invoke tools according to the characteristics of each query. Within a reinforcement learning framework, we design an explicit dual-mode reasoning strategy with mode-specific reward functions to guide the model toward producing accurate responses. Moreover, to prevent premature bias toward a single reasoning mode, AutoTool jointly explores and balances tool-assisted and text-centric reasoning throughout training, and promotes free exploration in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoTool exhibits outstanding performance and high efficiency, yielding a 21.8\% accuracy gain on V* benchmark compared to the base model, and a 44.9\% improvement in efficiency over existing tool-augmented methods on POPE benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/MQinghe/AutoTool.

</details>


### [42] [What Are LLMs Doing to Scientific Communication? Measuring Changes in Writing Practices and Reading Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19936)
*Filip Miletić,Neele Falk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明 LLM 显著改变了 NLP 领域的科学论文写作特征（如句法和词汇复杂度），虽然提升了文本的可读性和吸引力，但专家对 AI 介入写作的主观感受依然存在争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（LLM）的广泛使用是否以及如何改变了自然语言处理（NLP）领域的科学传播风格。

Method: 1. 构建了两个数据集：包含 2020-2024 年间 ACL Anthology 的 3.7 万篇论文自然语料库，以及 3000 个由人类撰写及 LLM 修改的对照文本；2. 进行了词汇历时分析（词频与使用语境）；3. 对句法结构、词汇复杂度和多样性等风格特征进行建模分析；4. 开展了包含 20 位领域专家的主观注释研究。

Result: 1. 论文的词汇使用在语义上呈现出专门化与泛化并存的趋势；2. LLM 修改后的文本倾向于使用更复杂的句法结构、更长更复杂的词汇，且词汇多样性较低；3. 专家评价认为 LLM 辅助后的文本更具可读性和吸引力，但在主观态度上流露出对 AI 干预的负面倾向。

Conclusion: LLM 的使用确实改变了科学论文的写作风格（包括词汇使用和句法结构），专家认为 LLM 改进后的文本更易懂且更有吸引力，但对 AI 辅助写作的整体态度参差不齐，反映了主观阅读体验的复杂性。

Abstract: Has the style of scientific communication changed due to the growing use of large language models in the writing process? We address this question in the domain of Natural Language Processing by leveraging two data resources we create: a naturalistic corpus of over 37,000 papers from the ACL Anthology (2020-2024); and a synthetic dataset of 3,000 human-written passages and their LLM-generated improvements. We first implement a series of diachronic lexical analyses, showing that both word frequency and usage contexts have changed significantly over time, indicating semantic specialization in some cases and generalization in others. Broadening our perspective, we then model a range of more complex stylistic features and find that LLM-modified texts more frequently contain certain syntactic constructions, more complex and longer words and a lower lexical diversity. Finally, we connect these changes in writing practices to subjective reading experience through a pilot annotation study with 20 domain experts. They overall rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but also express negative qualitative attitudes towards LLMs, highlighting the strongly subjective effect of AI-assisted writing on reading experience.

</details>


### [43] [FlexDraft: Flexible Speculative Decoding via Attention Tuning and Bonus-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20022)
*Yaojie Zhang,Jianuo Huang,Junlong Ke,Yuhang Han,Yongji Long,Tianchen Zhao,Biqing Qi,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexDraft 是一种无损投机解码框架，通过注意力微调、奖惩校准和自适应解码策略，有效解决了并行投机解码在大规模并发推理时的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行投机解码方法在处理大批量任务时，因 bonus token 和接受长度的不确定性导致验证不匹配，造成吞吐量提升显著下降，且通常伴随质量损失或高昂的预训练成本。

Method: 1. Attention Tuning：仅微调末端层的注意力投影层，实现高效的 Block Diffusion 投机；2. Bonus-guided Calibration：引入轻量级 MLP 根据 bonus token 校准 draft logits，缓解验证不匹配；3. Flex Decoding：根据批次大小动态切换并行与串行模式，并根据置信度自适应调整验证长度。

Result: FlexDraft 通过灵活的架构设计，提升了大批量场景下的推理吞吐量，同时保持了模型生成质量的无损性（lossless）。

Conclusion: FlexDraft 通过 Attention Tuning、Bonus-guided Calibration 和 Flex Decoding 三项核心设计，解决了并行投机解码在处理大批量任务时的性能坍塌问题，实现了在不同负载下无损且高效的 LLM 推理。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM inference without quality degradation by using a fast drafter to propose multiple candidate tokens and the target model to verify them in parallel. However, conventional sequential speculative decoding suffers from mutual waiting between drafting and verification, and repeated exchange of intermediate states further increases memory access overhead. Parallel speculative decoding addresses this limitation by performing drafting and verification within a single target forward pass, allowing future drafts to be prepared while current candidates are being verified. Although effective at small batch sizes, existing parallel speculative decoding methods either require costly continual pretraining with quality degradation or suffer from low acceptance rates. More importantly, this paradigm inherently suffers from uncertainty in both the bonus token and the accepted length, leading to draft verification mismatch and causing throughput gains to collapse at large batch sizes. To address these limitations, we introduce FlexDraft, a lossless speculative decoding framework that flexibly adapts to varying batch sizes through three key designs. (1) Attention Tuning enables block diffusion drafting by tuning only the attention projectors of the final few layers on mask tokens, while keeping the autoregressive path frozen to preserve the target distribution and produce high quality drafts with minimal trainable parameters. (2) Bonus-guided Calibration uses a lightweight MLP conditioned on the resolved bonus token to calibrate draft logits, mitigating draft verification mismatch caused by bonus token uncertainty. (3) Flex Decoding dynamically switches between parallel draft and verify at small batch sizes and sequential draft then verify at large batch sizes, and adjusts verification length based on draft confidence to eliminate redundant computation.

</details>


### [44] [Mind Your Moras: Orthography-Aware Error Analysis of Neural Japanese Morphological Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20043)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对日语过去式变形的错误分析发现，字符级模型虽然整体表现良好，但在处理特定的拼写结构（尤其是促音）时存在高度一致的、可预期的系统性错误，反映了拼写表征与形态结构交互对模型泛化的深刻影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往忽视平假名作为一种编码形态音位差异的表征系统对模型泛化能力的影响，本研究旨在通过拼写感知错误分析揭示隐藏在序列模型高聚合准确率下的系统性失效模式。

Method: 基于SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务标准，构建包含七种错误模式的详细分类法，利用两种字符级序列到序列（Seq2Seq）架构进行日语过去式形态变形的实验。

Result: 尽管模型整体准确率高，但存在系统性的语言学可解释错误，其中促音（gemination）相关错误占主导（75-80%），特别是在以元音e结尾的动词词干中。错误模式在不同架构和随机种子间高度一致。

Conclusion: 模型在处理形态转换时表现出与拼写属性高度相关的系统性错误，强调了在形态复杂语言中进行“感知拼写”评估的必要性。

Abstract: We present an orthography-aware error analysis of Japanese past-tense morphological inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium, but as a representational system encoding morphophonological distinctions that may influence model generalization. We evaluate two character-level sequence-to-sequence architectures on past-tense formation using datasets formatted according to the SIGMORPHON 2020 and 2023 shared task conventions. Despite high aggregate accuracy, models exhibit systematic, linguistically interpretable errors that cluster around specific orthographic properties of hiragana. We introduce a concise error taxonomy capturing seven primary failure modes and provide both quantitative and qualitative analyses. Gemination-related errors dominate residual failures, accounting for 75-80% of errors, particularly in verbs whose stems end in the vowel e and require gemination before the past-tense suffix. Error patterns remain highly consistent across architectures and random seeds, suggesting a robust interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency effects in shaping model generalization. These results underscore the necessity of orthography-aware evaluation for understanding neural generalization in morphologically complex languages.

</details>


### [45] [Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20050)
*Calvin Yixiang Cheng,Dorian Quelle,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发现阴谋论通过不断的语言与结构“变异”（如简化和同化），能够显著延长在社交媒体上的活跃周期，因此建议内容审核策略应针对阴谋论的本质核心而非仅仅监控具体文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究语言变异（language mutations）如何影响虚假信息/阴谋论在社交媒体上的持久传播机制。

Method: 结合计算语言学分析与生存模型（Survival Modelling）；利用基于X（原Twitter）平台为期三年的阴谋论帖子数据集进行定量分析，并辅以定性分析归纳变异模式。

Result: 语义变异程度越高的阴谋论，其生命周期越长；具体表现为心理语言学特征（代词、社会参考词、认知过程词等）及AAT结构（行动者、动作、目标）的变异均能显著延长内容的存续时间；识别出“简化”和“同化”两种主要的变异模式。

Conclusion: 阴谋论内容的“可变异性”是其持续传播的关键因素；社交媒体内容审核机制应超越对单一文本的干预，转向识别并应对阴谋论的核心要素及其随时间演变的多种变体。

Abstract: This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [46] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptRad 是一种针对医学放射报告的低资源标注方案，通过知识增强的提示学习（Prompt-tuning）替代传统微调，仅需极少量样本即可实现精准的多标签分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的规则驱动标注器难以应对临床报告中高度多样化的描述，而传统的预训练语言模型（PLM）微调方法又极度依赖大规模标注数据，这在临床实践中往往无法满足。

Method: 提出了一种名为 PromptRad 的知识增强型提示学习（Prompt-tuning）方法。该方法将多标签分类任务重构为掩码语言建模（Masked Language Modeling, MLM）任务，并利用 UMLS Metathesaurus 中的同义词构建多词动词化器（Multi-word Verbalizer）来增强类别语义表示。模型直接在预训练语言模型上进行提示调优，无需额外的分类层。

Result: 在肝脏 CT 报告数据集上，仅使用 32 个标注样本即优于传统的基于字典和微调的基线方法；与 GPT-4 相比，在模型规模远小的情况下达到了具有竞争力的性能，且在处理复杂否定逻辑方面表现更强。

Conclusion: PromptRad 是一种数据高效的放射科报告标注方法，能够有效地处理标注数据稀缺的临床场景，特别是在复杂否定模式的识别上表现优异，是现有规则驱动或传统微调方案的有效替代。

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [47] [Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20066)
*Jann Pfeifer,Debayan Banerjee,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究证明，即使没有金标准查询标注，通过GRPO等强化学习方法利用执行反馈训练小型模型，也能实现高效的Text-to-SPARQL生成，为低资源KGQA提供了可行的优化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识图谱问答（KGQA）方法通常依赖庞大的模型或详尽的金标准查询标注，本研究旨在探索能否通过基于结果的强化学习，利用小型模型实现零样本（Zero-shot）Text-to-SPARQL，从而降低对高质量标注数据的依赖。

Method: 利用GRPO（Group-Relative Policy Optimization）算法对Qwen3-1.7B小型指令微调模型进行训练，在DBLP-QuAD数据集上通过执行反馈、结构约束及回答级奖励进行优化，并引入了基于金查询的奖励塑造（Shaping）作为变体对比。

Result: GRPO模型显著优于未微调的零样本基线，并展现出良好的泛化能力；尽管其整体准确率略逊于有监督的DoRA微调模型，但验证了仅依靠执行反馈即可实现有效训练的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习（Outcome-based RL）是当缺乏金标准查询（Gold Queries）进行监督时，训练小型大模型进行Text-to-SPARQL生成的有效策略。执行反馈和结构性约束是性能提升的核心驱动力。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.

</details>


### [48] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CopT 是一种“先回答后反思”的推理架构，通过引入基于嵌入空间的对比验证器来评估草稿答案的有效性，从而在不经过额外训练的情况下，实现了推理准确率的提升与计算成本的大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链（CoT）范式要求模型在回答前必须进行完整推理，导致模型在能够直接给出正确答案的问题上也产生了不必要的延迟和 token 成本（即“执行性推理”问题）。

Method: 1. 提出“CopT”推理范式：颠倒思考与回答顺序，先生成草稿答案，再根据草稿进行反思与校验（On-policy thinking）。
2. 构建对比验证机制：利用离散 token 输入与连续嵌入输入之间的差异，通过序列级逆向 KL 散度（Reverse KL estimator）评估答案的置信度。
3. 动态控制机制：基于可靠性评估动态决定是否进行深度思考，并控制草稿答案在反思过程中的可见性。

Result: 在数学、编码和智能体推理任务中，CopT 的峰值精度最高提升了 23%，在保持相同或更高精度的情况下，token 消耗降低了 57%。

Conclusion: CopT 通过动态调整思考顺序（先回答后思考）和引入基于对比验证的可靠性评估，证明了在无需额外训练的前提下，能够显著提升大模型的推理准确率并大幅降低推理阶段的 token 成本。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [49] [BalanceRAG: Joint Risk Calibration for Cascaded Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20084)
*Zijun Jia,Yuanchang Ye,Sen Jia,Yiyao Qian,Haoning Wang,Baojie Chen,Diyin Tang,Jinsong Yu,Zhiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BalanceRAG 是一种通过序列图形检验优化级联式 RAG 决策阈值的框架，实现了在可控风险下平衡模型自回答与检索增强生成的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 系统对所有查询进行检索既冗余又昂贵。虽然级联式 RAG 可以通过置信度判断是否启用检索，但传统的阶段性校准过于保守，无法在系统层面实现最优的风险与效用平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于二维格点路径的级联式 RAG 框架。利用 LLM-only 和 RAG 两个分支的置信度分数，通过序列图形检验（Sequential Graphical Testing）来校准阈值对，实现风险自适应的决策控制与多目标风险约束。

Result: 在三个开放域问答基准测试中，BalanceRAG 成功控制了系统级错误率，相比始终开启 RAG 的基线方法，在保持更高回答覆盖率的同时，显著减少了不必要的检索调用。

Conclusion: BalanceRAG 通过序列图形检验实现风险可控的级联式 RAG 系统，在保证预设风险水平的同时，有效平衡了模型回答的覆盖率与准确率，降低了不必要的检索开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can enhance factuality via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but applying RAG to every query is unnecessary when the model-only answer is reliable. This motivates cascaded RAG: each query is first handled by an LLM-only branch, escalated to a RAG fallback only if the primary branch is uncertain, and abstained from when neither branch is sufficiently trustworthy. However, calibrating such cascades stage by stage may be conservative, since the final utility depends on joint uncertainty thresholding of LLM-only and RAG. In this work, we develop BalanceRAG to certify threshold pairs at a target risk level. Given uncertainty scores from the two branches, BalanceRAG frames each threshold pair as an operating point on a two-dimensional lattice and identifies safe operating points using sequential graphical testing. This enables risk-adaptive threshold calibration, controlling the system-level error rate among accepted points, while retaining more examples. Furthermore, BalanceRAG extends to multi-risk calibration, allowing retrieval usage to be bounded together with the selection-conditioned risk. Experiments on three open-domain question answering (QA) benchmarks across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that BalanceRAG meets prescribed risk levels, preserves higher coverage and more accepted correct examples, and reduces unnecessary retrieval calls compared with always-on RAG.

</details>


### [50] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发布了首个大规模标注数据集 ThoughtTrace，首次将用户在对话中的“心理活动”（动机与反馈）显性化，证实了利用此模态能显著提升AI对用户目标的理解、行为预测及个性化对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式AI数据集仅记录了用户“说了什么”，而缺乏对用户“在想什么”（潜在目标、偏好和动机）的记录，这限制了AI对人类认知动态的深度理解与适应。

Method: 构建了大规模数据集 ThoughtTrace，包含1,058名用户、2,155次对话共10,174条思维标注（包含发送提示词的原因及对回答的反应）。并通过分析这些数据，验证了其在用户行为预测（作为推理时上下文）和思维引导下的对话重写（作为个性化对齐信号）中的应用价值。

Result: 研究表明：1. 思维与对话文本在语义上具有显著差异；2. 现有顶尖LLM难以单纯从对话语境中准确推断用户思维；3. 思维具有高度多样性且与对话阶段强相关；4. 引入思维信息可提升用户行为预测准确率，并为个性化辅助提供更细粒度的对齐信号。

Conclusion: ThoughtTrace引入了“用户思维”这一新数据模态，证明了其在理解人类-AI交互潜在认知动态方面的重要性，并为构建更能理解和适应用户需求、目标及偏好的个性化智能体奠定了基础。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [51] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在复杂任务中普遍存在类似人类的“不注意盲视”现象，并提出了一种名为PRCP的提示方法来改善模型对关键隐式信息的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中“不注意盲视（inattentional blindness）”理论启发，探究经人类偏好语料训练的大语言模型是否会在处理明确任务指令时，因注意力偏差而忽视关键的隐式上下文线索。

Method: 1. 提出“显式-隐式推理（explicit-implicit reasoning）”任务；
2. 构建包含2,246道多选题的MixRea基准测试集；
3. 评估21种先进LLM的性能；
4. 提出“潜在关系补全提示（PRCP）”方法以缓解该问题。

Result: 即便表现最好的Gemini 2.5 Pro，其一致性也仅达到42.8%，表明该问题在大模型中普遍且严重；PRCP方法能有效改善大模型对被忽略因果关系的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 当前主流大语言模型普遍存在“不注意盲视”现象，即在面对显式指令时容易忽略微妙的隐式语境信息。研究强调了开发更具认知对齐能力的大模型对提升高风险决策准确性的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [52] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，通过使用结构化的“检查清单”提示词，可以显著提升大语言模型的回复质量，同时减少交互次数，实现更好的效率与效果平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）常因提示语不明确导致回复质量低，进而增加用户交互成本；研究旨在探讨结构化提示设计方案是否能在提升回答质量的同时减少用户负担。

Method: 通过对比实验，分析了“原始提示”、“检查清单改进提示”和“澄清问题提示”三种方式；在总结、规划、解释和编码四类任务中进行测试；使用ChatGPT、Claude和Grok三个模型，并通过统一的评分标准（涵盖完成度、准确性、合规性和清晰度）进行量化评估。

Result: 检查清单改进提示的评分最高（平均7.50/8分），优于澄清问题提示（6.67分）和原始提示（5.67分）；且检查清单在生成高质量内容的同时，消耗的平均标记（tokens）数量更少，展示了最优的质量与效率权衡。

Conclusion: 使用结构化提示（特别是检查清单）可以有效提高大语言模型的回复质量，并以更低的交互成本实现更好的效能均衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [53] [ClinSeekAgent: Automating Multimodal Evidence Seeking for Agentic Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20176)
*Juncheng Wu,Letian Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Haoqin Tu,Hardy Chen,Zijun Wang,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinSeekAgent是一个主动检索多模态证据的临床智能体框架，通过解决原始临床数据获取难题，显著提升了LLMs在实际临床工作流中的决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床决策支持系统多依赖预先筛选好的证据，无法满足现实临床场景中需要主动、迭代地从异构源检索并合成多模态证据的需求。

Method: 1. 构建了一个自动化智能体框架（ClinSeekAgent），具备主动查询医学知识库、导航原始电子病历记录（EHR）及调用医学影像工具的能力；2. 建立新基准 ClinSeek-Bench，用于评估从原始数据中获取证据的能力；3. 提出一种训练管线，通过蒸馏高质量智能体轨迹来优化小型开源模型。

Result: 在纯文本EHR任务中，ClinSeekAgent显著提升了多种闭源大模型的F1得分；在多模态任务中（如胸部X光分析），Claude Opus的得分大幅提升了15.1个百分点。此外，通过该框架蒸馏出的ClinSeek-35B-A3B模型，在AgentEHR-Bench上取得了显著超越基线的效果。

Conclusion: ClinSeekAgent通过将临床决策支持从“被动证据消费”转向“主动证据获取”，显著提升了大型语言模型在处理原始临床数据时的推理能力和决策准确性，为自动化临床工作流开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have shown promise for clinical decision support, but existing works largely assume that evidence has already been curated and handed to the model. Real-world clinical workflows instead require agents to actively seek, iteratively plan, and synthesize multimodal evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this paper, we introduce ClinSeekAgent, an automated agentic framework for dynamic multimodal evidence seeking that shifts the paradigm from passive evidence consumption to active evidence acquisition. Given only a clinical query and access to raw data sources, ClinSeekAgent gathers evidence by querying medical knowledge bases, navigating raw EHRs, and invoking medical imaging tools; refines its hypotheses as new information emerges; and integrates the collected evidence into grounded clinical decisions. ClinSeekAgent serves both as an inference-time agent for frontier LLMs and as a training-time pipeline for distilling high-quality agent trajectories into compact open-source models. To validate its inference-time effectiveness, we construct ClinSeek-Bench, which pairs Curated Input reasoning from fixed pre-selected evidence with Automated Evidence-Seeking over raw clinical data. On text-only EHR tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 60.0 to 63.2 overall F1 and MiniMax M2.5 from 43.1 to 47.3, with positive risk-prediction gains in 7 out of 9 evaluated host models. On multimodal tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 47.5 to 62.6 (+15.1); all evaluated models improve across the three CXR-related task groups. We further validate ClinSeekAgent as a training pipeline by distilling agentic evidence-seeking trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, which achieves 34.0 average F1 on existing AgentEHR-Bench, improving over its Qwen3.5-35B-A3B baseline by +11.9 points and approaching Claude Opus 4.6.

</details>


### [54] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究指出 VLM 推理受限于感知而非思维，提出将训练分为“感知-视觉推理-文本推理”三个阶段，通过强化感知能力，在提升模型精度的同时有效缩短了推理思维链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员发现现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在长链思维推理上的瓶颈并非推理能力不足，而是视觉感知能力较差。因此，本研究旨在探索如何通过分阶段训练来理清感知与推理之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出一套能力驱动的分阶段训练框架，将训练过程分解为三个阶段：视觉感知（使用强化学习优化）、视觉推理、以及文本推理。同时引入了与传统难度课程互补的“能力课程”维度，用于系统化改进 VLM 的训练流程。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现显著： reasoning 准确率提升 1.5% 且推理路径缩短 20.8%；在 WeMath 和 RealWorldQA 等任务上分别取得 +5.2% 和 +3.7% 的提升；在同类开放权重 VLM 中达到领先水平。

Conclusion: 通过任务解耦的分阶段训练（感知 -> 视觉推理 -> 文本推理）能显著优于混合训练，证明了视觉感知是推理能力的基石，且强化学习（RL）在感知训练中比 SFT 更有效。此外，该方法证明了感知能力的提升可以缩短推理链，实现更高效的“少而精”推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [55] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE 是一种针对 MoE 架构扩散大模型（dLLM）的无损推理加速系统，通过数学建模优化专家激活的刷新间隔，显著降低 I/O 开销并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大模型（dLLMs）结合专家混合（MoE）架构在大规模部署时受到资源限制，传统的自回归推断方法存在严重的 I/O 开销与计算瓶颈，缺乏高效的推理调度机制。

Method: 提出一种基于区间的专家刷新策略（interval-based expert refresh strategy），该策略通过数学规划模型求解最优刷新周期，旨在最小化内存 I/O 开销并平衡 CPU 与 GPU 之间的计算负载。

Result: 在 LLaDA2.0-mini 和 LLaDA2.0-flash 模型上，TIDE 实现了高达 1.4 倍和 1.5 倍的吞吐量提升，且无需进行模型重训练。

Conclusion: TIDE 是一种面向 dLLM 的无需训练（lossless）的推理加速系统，通过利用专家激活的临时稳定性，在计算性能和 I/O 开销之间取得了最优平衡，有效解决了资源受限环境下的大规模 MoE dLLM 部署难题。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


### [56] [IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19346)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者发布了印度法律判决数据集 IMLJD（3,613份案例），并通过数据分析发现，在处理婚姻纠纷撤销请求时，印度最高法院的胜诉率显著高于卡纳塔克邦高等法院。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了填补印度法律领域开放数据集的空白，并量化研究司法层级（最高法院 vs 高等法院）在处理婚姻纠纷撤销请求时的裁判结果差异。

Method: 构建了一个包含3,613份印度法律判决书的开放数据集，涵盖了特定婚姻纠纷法条（IPC 498A、DV Act、CrPC 482）。采用结构化标签、元数据指标提取以及知识图谱构建的方法，并对比分析了最高法院与高等法院在特定时间窗口内的判决结果。

Result: 建立了 IMLJD 数据集；统计发现最高法院的撤销请求成功率为 57.6%，而卡纳塔克邦高等法院为 39.7%。研究证实该差异在时间调整后依然稳健。

Conclusion: IMLJD 为法律大语言模型研究提供了高质量、结构化的实证数据。数据分析揭示了印度最高法院与卡纳塔克邦高等法院在撤销请求（quashing petitions）成功率上存在显著且稳健的差距（差距接近20个百分点）。

Abstract: We present IMLJD, an open dataset of 3,613 Indian court judgments covering matrimonial disputes under IPC Section 498A, the Protection of Women from Domestic Violence Act, and CrPC Section 482. The dataset covers the Supreme Court of India from 2000 to 2024 (1,474 cases) and the Karnataka High Court from 2018 to 2024 (2,139 cases), with structured outcome labels, metadata-derived indicators, and a knowledge graph. We find that 57.6% of quashing petitions succeed at the Supreme Court level compared to 39.7% at the Karnataka High Court level. On a matched 2018 to 2024 period, the SC quash rate is 59.3%, widening the differential to 19.6 percentage points and confirming the finding is robust to temporal adjustment. The dataset, code, and knowledge graph are released openly at https://github.com/joyboseroy/imljd and https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/imljd.

</details>

### [57] [HSCO-Bench: An Agent-Driven End-to-End Hardware-Software Co-design Benchmark for Systems-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19399)
*Pei-Huan Tsai,Kuan-Lin Chiu,William Baisi,Pin-Yu Chen,Luca P. Carloni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HSCO-Bench，这是首个用于评估大模型进行端到端硬件-软件协同设计能力的基准测试。实验表明，当前前沿模型虽具备一定的加速设计能力，但在复杂系统集成和资源利用优化方面仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试将软硬件设计割裂开来，缺乏评估大模型进行端到端硬件-软件协同设计（Co-design）能力的工具，这阻碍了LLM在系统级全栈优化中的应用。

Method: 构建了基于开源SoC平台的HSCO-Bench基准测试，涵盖应用分析（定位加速核）、异构加速器设计集成（SoC级）、以及内核映射三个环节，并要求大模型在资源受限的情况下生成可部署至FPGA（AMD Virtex-7）的SoC原型。

Result: 五款评估中的前沿模型仅有两款成功生成了有效的SoC原型；尽管观察到最高16.22X的加速比，但模型对硬件资源的利用率极低（最高仅23.67%），表明大模型处理复杂系统集成的能力尚不成熟。

Conclusion: HSCO-Bench 填补了系统级硬件-软件协同设计评估的空白，揭示了当前大模型在端到端系统生成任务中的潜力与显著局限性，特别是在资源利用效率方面仍有巨大提升空间，为未来的协同优化研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are adopted for software and hardware design, yet these domains are still evaluated separately. Software benchmarks typically assume fixed hardware targets, while hardware benchmarks focus on component-level optimization without considering the full hardware-software stack. Consequently, no existing benchmark evaluates whether an LLM agent can perform end-to-end, system-level hardware-software co-design. Such a process requires: 1) analyzing applications to identify kernels requiring acceleration, 2) designing and integrating heterogeneous accelerators into a System-on-Chip (SoC) under resource constraints, and 3) mapping kernels onto the generated accelerators. We present HSCO-Bench, an end-to-end hardware-software co-design benchmark for accelerator-rich heterogeneous SoC generation. Built upon an open-source SoC platform with a curated repository structure, HSCO-Bench evaluates the ability of LLMs to jointly optimize software and hardware stacks, producing SoC prototypes deployed on the AMD Virtex-7 FPGA VC707 Evaluation Kit. Experimental results show that end-to-end integration remains challenging for current models. Among the five frontier models evaluated, only two of them could successfully generate valid SoC prototypes. Yet, even in these successful instances, the generated designs are far from optimal. While we observe a promising peak speedup of 16.22X, the maximum additional resource utilization reaches only 23.67%. This highlights that while state-of-the-art models demonstrate an emerging capability for hardware acceleration, they still heavily underutilize the available hardware capacity, leaving room for future optimization. To the best of our knowledge, HSCO-Bench is the first benchmark targeting this complete co-design flow, enabling LLMs to jointly reason about and modify both the software and hardware stacks of heterogeneous SoCs.

</details>


### [58] [A complete discussion on fully reconfigurable, digital, scalable, graph and sparsity-aware near-memory accelerator for graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19405)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman,Lizy John,Jaydeep P. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NEM-GNN 是一种无需 DAC/ADC 的可扩展近存计算架构，通过软硬件协同的优化机制，显著解决了 GNN 处理中非规则访存带来的性能与能效挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CPU/GPU 处理 GNN 时存在由于组合阶段（高强度运算）和聚合阶段（稀疏/不规则访问）的异构行为导致的数据移动开销大、能效低的问题；现有专用加速器及 PIM 方案则受限于可扩展性、面积开销和模拟计算的低效性。

Method: 提出了 NEM-GNN（一种无需 DAC/ADC 的近存计算架构），核心技术包括：1. 早期计算终止机制（early compute termination）；2. 基于可重构 SoC 的预计算；3. 采用“计算即就绪 (CAR)”与基于广播的执行模型的图/稀疏性感知近存聚合技术。

Result: 实验表明，与现有最先进方案相比，NEM-GNN 性能提升约 80-230 倍，吞吐量提升 80-300 倍，能效提升 850-1134 倍，计算密度提升 7-8 倍。

Conclusion: NEM-GNN 是一种高效的 GNN 加速架构，通过架构创新克服了传统 CPU/GPU 及现有 PIM 加速器的性能瓶颈，实现了数量级的能效与性能提升。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have gained significant interest for applications such as citation network analysis and drug discovery due to their ability to apply machine learning techniques on graph-structured data. GNNs typically employ a two-stage execution pipeline consisting of combination and aggregation kernels. The combination stage performs data-intensive convolution operations with relatively regular memory access patterns, whereas the aggregation stage operates on sparse graph data with highly irregular accesses. These heterogeneous memory behaviors make conventional CPU- and GPU-based execution energy inefficient due to substantial data movement overheads.
  Existing accelerators attempt to mitigate these challenges using specialized architectures and processing-in-memory (PIM) techniques. However, prior approaches often suffer from scalability limitations, area overheads, restricted parallelism, and energy inefficiencies associated with analog compute and dedicated accelerator structures.
  This paper presents NEM-GNN, a scalable DAC/ADC-less processing-in-memory architecture for graph neural network acceleration. The proposed design introduces early compute termination mechanisms, pre-computation using reconfigurable system-on-chip components, and graph- and sparsity-aware near-memory aggregation using a compute-as-soon-as-ready (CAR) and broadcast-based execution model. Experimental results demonstrate that NEM-GNN achieves approximately 80--230x higher performance, 80--300x higher throughput, 850--1134x better energy efficiency, and 7--8x higher compute density compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [59] [Example-Driven Intent Synthesis for Constrained Data Bundle Retrieval: Focused Text Snippet Extraction and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19246)
*Whanhee Cho,Kuangfei Long,Mahmood Jasim,Matteo Brucato,Alexandra Meliou,Peter J. Haas,Anna Fariha*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Ex2Bundle 是一种新型示例驱动束检索框架，通过用户提供的示例自动生成聚合查询约束，并利用数据感知松弛技术解决检索结果为空的约束冲突问题，提高了组合检索的易用性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的束检索任务（Bundle Retrieval）虽然在数据库和推荐系统中很重要，但面临两大用户挑战：一是如何通过用户友好的方式精确表达多维束意图；二是如何在查询结果为空时处理约束不可行的问题。

Method: 1. 引入示例驱动（Example-driven）交互框架，允许用户通过示例束表达复杂意图；2. 实现自动查询合成（Query Synthesis），将示例隐含的意图转化为聚合约束；3. 提出数据感知约束松弛（Data-aware Constraint Relaxation）算法，在保持用户意图的前提下自动调整不可行约束的边界。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验及用户研究表明，Ex2Bundle 显著提升了系统的易用性，即使在目标数据库分布发生变化的情况下，也能始终返回与用户意图高度匹配的束。

Conclusion: Ex2Bundle 通过示例驱动的交互方式有效解决了组合束检索中的用户意图捕捉问题，并在约束不可行时通过数据感知松弛机制保证了检索的鲁棒性，为复杂的束检索任务提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Selecting a bundle of items that collectively satisfies constraints is a fundamental task across databases, recommender systems, and text summarization. Unlike traditional retrieval that returns individual or top-k items, bundle retrieval is inherently combinatorial and, in general, NP-hard. Although package queries can efficiently retrieve bundles given a well-formed query, two key user-centric challenges remain: (1) expressing and tuning multi-dimensional bundle intent through a user-friendly interface, and (2) ensuring feasibility when the query yields empty results. We introduce Ex2Bundle, an Example-driven Bundle retrieval framework that enables users to specify their intent through example bundles and automatically synthesizes package queries that capture the intent implicit in those example bundles via aggregate constraints. Ex2Bundle also addresses a challenge unique to bundle retrieval: when inferred aggregate constraints are infeasible over the target data, our data-aware constraint relaxation minimally adjusts the constraint bounds while preserving alignment with user intent. We instantiate a specific application of focused text snippet extraction by example to demonstrate the efficacy of the Ex2Bundle framework. Extensive experiments over real-world datasets and a user study demonstrate that Ex2Bundle improves usability and consistently returns intent-aligned bundles even under distributional shifts of the target database.

</details>


### [60] [Leveraging I/O Stalls for Efficient Scheduling in ANNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19335)
*Juncheng Zhang,Yuanming Ren,Yongkun Li,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LIOS 是一种利用 ANNS 系统搜索线程的磁盘 I/O 等待时间来执行索引更新的框架，通过细粒度任务拆解和动态调度，在保证搜索延迟的前提下显著加速了数据更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于硬盘的 ANNS 系统中，搜索线程在磁盘 I/O 上花费了大量等待时间（超过 40% 的 CPU 时间），导致资源闲置；而系统又需要同时满足实时搜索和高性能索引更新的需求，现有的资源调度方式未能充分利用这些隐性的 I/O 等待周期。

Method: LIOS 框架包含三项关键技术：1. 将更新操作拆解为可恢复的细粒度子任务，以适应 I/O 停顿窗口；2. 设定更新子任务的预期开销阈值，控制对主搜索线程的干扰；3. 通过自适应调节机制，动态平衡空闲时间分配，确保搜索延迟下降控制在用户设定的范围内。

Result: 将 LIOS 集成到 FreshDiskANN 和 OdinANN 系统中，插入性能提升高达 2.68 倍，删除性能提升高达 2.18 倍，且搜索延迟降幅保持在用户指定的预设目标之内。

Conclusion: LIOS 证明了通过在 I/O 等待期间执行索引更新，可以在不显著增加搜索延迟的情况下，大幅提升 ANNS 系统的更新性能。该技术为硬盘存储导向的图索引在高负载更新场景下提供了有效的资源利用策略。

Abstract: Disk-based graph indexes for approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) must serve latency-sensitive queries and throughput-demanding updates concurrently. We observe that over 40% of search-thread CPU time is spent stalling on disk I/O; such idle cycles are invisible to thread-level scheduling yet available for other work. We present LIOS(Leverage I/O Stall), a framework that executes index updates inside search-side I/O stall windows. LIOS introduces three techniques: (i) splitting each update into resumable subtasks small enough to fit within a single stall window; (ii) bounding the expected overrun of update subtasks to a given threshold; and (iii) dynamically adjusting the fraction of idle time devoted to updates to drive end-to-end search latency degradation toward a user-specified target. We integrate LIOS into two update-optimized ANNS systems, FreshDiskANN and OdinANN. LIOS achieves speedups of up to 2.68$\times$ in insertion and 2.18$\times$ in deletion, with search latency degradation maintained near the user-specified target.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时空预测任务中复杂度不匹配导致的性能瓶颈，提出了ST-Balance框架，通过时空熵诊断与针对性的特征表示调节，在多个领域实现了预测精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理复杂的时空数据时存在性能瓶颈，且跨领域迁移能力有限，本质原因是缺乏对数据时空复杂度不匹配现象的有效分析与调节。

Method: 1. 引入时空熵度量，诊断复杂度不匹配问题；2. 通过低秩矩阵嵌入进行空间降维以保留关键结构；3. 扩展时间跨度以捕捉长距离依赖并缓解时间异质性引起的累积误差。

Result: 在多个城市交通、气象和流行病数据集上均取得了显著的准确性提升，并验证了其在不同时空预测任务中的通用性。

Conclusion: ST-Balance框架通过解决时空复杂度不匹配问题，显著提升了在交通、气象和流行病等多个领域的预测准确性，具有良好的跨领域适用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [62] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HELLoRA，通过仅对MoE模型的“高频激活专家”进行LoRA微调，在大幅减少参数量与计算开销的同时，提升了微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效微调方法（如LoRA）多针对密集模型，未充分利用MoE模型稀疏激活的特性，导致适配器参数和计算量冗余。

Method: 提出HELLoRA方法，仅在各层最常被激活的专家（Hot-Experts）上附加LoRA模块；并引入HELLoRI进一步压缩训练参数。

Result: 在三个MoE基座模型及三个任务系列上，HELLoRA在显著降低训练参数（约原先的15%-23%）和计算量（减少38.7%）的同时，实现了更高的训练吞吐量和精度（比LoRA提升9.2%）。

Conclusion: HELLoRA通过利用MoE模型的稀疏激活特性实现了高效微调，证明了基于激活的适配器布局是优化MoE模型PEFT的有效且实用的策略。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [63] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UCCI 是一种新型 LLM 路由框架，通过引入校准机制和自动阈值优化，在保证精度的前提下显著降低了推理成本，优于现有多种路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 级联与路由架构依赖未校准的置信度评分，且往往需要针对每个工作负载手动调整阈值，导致系统效率低且部署困难。

Method: 提出 UCCI（Uncalibrated Confidence-based Cascade Interpolation）路由框架：1. 使用等渗回归（Isotonic Regression）将 Token 级边际不确定性映射为查询层面的错误概率；2. 基于约束成本最小化选择升级阈值；3. 理论证明在特定假设下该策略具有成本最优性及 $O(n^{-1/3})$ 的样本复杂度。

Result: 在75,000个实体识别查询的生产环境测试中，UCCI 相比现有方法（熵阈值法、分割一致性路由和 FrugalGPT）表现更优，将 ECE 从 0.12 降低至 0.03，并将推理成本降低了 31%。

Conclusion: UCCI 通过校准路由器成功实现了在保持高性能（Micro-F1=0.91）的前提下，显著降低推理成本（提升31%效率），证明了校准置信度在LLM级联架构中的关键作用。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [64] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fully Looped Transformer 通过引入全循环架构和注意力注入机制，解决了循环 Transformer 在高迭代次数下的训练不稳定性问题，实现了参数高效且性能更佳的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Looped Transformer 虽然能通过循环利用固定参数模块来提升性能，但在增加循环次数时会面临训练不稳定性问题（源于梯度震荡和残差爆炸）。

Method: 提出了两种无参数修改方案：1) Fully Looped Architecture（全循环架构），通过在各层间分发跨循环信号以抑制残差爆炸；2) Attention Injection（注意力注入），通过重用现有的注意力模块以抑制梯度震荡。

Result: 成功将模型训练的稳定性提升至 12 次循环；在较轻量设置下，下游任务性能平均提升高达 13.2%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 Fully Looped Transformer 有效解决了循环 Transformer 训练中的不稳定性问题，在保持相同参数量的情况下，显著提升了模型训练的稳定性和推理阶段的下游任务性能，并支持通过调整循环次数灵活权衡计算成本与性能。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [65] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对快速变点检测（QCD）在有限/不规则数据集上难以评估性能的问题，本文提出基于生存分析的非参数估计量（KM-ARL和KM-ADD），有效解决了模型选择与评估的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速变点检测（QCD）中的ARL和ADD指标虽在理论研究中占核心地位，但由于现实数据集序列长度有限且分布不规则，传统的估计方法难以直接应用。

Method: 通过类比QCD与生存分析（Survival Analysis），利用Kaplan-Meier估计思想来建模序列截断下的检测概率，并推导了估计偏差界。

Result: 证明了估计量在无需外推时的渐近无偏性；通过模拟和真实数据集实验，验证了该方案在处理数据长度限制时的稳健性，并提升了模型选择的直观性。

Conclusion: 提出的KM-ARL和KM-ADD估计量为处理有限且不规则长度的序列数据提供了稳健且可解释的工具，弥补了QCD理论评价指标在实际应用中的局限性。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [66] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM在交互中因盲目顺从用户反馈而放弃正确答案的问题，ReCrit通过细分交互行为象限和转化感知强化学习，提升了模型在科学推理交互中的抗干扰能力与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在交互式批判（critic interaction）过程中，倾向于被错误的或误导性的用户反馈所左右，从而放弃原本正确的科学解答，这种“唯唯诺诺”行为在科学推理领域具有高风险。

Method: 提出ReCrit框架，通过将交互行为解构为“纠错、唯唯诺诺（Sycophancy）、鲁棒性、边界”四个象限，结合基于转化感知的强化学习策略，以及动态异步滚动（dynamic asynchronous rollout）与长尾自适应补全技术进行优化。

Result: 在ChemBench、TRQA和EarthSE三个科学推理基准测试上，ReCrit显著提升了Qwen3.5系列模型的批判准确率（4B版本从38.15提升至51.49，9B版本从45.40提升至55.59）。

Conclusion: 在科学推理场景下，基于转化逻辑（transition-aware）的强化学习显著优于传统的最终答案奖励方法，能有效提升模型在交互过程中的韧性与正确性。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [67] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BDQ (Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization) 框架，通过引入平坦度度量并采用对角矩阵变换技术重塑异常值分布，有效解决了 LLM 量化中的异常值问题，显著提升了在极端低比特下的推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法难以应对大模型（LLM）中激活值异常值（outliers）导致的严重精度损失。现有的线性变换方法无法完全消除这些异常值带来的集中分布问题。

Method: 提出了“平坦度”（Flatness）度量标准，量化异常值分布的集中程度；引入“双向对角量化”（BDQ）框架，通过学习到的对角变换矩阵，将异常值幅值分散到矩阵不同维度，从而补偿量化误差。

Result: 在 LLaMA-3-8B 模型上实现 W4A4 量化且精度下降小于 1%；在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B 模型的 W2A4KV16 极端测试中，性能差距相较于 SOTA 方法缩小了 39.1%。

Conclusion: BDQ 提供了一种有效的后训练量化方案，通过对角线变换平滑异常值分布，显著降低了 LLM 在低比特量化下的精度损失，设定了新的性能基准。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [68] [Block-Sphere Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972)
*Heesang Ann,Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了现有旋转量化方法的理论评价体系，指出不同算法适用于不同的失真指标，并提出了基于球形几何的改进算法 BlockQuant，该算法在理论和实践中均展现出比现有方法更优的量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转量化方法（如 EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant）缺乏统一的评价标准，导致性能对比分析困难；同时，现有的坐标级量化方法在维护旋转后嵌入的复杂几何特性方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 1. 统一理论框架：对比了现有旋转量化方法（EDEN, RabitQ, TurboQuant）的性能差异，揭示了其优势依赖于特定的失真评价指标。
2. 提出 BlockQuant：设计了一套基于旋转向量球形几何特性的块量化算法，通过在球面上对向量块进行量化，更忠实地保留旋转后嵌入的几何结构。

Result: 理论分析表明 BlockQuant 在重构 MSE 和期望内积失真方面均优于现有基准方法；实验结果证实该方法在实际应用（嵌入数据集与长上下文 LLM 推理）中实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: BlockQuant 通过在球面上进行块量化，不仅在理论上为重构均方误差（MSE）和期望内积失真提供了更强的保证，并且在实际大规模嵌入数据集及长上下文大模型推理任务中验证了其优越性，克服了既有旋转量化方法在特定评价指标下的局限。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provide a unified theoretical comparison of these methods and show that their relative advantages are criterion-dependent rather than absolute: EDEN and TurboQuant are favorable for MSE distortion, EDEN is also effective for expected inner-product distortion, and RabitQ provides strong high-probability control. This comparison further clarifies that EDEN provides particularly strong guarantees for expected distortion measures. As our second contribution, we introduce Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), a new rotation-based block quantization algorithm designed around the spherical geometry of randomly rotated vectors. Unlike coordinate-wise quantizers, BlockQuant quantizes blocks on the sphere, preserving the geometry of rotated embeddings more faithfully. We prove that this block-spherical design theoretically improves over the baselines considered in this paper for both reconstruction MSE and expected inner-product distortion. Our experiments on real embedding datasets and long-context LLM inference tasks show practical gains that are consistent with our theoretical improvements.

</details>


### [69] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PROWL框架，通过训练一个对抗性策略主动挖掘视频世界模型的失败案例，并利用PAT缓冲区聚焦微调这些弱点，从而显著提升了世界模型处理关键交互任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频世界模型在短时视觉呈现上表现良好，但在下游规划和决策任务至关重要的“少见且关键的交互转折点”上表现不可靠，而传统被动演示数据无法有效覆盖这些关键状态。

Method: 提出了PROWL框架，包括：1. KL约束的对抗性课程学习：通过策略模型寻找世界模型的高误差（失败）轨迹；2. 对抗性训练循环：利用这些轨迹持续微调世界模型；3. 优先排序的对抗轨迹（PAT）缓冲区：聚焦于未解决的错误模式，避免过度学习已掌握的案例。

Result: 在MineRL框架中进行了评估，证明了PROWL在分布外（OOD）轨迹上优于被动学习模型，有效地识别并修正了模型弱点，并揭示了弱行为约束下可能出现的奖励劫持行为。

Conclusion: 世界模型的鲁棒性不仅依赖于数据规模，更依赖于通过对抗性训练策略主动生成高质量、信息丰富的训练数据。PROWL证明了平衡探索性故障发现与行为正则化对于提升视频世界模型在关键交互场景下的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [70] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型Transformer架构，通过特殊的分块注意力掩码，使其既具备仅解码器模型的高效训练能力，又拥有编码器-解码器模型的高效推理性能，显著降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统编码器-解码器模型在大规模预训练中存在的监督稀疏和动态序列长度问题，同时利用其优于仅解码器模型的推理效率（显著减少KV缓存和计算量）。

Method: 提出了“基于分块的双解码器”（Block-based Double Decoders）架构，引入双重因果分块注意力掩码（doubly-causal block-based attention masks），支持全损失监督训练以及静态序列打包。

Result: 在大规模扩展律实验中，该模型表现优于编码器-解码器模型，并媲美仅解码器模型；在推理阶段，KV缓存和每Token计算量至少减少了2/3。

Conclusion: 基于分块的双解码器架构成功弥合了编码器-解码器与仅解码器模型之间的差距，在保持仅解码器训练效率的同时，实现了显著的推理加速，是高性能大模型的有力候选方案。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [71] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过SAE算法在大模型内部提取出控制情感与文学风格的“基元特征”，实现了对模型输出文风与情感的精准编辑，并以此揭示了不同模型在情感表达机制上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型（LLM）内部隐含的“文学基元（literary primitives）”架构，旨在解释模型如何通过内部特征组合生成特定的情感表达和语体风格，从而提高模型生成内容的可控性。

Method: 1. 对Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct和Gemma 2 9B-IT在中间层残差流应用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取特征；2. 通过特征打分与操控（steering）触发特定情感和风格；3. 采用logit-lens与5-LLM评委面板（27类情感分类体系）进行定量验证。

Result: 1. 识别出命名门、第一人称自指簇、语体调节器和复合情感特征四类基元；2. Llama实现了27/27的情感覆盖，Gemma实现23/27；3. Llama倾向于显式命名情感，而Gemma倾向于通过场景和意象暗示，表现出跨模型结构的生成机制差异。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）成功提取并操控了大模型中的文学语义基元，证明了模型内部存在能够精准操控情感与风格的可解释特征，并验证了这些特征在生成模型中的因果效应。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [72] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTrain 是一种新型分布式训练系统，通过 VPS 抽象和智能调度，实现了大模型训练过程中毫秒级的在线并行策略重构，性能远超传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型训练框架大多基于静态执行模型，无法适应现代训练中常见的计算资源波动、RLHF阶段切换及集群弹性伸缩，导致重构开销巨大。

Method: 1. 提出虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象：将分布式训练状态统一映射为逻辑坐标空间，简化复杂的物理并行布局转换；2. 状态路由与转换层：实现内存感知且无死锁的秩局部（rank-local）数据传输；3. 弹性设备管理器：将新拓扑构建进程与训练负载重叠，有效掩蔽重构开销。

Result: 在高达 235B 参数的稠密模型和 MoE 模型上，DynaTrain 可在 2-4.36 秒内完成复杂重构，性能较现有基于检查点的弹性系统提升了三个数量级。

Conclusion: DynaTrain 通过引入虚拟参数空间（VPS）成功实现了大模型的亚秒级在线并行重构，解决了传统静态训练框架在动态环境（资源波动、RLHF等）下的性能与灵活性瓶颈，实现了接近零开销的拓扑迁移。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [73] [Metric-Gradient Projection for Stable Multi-Agent Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18809)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Sizhe Tang,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体协作学习中因策略耦合导致的动力学不稳定问题，本文提出了 HPML 方法，通过 Hodge 分解将联合更新场投影为稳定的度量梯度流，有效提升了学习的稳定性和收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用和博弈（general-sum）环境下的多智能体强化学习中，智能体之间的策略更新相互耦合，导致优化景观动态变化。这种耦合产生的循环交互动力学常导致学习过程缓慢或不稳定，现有的正则化或信用分配方法难以彻底解决这一根本的动力学问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 HPML（Hodge-Projected Multi-agent Learning）的新方法。该方法将多智能体系统的联合更新场视为 $L^2$ 空间中的向量场，并利用 Hodge 分解，通过求解泊松型方程，将其投影到最接近的度量梯度势流上，并利用基于图的神经算子实现该投影。

Result: 在受控实验中验证了其几何机制的有效性；在 CTDE（集中式训练，分散式执行）基准测试中，HPML 能够作为即插即用的组件，显著改善 MARL 的训练稳定性和归一化回报。

Conclusion: HPML 是一种有效的多智能体学习稳定性增强方法。它通过理论推导证明了投影后的动力学具备 Lyapunov 势函数，并建立了平衡间隙界限。实证结果表明，HPML 可以作为一种通用的插件层，显著提升多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: General-sum multi-agent learning is often governed by a stacked update field in which each agent's policy update changes the optimization landscape faced by the others. This coupling can entangle an integrable component of collective improvement with cyclic interaction dynamics, leading to slow or unstable multi-agent learning. Existing approaches, such as regularization, credit assignment, and consensus methods, stabilize MARL through local or algorithmic modifications; HPML complements them by projecting the joint update field onto a metric-gradient component.
