We introduce \textbf{HPML} (\textbf{H}odge-\textbf{P}rojected \textbf{M}ulti-agent \textbf{L}earning), which views the joint update field of a multi-agent system as an element of an $L^2$ space of vector fields and computes a Hodge-type projection onto the closest metric-gradient potential flow. HPML follows the projected component as the update direction, yielding the closest metric-gradient field under the chosen metric and sampling measure. The projection is defined variationally, characterized by a Poisson-type equation, and implemented through graph-based and amortized neural realizations that recover projected directions from samples. We show that the projected dynamics admit a Lyapunov potential and yield equilibrium-gap bounds with an explicit additive non-potentiality term. Controlled experiments validate the geometric mechanism, and CTDE benchmarks show improved stability and normalized return when HPML is used as a plug-in projection layer in MARL pipelines.

</details>


### [74] [Not All Tokens Are Worth Caching: Learning Semantic-Aware Eviction for LLM Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18825)
*Shaoke Fang,Ziang Li,Wenfei Wu,Jiatong Ji,Qingsong Liu,Ruizhi Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAECache是一种针对LLM前缀缓存的语义自适应驱逐策略。它通过挖掘不同Token类型的复用差异，并利用在线学习自动调优，实现了比传统LRU更高效的KV缓存管理，显著改善了LLM服务的首字延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前缀缓存驱逐策略（如LRU）未能区分不同Token类型的复用潜力，导致在GPU内存受限时缓存利用率低下。研究发现Token复用率存在高达756倍的差异。

Method: 1. 多队列架构：根据任务类型（会话级与模板级）对KV块进行分类路由；2. 语义感知权重机制：通过在线反馈学习不同Token类型的复用价值；3. 全自适应在线学习方案：自动调节包括定时、衰减和阈值在内的核心参数，无需人工干预。

Result: 在多种异构负载下，SAECache相对于生产级的基准策略，首字延迟（TTFT）提升了1.4倍至2.7倍；同时，该方法完全避免了固定参数策略在负载不匹配时性能大幅下降（高达2.7倍）的缺陷。

Conclusion: SAECache通过语义感知的方法有效提升了LLM服务中前缀缓存的命中率，特别是在负载动态变化的情况下，不仅提供了显著的性能提升，还具有极强的自适应性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key optimization in Large Language Model (LLM) serving, reusing attention Key-Value (KV) states across requests with shared prompt prefixes to reduce expensive prefill computation. However, its benefit depends critically on the eviction policy as GPU memory is scarce, and existing policies such as LRU largely treat cached blocks uniformly. This view ignores a fundamental property of LLM prompts: not all tokens are equally worth caching. We show that different token types within a prompt, including system prompts, user queries, tool outputs, model responses, and chain-of-thought reasoning, exhibit up to 756x variation in reuse rates, yet no existing eviction policy exploits this signal. In this paper, we present SAECache (Semantic-Adaptive Eviction for prefix caches), a semantic-adaptive prefix cache eviction policy that addresses this gap through three innovations: (1) a multi-queue architecture that routes KV blocks to task-specific queues with tailored priority metrics, capturing both session reuse in multi-turn requests and structural reuse in templated single-turn requests; (2) a semantic-aware token weighting mechanism that learns the reuse value of different token types online through eviction feedback; and (3) a fully adaptive online learning schema for all parameter updates, including log-normal timing parameters, position decay power, queue weights, and meta-parameters, which eliminates manual tuning and enables automatic adaptation to deployment-specific workload characteristics. Through extensive evaluation across heterogeneous workloads, we demonstrate that SAECache achieves 1.4x-2.7x TTFT improvement over production-style baselines, while fixed-parameter alternatives can degrade by up to 2.7x under workload mismatch -- a failure mode our adaptive approach avoids entirely.

</details>


### [75] [INAR-VL: Input-Aware Routing for Edge-Cloud Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18853)
*Ahmed Šabanović,Paul Joe Maliakel,Ivona Brandić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INAR-VL是一个用于视觉-语言模型的轻量级边缘-云端路由系统，通过根据查询复杂度动态调度推理任务，有效地在边缘和云端之间实现了性能与资源的最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）边缘部署存在“准确性与延迟”的权衡困境：云端运行虽然精度高但延迟大且耗能，而纯边缘运行虽然速度快但模型能力受限。此外，实际场景中图像质量和推理要求的复杂性差异巨大，传统的静态分配策略难以适应这种动态需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为INAR-VL的轻量级边缘-云端路由系统。该系统采用了两层部署架构，通过视觉（图像）和语言（文本）的轻量级复杂度指标，实时评估查询难度，从而决定请求是在边缘设备本地执行还是卸载至云端执行。

Result: 在视觉问答（VQA）任务测试中，INAR-VL将36%的请求分配至边缘处理，在成功保留云端97%推理精度的基础上，使整体推理延迟降低了24%，能耗降低了26%。

Conclusion: INAR-VL通过轻量级的复杂性评估机制，实现了视觉-语言模型在边缘与云端之间的动态路由，在保持高预测精度的同时，显著降低了延迟和能耗，有效解决了边缘部署中的性能与资源权衡问题。

Abstract: Edge deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) faces a tradeoff between latency and accuracy: cloud execution provides high-quality predictions but incurs communication delay and energy cost, while edge-only execution is faster but less accurate due to limited model capacity. This trade-off is further complicated by heterogeneity in image quality and reasoning complexity, making static placement suboptimal. We present INAR-VL, a lightweight edge-cloud routing system for multimodal inference in a two-tier deployment. INAR-VL maintains complementary VLMs across edge and cloud and uses lightweight image and text complexity signals to guide routing and model selection, executing simple queries locally while offloading complex ones when beneficial. Evaluation on visual question answering shows that INAR-VL executes 36% of requests on the edge, reduces latency by 24%, lowers energy by 26%, and preserves 97% of cloud-level accuracy.

</details>


### [76] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多阶段流水线系统（如 LLM Agent），作者提出了 PASC 方法，通过将联合覆盖问题简化为单一标量计算，在保证严格覆盖概率的前提下，显著提升了多阶段系统的鲁棒性和不确定性量化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 NLP 和 LLM 系统多为多阶段流水线，现有的不确定性量化方法要么无法保证阶段间的联合覆盖（独立校准），要么过于保守（Bonferroni 联合界），导致处理长链路流水线时效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种名为“流水线感知分裂一致性预测（PASC）”的方法，该方法通过计算流水线每一阶段的最大非一致性分数的联合分布，将其转化为一个单一的一致性预测问题，从而在有限样本下提供联合覆盖的分布自由保证。

Result: 在三阶段 NER-NED-实体类型标注流水线上，PASC 实现了 96.4% 的端到端覆盖率（显著高于 Bonferroni 的 93.4% 和独立 CP 的 86.5%）；在存在分布偏移和更长流水线（K=6）的情况下，PASC 依然能稳健地保持目标覆盖率且计算效率更高。

Conclusion: PASC 是一种高效且具备统计保障的多阶段流水线不确定性量化方法。它通过将多阶段联合覆盖问题归约为单一标量的一致性预测问题，实现了对流水线各阶段的同时覆盖，且在保持预测集大小不变的情况下显著优于传统的独立校准及 Bonferroni 方法。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [77] [Data-Free Client Contribution Estimation via Logit Maximization for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18892)
*Asim Ukaye,Nurbek Tastan,Mubarak Abdu-Aguye,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中因数据分布不均导致的性能不振，本文提出 CELM 框架，通过分析客户端更新中的类别证据来实现加权聚合，在保护隐私的前提下显著提升了模型对少数类的处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在处理计算机视觉任务时，常面临严重的数据不平衡和标签偏置问题，导致聚合过程中偏向主要类别，导致少数类性能下降。现有方法通常依赖共享原始数据、元数据或辅助公共数据集，这存在隐私与合规风险。

Method: 提出了一种基于 Logit 最大化的数据无关（data-free）框架 CELM。该方法通过服务器探测客户端更新以获取类别证据分数（evidence scores），构建跨客户端证据矩阵，并据此计算各客户端对各类的贡献权重，通过单形约束（simplex constraints）和动量平滑（momentum smoothing）实现更稳健的模型聚合。

Result: 在代表性视觉基准测试中，CELM 相比标准聚合协议能更好地处理非独立同分布及病态标签划分场景，在不增加任何额外数据交换的前提下提升了模型整体性能。

Conclusion: CELM 框架有效缓解了联邦学习中数据不平衡和标签偏置带来的性能退化问题，在无需额外辅助数据的情况下，显著提升了模型在非独立同分布（Non-IID）场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning of computer vision models, where privacy and regulatory constraints prevent centralizing data across devices or organizations. However, practical FL deployments often exhibit severe class imbalance and label skew, causing standard aggregation protocols to overfit dominant clients and degrade minority-class performance. We propose a data-free, class-wise contribution estimation and aggregation framework based on logit maximization (CELM) that does not require sharing raw data, client metadata, or auxiliary public datasets. The FL server probes client updates to obtain class-wise evidence scores and assembles a cross-client evidence matrix, which quantifies both per-class competence and class coverage. Using this matrix, we compute contribution weights that upweight clients providing strong, discriminative evidence for underrepresented classes. The resulting aggregation is stable due to simplex constraints and momentum smoothing, and it remains compatible with standard FL training pipelines. We evaluate the approach on representative vision benchmarks under controlled non-IID and pathological label splits, demonstrating that CELM-based aggregation improves robustness to imbalance and statistical heterogeneity, while yielding better performance without requiring any additional data exchange.

</details>


### [78] [When Individually Calibrated Models Become Collectively Miscalibrated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18858)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了独立校准的模型在合并预测时会因策略交互而产生系统性失准，提出采用VCG机制进行聚合可实现激励兼容并显著提升预测的稳健性与准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设多个已校准模型的聚合仍是校准的，但该假设在智能体存在策略交互（如依赖重叠数据训练）的环境中往往失效。本文旨在揭示这种系统性错误，并寻找更稳健的聚合机制。

Method: 1. 理论建模：分析Brier分数下的多智能体博弈行为，证明当预测模型存在正相关性时会产生系统性偏差；2. 机制设计：引入VCG（Vickrey-Clarke-Groves）激励机制来对齐智能体目标；3. 实验验证：在NSL-KDD、UNSW-NB15及信用卡欺诈数据集上对比Brier聚合与VCG聚合的表现。

Result: 理论证明了正相关的模型会导致对正类概率的系统性低估（PoA > 1）；实验显示在使用VCG机制后，系统在数据稀疏及对抗环境下表现出极强的稳健性，且能有效应对分布偏移。

Conclusion: 在多智能体概率预测中，传统的模型聚合方法存在个体校准但群体失准的问题。引入基于VCG机制的激励一致性聚合方案可有效解决这一偏差，在提升稳健性的同时实现近乎最优的预测性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction systems often aggregate probability estimates from multiple models into a single decision. A common assumption is that if each model is individually calibrated, the aggregate prediction will also be well calibrated. We show that this assumption fails in multi-agent settings: individually calibrated predictors can become collectively miscalibrated when their predictions interact strategically, in the game-theoretic sense of Brier-optimal local response, even without deliberate coordination.
  This phenomenon arises naturally when agents are independently trained on overlapping data. We prove that under Brier-score-based aggregation with positively correlated beliefs, each agent's individually optimal report systematically underestimates the positive-class probability, yielding a Price of Anarchy greater than one whenever Cov(b_i, b_j) > 0.
  In a canonical setting (n = 5 agents, pairwise correlation = 0.5, base rate = 0.3), the empirically measured PoA in false-negative rate reaches 7.25x. In contrast, VCG-based aggregation aligns incentives by rewarding marginal contribution, achieving dominant-strategy incentive compatibility and near-optimal performance.
  Experiments on three real-world datasets (NSL-KDD, UNSW-NB15, Credit Card Fraud) show that VCG provides strong robustness while maintaining comparable accuracy. It performs particularly well in data-sparse and adversarial settings, and adaptive weighting further improves performance under distribution shift.

</details>


### [79] [ScheduleFree+: Scaling Learning-Rate-Free & Schedule-Free Learning to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095)
*Aaron Defazio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ScheduleFree+，通过优化 Schedule-Free Learning 解决了其在大模型训练中的扩展难题，并在长周期训练中显著超越了传统的 WSD 调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Schedule-Free Learning 在大模型（LLM）训练中的表现仅限于小规模，缺乏在大规模场景下的有效扩展方案。

Method: 提出了 ScheduleFree+ 方法，针对大规模批次和模型尺寸进行了优化，实现了学习率无关（learning-rate-free）和无调度（schedule-free）的训练。

Result: 在 1000 tokens/parameter 的长周期训练中，ScheduleFree+ 比当前最先进（SOTA）的调度策略性能提升了 31%。

Conclusion: ScheduleFree+ 是一种高效的大模型训练方法，特别适合长周期训练，并在大规模预训练中通过模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论基础，优于传统的 WSD 调度策略。

Abstract: Schedule-Free Learning has shown promise as a practical anytime training method for machine learning, showing success across dozens of standard benchmark problems. However, strong performance for LLM training has only been demonstrated at small scales. We identify a number of fixes necessary to scale up Schedule-Free Learning to larger batch sizes and model sizes, and present a learning-rate-free and schedule-free method (ScheduleFree+) for training large language models which greatly outperforms Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedules. We also demonstrate that Schedule-Free Learning is most effective for long duration training, and at 1000 tokens per parameter, it outperforms SOTA schedules by 31%. Schedule-Free Learning provides a theoretical foundation for the use of model averaging and checkpoint merging during pretraining.

</details>


### [80] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Transformer通过“Möbius吸引子”架构和“级联监督”机制，能克服梯度衰减并自动习得深度的超位置推理能力，解决了深度学习中推理机制的可达性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Transformer是否可以通过梯度下降自动学习“超位置”推理能力（在有限深度的前向传递中并行处理推理边界），以解决手写超位置方案在复杂优化环境下的缺失问题。

Method: 1. 理论分析：利用动力系统方法识别出层间动态为1D Möbius映射，其零集即为最优解流形。2. 监督方案设计：提出级联监督（Cascade Supervision），相比端到端监督，能有效维持梯度持久性。3. 实验验证：在$D=3$的Erdős-Rényi图上验证了预测的余弦相似度。

Result: 在Erdős-Rényi图的连通性任务中，端到端监督因梯度衰减表现不佳，而级联监督实现了与理论预测高度一致的结果（实验值0.69 vs 预测值0.71）。

Conclusion: 通过Möbius吸引子（架构）与级联监督（Cascade Supervision）的结合，研究证实了Transformer可以自发学习实现超位置（Superposition）推理，解决了梯度下降在排列对称鞍点中难以收敛的问题。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了“量子对抗机器学习”领域，分析了量子机器学习模型在对抗性攻击下的脆弱性，并总结了现有的防御方案、理论基础及未来面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子机器学习在处理复杂任务方面具有潜力，但其与经典机器学习一样，面临对抗性攻击的威胁。为了保护量子人工智能系统的安全，有必要深入探讨其漏洞、攻击机制及量子增强的防御策略。

Method: 通过文献综述（Survey）的方法，详细概述了量子对抗机器学习的现状，涵盖了现有的攻击手段、防御策略、理论基础、新兴趋势以及面临的关键挑战。

Result: 该综述系统性地整理并分类了量子对抗机器学习的主要攻击方式和防御方法，明确了该领域当前的研究进展及其在处理复杂机器学习任务时的安全局限性。

Conclusion: 量子对抗机器学习是一个新兴领域，旨在应对量子机器学习模型面临的安全威胁。通过系统梳理攻击与防御策略、总结理论基础，该领域正处于探索关键挑战和确立未来研究方向的阶段。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [82] [EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对序列预测中早期观测模糊导致推断失效的问题，本文提出EviTrack框架，通过维护竞争性轨迹假设并延迟决策，实现了比增加采样更有效的稳健推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决序列预测中“延迟消歧”（delayed disambiguation）的问题。在这一场景下，早期观察具有模糊性，多种潜在解释均合理，传统的边缘推断方法往往会导致过早收敛或在获得确凿证据后难以恢复。

Method: 提出了EviTrack框架，该框架基于潜在轨迹（latent trajectories）进行操作，维护一组竞争性的轨迹假设，并利用基于证据和似然比的选择机制来延迟决策，直至获得充分数据支持。

Result: 在受控的合成基准测试中，EviTrack在同等推断预算下，显著优于基于采样的基准方法，并表现出更快的消歧后恢复速度。

Conclusion: 在延迟消歧场景中，相比于单纯增加采样覆盖率，采用轨迹层面的假设选择策略是实现稳健序列推断的关键。

Abstract: Sequential prediction is challenging in regimes of delayed disambiguation, where early observations are ambiguous and multiple latent explanations remain plausible until sufficient evidence accumulates. Standard approaches based on marginal inference struggle in this setting, either collapsing uncertainty prematurely or failing to recover once informative evidence arrives.
  We introduce EviTrack, a test-time inference framework that operates over latent trajectories rather than marginal states. EviTrack maintains a set of competing trajectory hypotheses and applies evidence- and likelihood-ratio-based selection to delay commitment until supported by data, drawing inspiration from hypothesis management in multiple hypothesis tracking and track-before-detect.
  To evaluate this setting, we construct a controlled synthetic benchmark with known latent ground truth that explicitly exhibits delayed disambiguation. At matched inference budget, EviTrack substantially outperforms sampling-based baselines, achieving faster post-disambiguation recovery.
  These results show that, in delayed disambiguation regimes, moderate trajectory-level selection is more effective than increasing sampling coverage, highlighting selection over sampling as a key principle for reliable sequential inference.

</details>


### [83] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大型语言模型在复杂推理任务中，全参数微调开销大且传统 LoRA 性能受限的问题，作者提出了 Hybrid-LoRA，通过智能筛选模块结合部分全参微调与 LoRA 优化，实现了高效且高性能的模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有强化学习微调范式中，全参数微调（FFT）成本过高，而传统参数高效微调（PEFT/LoRA）在复杂推理任务中性能显著逊于 FFT 的矛盾。

Method: 提出 Hybrid-LoRA 框架，根据“Hybrid-LoRA 评分”筛选出对低秩适应敏感度低的模块进行全参数更新，其余模块则采用 LoRA 进行微调，从而在有限的参数预算下最大化模型性能。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，Hybrid-LoRA 的表现优于现有的 PEFT 方法，最高提升了 5.65% 的性能，有效弥补了 PEFT 与 FFT 之间的差距。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA 在 10% 全参数微调预算下，能够达到甚至逼近全参数微调（FFT）的性能，并显著优于现有的四种 PEFT 基准方法（平均提升 4.36%），证明了混合微调策略在平衡计算开销与推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>


### [84] [The Routing and Filtering Structure of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18826)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将注意力机制拆解为“路由”与“过滤”两部分，发现其实际路由需求远低于现有模型分配。基于这一发现提出的 $S$-$D$ 注意力机制实现了两者的有效解耦，并揭示了注意力随层深变化的“谱级联”结构，从而为模型的高效压缩与架构设计提供了理论依据和直接的量化手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的注意力机制中，$QK^{\top}$ 矩阵将“路由”（信息重分布）和“过滤”（相互关联度缩放）两种计算纠缠在一起。研究者试图通过解耦这两种组件，深入理解注意力机制的工作原理，并探索优化模型参数效率和计算复杂度的路径。

Method: 1. 将注意力矩阵 $QK^{\top}$ 分解为斜对称的“路由”组件和对称的“过滤”组件。
2. 提出 $S$-$D$ 注意力（S-D attention），通过参数化方式实现路由与过滤的解耦。
3. 引入稳定性约束（$\mathrm{Re}(λ) \le 0$），使其能在无层归一化（Layer Normalization）下稳定训练。
4. 分析模型内部的谱级联特性，并将此作为简化模型架构的依据（如在浅层采用线性注意力）。

Result: 1. 发现路由操作实际上处于低秩状态，远低于传统权重核分配的容量。
2. 路由呈现随深度演化的“谱级联”规律，有效秩由浅入深扩展。
3. 利用级联特性，在125M模型的前七层使用线性映射仅导致小于5%的困惑度损失。
4. 基于级联分配的架构在减少47%-65%注意力参数的同时，仅带来较小的困惑度增长。

Conclusion: S-D注意力机制揭示了模型中“路由（Routing）”与“过滤（Filtering）”的解耦结构，证明了路由行为呈现谱级联（spectral cascade）特性。通过利用这一特性，可以在保持较低困惑度（PPL）增长的前提下，大幅简化低层注意力结构（如替换为线性注意力），从而实现模型参数的有效压缩与效率优化。

Abstract: The attention interaction matrix $QK^{\top}$ contains two entangled computations: a skew-symmetric component that redistributes information between positions (routing) and a symmetric component that scales mutual relevance (filtering). We decompose 1776 heads across five pretrained transformers and find routing operating at low rank, well below the routing capacity allocated by the weight kernel. We introduce $S$-$D$ attention as a diagnostic parameterization that disentangles routing from filtering by construction with guaranteed stability ($\mathrm{Re}(λ) \le 0$) and trains stably without layer normalization. When disentangled and unnormalized, routing self-organizes into a spectral cascade, effective rank $2$ at the first layer, expanding with depth across six scales from 7M to 355M parameters. The cascade predicts where attention can be simplified: linearizing the first seven layers of 125M $S$-$D$ attention costs ${<}5\%$ perplexity, whereas standard attention collapses under the same intervention. The linearizable region widens with depth. Replacing the first four layers with ELU+1 linear attention reaches within $1.4\%$ of baseline at full head dimension. Cascade-allocated architectures trade attention parameters for perplexity ($47\%-65\%$ fewer attention parameters at $+3.9\%$ to $+8.4\%$ PPL). The routing-filtering decomposition makes the spectral budget legible; the cascade makes it actionable.

</details>


### [85] [Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18829)
*Zibo Diao,Jingchu Gai,Xinyue Ai,Zhang Zhang,Zhenyu He,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LADS 通过根据查询语义和历史计数引入关联性随机采样，在确保正常用户获得高质量响应的前提下，通过降低蒸馏数据质量来对抗多账户模型蒸馏攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的商业生成式模型面临“模型蒸馏”攻击（通过获取输出数据训练低成本竞争模型）。现有防御手段存在两难困境：要么降低正常用户的生成质量，要么容易被多账户查询策略破解。

Method: 提出 LADS (Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling) 采样方案。利用查询的语义内容和用户查询计数作为私有种子生成随机性。对于正常用户，每次生成相互独立；对于使用多账户的蒸馏者，相同的语义查询会导致生成的随机性关联，从而降低蒸馏数据的多样性和最终模型的泛化能力。

Result: 理论上，利用一致收敛理论证明了 LADS 能有效降低蒸馏者泛化差距的收敛速度。实验表明，在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成任务中，LADS 能显著削弱蒸馏模型性能，且对单用户响应的统计保真度无损。

Conclusion: LADS 是一种有效的反蒸馏方案，能显著降低蒸馏模型的泛化性能，同时保持对正常用户零影响（无损体验），在学术与商业应用中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Frontier commercial generative models face a growing threat from distillation, whereby a distiller harvests generated responses and trains a competing model of its own at drastically lower cost. Existing defenses either rely on modifying the models outputs, thereby sacrificing response quality for benign users, or on behavioral detection methods, which can be readily circumvented by distributing queries across multiple accounts. In this work, we propose Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS), a novel sampling scheme specifically designed to counter multi-account distillation while maintaining a lossless experience for benign users. Concretely, LADS derives the randomness underlying each generation from a private seed determined by the semantic content of the query and the number of times the user has queried the model. By construction, every benign user receives a response independently sampled from the original model at each visit, and thus experiences no distortion. In contrast, for a distiller, different accounts share latent randomness whenever their queries fall in the same semantic bucket. As a result, the harvested data becomes correlated, potentially reducing sample diversity and degrading generalization. Using uniform convergence theory, we show that LADS provably degrades the convergence rate of the distillers generalization gap relative to standard i.i.d. sampling in both unconditional and conditional generation settings. Experiments on image generation, mathematical reasoning, and code generation confirm that LADS substantially degrades the performance of distilled students while preserving exact statistical fidelity for individual users.

</details>


### [86] [SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20157)
*Sudheer Tubati,Amit Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对音乐流媒体欺诈检测，SAGE 引入了一种反事实感知的前沿方法，通过创新的采样与集成门控机制，精准区分复杂异常行为与真实欺诈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流媒体欺诈严重损害平台与创作者利益；传统方法难以区分恶意欺诈与正当的异常行为（如超级粉丝或助眠音乐播放），且存在正例-无标签（PU）学习中的表示偏差问题。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架，包含：1. 基于 SimHash 的分层采样（解决表示偏差）；2. 反事实感知负采样（从无标签数据中挖掘置信负样本）；3. 模块化门控集成架构（融合 Mahalanobis 距离与 k-NN 密度，支持自适应精度-召回率权衡）。

Result: 在留出数据集上表现出极高的精确率与召回率；无需修改核心架构即可同时处理用户级与艺术家级欺诈，验证了方法的普适性。

Conclusion: SAGE 是一种通用且鲁棒的流媒体欺诈检测框架，通过反事实感知负采样和模块化门控集成，有效解决了正负样本不平衡及表示偏差问题，同时具备良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.

</details>


### [87] [Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805)
*Zinuo You,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对不规则多变量时间序列预测，本文提出了LLapDiff框架。它跳过了传统的循序积分，通过在拉普拉斯域对潜在动力学进行模态参数化，实现了高效的长周期预测与缺失值处理，有效克服了传统方法存在的时序失真与漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非规则多变量时间序列，现有的离散方法（如重采样）会扭曲时序结构，而连续时间模型（如神经ODE）在长周期预测中容易产生积累性积分误差（漂移），本文旨在寻求一种既能保留时间连续性又能避免累积误差的长周期预测生成式框架。

Method: 1. 提出Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff)，将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹进行整体生成。2. 利用随机端口哈密顿动力学进行稳定模态参数化。3. 在拉普拉斯域内通过可学习的复共轭极点参数化均值演化，实现对不规则时间戳的直接评估。4. 引入基于更新平均分析（renewal-averaging analysis）的间隙感知历史摘要器，优化对观测间隔的处理。

Result: 在长周期预测任务中，LLapDiff的表现优于现有基准模型，且其生成的连续时间特性支持对历史缺失值进行直接插值。

Conclusion: LLapDiff通过拉普拉斯域的模态参数化与随机端口哈密顿动力学相结合，成功在不牺牲长周期预测准确性的前提下，解决了不规则多变量时间序列处理中的时序失真与计算偏移问题。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series impose a trade-off for long-horizon forecasting: discrete methods can distort temporal structure via re-gridding, while continuous-time models often require sequential solvers prone to drift. To bridge this gap, we present Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff), a generative framework that models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory, enabling horizon-wide generation without step-by-step integration over physical time. We guide the reverse process utilizing a stable modal parameterization motivated by stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, and parameterize its mean evolution in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles, enabling direct evaluation over irregular timestamps. We also link continuous dynamics to irregular observations through renewal-averaging analysis, which maps sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles and motivates a gap-aware history summarizer. Extensive experiments show that LLapDiff improves over baselines in long-horizon forecasting, and its continuous-time generative nature supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps. Code is available at https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion.

</details>


### [88] [In-Context Learning Operates as Concept Subspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18830)
*Wei Tang,Xinyan Jiang,Fakhri Karray,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过“概念子空间”理论分析与因果干预实验，证明了大型语言模型在处理结构化ICL任务时，主要利用一个低维、任务对齐的子空间进行推理，而非依赖全维空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的回归和贝叶斯模型解释了ICL的概率机制，以及 mechanistic interpretability 研究定位了激活方向，但大模型是否真的通过“低维概念推理”来处理结构化演示仍不明确。

Method: 提出“概念子空间”视角，结合岭回归和最小二乘法进行理论建模；在CounterFact数据集上进行因果干预实验（激活修补），通过比对特定子空间与补集子空间的性能差异来验证假设；并在Llama-3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上进行了跨模型与跨语言验证。

Result: 对于Llama-3-8B，在4096维残差流中，仅需68-73维的概念子空间即可恢复78.8%的性能，而补集子空间恢复率为0%；概念交换实验成功引导预测，且模型在多模型及跨语言任务中表现出一致性。

Conclusion: 研究证实了概念子空间是结构化任务中ICL行为的重要、紧凑且任务对齐的调节器。虽然这不代表完整电路的恢复，但表明大模型通过在低维子空间中进行概念推理来执行ICL。

Abstract: Regression and Bayesian accounts of in-context learning (ICL) explain how demonstrations can induce predictors, while mechanistic analyses often identify compact activation directions that steer prompted behavior. However, it remains unclear whether structured demonstrations induce low-dimensional concept inference. We study this question through a concept-subspace view of ICL, in which tasks vary only along intrinsic concept coordinates, although inputs are observed in a high-dimensional ambient space. For ridge and least-squares ICL proxies, prediction decomposes exactly into concept-coordinate regression and off-subspace leakage. Under block-diagonal or near-block-diagonal covariance assumptions, the leading estimation and nuisance-sensitivity terms scale with the dimension of the concept subspace, while residual effects are controlled by cross-subspace coupling. This separation gives a mechanistic prediction: recoverable task information should concentrate in a low-dimensional, task-aligned activation subspace. On CounterFact-derived multi-relation prompts with Llama-3-8B, a 68--73-dimensional subspace of the 4096-dimensional residual stream restores 78.8% of the clean--corrupted accuracy gap, whereas patching the complementary subspace restores 0%. Concept swaps redirect predictions toward injected relations, while random and cross-task matched-rank controls are largely ineffective. Additional experiments on Qwen2.5-7B and a controlled cross-lingual rule task show the same qualitative pattern. These results support concept subspaces as compact, task-aligned mediators of recoverable ICL behavior in structured task families, without implying full-circuit recovery.

</details>


### [89] [Automated Big Data Quality Assessment using Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18833)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Rima Kilany,Mitri Haber,Ali Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于知识图谱嵌入的新型自动化数据质量评估框架，通过建模数据集上下文与评估规则，实现了更精准、个性化的数据质量评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模数据质量评估解决方案难以实现准确的“上下文感知”（context-aware）。传统评估方法多依赖严格匹配，忽略了数据集具体的应用背景和特性，导致评估方案不够精准。

Method: 提出了一种基于知识图谱嵌入（Knowledge Graph Embeddings）的方法；通过构建包含上下文特征和质量评估规则的知识图谱，利用图嵌入预测输入数据集上下文与质量规则之间的缺失边，并引入数值化边属性来对评估指标进行加权，从而生成定制化的数据质量评估计划。

Result: 通过使用AmpliGraph框架以及LAEC-CNRS提供的真实辐射传感器数据集验证，该方法成功为输入数据集生成了全面且符合上下文要求的数据质量评估计划，超越了传统方法的评估能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于知识图谱的上下文感知方法能够有效克服传统数据质量评估方法在灵活性和上下文理解上的局限性，为自动化数据质量评估提供了一种可行且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Automated data quality assessment is crucial for managing big data, but existing solutions face challenges in achieving accurate context-aware assessment. This paper presents a novel knowledge-based approach to enhance automated data quality assessment. Our approach utilizes knowledge graph embeddings to predict missing edges between the input dataset's context representation and the relevant quality rules and dimensions within a knowledge graph representing contextual data characteristics and the required quality assessment operations. We surpass conventional practices by integrating diverse representations within the knowledge graph, drawing insights from contextual information from a thorough literature investigation. This integration allows us to develop a comprehensive and context-specific data quality assessment plan tailored to each context. Leveraging the knowledge graph improves our understanding of the input dataset's context, overcoming the limitations of traditional methods that rely solely on strict matching and overlook contextual characteristics. By injecting numerical edge attributes, we assign corresponding weights to each predicted quality measurement, providing a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the input dataset.
  To evaluate our approach, we leverage AmpliGraph, a framework developed and benchmarked by AccentureLabs. The evaluation involves employing a real-world radiation sensors dataset provided by the Lebanese Atomic Energy Commission (LAEC-CNRS). The results obtained from this evaluation demonstrate the capability of our solution to generate a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the given input dataset.

</details>


### [90] [Lying Is Just a Phase: The Hidden Alignment Transition in Language Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的缩放定律忽略了不同能力间的互动；该研究发现模型在推理能力与真实性之间存在依赖于规模的“相位变化”，且数据策划和架构优化可以有效跨越或延迟此临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律（Scaling Laws）仅描述了计算量与损失（Loss）的关系，无法预测模型在不同规模下核心能力（如推理与真实性）之间是如何互动的。研究旨在揭示这种能力耦合背后的潜在“相位变化”。

Method: 基于63个基础模型（16个模型家族）进行实证研究，分析推理能力与真实性得分之间的耦合关系；利用稀疏回归常微分方程（ODE）进行预测；通过宽度归一化及模型内部注意力头分析探讨机制；利用外部公共基准评分进行诊断。

Result: 1. 发现存在一个临界规模 $N_c$（约3.5B），低于此点能力呈反相关，高于此点呈正相关。2. $N_c$并非固定值，受架构、数据质量（如Phi模型）、 distillation（蒸馏）等因素影响。3. 宽度归一化可消除反相关，揭示了输出投影瓶颈。4. 开发了一个无需模型内部结构的诊断工具及预测ODE。

Conclusion: 研究发现量化大模型推理与真实性能力的“相位变化”现象，即模型存在超越简单的损失函数缩放定律的结构性行为转化（临界点约3.5B参数），该结论揭示了训练策略对模型核心能力耦合的深远影响，并提供了量化诊断工具。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict loss from compute but not how capabilities interact. We measure the coupling between reasoning and truthfulness across 63 base models from 16 families and find a regime change invisible to loss curves: below a family-dependent critical scale $N_c$, capabilities anticorrelate; above it, they cooperate. $N_c \approx 3.5$B parameters [2.9B, 13.4B] (bootstrap 95% CI), but model size is not the only variable that determines phase. Architecture, data curation, and training recipe each shift $N_c$ independently: curated training eliminated the coupling dip between Qwen generations ($0.025 \to 0.830$ at matched scale), Gemma-4 at 4B achieves coupling 0.871, characteristic of 13B+ standard-trained models, through distillation and architectural innovation, and Phi at 1B matches web-trained coupling at 10B through data curation alone. Width normalization eliminates the anticorrelation across all tested families, supporting an output-projection bottleneck. Internally, 38 of 40 models show zero competing attention heads. A sparse-regression ODE cross-predicts held-out Llama-2 at 5.6% error. The diagnostic requires no model internals -- only public benchmark scores across a model family. The cooperative regime extends to the frontier ($r = +0.72$, 34 models, 10 labs). Code, data, and an open-source activation-steering tool for any open-weight model are released alongside an interactive dashboard that diagnoses any model's coupling phase, suggests concrete interventions (data curation, width, benchmark rotation), and provides ODE scaling predictions, frontier diagnostics, and eigenstructure analysis: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [91] [An Integrated Forecasting Prototype for Emergency Department Boarding Time to Support Proactive Operational Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18839)
*Orhun Vural,Abdulaziz Ahmed,Ferhat Zengul,James Booth,Bunyamin Ozaydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一套基于深度学习的时间序列模型（DLinear/NLinear），用于预测急诊室患者的滞留时间，并配合MLOps系统实现临床应用，以缓解急诊室拥挤问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊室（ED）拥挤是一个全球性挑战，急诊滞留时间（ED boarding time）是衡量拥挤的关键指标；现有研究缺乏有效的多步长预测方法以帮助实现主动的运营决策。

Method: 利用真实世界的医疗数据，结合外部环境（天气、节假日、重大活动）因素，采用基于分解的线性模型（DLinear）和基于归一化的线性模型（NLinear）进行多步长（6-24小时）时间序列预测，并开发了相应的MLOps原型系统。

Result: DLinear和NLinear模型在多个预测步长上表现出优越性；该模型在极端拥挤情形下同样有效；所开发的MLOps工具实现了数据处理、可视化及模型更新的集成。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建并验证了一个能够准确预测急诊室滞留时间的深度学习框架，并通过MLOps原型系统促进了研究成果的临床转化，为急诊科资源的预判性管理提供了技术支持。

Abstract: Overcrowding in emergency departments (ED) remains a persistent operational challenge worldwide, causing delays in care delivery and downstream congestion. ED boarding time, defined as the duration admitted patients remain in the ED while awaiting inpatient bed placement, is a key indicator of this congestion. Predicting ED boarding time in advance enables proactive operational decision making before congestion escalates. We developed and evaluated a multi-horizon time series forecasting framework to predict ED boarding time at 6, 8, 10, 12, and 24-hour horizons. Real-world data from a university-affiliated urban hospital in the United States were utilized and integrated with external contextual data sources, including weather, holidays, and major local events. Decomposition-based Linear (DLinear) and Normalization-based Linear (NLinear) time series forecasting deep learning models showed superior performance across multiple horizons. Models were also evaluated under extreme congestion scenarios characterized by elevated boarding times. In addition, a Machine Learning Operations (MLOps) web application prototype was developed to support translation of the forecasting framework into practice through integrated data ingestion, forecast visualization, experimentation, and retraining.

</details>


### [92] [The Growing Pains of Frontier Models: When Leaderboards Stop Separating and What to Measure Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18840)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个诊断前沿大模型能力演化的框架，通过分解编码与推理任务的得分，揭示了各实验室模型开发策略的动态耦合特征，并为未来基准测试的选择与模型发展预测提供了量化指导及交互式看板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排行榜仅提供孤立的能力排名，无法揭示前沿模型在不同能力（如编码与推理）之间是相互促进还是权衡取舍，亟需更深入的动态演化诊断工具。

Method: 通过将SWE-bench（编码）和GPQA Diamond（推理）的得分分解为总体耦合趋势和各版本的残差（$h$-field），并结合常微分方程（ODE）进行扩展预测，分析了34个模型的能力演变。

Result: 研究发现前沿模型表现出正向能力耦合（$r=+0.72$），但各实验室路径差异显著；发现了模型规模在30-72B阶段存在能力转折；SWE-bench已趋于饱和，HLE和指令遵循能力成为新的衡量关键，该诊断模型在后续发布样本中表现出高预测准确性。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的发展呈现出动态的能力耦合与分化，通过“定位-诊断-旋转”的三级框架，研究者可以量化不同实验室的开发策略，并在基准测试饱和时精准识别下一个能力转折点。

Abstract: Leaderboards rank frontier models on independent axes but do not reveal whether capabilities reinforce or trade off across releases -- and at the frontier, this interaction is the more informative signal. We decompose paired SWE-bench and GPQA Diamond scores into a population coupling trend and per-release residual ($h$-field) that diagnoses capability emphasis and identifies which measurement or stress test is most informative next. Across 34 models from 10 labs (2024--2026), capabilities cooperate ($r = +0.72$, $p < 10^{-6}$), but cooperation varies by lab and over time: DeepSeek reversed from reasoning-rich to coding-first ($h$: $+11.2 \to -4.7$, 15.9-pp swing); Google maintains consistent reasoning emphasis; Anthropic oscillates between coding excursions and recovery. Cooperation is not static -- it cascades. Six open-weight architectures confirm a second capability transition at 30--72B, and SWE-bench is now saturating while HLE and instruction-following retain discriminatory spread -- signaling the next axis rotation. We provide a three-level playbook (locate, diagnose, rotate), a per-lab measurement-priority table, and seven falsifiable predictions with timestamped criteria for the next 12 months of frontier releases. Per-lab coupling slopes vary $5\times$ (Google $1.15$ vs. DeepSeek $0.23$), quantifying how efficiently each recipe converts coding gains into reasoning. Five April 2026 releases confirm the diagnostic out of sample ($r$ rises from $+0.72$ to $+0.75$). An interactive dashboard provides phase classification with actionable recommendations, $h$-field diagnostics, per-lab coupling trajectories, ODE-based scaling predictions, benchmark rotation guidance, self-steering demo, and live tracking of all seven predictions: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [93] [From Cumulative Constraints to Adaptive Runtime Safety Control for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18841)
*Timofey Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CPSS 机制，通过将累积安全性预算实时自适应地投影为状态级安全阈值，解决了非平稳环境下强化学习中难以平衡累积约束与即时安全性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的累积成本约束无法直接防止非平稳环境下的个体不安全决策，而固定的状态级阈值往往过于保守或宽松，难以适应动态变化的环境风险。

Method: 提出了“约束投影安全盾”（CPSS），一种运行时机制。该机制将累积安全性预算转化为随时间变化的自适应状态级风险阈值，通过上下文信号动态调整阈值，并对预测风险超过阈值的策略动作进行过滤。

Result: 在 highway-env 非平稳高速公路合并场景测试中，CPSS显著降低了基于接近度的安全违规次数，增加了安全间距，且仅在必要时进行干预，优于传统方法。

Conclusion: CPSS 通过自适应的预算到阈值投影机制，为非平稳强化学习环境中的累积安全性约束提供了一种有效的实时控制方案，实现了安全性与性能的平衡。

Abstract: Safety in reinforcement learning is often specified through cumulative cost constraints, but these trajectory-level guarantees do not directly prevent unsafe individual decisions, especially under nonstationarity. In continual and nonstationary settings, the difficulty is amplified because the risk associated with the same action can vary across contexts, while a fixed state-level threshold may be either too conservative or too weak. We propose Constraint Projection Safety Shield (CPSS), a runtime mechanism that converts a cumulative safety budget into adaptive state-level control constraints during execution. CPSS tracks the remaining safety budget, projects it into a time-varying admissible risk threshold, and filters policy actions whose predicted safety cost exceeds the active threshold. The threshold is adjusted online using contextual signals so that enforcement becomes stricter in more demanding or rapidly changing regimes and less restrictive when the available safety budget is sufficient. We analyze the resulting shielded policy and show that the mechanism guarantees per-state threshold satisfaction for executed actions, induces finite-horizon cumulative cost bounds, and yields a performance degradation bound in terms of intervention frequency and per-step reward distortion. We evaluate CPSS in nonstationary highway merging scenarios using highway-env. Across multiple seeds, CPSS substantially reduces proximity-based safety violations and increases separation margins while intervening selectively rather than dominating the learned policy. These results support adaptive budget-to-threshold projection as a practical way to transform cumulative safety specifications into effective local safety control for continual reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [94] [Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning under Nonstationarity via Adaptive Safety Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18842)
*Timofey Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非平稳环境下的强化学习安全问题，提出了LILAC+框架，通过整合基于上下文的安全约束、适应速度限制及预算执行机制，实现了主动且灵活的安全适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的强化学习方法通常假设环境稳定或约束固定，但在存在分布偏移（非平稳）的环境中，这些方法往往失效且无法保障安全。

Method: 提出了LILAC+框架，包含三种自适应安全机制：1. 基于上下文的安全约束（调整安全要求）；2. 自适应速度限制（防止环境变化过快导致 unsafe）；3. 预算到状态的安全执行（将累积约束转化为实时状态级控制约束）。

Result: 在模拟驾驶环境中，LILAC+在平稳、已知非平稳及未知非平稳条件下，显著降低了安全违规次数，且在保持任务性能方面与基准方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 在非平稳环境下实现安全的持续强化学习，必须具备能够响应环境背景、适应需求及剩余安全预算的自适应约束机制。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning in nonstationary environments requires safety mechanisms that adapt as environmental conditions change. Standard safe reinforcement learning methods often assume fixed constraints or stable environmental conditions, which can become inadequate under distribution shift. We propose LILAC+, a framework for safe continual reinforcement learning under nonstationarity that combines three adaptive safety mechanisms: context-based safety constraints, adaptation-speed constraints, and budget-to-state safety enforcement. Context-based constraints adjust safety requirements using inferred and predicted environmental context. Adaptation-speed constraints tighten safety requirements when the rate of environmental change exceeds the agent's ability to adapt safely. Budget-to-state enforcement converts cumulative safety requirements into local state-level control constraints that can be enforced at decision time. Together, these mechanisms provide a unified approach for proactive and reactive safety adaptation in continual reinforcement learning. We evaluate the framework in simulated driving environments under stationary, seen nonstationary, and unseen nonstationary conditions. The results show that adaptive safety constraints substantially reduce safety violations under distribution shift while maintaining competitive task performance compared with unconstrained and fixed-constraint baselines. These findings suggest that safe continual reinforcement learning requires adaptive constraint mechanisms that respond not only to current state information but also to predicted environmental context, adaptation demand, and remaining safety budget.

</details>


### [95] [TEMPO: Temporal Enforcement via Mode-Separated Policy Optimization for Trustworthy LLM Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18843)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在回测历史事件时存在的“未来知识泄露”问题，本文提出 TEMPO 训练框架，通过双模式强化学习策略，强制模型在推理时排除指定时间点后的信息，从而实现了合规且更精准的预测任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在进行历史事件回测时，往往会泄露预训练数据中的“未来信息”（post-cutoff knowledge），导致评估失效。现有的提示词约束无效，且传统的模型去学习（unlearning）无法解决依赖特定时间节点的动态合规要求。

Method: 提出 TEMPO (Temporal Enforcement via Mode-separated Policy Optimization) 框架。包括：(1) 双模式奖励机制：先将泄露惩罚作为硬性约束，再进行任务性能优化；(2) 基于 GRPO 的训练管线，引导模型学习进行时间上合规的推理策略。

Result: 在三个预测任务和两个模型上测试，TEMPO 将知识泄露率从 2~13% 显著降低至 0.6~3.7%，并在存在有效前提信号的情况下，使任务性能提升了 6~13%。

Conclusion: TEMPO 训练框架通过双模式奖励机制和基于 GRPO 的训练管线，成功引导模型学会根据特定的时间节点选择性利用信息，有效抑制了“未来知识泄露”问题，同时提升了预测任务的准确性。

Abstract: Backtesting large language models on historical events requires reasoning exclusively from information available before a specified cutoff date. Yet models routinely leak post-cutoff knowledge from pre-training into their reasoning, inflating apparent accuracy and undermining evaluation validity. Prompt-based constraints fail when suppressed content is causally related to the prediction, and knowledge unlearning cannot address this problem because temporal compliance is instance-specific: the same fact may be legitimate evidence for one cutoff date and a violation for another. Rather than erasing knowledge, the model must learn temporal discipline: selecting evidence conditioned on each instance's cutoff date. We propose TEMPO (Temporal Enforcement via Mode-separated Policy Optimization), which trains this discipline via two contributions: (1) a two-mode reward where a leakage mode drives post-cutoff claims to zero as a hard prerequisite before a performance mode optimizes task performance; and (2) a GRPO-based training pipeline that enables the model to discover temporally valid reasoning strategies. We prove that training monotonically decreases leakage, converges to the leak-free optimum, and improves task performance once compliance is achieved. On three prediction tasks and two models, TEMPO reduces leakage from 2~13% to 0.6~3.7% across all conditions, with task performance improving 6~13% where strong pre-cutoff signals exist and maintained where the prediction task is inherently difficult from valid information alone.

</details>


### [96] [Transformers Linearly Represent Highly Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18847)
*Roman Kniazev,Nathanaël Fijalkow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对数独求解Transformer的机制可解释性分析，发现模型构建了反映任务约束代数的内部世界模型，并定位了具体执行逻辑推理的稀疏且可解释的神经回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer在经过序列推理训练后，内部是否构建了任务的“世界模型”，以及这些内部表示的结构是否与任务领域的内在逻辑结构相映射。

Method: 研究人员对一个8层Transformer在Sudoku求解轨迹（Reasoning Traces）上进行训练，并应用机制可解释性分析方法对其内部计算过程进行解构。主要方法包括特征表示的几何分析和针对特定逻辑推理任务（裸单规则）的神经元回路定位。

Result: （1）Transformer构建了基于数独约束（行、列、宫）的子结构世界模型，而非简单的单元格映射；（2）识别并定位了一个“裸单”电路，该电路位于最终MLP层，包含少量专用神经元，可精确检测并执行“裸单”逻辑推理。

Conclusion: Transformer学习到的内部世界模型是由任务的约束代数结构决定的，而非表层的输入形式。此外，模型通过稀疏且单义的“裸单（naked-single）”回路实现了逻辑推理，证明了机制可解释性工具可以揭示模型完成组合推理任务的具体算法机制。

Abstract: Do transformers, when trained on sequential reasoning traces, build internal models of the underlying task? And if so, does the structure of those internal representations mirror the structure of the domain? We train an 8-layer transformer on Sudoku solving traces and perform a mechanistic analysis of its internal computation. We establish two results. First, the model builds a substructure world model: it does not represent the board state cell by cell, as a human analyst would expect, but organizes information around the rows, columns, and boxes that Sudoku's constraints act on. Second, we identify a naked-single circuit: a small set of dedicated neurons in the final MLP layer, each individually detecting when exactly one digit remains possible for a specific cell, and reliably promoting that digit. These findings show that the geometry of an emergent world model is shaped by the constraint algebra of the domain, not its surface presentation, and that the resulting decision circuit is sparse, monosemantic, and fully interpretable. More broadly, they demonstrate that mechanistic interpretability tools can recover an end-to-end algorithmic account of how a transformer solves a combinatorial reasoning task.

</details>


### [97] [Exact Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18848)
*Weinuo Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Exact Linear Attention (ELA)，通过精确的核函数分解实现了Transformer的线性复杂度，并引入了多种核函数及结构创新（Hyper Link, Memory Lobe等）以解决梯度问题并增强模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer的注意力机制具有平方复杂度，限制了长序列处理；既有的线性注意力近似方法存在精度损失、梯度爆炸及注意力稀释等问题。

Method: 1. 提出Exact Linear Attention (ELA)，利用核函数分解消除近似误差；2. 引入核约束（非负性、可辨别性、几何可解释性）解决梯度爆炸与注意力稀释；3. 研发三种新核函数（Hadamard Exp, Summation Squared Euclidean, Subtraction Squared Euclidean）；4. 三项工程创新：Hyper Link（替代残差连接）、Memory Lobe（双向线性注意力模块）、基于路由评分的MoE偏置机制。

Result: ELA实现了计算复杂度的线性化，同时保持了无需近似的精确性；通过工程创新有效地缓解了梯度退化并增强了模型的语义对齐与记忆能力。

Conclusion: ELA通过精确的核函数分解实现了Transformer的高效计算，并通过结构创新解决了线性注意力机制中的梯度与表达力问题，证明了该方法在性能与效率上的平衡优越性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Exact Linear Attention (ELA), a mechanism that achieves linear computational complexity for Transformer attention by leveraging the exact decomposition property of kernel functions, without any approximation error. It identifies and addresses gradient explosion and token attention dilution in prior linear attention methods by imposing kernel constraints that ensure non-negativity, discriminability, and geometric interpretability. Several kernel functions are proposed, including the Hadamard Exp Kernel, Summation Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel, and Subtraction Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel. Beyond the core attention formulation, the paper presents three engineering innovations: a Hyper Link structure that replaces traditional residual connections to mitigate gradient degradation, a Memory Lobe module based on bidirectional linear attention that captures transformation flow across layers to implement qualitative memory and an implicit reinforcement learning paradigm, and a routing score based bias mechanism for Mixture of Experts to improve interpretability and semantic alignment.

</details>


### [98] [INSIGHTS: Demonstration-Based Summaries of Time Series Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18849)
*Bar Eini Porat,Rom Gutman,Uri Shalit,Ofra Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSIGHTS 填补了时间序列模型全局可解释性的空白，通过筛选具备代表性和多样性的样本子集，为用户提供简洁、有效的模型总体行为概述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列可解释性方法大多局限于局部（实例级）归因，缺乏能够提供整体视图的全局解释手段。

Method: 引入 INSIGHTS 框架，这是一种模型无关（model-agnostic）且以用户为中心的全局解释方法；利用效用函数（utility functions）平衡时间序列样本的重要性与多样性，以筛选出最具代表性的样本子集。

Result: 实验和用户研究表明，INSIGHTS 生成的摘要不仅具有管理上的便利性，且在稳定性、质量和提升用户模型认知方面优于现有方法，得到了领域专家的一致认可。

Conclusion: INSIGHTS 是一种有效的全局可解释性方法，能够为时间序列模型生成简洁、多样且具有代表性的样本摘要，从而显著提升用户对复杂模型行为的理解。

Abstract: Explainability methods have progressed rapidly, but global explanations for time-series models remain underdeveloped, with most approaches focusing on local, instance-level attributions. We introduce INSIGHTS, a model-agnostic, user-centric approach for providing global explanations of time series models. Our approach prioritizes simplicity, efficiency, and transparency in its design, ensuring that stakeholders can readily adopt its outputs. While current methods focus on local explanations, INSIGHTS generates sample summaries that offer a comprehensive overview of model behavior. It balances the importance and diversity of time series samples to create informative subsets using utility functions that capture domain-specific aspects of time series behavior, such as exceeding domain norms. We evaluate INSIGHTS through experiments, interviews, and a user study. Our results indicate INSIGHTS effectively constructs comprehensive, diverse time series subsets, producing summaries manageable for individual evaluation. It is preferred by domain experts for its ability to provide a stable understanding of model behavior and the quality of the samples identified. Moreover, user study participants presented with INSIGHTS-based summaries exhibit an enhanced understanding of the model's overall behavior.

</details>


### [99] [STRIDE: Learnable Stepwise Language Feedback for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18851)
*Junjie Zhang,Guozheng Ma,Shunyu Liu,Zetian Hu,Yongcheng Jing,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STRIDE 是一种通过可学习的逐步语言反馈来优化 LLM 推理的框架。它利用联合训练的生成式验证器提供中间步骤的反馈，从而替代昂贵的外部标注和受限的标量奖励，实现了更有效且可扩展的推理轨迹重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在提升 LLM 推理能力时存在缺陷：基于步骤的监督需要昂贵的标注；基于标量奖励的方法存在信息瓶颈；依赖外部反馈的语言批评方法扩展性较差。研究旨在寻找一种无需外部标注、信息量丰富且具备可持续改进能力的监督方案。

Method: 提出 STRIDE 框架，联合训练生成器（Generator）和生成式验证器（Generative Verifier）。仅使用基于结果的奖励（outcome-based rewards）进行训练，无需外部注释。验证器提供逐步的语言批评，通过轨迹重定向机制引导生成器在中间步骤纠正错误。

Result: STRIDE 在多个推理基准测试中显著优于现有最先进的基线模型。尤其在零通过率（zero-pass-rate）的困难问题上表现出色，证明了其在标量奖励方法无法提供学习信号时的有效性。

Conclusion: STRIDE 通过引入可学习的逐步语言反馈，成功克服了强化学习在 LLM 推理任务中面临的消息瓶颈和标注成本问题，证明了语言反馈在引导模型进行复杂推理和轨迹重定向方面的有效性，显著提升了模型在困难任务上的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) have underscored its potential for incentivizing reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing step-level efforts suffer from costly annotations that limit domain coverage, while scalar scores further impose an information bottleneck, offering insufficient semantic bandwidth to improve intermediate decisions. Alternative language-critique approaches, which rely on frozen or external critics, provide richer textual feedback but lack the scalability needed for sustained policy improvement. In this work, we propose language-driven stepwise trajectory redirection, termed as STRIDE, a novel training framework that shifts process supervision from scalar rewards to learnable stepwise language feedback. Specifically, we co-train a generator and a generative verifier using only outcome-based rewards, eliminating external annotations, while delivering sustained policy improvement through jointly aligned verifier training. The verifier's stepwise language critiques explicitly localize and explain failures, enabling the generator to redirect reasoning trajectories at intermediate steps toward alternative decisions. The trajectory redirection design guarantees harmless policy improvement, even under noisy or suboptimal verifier feedback. Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks show that STRIDE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, as well as achieving breakthroughs on zero-pass-rate problems where scalar methods yield no learning signal in our ablation studies, demonstrating the effectiveness of learnable stepwise language feedback for enhancing LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [SPHERICAL KV: Angle-Domain Attention and Rate-Distortion Retention for Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18856)
*Anay Chauhan,Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Arion Das,Amit Dhanda,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Spherical KV 方法，通过将键向量转化为紧凑的球形参数并结合率失真保留策略，解决了大模型长上下文推理中 KV Cache 带来的内存与带宽瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受限于 KV Cache 内存增长和解码阶段频繁的 HBM 带宽瓶颈，现有方案（淘汰、量化、卸载等）未能完全解决这些限制，且常因需要重构密集向量而导致性能损失。

Method: 1. 角度域注意力（ADA）：将键（Keys）进行球形参数化（标量半径+紧凑角度码），无需重构密集向量即可直接计算注意力分值；2. 率失真保留（RDR）：根据预测的未来效用，在固定预算下动态决定 token 的保留与精度分层，实现内存的高效管理。

Result: 该方法在减少 KV Cache 占用空间的同时，优化了 HBM 的数据传输路径，提升了长上下文推理的解码效率。

Conclusion: Spherical KV 通过角度域注意力（ADA）和率失真保留（RDR）机制，有效解决了长上下文推理中 KV Cache 导致的内存占用和 HBM 带宽瓶颈，为部署场景提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly constrained by the KV cache: resident memory grows with context length, and decoding becomes limited by repeated High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) streaming rather than arithmetic. Existing methods such as eviction, windowing, quantization, and offloading reduce footprint, but often leave the critical-path bottleneck only partially addressed, especially when compressed states must still be reconstructed into dense vectors during decoding.
  We present Spherical KV, a long-context inference method that treats KV allocation as a rate-distortion problem grounded in attention geometry for efficient decoding. The method is built on two ideas: (i) represent directional information cheaply in the decode hot loop, and (ii) allocate retention and precision according to estimated future utility. Its first component, Angle-Domain Attention (ADA), stores keys in a spherical parameterization consisting of a scalar radius and compact angle codes, and computes attention logits directly from these codes without reconstructing dense keys. This preserves a paged, block-local, fusion-friendly decode path and directly targets HBM traffic in realistic serving settings. Its second component, Rate-Distortion Retention (RDR), jointly chooses keep/drop decisions and precision tiers per token and head under a fixed budget, producing tier-homogeneous pages with lightweight metadata and coalesced reads. Together, ADA and RDR provide a deployment-oriented mechanism for reducing KV residency while preserving decode efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning on Sub-5KB Models: A Feasibility Study of Privacy-Preserving ECG Monitoring for Ultra-Resource-Constrained Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18862)
*Hangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Family-FL架构及超轻量级Tiny CNN-LSTM模型，解决了在超低功耗微控制器上进行隐私保护型心律失常监测的通信与存储瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病早期监测需求迫切，但现有联邦学习通信成本过高，且深度模型难以部署在超低功耗微控制器（MCU）上，需平衡隐私保护、通信开销与算力约束。

Method: 1. 提出Family-FL：一种基于家庭分组的三层架构，在全局同步前进行家庭内聚合，以降低通信开销；2. 设计Tiny CNN-LSTM模型：参数量仅669个，支持INT8量化（Flash占用4.65KB，RAM占用2.95KB），适配超低功耗微控制器。

Result: 通信量较FedAvg降低了76.7%（Family-FL）；Family-FL-Tiny模型通信量仅为FedAvg的0.31%，准确率达91.9%，对室性心律失常检测的F1分数为0.80。

Conclusion: Family-FL通过分层联邦学习架构与超轻量级模型设计，在极度受限的微控制器设备上实现了隐私保护下的心律失常监测，验证了其低通信开销与临床可行性，尽管仍存在硬件部署缺失等局限性。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death worldwide, and early detection of arrhythmias through continuous ECG monitoring on wearable devices can prevent life-threatening events. Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training by keeping raw ECG data on device, yet standard FL incurs prohibitive communication overhead and standard deep learning models cannot fit on ultra-low-power microcontrollers. We propose Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning (Family-FL), a three-tier architecture that uses the family as a natural privacy boundary for intra-family aggregation before global synchronization. We further design a hardware-constrained Tiny CNN-LSTM architecture with only 669 parameters, INT8-quantized to occupy merely 4.65KB Flash and 2.95KB RAM, meeting the constraints of STC32G12K128-class microcontrollers. Experiments on the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database (mean of 5 independent runs with different seeds) demonstrate that Family-FL reduces communication volume by 76.7% compared to FedAvg while maintaining comparable accuracy. Family-FL-Tiny achieves 91.9 +/- 1.2% accuracy with macro-F1 of 0.483 +/- 0.031, reducing total communication to 0.31% of FedAvg. The model achieves reliable ventricular arrhythmia detection (per-class F1 = 0.80), the most clinically critical abnormality for home-based preliminary screening. These results demonstrate the technical feasibility of privacy-preserving federated learning on ultra-resource-constrained microcontrollers through simulation-based evaluation. We honestly discuss limitations: no hardware deployment, single-dataset validation (MIT-BIH, 47 subjects), reduced rare-class sensitivity, and absence of formal differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [102] [SAGE: Shaping Anchors for Guided Exploration in RLVR of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18864)
*Chanuk Lee,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决RLVR中因逆KL正则化导致模型难以探索新推理模式进而限制 pass@k 的问题，本文提出了 SAGE 框架，通过重塑参考分布实现可控的探索，有效提升了模型推理的质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）方法虽然能提升 pass@1，但无法有效提升 pass@k，这被认为是因为逆KL正则化过度限制了模型对新推理模式的探索。现有解决方法（去KL或改用前向KL）会导致奖励欺骗或偏离目标，因此需要一种能平衡探索与稳定性的新机制。

Method: 提出 SAGE (Support-Aware Guide Expansion) 框架，通过引入一个可控的引导函数 q(x,y) 重塑逆KL正则化中的锚点分布，替代传统的固定参考分布，从而缓解模型被锚定在原有推理模式的问题。

Result: SAGE 在多个具有挑战性的数学推理基准测试中，在 pass@1 和 pass@k 指标上均表现出一致的性能提升，证实了其在引导模型学习多样化推理能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过重塑逆KL正则化的参考分布（引入引导函数 q(x,y)），SAGE框架成功平衡了训练稳定性与探索压力，使模型能探索新的推理模式，从而在 pass@1 和 pass@k 指标上均实现持续提升。

Abstract: Recent studies observe that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) reliably improves pass@1 on reasoning tasks, yet often fails to yield comparable gains in pass@k, raising the question of whether RLVR genuinely enables large language models to acquire novel reasoning abilities or merely enhances the efficiency of sampling reasoning modes already present in the base model. Prior analyses largely support the latter view, attributing this limitation to structural properties of standard RLVR objectives that result in insufficient exploration pressure. In this work, we argue that a central structural constraint arises from reverse-KL regularization, which stabilizes training but inherently anchors the policy to the reference distribution, thereby suppressing the emergence of alternative reasoning modes. However, we show that neither removing the KL term nor replacing it with forward-KL provides a satisfactory solution, as both disrupt the efficiency-coverage trade-off by either inducing reward hacking or allocating probability mass to off-target regions. To resolve this tension, we propose SAGE, a principled framework that enables controllable empirical support expansion by reshaping the reverse-KL anchor distribution itself through a guide function q(x,y), achieving consistent improvements in both pass@1 and pass@k across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/SAGE.

</details>


### [103] [From Sparsity to Simplicity: Enabling Simpler Sequential Replacements via Sparse Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18865)
*Yuxin Ren,Maxwell D Collins,Miao Hu,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析ViT层级的注意力稀疏性，提出了一种利用稀疏性引导的蒸馏框架，将高效的注意力层替换为轻量级模块，在降低推理延迟的同时最小化了模型精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制带来的二次方计算复杂度导致推理成本高昂。虽然可以替换注意力层，但传统的直接替换方式会导致较大的性能损失，且在模型规模增大时尤其明显。

Method: 提出了一种基于分层蒸馏（Layer-wise Distillation）的框架。通过观察ViT模型中不同层级的注意力稀疏性模式，对注意力稀疏度高的层进行简单的线性/序列模块替换，并利用稀疏性指导的蒸馏（Sparsity-guided Distillation）来减小学生和教师模型之间的性能差距。

Result: 实验表明，注意力稀疏度越高的层，在被替换时对模型精度的影响越小。通过实施AViT风格的显式注意力稀疏化并结合该蒸馏方法，可以显著缩小替换模型与原预训练模型的性能差距，实现参数量与推理延迟的降低。

Conclusion: 通过利用注意力稀疏性指导模型蒸馏，可以有效地将Transformer中的注意力层替换为高效的线性/序列模块，从而在保持精度的同时降低模型参数量与推理延迟。

Abstract: Self-attention serves as the core foundation of large-scale transformer pretraining, but its quadratic token interaction cost makes inference expensive. Replacing attention with simpler sequential modules is appealing, yet naive substitution is often lossy, especially at larger scales. This paper revisits attention replacement through the lens of sparsity. Based on the observation of diverse sparsity patterns across transformer layers, we posit that pretrained transformers decompose the complex token dependency across tokens into various sequence-to-sequence mappings of diverse complexities, where some layer functionalities can be approximated and replaced with much simpler sequential modules without loss. We evaluate this premise using a plug-and-play layer-wise distillation framework to approximate and replace attention functionalities in pretrained vision transformer models. Controlled group-wise replacements under a fixed training budget reveal a clear pattern: substituting layers with sparser attention incurs substantially smaller accuracy drops than replacing denser ones. We further impose explicit attention sparsity on the pretrained ViT via AViT-style token retention and perform sparsity-guided distillation for sequential replacing models, where we see increasing teacher sparsity consistently reduces the student-teacher gap. The proposed method achieves efficient attention replacement for reduced parameter size and latency through the guidance of attention sparsity.

</details>


### [104] [EVA-0: Test-Time Model Evolution with Only Two Forward Passes per Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18867)
*Guohao Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Geng Li,Yunbei Zhang,Shilin Shan,Chunyan Miao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVA-0 是一种无需反向传播的高效测试时适应框架，在仅进行两次前向传播的极端预算下，解决了零阶优化中的捷径解、权重漂移及梯度估计不准确问题，实现了高性能与高效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时模型进化方法大多依赖反向传播 (BP)，导致显存开销大，难以在边缘设备、量化模型或黑盒模型中部署。本研究致力于在“两次前向传播预算”的极端约束下实现高效的模型测试时适应。

Method: 提出 EVA-0 零阶适应框架，通过以下三种技术解决零阶优化的核心难点：1) 尺度不变损失函数（Scale-invariant loss）以防止捷径解；2) 基于锚点的优化策略（Anchor-guided optimization）控制权重漂移；3) 样本级对称双侧扰动（Sample-wise symmetric two-sided perturbation）精确估计更新方向。整个过程仅需两次前向传播。

Result: 在 ImageNet-C 数据集和 ViT-Base 模型上，EVA-0 的性能优于基于 BP 的 DeYO 和基于零阶的 FOA，同时推理和适应速度比 FOA 快 14 倍。

Conclusion: EVA-0 通过零阶优化成功实现了在极低计算预算（两次前向传播）下的测试时适应，在无需反向传播的前提下，性能优于现有的基于反向传播和基于零阶的方法，且计算效率大幅提升。

Abstract: Test-time model evolution offers a promising way for deployed models to improve from unlabeled test-time experience, yet most existing methods depend on backpropagation (BP), which incurs substantial memory overhead and makes them difficult to deploy on edge devices, quantized models, specialized accelerators, or black-box models. In this work, we study test-time model evolution under a strict two-forward budget, a setting that pushes adaptation toward highly efficient real-world deployment. We reveal three key obstacles in zeroth-order test-time optimization: susceptibility to shortcut solutions, uncontrolled weight drift, and ineffective update direction estimation. To overcome them, we propose EVA-0, a minimal zeroth-order adaptation framework that: 1) keeps the loss scale-invariant to prevent shortcut solutions; 2) devises an anchor-guided optimization strategy to alleviate weight drift; 3) uses sample-wise symmetric two-sided perturbation for update direction estimation and inference. EVA-0 requires no BP and performs both inference and adaptation within only two forward passes per sample. Results on ImageNet-C & ViT-Base show that EVA-0 outperforms both BP-based DeYO and BP-free FOA, while achieving a 14x speed-up over FOA. Code will be released.

</details>


### [105] [MO-CAPO: Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18869)
*Jan Büssing,Moritz Schlager,Timo Heiß,Tom Zehle,Matthias Feurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 MO-CAPO 的多目标提示词优化框架，在兼顾性能的同时降低 LLM 的推理成本，并提升了多目标优化在实际部署场景中的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示词优化方法多关注单一性能目标，忽略了推理成本和延迟；现有的多目标优化方法效率低下且缺乏对部署环境的真实评估。

Method: 提出 MO-CAPO 算法，联合优化性能与推理成本；引入预算分配机制提高优化效率；定义了面向部署的推理计算代价目标；并首次使用 noisy R2 和 approximation gap 评估多目标优化效果。

Result: 在 4 个任务和 3 个 LLM 上表现出色，在 12 个案例中的 8 个超过基准 NSGA-II，能以更少的代价产生多样且高质量的 Pareto 前沿解。

Conclusion: MO-CAPO 实现了在性能和 inference 成本之间的平衡优化，为用户提供了多样化的提示词（prompt）选择，且在效率和评估指标上优于基准方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are highly sensitive to prompt design, motivating the need for automatic prompt optimization. Existing methods predominantly focus on performance alone, ignoring competing objectives such as inference cost or latency. At the same time, existing work on multi-objective prompt optimization relies on off-the-shelf NSGA-II, ignoring optimization efficiency. As a remedy, we introduce MO-CAPO, a novel multi-objective prompt optimization algorithm that jointly optimizes performance and inference cost while leveraging budget allocation for cost-efficient optimization. We further propose a deployment-oriented cost objective that captures the full computational profile of LLM inference. We evaluate our approach across four tasks and three LLMs and compare it to an NSGA-II-based multi-objective method and state-of-the-art single-objective prompt optimizers. Results show that MO-CAPO consistently identifies strong, robust, and diverse Pareto front approximations while maintaining cost-efficiency. It outperforms the NSGA-II baseline on 8 out of 12 cases in terms of the noisy R2 metric and achieves competitive performances often already at a considerably lower budget. The discovered solution sets span diverse performance-cost trade-offs that are omitted by single-objective optimizers, yet the top-performance candidates remain competitive with single-objective solutions. Additionally, we conduct the first evaluation of multi-objective machine learning experiments that considers generalization and robustness through noisy R2 and approximation gap, enabling a more realistic assessment of solution quality. MO-CAPO enables practitioners to select from an efficiently discovered set of multiple prompts offering different trade-offs between performance and cost.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-Headed Transformer Architectures as Time-dependent Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18870)
*Alex Massucco,Leonardo Del Grande,Marcello Carioni,Christoff Brune,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过非自治梯度流和相互作用能量建模多头Transformer，弥合了理论与实际架构间的鸿沟，证明了该模型在扰动下的稳定性与收敛性，并阐明了其复杂的动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer理论模型通常基于较强的简化假设，与实际架构之间缺乏直接的数学联系，导致对实际复杂多头机制的理论刻画不足。

Method: 利用时间依赖的梯度流（time-dependent gradient flows）来建模多头Transformer中的数据流，引入能够反映注意力机制设计的相互作用能量（interaction energy），并利用Γ-收敛理论分析系统对权重和初始数据的扰动稳定性。

Result: 建立了非自治梯度流模型；证明了系统在特定条件下的收敛性和稳定性（包括输入噪声下的连续依赖性和权重扰动下的Γ-收敛）；通过数值实验揭示了在不同动力学体制（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck与震荡权重）下的能量耗散行为。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将多头Transformer架构建模为非自治梯度流的数学框架。证明了在特定条件下，该流的ω-极限集是相互作用能量的稳定点，并建立了关于初始化和权重扰动的鲁棒性与稳定性，同时通过数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: In recent years, transformer architectures have revolutionized the field of language processing, opening the door to previously unforeseen possibilities. However, from a theoretical point of view, the mathematical models proposed in the literature often lack direct contact with the actual architectures and depend on strong simplifying assumptions. In this paper, we reduce this gap by modelling the data flow in multi-headed transformer architectures as time-dependent gradient flows for a suitable interaction energy capturing the design of the attention mechanism. The explicit dependence on time allows us to consider different weights for each head and for each layer, without imposing constraints on the initialization method. Moreover, we prove that, under a suitable integrability assumption on the evolution of the weights, each element of the $ω$-limit set of the gradient flows is a stationary point of the interaction energy at a limiting weight distribution. Finally, we analyse the stability of the gradient flows considering perturbations of both the initial data and the weights. Specifically, on the one hand, we study the robustness of the proposed models with respect to noisy inputs, establishing a continuous dependence of the gradient flows on the initial data and uniqueness of the flows. On the other hand, we prove the $Γ$-convergence of the perturbed interaction energy to the unperturbed one, leading to the convergence of the corresponding gradient flows. We complement these theoretical results with numerical experiments that confirm the predicted energy-dissipation identity and clarify the asymptotic behavior of the dynamics in both the autonomous-like (Ornstein--Uhlenbeck) and the genuinely non-autonomous (oscillating-weights) regimes.

</details>


### [107] [ZeroUnlearn: Few-Shot Knowledge Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18879)
*Yujie Lin,Chengyi Yang,Zhishang Xiang,Yiping Song,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型中敏感信息的擦除问题，提出 ZeroUnlearn 框架，通过精准的模型参数重映射实现高效、低损耗的机理反学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在预训练过程中不可避免地摄入敏感信息，导致隐私和安全隐患；现有的反学习方法（如重训练或激进微调）存在计算成本过高或破坏模型原始任务效能的问题。

Method: 将机器反学习重构为基于模型编辑的知识重映射问题。提出了 ZeroUnlearn 框架：1. 使用带有闭式解的乘法参数更新来实现表示正交性；2. 将敏感输入映射到中性目标状态；3. 扩展出基于梯度的变体以支持多样本反学习。

Result: 实验表明，ZeroUnlearn 在消除敏感信息的同时，优于现有的基准方法，且能更好地维持模型的整体实用性。

Conclusion: ZeroUnlearn 是一种高效且精准的机理反学习方案，通过将输入重映射至中性状态并强制实现表示正交性，在消除敏感信息的同时，能够有效保留模型的通用性能。

Abstract: Large language models inevitably retain sensitive information, defined as inputs that may induce harmful generations, due to training on massive web corpora, raising concerns for privacy and safety. Existing machine unlearning methods primarily rely on retraining or aggressive fine-tuning, which are either computationally expensive or prone to degrading related knowledge and overall model utility. In this work, we reformulate machine unlearning as a precise knowledge re-mapping problem via model editing. We propose ZeroUnlearn, a few-shot unlearning framework. It overwrites sensitive inputs by mapping them to a neutral target state and removing their original representations. ZeroUnlearn enforces representational orthogonality through a multiplicative parameter update with a closed-form solution, enabling efficient and targeted unlearning. We further extend ZeroUnlearn to a gradient-based variant for multi-sample unlearning. Experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing baselines while preserving general model utility. Our code is available at the github: https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/ZeroUnlearn.

</details>


### [108] [A Multi-Dimensional Clustering Approach for Identifying Inborn Errors of Immunity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18880)
*Nishad Kulkarni,Alexandra K. Martinson,Nicholas L. Rider,Michael Keller,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种处理罕见病（IEI）EHR数据的机器学习流水线，通过将原始医疗数据向量化并应用聚类算法，旨在自动识别罕见病模式，解决罕见病临床数据分析匮乏的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病（如先天性免疫缺陷，IEI）需早期诊断以改善预后，但高质量EHR数据的获取和处理存在巨大障碍，且目前缺乏针对罕见病自动进行模式识别及系统性数据整合的机器学习方法。

Method: 提出了一种专门用于电子健康记录（EHR）数据处理的标准化流程（Pipeline），包括数据清洗、格式化，将原始免疫学实验室指标转化为机器学习可理解的向量，并结合超参数优化与无监督学习聚类算法进行疾病模式识别。

Result: 开发出一套能够从国家级数据注册系统中提取IEI相关特征的数据处理工具包，实现了对复杂医疗记录的结构化转化，支持通过无监督学习算法发现潜在的罕见病临床模式。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建并验证了一个将原始免疫学实验室数据转化为可解释特征向量的流程，为罕见病（特别是IEI）的模式识别和数据驱动分析提供了可扩展的工具链。

Abstract: Rare diseases such as inborn errors of immunity (IEI) require early diagnosis to prevent end organ damage and improve quality of life. Hurdles in accessing and curating large scale electronic health record (EHR) data limit routine data driven analyses to remain on the forefront of IEI and other rare disease trends. Development of machine learning (ML) algorithms in IEI for pattern recognition as well as published methodology examining how to systematically process and integrate complex medical data is limited. Our proposed pipeline, including data curation and ML clustering algorithms, is designed to recognize novel rare disease patterns and extract IEI- associated features from a national data registry. Our methodology for EHR data formatting and processing presents the pipeline that transforms raw immunologic lab data into vectors. This is further combined with hyperparameter tuning for diseases pattern recognition via clustering. This study refines IEI feature awareness, develops data tool kits for rare disease populations analysis, and expands on transforming complex medical records in data structures interpretable by unsupervised ML.

</details>


### [109] [Emergence of a Flow-Assisted Casting Strategy for Olfactory Navigation via Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18881)
*Changxu Zhao,Dongxiao Zhao,Xin Bian,Gaojin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，在动态气味搜索中，智能体通过强化学习能够自适应调整搜索策略，且存在一个最优的记忆整合窗口来平衡搜索效率，该现象可由提出的“扇区搜索”模型有效解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
