Motivation: 基于优化的对抗后缀能够绕过对齐的LLM，且因其保持语义流畅性，现有的基于困惑度（Perplexity）的检测器表现较差。

Method: 将对抗后缀检测建模为标记级预测熵流的在线变点检测（CPD）问题；利用系统提示词建立基准，标准化用户标记熵，并应用单边CUSUM（累积和）统计量来定位对抗后缀的触发。

Result: 在包含1012次优化攻击的基准测试中，CPD在所有6种模型上均优于现有的窗口困惑度基线；在LLaMA-2-7B上达到AUROC 0.88和F1 0.82；能精准定位79.6%的对抗后缀触发区域；作为防火墙门禁可减少17-22%的请求调用，同时维持防御质量。

Conclusion: CPD是一种有效、轻量且与模型无关的实时对抗后缀检测方法，能够显著优于传统的困惑度检测基线，并能作为LLM安全防御的前置过滤器，平衡安全性和计算效率。

Abstract: Optimization-based adversarial suffixes can jailbreak aligned large language models (LLMs) while remaining fluent, weakening static and windowed perplexity-based detectors. We cast adversarial suffix detection as an online change-point detection problem over the token-level next-token entropy stream. Using the LLM system prompt to estimate a robust baseline, we standardize user-token entropies and apply a one-sided CUSUM statistic. The resulting detector, CPD Online (CPD), is model-agnostic, training-free, runs online, and localizes the adversarial suffix onset. On a benchmark of 1,012 optimization-based suffix attacks (GCG, AutoDAN, AdvPrompter, BEAST, AutoDAN-HGA) and 1,012 perplexity-controlled benign prompts, CPD improves F1 over the strongest windowed-perplexity baseline on all six open-weight chat models (LLaMA-2-7B/13B, Vicuna-7B/13B, Qwen2.5-7B/14B). On LLaMA-2-7B at the canonical CUSUM setting ($k=0$), CPD reaches AUROC $0.88$ and F1 $0.82$. Beyond prompt-level detection, CPD concentrates 79.6% of its triggers inside the adversarial suffix, versus 17-46% for windowed perplexity. Finally, when used as a lightweight gate for LLaMA Guard, CPD reduces guard calls by 17-22% on a high-volume, benign-dominated deployment while preserving guard-level detection quality

</details>


### [181] [Your Neighbors Know: Leveraging Local Neighborhoods for Backdoor Detection in Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19969)
*Sayan Biswas,Antoine Boutet,Davide Frey,Romaric Gaudel,Rachid Guerraoui,Maxime Jacovella,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Dimitri Lerévérend,François Taïani,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Argus 是首个去中心化学习的原生后门防御框架，通过邻居间协作验证更新的一致性来检测并剔除后门，在不依赖中心节点的情况下，显著降低了攻击成功率并保障了模型收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中的后门攻击研究尚不充分，现有的防御机制往往忽略了去中心化的约束（如缺乏中心节点），且易受数据异构性影响。

Method: 提出了 Argus 框架：1. 节点本地分析模型更新以识别潜在触发器；2. 通过邻居间交换触发器信息，利用结构相似度指标区分真后门与非独立同分布（non-IID）数据导致的误报；3. 基于一致性协作测试过滤恶意更新，并剔除持续恶意者。

Result: 实验证明 Argus 可将攻击成功率降低高达 90 个百分点，同时模型效用保持在全知预言机（oracle）的 5 个百分点以内；且在数据异构性越高的情况下，其性能表现比基准方法更优。

Conclusion: Argus 是首个专为去中心化学习设计的后门检测框架，无需中心化协调或先验触发器知识。该框架通过协作验证和一致性检查，有效平衡了安全性与模型性能，并提供了理论收敛性保证。

Abstract: Decentralized learning (DL) is an emerging machine learning paradigm where nodes collaboratively train models without a central server. However, the collaborative nature of DL makes it vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a model is taught to behave normally on standard inputs while executing hidden, malicious actions when encountering data with specific triggers. Backdoor attacks in DL remain understudied and existing defenses often overlook DL constraints. We introduce Argus, a novel backdoor detection framework native to DL that requires neither a central coordinator nor prior knowledge of the trigger. In Argus, honest nodes locally analyze received model updates to identify potential backdoor triggers. Nodes then collectively share their triggers with their neighbors and use a structural similarity metric to separate true backdoors from false alarms induced by data heterogeneity. A key insight is that false positive triggers exhibit inconsistencies across participants while true positive ones show consistent patterns. Model updates that fail this collaborative test are rejected, and persistently malicious senders are eventually evicted. We provide the first theoretical convergence guarantees for a DL-specific backdoor detection mechanism, showing that filtering out suspicious model updates with high probability preserves a convergence rate comparable to standard DL. We implement and evaluate Argus on three standard datasets and against three state-of-the-art baselines. Across settings, Argus reduces attack success rates by up to 90 points compared to no defense, while preserving model utility within 5 percentage points of an omniscient oracle. Furthermore, the effectiveness of Argus compared to baselines improves as data heterogeneity increases.

</details>


### [182] [Learning with Foresight: Enhancing Neural Routing Policy via Multi-Node Lookahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19975)
*Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Yew-Soon Ong,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对神经路径规划模型的“短视”问题，MnLP 引入多节点前瞻预测训练策略及多深度辅助损失，在维持推理效率的同时，显著提升了模型的长程规划与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前神经策略在求解车辆路径问题时主要依赖单步预测，导致决策短视、缺乏长程规划能力的问题。

Method: 提出多节点前瞻预测（MnLP）策略，在训练过程中通过因果与可丢弃的辅助模块同时预测多个未来节点，并结合多深度辅助监督损失函数来增强模型对长距离上下文的理解。

Result: 实验证明 MnLP 在多种问题规模、数据分布及现实基准测试中均优于现有的训练方法，且能无缝集成到多种架构中，不引入推理开销。

Conclusion: MnLP 是一种有效的训练策略，通过在监督学习中引入多节点辅助任务，显著提升了神经策略在车辆路径规划中的长程规划能力与泛化性能，且不增加推理阶段的计算开销。

Abstract: Neural policies have shown promise in solving vehicle routing problems due to their reduced reliance on handcrafted heuristics. However, current training paradigms suffer from a fundamental limitation: they primarily focus on next-node prediction for solution construction, resulting in myopic decision-making that undermines long-horizon planning capacity. To this end, we introduce Multi-node Lookahead Prediction (MnLP), a novel training strategy that extends the supervised learning paradigm to predict multiple future nodes simultaneously. We incorporate causal and discardable MnLP modules that operate exclusively during training, facilitating models to anticipate multi-step decisions while preserving inference-time efficiency. By incorporating multi-depth auxiliary supervision into the loss function, MnLP equips neural policies with the ability of long-range contextual understanding. Experimentally, MnLP outperforms existing training methods, improving the generalization capability of neural policies across various problem sizes, distributions, and real-world benchmarks. Moreover, MnLP can be seamlessly integrated into diverse neural architectures without introducing additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [183] [LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Jason Lucas,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型基准测试集在预训练中被广泛污染的问题，提出构建“抗污染”评估集的技术路径，旨在通过数学手段实现测试数据“不可学习”但“可推理”，从而提升基准测试的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型基准测试集普遍存在于预训练语料中（即“数据污染”），导致模型泛化能力的评估结果不可靠，亟需构建能够抵抗训练学习但支持推理能力评估的新型基准。

Method: 提出了一种利用Transformer架构在推理与训练流水线之间的不对称性，通过数学方法构建在不同模型架构间具有互操作性的“抗污染”评估数据集，确保模型无法通过预训练获得测试答案，但仍能有效执行测试请求。

Result: 明确了抗污染数据集的特征，构建了理论基础，并为社区提供了从方法论、平台建设到工作流集成三个层面的标准化行动指南。

Conclusion: 指出了基准测试污染问题的严重性，并提出“抗污染”（contamination-resistant）是未来大模型评估的必要方向，呼吁社区开发不可学习（unlearnable）但支持推理的评估数据集。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are critical for reproducible, reliable, and discriminative evaluation of LLMs. However, recent studies reveal that many benchmark datasets are included in pretraining corpora, i.e., $\textit{contaminated}$, which diminishes their value as reliable measures of model generalization. In this paper, we argue that benchmark datasets should be $\textit{contamination-resistant}$, i.e., $\textit{unlearnable}$, but support $\textit{inference}$. To accomplish this, we first highlight the wide prevalence of benchmark dataset contamination and outline the properties of contamination-resistant datasets. Second, we highlight how the asymmetry between the inference and training pipelines in the Transformer architecture can be leveraged to support contamination-resistance. Third, we outline mathematical advancements to make these datasets interoperable across various LLM architectures. Based on the above, we call on the community to ensure the reliability of LLM benchmarking by: (i) advancing novel contamination-resistant methodologies, (ii) developing supporting methods and platforms, and (iii) adopting contamination-resistant benchmarks into existing evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [184] [Fine-Tuning Without Forgetting via Loss-Adaptive Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20005)
*Parjanya Prajakta Prashant,Jiongli Zhu,Aldan Creo,Babak Salimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出了 FINCH，一种无需修改目标函数、通过自适应调整学习率来降低模型对高损失批次敏感度的算法，有效实现了性能提升与遗忘抑制的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缓解灾难性遗忘的方法通常通过修改微调目标函数来抑制高损失标记（high-loss tokens），这往往会阻碍模型对新任务（尤其是预训练覆盖率低的任务）的学习。因此，需要一种不抑制重要学习信号，同时能有效控制遗忘的机制。

Method: 提出 FINCH（Loss-Adaptive Learning-Rate Schedule）：一种基于损失自适应的学习率调度方法。该方法基于理论观察（遗忘上限与学习率和当前损失平方根的乘积成正比），通过在处理高损失批次时降低学习率，并在模型收敛时提高学习率，实现对遗忘的控制。

Result: 在多个基准测试（知识获取、科学、低资源语言适配）中，FINCH 平均减少了 93% 的遗忘，同时微调任务性能与标准微调持平。在 Qwen3-4B 模型上，TruthfulQA 的退化减少了 5 倍，并逆转了 HaluEval 的退化，同时更好地保持了置信度校准。

Conclusion: 学习率调度（Learning-rate schedules）是微调阶段调节模型行为的有效工具。FINCH 方法证明了在不改变损失函数的情况下，通过针对性控制学习率，可以有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持甚至提升目标任务的性能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new data improves task performance but degrades capabilities learned during pretraining, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods mitigate this by modifying the fine-tuning objective to suppress high-loss tokens or sequences, but these tokens are essential for learning new tasks, especially those with poor pretraining coverage. In such settings, hard tokens should still contribute to learning, so forgetting must be controlled without suppressing them. We identify a simple mechanism for doing so: per-step forgetting is bounded by the product of the learning rate and the square root of the current training loss. This suggests that high-loss batches are especially prone to inducing forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FINCH, a loss-adaptive learning-rate schedule that reduces the learning rate on high-loss batches and increases it as the model converges, while leaving the fine-tuning objective unchanged. Across knowledge acquisition, science, and low-resource language adaptation benchmarks, FINCH reduces forgetting by 93% on average while matching the task performance of standard fine-tuning. On Qwen3-4B knowledge acquisition, FINCH cuts TruthfulQA degradation by 5x and reverses HaluEval degradation, while better preserving confidence calibration. Overall, our results show that learning-rate schedules are an effective tool to shape model behavior during fine-tuning, beyond just target-task optimization.

</details>


### [185] [Training-Free Bayesian Filtering with Generative Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20028)
*Thomas Savary,François Rozet,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型作为动力系统模拟器，成功克服了传统粒子滤波在高维非线性系统中扩展性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子滤波在处理非线性动力系统时虽然理论上精确，但在高维空间中面临严重的可扩展性（Scalability）瓶颈。

Method: 利用扩散模型（Diffusion-based emulators）作为动力系统的仿真器，结合粒子滤波框架，通过扩散模型的生成特性解决传统数值解算器在高维环境下的实现困难。

Result: 在非线性混沌系统（包括大气动力学模拟）的实验中，该方法成功地将粒子滤波扩展到了高维应用场景，并克服了传统方法的实现挑战。

Conclusion: 扩散模型模拟器能够有效地解决高维状态下的粒子滤波难题，提供了一种无需额外训练即可实现最优粒子滤波的方法，并在非线性混沌系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a well-known problem that aims to estimate plausible states of a dynamical system from observations. Among existing approaches to solve this problem, particle filters are theoretically exact for non-linear dynamics and observations, but suffer from poor scalability in high dimensions. In this work, we show that diffusion-based emulators of dynamical systems can be used to implement, without additional training, an optimal variant of particle filters that has remained largely unexplored due to implementation challenges with classical numerical solvers. Experiments on nonlinear chaotic systems, including atmospheric dynamics, demonstrate that the proposed approach successfully scales particle filtering to high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [186] [Take It or Leave It: Intent-Controlled Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20030)
*Salil Parth Tripathi,Bertrand Chapron,Fabrice Collard,Nicolas Courty,Ronan Fablet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了意图控制部分最优传输（IC-POT），通过逐点拒斥机制取代了传统全局拒斥规则，使得最优传输能够灵活地结合先验信息进行匹配，并在多个实际学习和科学应用中展现了更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的部分最优传输通常依赖于全局性的拒斥约束，无法满足许多应用中对逐点拒斥机制的需求（即根据具体的侧面信息、几何结构或可靠性决定是否匹配）。

Method: 引入“意图控制部分最优传输”（IC-POT），通过在两个测度上设置逐点拒斥成本来取代全局拒斥范式，并将其重新表述为增强支持集上的平衡 Kantorovich OT 问题进行求解。

Result: 验证了 IC-POT 在正无标签学习（PU learning）、开放域自适应以及多模式卫星海洋测量等场景中优于固定基准模型，有效利用了侧面信息来编码统计结构。

Conclusion: IC-POT 提供了一种更具灵活性和结构化的部分最优传输框架。通过引入逐点拒斥机制，它不仅理论上优越，而且在正无标签学习、领域自适应和地球物理数据分析等实际应用中表现出显著的优势。

Abstract: While optimal transport (OT) enforces a rigid constraint by requiring two measures to be matched exactly, partial optimal transport relaxes this requirement by allowing mass to remain unmatched through a global budget, scalar rebate, or uniform rejection rule. However, many applications call for more structured, pointwise rejection mechanisms, where the decision to leave mass unmatched depends on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in the comparison. We introduce \emph{intent-controlled partial optimal transport} (IC-POT), a targeted generalization of partial transport that replaces the global rejection paradigm with pointwise rejection costs over both measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem admits a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds and can be solved by recasting it as a balanced Kantorovich OT problem on an augmented support. Beyond theoretical analysis, we demonstrate the practical relevance of IC-POT in settings where rejection is driven by side information. In positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves fixed baseline pipelines. Finally, we motivate the use of IC-POT with a geophysical practical case: multi-modal satellite ocean measurements, for which physical and sensors priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism and define the retrieved comparable signal information.

</details>


### [187] [CAMERA: Adapting to Semantic Camouflage in Unsupervised Text-Attributed Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20032)
*Junjun Pan,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线平台中欺诈者利用文本伪装逃避检测的问题，本文提出了无监督框架 CAMERA。该框架通过解耦的专家模型学习特征，并利用门控机制动态整合邻域上下文，通过聚焦于建模“良性模式”而非直接学习欺诈特征，显著提升了无监督图欺诈检测的精准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交与电商平台中，欺诈者通过通过模拟良性用户的文本行为进行“语义伪装”，导致传统的结构与属性关联假设失效，使得现有的无监督图欺诈检测（TAGFD）方法难以准确识别此类欺诈行为。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CAMERA（Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework）的框架：1. 采用自我解耦的专家混合（MoE）架构，不同专家建模不同的欺诈特征；2. 引入上下文感知的门控机制，根据节点及其邻域动态融合特征；3. 基于欺诈者的稀缺性，采用单类学习（One-class learning）策略，通过建模支配性的良性模式来实现无监督检测。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的数据集上进行实验，结果表明 CAMERA 始终优于现有的基准模型，特别是在对抗语义伪装欺诈者方面表现出显著的有效性。

Conclusion: CAMERA 通过解耦图中的复杂特征，并利用专家模型聚焦于良性模式的学习，有效解决了无监督场景下欺诈者“语义伪装”的问题，在多个数据集上验证了其鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: Text-attributed graph fraud detection (TAGFD) plays a critical role in preventing fraudulent activities on online social and e-commerce platforms. However, to evade detection, fraudsters continuously evolve their camouflaging strategies by deliberately mimicking textual responses of benign users, thereby concealing their malicious purposes. This phenomenon, referred to as semantic camouflage, fundamentally undermines commonly relied assumptions on how structural and attribute cues can be exploited to identify fraudsters, and makes it difficult to spot fraudsters with unsupervised TAGFD. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework (CAMERA) for unsupervised TAGFD. CAMERA employs an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert specializes in modeling a distinct type of fraud-indicative cue. A context-informed gating model is introduced to jointly consider the ego node representation and its local neighborhood context for adaptive integration of cues learned by different experts. Furthermore, CAMERA leverages the inherent rarity of fraudsters to support unsupervised one-class learning with expert-level objectives that encourage modeling dominant benign patterns, thereby enabling reliable unsupervised detection of camouflaged fraudsters. Experiments on 4 challenging datasets show that CAMERA consistently outperforms competitors, showing its effectiveness against semantically camouflaged fraudsters. Code available at https://github.com/CampanulaBells/CAMERA

</details>


### [188] [D$^3$-Subsidy: Online and Sequential Driver Subsidy Decision-Making for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20036)
*Taijie Chen,Rui Su,Siyuan Feng,Laoming Zhang,Hongyang Zhang,Haijiao Wang,Zhaofeng Ma,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对网约车平台补贴优化难题，D$^3$-Subsidy 提出了一种分层扩散模型框架，通过前缀条件化生成未来预测，并配合拉格朗日约束映射，实现了城市级低延迟、高合规的智能补贴控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车平台需在高度动态的环境中平衡供需，传统的优化方法因实时性、计算成本和严格的补贴约束等问题难以满足在线大规模生产环境的需求。

Method: 提出 D$^3$-Subsidy 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 前缀条件化扩散模型（Prefix-conditioned Diffusion），用于预测未来轨迹；(2) 上下文条件化逆模块，将轨迹解码为低维控制信号；(3) 基于拉格朗日对偶的映射方案，实现无迭代的快速调度与合规约束；同时采用了多城市预训练与参数高效微调策略。

Result: 离线评估显示其有效提升了完成单量（Rides）和总交易额（GMV），并提高了合规性；真实世界的 A/B 测试验证了其显著的业务增长且预算违规率保持在阈值内。

Conclusion: D$^3$-Subsidy 通过分层扩散模型，成功在满足严格限制（如补贴率上限和低延迟）的前提下，实现城市级网约车平台供需平衡的最优化，在提升订单量和GMV的同时保持了良好的合规性。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms like DiDi Chuxing operate in highly dynamic environments where balancing driver supply and passenger demand is critical. Although driver-side subsidies serve as a primary lever to align these forces and improve key KPIs like completed rides (\texttt{Rides}) and gross merchandise value (\texttt{GMV}), optimizing them in production requires simultaneously meeting three constraints: (i) responsiveness to stochastic shocks, (ii) strict subsidy-rate caps, and (iii) low-latency execution at city scale. These requirements rule out expensive per-order optimization, calling for a forward-looking, constraint-aware city-level controller for online sequential decision making. To meet these requirements, we introduce D$^3$-Subsidy (Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy), a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for deployable city-wide subsidy control. To bridge the train-inference gap, D$^3$-Subsidy employs a prefix-conditioned diffusion model that samples plausible future trajectories from immutable historical observations, ensuring the training protocol aligns with the fixed-history nature of online deployment. These generated plans are then decoded by a context-conditioned inverse module into low-dimensional city-level control signals. For scalable execution, we bridge the gap between city-level planning and fine-grained dispatch via a Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping, which embeds subsidy-rate caps directly into order-driver incentives without iterative optimization. Additionally, a multi-city pretraining strategy with parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables robust transfer across heterogeneous cities. Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.

</details>


### [189] [Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20040)
*Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Jalal Etesami,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了主动采样在情境多臂老虎机中的优势，通过优化上下文的采样分配，理论上显著降低了简单遗憾，并提出了适用于未知环境的EETC算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限上下文空间下的情境多臂老虎机问题，旨在通过主动选择上下文采样提高对各情境下奖励的估计精度，从而最小化上下文加权的简单遗憾。

Method: 1. 对于已知上下文分布 p，推导了被动与主动采样下的遗憾上界；
2. 提出主动采样策略：将采样概率分配设为 q_j ∝ p_j^{2/3}；
3. 对于未知上下文分布，提出 EETC (Explore-Explore-Then-Commit) 算法，平衡上下文估计与主动分配切换时机。

Result: 1. 主动采样策略将遗憾率从被动采样的 O(√n/T · ||p||_{1/2}) 提升至 O(√n/T · ||p||_{2/3})，最多可带来 Θ(k^{1/4}) 的增益；
2. 证明了有限预算下的主动采样策略是有效的；
3. EETC算法在大时间窗下实现了最优的遗憾收敛率。

Conclusion: 通过主动采样选择上下文分布，能够显著降低带权简单遗憾，且在未知分布下EETC算法能够达成与已知分布主动采样几乎相同的最优遗憾率。

Abstract: We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [190] [Towards Distillation Guarantees under Algorithmic Alignment for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20074)
*Thien Le,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合优化任务中的知识蒸馏，证明了当GNN架构与任务的动态规划算法保持“算法对齐”时，可以实现高效的知识蒸馏，并给出了相应的理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化预测场景中，利用特定算法架构（如GNN与DP对齐）来引导知识蒸馏，旨在解决如何通过蒸馏将大规模模型的知识高效迁移至更小、更适合部署的专用模型中的问题。

Method: 利用决策树（DT）蒸馏的学习理论分析框架（Boix-Adsera, 2024），结合线性表示假设（LRH），将蒸馏问题转化为在DP转移函数（表现为DT）的复杂度参数内进行有效求解。

Result: 在假设源模型具备足够的表征能力（LRH）的前提下，证明了蒸馏过程可以在DP转移函数的复杂度参数下实现高效求解。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析证明，当目标模型（GNN）的架构与任务的动态规划（DP）算法对齐时，蒸馏过程是高效的；该研究为算法对齐（algorithmic alignment）框架下的蒸馏成功提供了严谨的充分条件。

Abstract: Distillation transfers knowledge from a large model trained on broad data to a smaller, more efficient model suitable for deployment. In structured prediction settings, prior knowledge about the task can guide the choice of a target architecture that is algorithmically aligned with the underlying problem. Building on recent learning-theoretic analyses of decision-tree (DT) distillation (Boix-Adsera, 2024), we study when distillation succeeds for combinatorial optimization tasks. We focus on the case where the target model is a graph neural network whose architecture is aligned with a dynamic programming (DP) algorithm for the task. Assuming that the source model is sufficiently rich, formalized through the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) (Elhage et al., 2022; Park et al., 2024), we show that the distillation problem can be solved efficiently in the complexity parameters of the DP transition function, represented as a DT. Our results provide a rigorous sufficient condition for successful distillation in the flavour of algorithmic alignment.

</details>


### [191] [INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20088)
*Seongjun Lee,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSHAPE 是一种新型时序分类框架，通过挖掘每个时序专属的局部形状子并建模其依赖关系，实现了预测性能与实例级/全局级可解释性的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形状子研究多关注全局数据层面的模式，忽略了时序数据特有的实例差异性；且传统方法常将形状子视为独立个体，未能捕捉模式间的重要时序交互。

Method: 提出 INSHAPE 框架，采用自底向上的方法，为每个时序数据发现变长的、非重叠的局部形状子，并建模这些模式间的时序依赖关系，最后通过聚合实例级模式生成原型（全局级）形状子。

Result: 在 128 个 UCR 和 30 个 UEA 基准数据集上的实验表明，INSHAPE 在分类准确率上优于现有的前沿形状子方法，并能提供更直观、深入的解释。

Conclusion: INSHAPE 框架通过实例级形状子（Instance-level shapelets）发现机制，有效解决了传统方法在实例特异性特征捕捉和模式间依赖关系建模上的局限，实现了优异的时序分类性能与高度的可解释性。

Abstract: Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.

</details>


### [192] [Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20104)
*Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Xinyi Hu,Quan Kong,Baolin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graft 是一种针对推测解码的无损、无需训练框架，通过动态剪枝释放计算资源，并利用检索技术“嫁接”高质量 token，有效提升了大模型推理的并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码（SD）方法中，密集草稿树导致计算开销过大，而现有的“动态深度剪枝”策略虽然降低了延迟，却因丢弃潜在有效候选集导致接受率下降。研究旨在解决这种性能与效率之间的帕累托权衡（Pareto tradeoff）。

Method: 提出 Graft 框架，采用“先剪枝、后嫁接（prune-then-graft）”的机制。利用剪枝释放计算预算，并结合检索技术将高置信度 token 自动填充至剪枝后的树中，从而平衡计算开销与预测覆盖率。

Result: Graft 在多种场景（短/长上下文、大规模模型）下建立了新的帕累托边界；在短上下文基准测试中实现了高达 5.41 倍的加速；在 Qwen3-235B 模型上相比 EAGLE-3 的平均加速比提升了高达 21.8%。

Conclusion: Graft 通过“剪枝-再嫁接”策略打破了草稿树推理中“计算预算”与“覆盖率”之间的帕累托权衡，为大规模语言模型推理速度提升提供了一种无需训练、无损且高效的新方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of dense trees. In this paper, we identify a critical opportunity in resource allocation: the transition from dense to pruned drafting frees up significant computational budget. To break this Pareto tradeoff, we introduce Graft, a compensation framework that couples pruning and retrieval as mutually reinforcing operations. Pruning supplies sufficient budget for retrieval, while retrieval compensates for pruning-induced coverage loss and recovers accepted length. By employing a sequential `prune-then-graft' mechanism, Graft attaches highly predictive retrieved tokens into positions opened by pruning, filling the topological gaps with near-zero overhead. Graft is entirely training-free and lossless. Comprehensive evaluations show that Graft establishes a new Pareto frontier across practical deployment settings, including short-context generation, long-context generation, and large-scale models. On short-context benchmarks, it achieves up to 5.41$\times$ speedup and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8% on the large-scale Qwen3-235B. We also provide a preliminary exploration of applying Graft to the DFlash-style block drafting paradigm, offering initial evidence and insights for extending grafting beyond autoregressive draft trees.

</details>


### [193] [Optimal Representation Size: High-Dimensional Analysis of Pretraining and Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20105)
*Valentina Njaradi,Clémentine Dominé,Rachel Swanson,Marco Mondelli,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数学模型分析了预训练与微调范式，量化了表征维度、样本规模和任务对齐度对泛化误差的影响，揭示了调节表征压缩水平以优化下游表现的核心条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解“预训练+微调/线性探测”这一主流学习范式，探讨如何从有限的数据中实现更好的泛化，特别是揭示预训练表征结构如何影响下游任务的表现，以及如何平衡预训练数据与有标签数据。

Method: 建立了基于PCA（主成分分析）进行结构提取，并结合线性回归进行下游任务学习的分析模型。在极高维极限下，导出了训练误差与泛化误差的精确表达式，分析了表征维度、样本量及任务对齐度对泛化性能的影响。

Result: 导出了泛化误差的精确解析解，量化了预训练数据对标签数据的替代率，发现最优表征维度取决于下游任务参数和两阶段数据的相对规模。结论表明，在特定条件下，预训练阶段的压缩有助于提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 优化预训练表征维度对下游任务泛化至关重要。研究明确了预训练数据量与标签数据量之间的权衡关系，并指出在预训练数据充足但下游标签稀缺时，高度压缩的表征更优；反之则需要更高维的表征。这一结论在自编码器及大语言模型中也得到了验证。

Abstract: Learning to generalise from limited data is a fundamental challenge for both artificial and biological systems. A common strategy is to extract reusable structure from abundant unlabelled data, enabling efficient adaptation to new tasks from limited labelled data. This two-stage paradigm is now standard in modern training pipelines, where pretraining is followed by fine-tuning or linear probing. We provide an analytical model of this process: structure extraction is formalized as principal component analysis on unlabelled data, and downstream learning as linear regression on a separate labelled dataset. In the high-dimensional regime, we derive exact expressions for training and generalisation error showcasing their dependence on representation dimensionality, unlabelled and labelled sample sizes, and task alignment. Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.

</details>


### [194] [Beyond Isotropy in JEPAs: Hamiltonian Geometry and Symplectic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20107)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JEPAs传统的各向同性正则化方法限制了表征的几何适应性。本文提出的HamJEPA通过利用哈密顿相空间动力学进行跨视图预测，替代固定边际分布，取得了显著优于现有基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）通常强制地将单视图嵌入拟合为各向同性高斯分布，这种做法隐含了欧几里得对称性假设，可能与下游任务实际的几何结构不匹配，导致次优的表征学习。

Method: 1. 理论分析：推导了在已知/未知下游几何约束下，固定各向同性目标带来的“各向同性代价”（price of isotropy）。
2. 构建HamJEPA框架：将每个视图表示为相空间状态(q, p)，利用学到的哈密顿蛙跳积分器（Hamiltonian leapfrog map）进行视图间的动态过渡预测。
3. 引入正则化：通过非各向同性缩放和谱下限来防止模型坍塌。

Result: 在CIFAR-100（无头token协议下）和ImageNet-100数据集上，HamJEPA在kNN和线性探测指标上均显著优于现有基线（如SIGReg），且消融实验证明辛耦合（symplectic coupling）是性能提升的核心源头。

Conclusion: JEPAs传统的各向同性高斯编码目标存在结构性偏差。研究提出通过引入基于哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics）的跨视图耦合机制（HamJEPA），替代固定的编码器边际预测，能更有效地捕捉下游任务的几何结构，从而显著提升表征学习效率和性能。

Abstract: JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation. We show that this is not merely a benign default. For a known structured downstream geometry $H\succ0$, the minimax and maximum-entropy covariance under a Hamiltonian energy budget is $(c/d)H^{-1}$, and Euclidean isotropy incurs a closed-form price of isotropy. More importantly, when the downstream geometry is unknown, no geometry-independent fixed marginal target is canonical: every fixed covariance shape can be maximally misaligned for some structured geometry. We further show that even oracle one-view marginals do not identify the JEPA view-to-view predictive coupling. These results suggest that the structural bias in JEPAs should enter the cross-view coupling rather than a fixed encoder marginal. We instantiate this principle with \textbf{HamJEPA}, which encodes each view as a phase-space state $(q,p)$ and predicts view-to-view transitions with a learned Hamiltonian leapfrog map, while non-isotropic scale and spectral floors prevent collapse. In a deliberately headless token protocol, HamJEPA improves over SIGReg on CIFAR-100 by $+4.89$ kNN@20 and $+3.52$ linear-probe points at 30 epochs, and by $+6.45$ kNN@20 and $+10.64$ linear-probe points at 80 epochs, while a matched MLP predictor ablation shows that the symplectic coupling is the ingredient driving the neighborhood-geometry gain. On ImageNet-100, HamJEPA-$q$ improves by $+4.82$ kNN@20 and $+7.52$ linear-probe points at 45 epochs.

</details>


### [195] [Toto 2.0: Time Series Forecasting Enters the Scaling Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20119)
*Emaad Khwaja,Chris Lettieri,Gerald Woo,Eden Belouadah,Marc Cenac,Guillaume Jarry,Enguerrand Paquin,Xunyi Zhao,Viktoriya Zhukov,Othmane Abou-Amal,Chenghao Liu,Ameet Talwalkar,David Asker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可扩展的训练配方和 u-muP 技术，推出了参数高达 2.5B 的高性能时间序列基础模型家族 Toto 2.0，在多个关键基准测试中实现了性能突破并进行了开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证时间序列基础模型在参数扩展（Scaling）方面的潜力，并解决现有时序模型在通用性、可靠性和性能上的局限性。

Method: 1. 提出了一种可扩展的训练配方（4M 到 2.5B 参数）；2. 采用 u-muP (unit-multiplicative Parameterization) 超参数迁移流水线进行高效训练；3. 构建并发布了五个开源的 Toto 2.0 模型家族。

Result: Toto 2.0 在 BOOM、GIFT-Eval 和 TIME 三大基准测试上均刷新了 SOTA 纪录，并以 Apache 2.0 协议开源了所有五个检查点（Checkpoints）。

Conclusion: Toto 2.0 证明了利用大规模训练配方和 u-muP 超参数迁移技术，可以将时间序列基础模型扩展至 2.5B 参数，并在多个基准测试中达到了新的 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: We show that time series foundation models scale: a single training recipe produces reliable forecast-quality improvements from 4M to 2.5B parameters. We release Toto 2.0, a family of five open-weights forecasting models trained under this recipe. The Toto 2.0 family sets a new state of the art on three forecasting benchmarks: BOOM, our observability benchmark; GIFT-Eval, the standard general-purpose benchmark; and the recent contamination-resistant TIME benchmark. This report describes our experimental results and details the design decisions behind Toto 2.0: its architecture and training recipe, training data, and the u-muP hyperparameter transfer pipeline. All five base checkpoints are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [196] [TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20134)
*Zhen Xiong,Shang-Ling Hsu,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrajTok是一种通用的轨迹编码器，通过多分辨率六边形分词和掩码预训练，从噪声原始GPS中学习可迁移的轨迹表示，在几何与运动学相关任务中均表现出色，展示了成为轨迹基础模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GPS数据具有连续性、噪声大、采样不规则等挑战，且现有的空间分词方法（细粒度导致稀疏，粗粒度导致混淆）难以在保持轨迹表示泛化性的同时兼顾几何与运动学特征。

Method: 提出了TrajTok框架：1. 采用多分辨率六边形单元划分空间；2. 设计了基于因子分解的Transformer编码器，包含早期模态内自注意力、跨模态融合层及ST-RoPE（时空旋转位置嵌入）；3. 采用掩码标记建模（Masked-token modeling）进行预训练。

Result: 在Porto数据集的轨迹相似度搜索、分类、预计到达时间（ETA）及全行程时间回归等任务上，使用冻结的TrajTok编码器配合轻量级适配器，表现优于多种特定任务方法。

Conclusion: 多分辨率空间分词技术与掩码标记预训练相结合，是构建通用轨迹基础模型的有效途径。TrajTok模型证明了不仅能捕捉几何特征，还能捕捉运动学特征，具有强大的迁移能力。

Abstract: Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.

</details>


### [197] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有向图建模多生成模型间的交互，量化了训练数据不再服从真实分布且模型间相互依赖带来的坍塌风险，并给出了判定模型坍塌的数学条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单一模型自回归引发的坍塌，忽略了生成式AI时代多模型交互、数据分布偏离真实分布所带来的系统性风险。

Method: 通过有向图形式化模型间的复杂交互关系，针对线性回归模型和一般M估计推导有限样本界和渐近统计保证。

Result: 揭示了模型交互拓扑对模型性能衰退的关键影响，从理论上界定了模型坍塌存在的边界条件。

Conclusion: 模型坍塌是否发生取决于交互拓扑结构，作者推导出模型坍塌的充分必要条件，并证明了在线性回归和通用M估计下的收敛性质。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [198] [Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20182)
*Xinyang Tian,Ruitao Liu,Ziyi Ye,Siyang Xue,Xin Wang,Xuesong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“微状态分词器”的通用EEG表征学习方法，通过将EEG信号离散化为微状态序列，在多个任务上实现了优于传统时频特征的表现，且具备更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的EEG分析方法主要依赖人工提取时域或频域特征，缺乏通用性。本文旨在探索微状态这一表征方式，以实现跨不同任务的通用EEG表征学习。

Method: 通过在大型医疗EEG数据集上进行聚类，构建了一个通用的“微状态分词器（Microstate Tokenizer）”，将连续EEG信号转化为离散的微状态序列，并将其应用于睡眠分期、情绪识别和运动想象等多种下游任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于微状态的EEG表征在多种模型和不同下游任务上，均表现出超越传统时频特征的性能。

Conclusion: 微状态（Microstates）作为一种通用的EEG表征方法，在性能、可解释性和可扩展性方面均优于传统的时频域特征，为神经信息学和脑机接口研究提供了一种高效的通用范式。

Abstract: Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Markov Chain Decoders Overcome the Heavy-Tail Limitations of Lipschitz Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18931)
*Abdelhakim Ziani,Andras Horvath,Paolo Ballarini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 标准VAE因其高斯解码器和Lipschitz约束无法准确生成重尾分布数据，本研究提出引入基于马尔可夫链的相位型（PH）分布作为解码器以修正这一缺陷，显著提升了模型对重尾数据特征的拟合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度生成模型（如标准VAE）受限于高斯解码器似然及Lipschitz连续性约束，无法生成具有重尾特性的高质量数据。高斯分布呈指数衰减，且Lipschitz限制阻止了模型对稀有事件的有效放大。

Method: 提出使用相位型（PH）分布结构替代传统的高斯分布作为VAE的解码器似然函数，同时保持编码器、潜在空间和训练过程不变。利用PH分布能够对正值分布（包括重尾分布）进行任意精度逼近的特性来克服模型的结构限制。

Result: 在不同尾指数和维度下的合成Pareto数据实验表明，相比高斯基准模型，基于PH分布的模型在重尾数据上的尾部柯尔莫哥洛夫-斯米尔诺夫（K-S）距离降低至原来的1/6，极端分位数误差降低至原来的1/10。

Conclusion: 将基于马尔可夫链的相位型（PH）分布集成到变分自编码器（VAE）的解码器中，是解决生成模型处理重尾分布这一挑战的原则性且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed distributions are prevalent in performance evaluation, network traffic, and risk modeling. This behavior poses a fundamental challenge for modern deep generative models. Standard Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) employ Gaussian decoder likelihoods and Lipschitz-constrained neural networks, a combination that is structurally incapable of producing heavy-tailed outputs: the Gaussian tail decays exponentially, and Lipschitz continuity prevents the decoder from amplifying rare events from the latent space input to sufficiently overcome this decay. We provide both a theoretical characterization of this limitation and a controlled empirical demonstration using synthetic Pareto data across a grid of tail indices $α$ $\in$ {2, 3, 5, 30} and dimensions d $\in$ {1, 5, 10}. As a solution, we replace the Gaussian decoder with a Phase-Type (PH) distribution based on Markov chains, while keeping the encoder, latent space, and training procedure identical. PH distributions allow for arbitrarily precise approximations of any positive-valued distributions, including heavy-tailed families. Experiments showed that the PH-based model reduces tail Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance by up to x6 and extreme quantile error by up to x10 compared to the Gaussian baseline for heavy-tailed data. These results demonstrate that integrating Markov chain-based distributions into the decoder of a generative model institutes a principled and practically effective solution to the heavy-tail generation problem.

</details>


### [200] [Conformal Prediction via Transported Beta Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19024)
*Thiago R. Ramos,Helton Graziadei,Luben M. C. Cabezas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用 Beta 分布作为有限样本基准的分析框架，利用 Wasserstein 距离量化了共形预测在非 i.i.d. 环境下对边际覆盖保证的偏离，为理解复杂数据分布下的预测可靠性提供了更深层的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分裂共形预测（Split conformal prediction）仅能提供期望意义下的边际覆盖保证，无法揭示在特定校准样本下覆盖率的变化规律，也难以处理非独立同分布数据导致的覆盖失效。因此，需要一套更细致的、基于分布的有限样本推断框架。

Method: 建立以 Beta(k, n+1-k) 分布为基准的分析框架，利用 Wasserstein 距离在 [0,1] 区间上量化覆盖率的偏差。研究细分了测试侧偏移（transport map）与校准集依赖（order-statistic law 的形变）对覆盖效果的不同作用机制，并结合 Berry-Esseen 近似处理多种分布假设。

Result: 研究推导出在连续 i.i.d. 环境下，条件覆盖率服从精确的 Beta 分布。在尺度偏移、聚类数据及平稳混合等非 i.i.d. 场景下，该框架成功推导出了覆盖率偏差的显式边界。仿真实验验证了该方法在中小样本规模下对 Wasserstein 偏差具有良好的追踪性能。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用 Beta(k, n+1-k) 分布作为有限样本下的基准，可以有效量化共形预测对边际覆盖保证的偏离。通过 Wasserstein 距离框架，该方法能够清晰区分不同非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）行为的来源，并为复杂场景下的偏差提供了一致的分析工具。

Abstract: Split conformal prediction provides finite-sample marginal coverage under exchangeability, but this guarantee averages over the random calibration sample. We study instead the law of the calibration-conditional coverage induced by a realized conformal threshold. In the continuous i.i.d. setting this law is exactly $Beta(k,n+1-k)$, so the usual marginal guarantee corresponds to its mean. We take this beta law as a finite-sample reference object and quantify departures from it using Wasserstein distances on $[0,1]$. The framework yields direct bounds on marginal coverage gaps and on bad-calibration probabilities, and separates different sources of non-i.i.d. behavior according to how they deform the beta reference: test-side shift acts through a transport map on the coverage scale, while calibration dependence changes the order-statistic law itself. We instantiate the framework in scale-shift, clustered, and stationary mixing settings, where the induced deformations can be characterized explicitly or through Berry-Esseen approximations. Simulations on dependent processes confirm that the first-order approximation tracks the empirical Wasserstein distance even at moderate sample sizes.

</details>


### [201] [Provably Data-driven Lagrangian Relaxation for Mixed Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19052)
*Tung Quoc Le,Anh Tuan Nguyen,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对利用机器学习驱动拉格朗日乘子（LR）进行了严谨的理论分析，证明了该方法在统计上的可行性，通过SGA算法达到了极小极大最优收敛速率，并揭示了预热（warm-start）策略在理论上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管利用机器学习预测拉格朗日乘子（LR）在经验上表现良好，但缺乏严谨的理论支撑。本文旨在填补这一空白，从统计学习角度探讨此类方法在处理大规模MILP问题时的泛化性能和理论极限。

Method: 本文利用数据驱动算法设计的统计学习视角进行分析：1. 使用VC维或Rademacher复杂度等工具推导泛化界；2. 通过极小极大分析建立下界；3. 利用随机梯度上升（SGA）算法及其平均化策略实现求解；4. 设计并分析了学习预热（warm-start）方案。

Result: 1. 推导出习得乘子的广义泛化界为 $\mathcal{O}(s^{1.5}/\sqrt{N})$；2. 证明了极小极大下界为 $\Omega(s/\sqrt{N})$，说明线性依赖是不可避免的；3. 证明了带有平均化策略的随机梯度上升（SGA）算法可以达到极小极大最优解 $\Theta(s/\sqrt{N})$；4. 证明了学习预热（warm-start）策略可以实现更快的 $\Theta(s/N)$ 收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了通过数据驱动方法学习拉格朗日乘子是有效的，并确立了收敛速率的上下界。通过随机梯度上升法（SGA）达到了最优极小极大速率 $\Theta(s/\sqrt{N})$，并证明了“学会预热”（warm-start）机制在理论上优于直接预测乘子，其收敛速率可达 $\Theta(s/N)$。

Abstract: Lagrangian Relaxation (LR) is a powerful technique for solving large-scale Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP), particularly those with decomposable structures, such as vehicle routing or unit commitment problems. By relaxing the coupling constraints, LR enables parallel subproblem solving and often yields tighter dual bounds than standard linear programming relaxations, which is crucial for efficient branch-and-bound pruning. While recent empirical work has shown promising results using machine learning to predict these multipliers, a theoretical understanding of such methods remains an open question. In this work, we bridge this gap by analyzing the problem of learning LR through the lens of Data-driven Algorithm Design, i.e., a statistical learning problem over a distribution of problem instances. Our contributions are as follows: first, we derive a generalization bound of $\mathcal{O}(s^{1.5}/\sqrt{N})$ for the learned multipliers, where $s$ is the number of coupling constraints and $N$ is the sample size. Second, we provide a minimax lower-bound of $Ω(s/\sqrt{N})$, proving that a linear dependency is unavoidable. Third, we constructively close this theoretical gap by proving that Stochastic Gradient Ascent (SGA) with averaging achieves the minimax optimal rate $Θ(s/\sqrt{N})$. Finally, we extend our framework to the learning-to-warm-start setting, proving that it achieves a fast, minimax-optimal rate of $Θ(s/N)$ and establishing a theoretical advantage over direct multiplier prediction.

</details>


### [202] [Information Processing Capacity of Stationary Physical Systems: Theory, Data-efficient Estimation Methods, and Photonic Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19152)
*Rahul Uma Ramachandran,Serge Massar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究为物理计算系统提出了标准化的“信息处理能力”（IPC）评估框架，通过严谨的理论推导和实验验证，实现了对复杂物理系统计算能力的定量描述，并预测了其在机器学习任务中的性能潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前物理计算系统计算能力的表征缺乏原理性、任务无关且数据高效的方法，难以定量评估其硬件原生机器学习能力。

Method: 扩展了原有的信息处理能力（IPC）框架至平稳物理系统；推导了IPC的理论边界（0-1之间及其总和）、噪声影响及有限样本估计的偏差修正；提出了基于Richardson外推法和Sobol准随机采样的数据高效估计方法。

Result: 证明了IPC的理论极限；修正了估计器的偏差；实验验证了光子计算系统中Kerr效应导致的IPC分布变化；确认了总IPC与机器学习基准任务表现及系统有效维度的强相关性。

Conclusion: IPC是一种能够表征物理计算系统计算能力、并将其与其机器学习性能有效连接的指标，不仅通过了实验验证，还提供了衡量系统有效维度的客观手段。

Abstract: Physical computing systems provide a promising route toward hardware-native machine learning, but their computational capabilities remain difficult to characterize in a principled, task-independent, and data-efficient way. We extend the Information Processing Capacity (IPC) framework to stationary physical computing systems and establish several fundamental results: individual capacities are bounded between zero and one, their sum over a complete basis is bounded by the number of readouts, and noise strictly reduces this bound. We address the finite-sample estimation of IPC and derive the asymptotic form of the systematic positive bias affecting naive estimators. Building on these results, we introduce data-efficient estimation methods based on Richardson extrapolation and Sobol quasi-random sampling. We validate the framework experimentally using a photonic computing system based on picosecond laser pulses propagating through a nonlinear optical fibre. By varying the laser power and fibre length, we observe systematic shifts of the IPC distribution toward higher-order nonlinear capacities induced by the Kerr effect. Finally, we demonstrate that the total IPC strongly correlates with performance on benchmark machine-learning tasks and provides a reliable estimate of the effective dimensionality of the system. These results establish IPC as a practical bridge between the intrinsic dynamics of physical computing systems and their machine-learning performance.

</details>


### [203] [Reducing Diffusion Model Memorization with Higher Order Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19170)
*Benjamin Sterling,Mónica F. Bugallo,Tom Tirer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了高阶朗之万动力学（HOLD）通过对分数函数进行低通滤波来平滑数据轨迹的正则化机制，证明了增加模型阶数能显著降低扩散模型的记忆化（memorization）风险，从而更好地保护数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高，但存在严重的记忆（memorization）问题，可能引发侵权与隐私泄露风险；研究旨在探究高阶朗之万动力学（HOLD）作为一种正则化手段对缓解该问题的效果与机制。

Method: 理论分析与实验验证相结合：利用辅助变量将数据视为位置引入速度和加速度，通过数学证明揭示HOLD动力学本质上是学习分数函数的低通滤波版本，并对比了不同阶数下的分布坍缩风险与记忆化表现。

Result: 理论上证明了HOLD的阶数越高，分数函数的平滑效果越好；实验表明，随着模型阶数的增加，模型对训练数据的记忆程度显著降低，同时验证了该方法相比于标准扩散模型在防御记忆化方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了高阶朗之万动力学（HOLD）通过低通滤波效应平滑分数函数，从而有效缓解了扩散模型中的训练数据记忆（memorization）问题，并验证了其在实际应用中的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion/score-based models have emerged as powerful generative models, capable of generating high-quality samples that mimic the training data distribution. However, it has been observed that they are prone to reproducing training samples-known as "memorization"-potentially violating copyright and privacy. In this paper, we study the effect of Higher-Order Langevin Dynamics (HOLD) on this phenomenon. HOLD diffusion processes introduce auxiliary variables; if the data variable is interpreted as "position," then the auxiliary variables can be interpreted as "velocity" and "acceleration," depending on the chosen order of the model. They were originally proposed based on the intuition that they regularize the trajectories of the data variable by implicitly imposing additional dynamical constraints. Our work provides, to our knowledge, the first theoretical characterization of the regularization effect of HOLD. Specifically, we show that in HOLD, the dynamics of the data variable are governed by a low-pass-filtered version of the learned score function, with smoothness increasing with the order of HOLD. We then analyze the optimal empirical score and the possibility of distribution collapse. Together, our results explain the mitigation of memorization as the model order increases. Finally, we present an empirical study on real-world data that supports our theory and highlights this distinct advantage of HOLD over standard diffusion in practice.

</details>


### [204] [Factor Augmented High-Dimensional SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19291)
*Shubo Li,Yuefeng Han,Xiufan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FSGD 是一种新型的流式高维 SGD 优化算法，通过联合优化隐变量和模型参数，实现了无需预先降维的大规模机器学习扩展性，并给出了理论收敛保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统 SGD 在处理高维数据时效率低、难以直接应用的问题，并克服以往降维方法依赖离线处理和全量数据存储的局限性。

Method: 提出 Factor-Augmented SGD (FSGD) 算法，通过隐变量表示处理高维数据，摒弃了传统的离线降维两阶段法，直接在流式数据上进行端到端的联合训练。

Result: 建立了首个显式考虑隐变量估计误差的 SGD 理论分析框架，并证明了在衰减步长和 mini-batch 更新策略下，该算法在 $\ell^s$ 范数下的矩收敛性。

Conclusion: FSGD 为高维机器学习中的 SGD 应用提供了一个可靠且可扩展的理论基础，证明了在在线流式数据中隐变量表示学习与模型参数优化结合的可行性。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is a fundamental optimization algorithm widely used in modern machine learning. In this paper, we propose Factor-Augmented SGD (FSGD), a new optimization method that leverages latent factor representations in high-dimensional learning tasks. Unlike standard two-stage dimension reduction approaches that rely on offline representation learning and full data storage, a key novelty of FSGD is that it operates purely on streaming data, making it scalable to large-scale and high-dimensional problems. Furthermore, we establish the first theoretical framework that explicitly incorporates latent factor estimation error into the analysis of SGD, and provide moment convergence in $\ell^s$ norm under decaying step sizes and mini-batch updates. Our results provide a new foundation for employing SGD reliably and scalably in high-dimensional machine learning systems.

</details>


### [205] [A Unified Framework for Structure-Aware Clustering and Heterogeneous Causal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19313)
*Honglin Du,Muxuan Liang,Xiang Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ADMM的DAG依赖聚类框架（DAG-DC-ADMM），通过联合学习聚类和结构方程模型，有效解决了多元系统中跨受试者因果结构异构性的识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常忽略了多元系统中跨受试者的结构异构性，导致偏差并掩盖了特定子群体的依赖关系，因此需要一种能同时学习聚类和相关依赖结构的方法。

Method: 提出了DAG-DC-ADMM框架，该框架基于结构方程模型（SEM），利用平滑约束（smooth constraint）实现无环性，采用分组截断Lasso融合惩罚（gTLP）进行聚类引导，并使用针对差分凸规划定制的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 该方法能够以高真阳性率和低假阳性率恢复特定聚类的因果依赖结构，且在已知或未知子群体标签的情况下均能有效发现异构依赖性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能够同时估计聚类分配和特定聚类依赖结构的框架，在处理未知子群体的异构复杂系统时具有鲁棒性，并通过理论收敛性保证和实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In complex multivariate systems, interactions among variables are defined by dependency structures, often encoded as directed acyclic graphs ($\text{DAGs}$). However, dependency structures can vary across subjects, and ignoring this structural heterogeneity introduces bias and obscures subpopulation-specific dependencies. To address this, we propose Directed Acyclic Graph-based Dependency Clustering via Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (DAG-DC-ADMM), a unified framework built upon Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) that jointly learns cluster assignments and cluster-specific dependency structures. We encode acyclicity via a smooth constraint and integrate a groupwise truncated Lasso fusion penalty (gTLP) to cluster subjects based on their structural similarity. This yields a nonconvex optimization problem that incorporates sparsity, acyclicity, and structural consensus constraints. We address the nonconvexity by using the augmented Lagrangian method and solve it with an adapted version of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) for difference-of-convex programs. For certain graph structures, such as upper triangular adjacency matrices, our algorithm is guaranteed to converge to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) point. Experiments demonstrate that our method recovers cluster-specific causal dependency structures with a high true positive rate and a low false discovery rate. This capability enables the robust discovery of heterogeneous dependencies across subjects where the subpopulation label is unknown.

</details>


### [206] [Tweedie's Formulae and Diffusion Generative Models Beyond Gaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19391)
*Wenpin Tang,Nizar Touzi,Zikun Zhang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将 Tweedie 公式推广至非高斯随机过程（如 GBM、BESQ、CIR），并证实了这些非高斯扩散模型在图像与时间序列生成任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型多基于高斯噪声，对具有状态相关扩散系数的非高斯扩散模型及相应的 Tweedie 公式研究不足。

Method: 将 Tweedie 公式推广至几何布朗运动 (GBM)、平方贝塞尔 (BESQ) 过程和 Cox-Ingersoll-Ross (CIR) 过程，并推导出相应的去噪分数匹配目标函数。

Result: 成功实现了基于 GBM 和 CIR 的扩散模型在图像与金融时间序列生成中的应用，并实现了 BESQ 环境下的经验贝叶斯估计，实验结果验证了非高斯模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过将 Tweedie 公式推广至多种非高斯过程，证明了非高斯扩散模型在图像生成、金融时间序列生成及经验贝叶斯估计中具有显著的潜力，为扩散模型的研究开辟了新的范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating samples from unknown data distributions. Most popular stochastic differential equation-based diffusion models perturb the target distribution by adding Gaussian noise, transforming it into a simple prior, and then use denoising score matching, a consequence of Tweedie's formula, to learn the score function and generate clean samples from noise. However, non-Gaussian diffusion models with state-dependent diffusion coefficient have been largely underexplored, as have the corresponding Tweedie's formulae. In this work, we extend Tweedie's formula to important non-Gaussian processes, including geometric Brownian motion (GBM), squared Bessel (BESQ) processes, and Cox-Ingersoll-Ross (CIR) processes, thereby yielding the corresponding denoising score-matching objectives. We then apply the derived formulae to image and financial time series generation using GBM- and CIR-based diffusion models, and to empirical Bayes estimation under the BESQ setting. The reported experimental results demonstrate the potential of non-Gaussian models.

</details>


### [207] [Density-Ratio Losses for Post-Hoc Learning to Defer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19557)
*Alexander Soen,Ragnar Thobaben,Joakim Jaldén,Richard Nock*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过密度比估计实现了无需重训练的后验延迟决策（L2D），将L2D重塑为“理想分布”间的差异学习，统一了经典的Chow延迟规则并提升了模型在不同数据集下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种统一的、能够灵活调整延迟率且无需大规模重训练的后验L2D理论框架。

Method: 提出基于理想分布（divergence-regularized reweightings）的L2D框架，通过密度比估计（density-ratio estimation）归约为类概率估计（class-probability estimation），实现无需重训练的门限化延迟决策。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多种数据集设置中具备竞争力，且在鲁棒性方面优于常见基准方法；同时明确了其在KL散度下与Chow准则及专家倾斜贝叶斯后验的数学联系。

Conclusion: 后验学习延迟（Post-hoc L2D）可以被理解为理想分布之间的密度比学习。这种框架架起了Chow类准则、专家比较与异常检测等相关学习设置之间的桥梁，提供了更具通用性的理论视角。

Abstract: We study post-hoc Learning to Defer (L2D) through the lens of ideal distributions: divergence-regularized reweightings of the data distribution under which a model attains low loss. We define deferral via the density-ratio between a model's and an expert's ideals. Using the reduction from density-ratio estimation to class-probability estimation, we derive the DR CPE losses for post-hoc L2D scorers. Deferral decisions are then made by thresholding the scorer, allowing deferral rates to be adjusted without retraining. For KL-based ideal distributions, our deferral rules recovers Chow's rule under the original distribution and a connection to an expert-tilted Bayes posterior -- which incorporates the expert's performance -- depending on if the ideal distributions are joint or marginal distributions. Experimentally, our approach is competitive compared to common baselines and more robust across dataset settings. More broadly, our results cast post-hoc L2D as density-ratio learning between ideal distributions, bridging Chow-style rules, expert comparison, and elucidating connections to related learning settings including anomaly detection.

</details>


### [208] [Optimizing Computational-Statistical Runtime for Wasserstein Distance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20122)
*Peter Matthew Jacobs,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对计算经验测度间平方Wasserstein距离时的计算开销难题，提出了一种名为“采样-草图-求解”的新范式，通过在计算前进行正则化网格草图压缩，显著提升了算法在低维平滑分布数据下的运行效率，实现了理论上的最优或近最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方Wasserstein距离计算在处理样本量为n的经验测度时，即使在低维（d=2,3）空间中，现有的精确或近似算法随着n和精度要求的变化，运行效率极低。需要在样本采样难度和计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出“采样-草图-求解”（Sample-Sketch-Solve）范式：首先对样本进行正则化笛卡尔网格草图化处理（Regular Cartesian Grid Sketching），以压缩数据并正则化结构，随后对这些简化后的数据应用快速精确求解算法。

Result: 对于定义在(0,1)^d上且具有α-Hölder平滑性的分布P和Q，该方法能在 ε^{-max(2, (d+1+o(1))/(1+α))} 时间内将 Wasserstein 距离 W_2^2(P,Q) 近似到 ε 误差之内。在 d=2, α>1/2 时达到最优的 Θ(ε^-2)，当 d=3, α→1 时达到近最优的效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于样本草图（Sample-Sketch-Solve）的算法范式，通过正则化笛卡尔网格压缩数据，在处理平滑测度时实现了最优或近最优的计算复杂度，克服了计算传统Wasserstein距离在高维或高精度要求下的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Squared Wasserstein distance is a frequently used tool to measure discrepancy between probability distributions. This distance is typically computed between empirical measures of size $n$ from two underlying random samples. Unfortunately, even in lower dimensional Euclidean space problems $\left( d \in \{2,3\} \right)$, algorithms for Wasserstein distance computation with approximate or exact precision guarantees scale poorly in the runtime as a function of $n$ and the desired precision. In response, we consider the computational-statistical runtime, where the goal is to estimate from samples the Wasserstein distance between potentially smooth measures up to $ε$-additive error in expectation with respect to the sampling; we allow $O(1)$ computational cost for collecting a sample. Towards this, we develop a Sample-Sketch-Solve paradigm where we introduce a regular cartesian grid sketch of the samples. We show that (especially under $α$-Hölder smooth distributions) this can compress the data without increasing asymptotic error, and also regularizes the structure which enables faster exact algorithms. Ultimately, we approximate $W_2^2(P,Q)$ within $ε$ error in $ε^{-\max(2,\frac{d+1+o(1)}{1+α})}$ time for $0 < α< 1$ Hölder smooth distributions $P,Q$ on $(0,1)^{d}$; an optimal $Θ(ε^{-2})$ for $α> 1/2$ when $d=2$ and nearly optimal as $α\to 1$ when $d = 3$.

</details>


### [209] [Posterior Contraction of Lévy Adaptive B-spline Regression in Besov Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19610)
*Jeunghun Oh,Sewon Park,Jaeyong Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lévy过程的自适应B-样条（LABS）回归模型，实现了对未知平滑度函数的自适应估计，并从理论上证明了其在Besov空间中的近极小极大最优收缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有文献中关于LARK模型在Besov空间下后验收缩性质理论结果匮乏的问题，旨在构建一个能够自动适应未知平滑度并灵活捕捉函数不规则局部特征的回归模型。

Method: 采用贝叶斯非参数回归框架，将B-样条核整合进Lévy自适应回归核（LARK）模型，提出LABS模型；利用具有不同阶数和独立定义结点的样条进行函数估计。

Result: 理论上确立了LABS后验在Besov类中的收缩特性；实验结果表明LABS在处理诸如Blocks、Bumps、HeaviSine和Doppler等经典测试函数时表现良好，性能稳健。

Conclusion: 证明了LABS模型在Besov空间中具有自适应性，且后验收缩率在该类空间中达到了近极小极大最优性（仅差一个对数因子）。模拟实验验证了该模型在处理具有局部结构特征函数时的实用性。

Abstract: We investigate the asymptotic properties of the Lévy Adaptive B-spline (LABS) regression model, a Bayesian nonparametric method that incorporates B-spline kernels into the Lévy Adaptive Regression Kernel (LARK) model. LABS applies splines of varying degrees with independently defined knots, yielding a flexible model class capable of adapting to irregular and locally structured features of the true function. Within the nonparametric regression framework with univariate random design and Gaussian errors, we establish that the LABS posterior contracts around the true function in Besov classes at nearly minimax-optimal rates, up to a logarithmic factor, while adapting automatically to unknown smoothness. This study contributes to filling a gap in the literature, where theoretical results on posterior contraction of the LARK model in Besov spaces remain scarce. Simulation experiments on standard test functions in Besov spaces, including Blocks, Bumps, HeaviSine, and Doppler, complement the theoretical results and demonstrate the practical utility of LABS.

</details>


### [210] [Gaussian Approximation and Multiplier Bootstrap for Federated Linear Stochastic Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19629)
*Ilya Levin,Maksim Shuklin,Eric Moulines,Paul Mangold,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次为联邦线性随机近似提供了Berry-Esseen型误差界，量化了通信与异质性的影响，并提出了一种无需协方差估计的联邦推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究多关注收敛速度，缺乏对联邦环境下线性随机近似（LSA）精确分布近似（即Gaussian approximation）的理论分析，以及缺乏无需显式协方差估计的有效统计推断方法。

Method: 通过Berry-Esseen定理推导联邦LSA的误差界，分析了常数步长与变步长两种机制，并设计了一种基于在线乘子自助法（multiplier bootstrap）的推断程序。

Result: 获得了联邦LSA的首个高斯近似界限，量化了局部更新次数、步长及数据异质性对收敛速率的影响，并证明了所提在线乘子自助法在非渐近意义下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了联邦线性随机近似（LSA）的Berry-Esseen型界限，明确了通信-计算权衡与异质性对收敛误差的影响，并提出了一种无需显式协方差矩阵估计的在线乘子自助法（multiplier bootstrap），为联邦学习环境下的统计推断提供了非渐近有效性保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish Berry-Esseen-type bounds for federated linear stochastic approximation (LSA). Our results provide the first federated Gaussian approximations for LSA that explicitly capture communication-computation trade-offs and heterogeneity-aware error terms, quantifying the effects of local step size, number of local updates, and heterogeneity on convergence rates. We present results for both (i) constant step size regime and (ii) decreasing step size with an increasing number of local iterations, recovering the recent rates of Bonnerjee et al. [2025] as a special case. As a primary application of our results, we develop an online multiplier bootstrap procedure for inference on the last iterate, which avoids explicit estimation of the asymptotic covariance matrix, and obtain non-asymptotic validity guarantees for this procedure.

</details>


### [211] [Increasing Missingness to Reduce Bias: Richardson-SGD with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19641)
*Ferdinand Genans,Erwan Scornet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对不完整数据处理中的随机梯度偏差问题，本文提出了一种基于Richardson外推的去偏方法，通过在原始缺失数据上进一步人为引入受控缺失，有效抵消系统性梯度误差，提升了模型学习的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模机器学习中，针对不完整数据进行填补（Imputation）往往会引入系统性的梯度偏差，导致随机梯度下降（SGD）算法的性能下降。现有研究多局限于特定模型，缺乏通用的去偏机制。

Method: 1. 基于泰勒展开和偏差分析，精确刻画了参数模型中由数据缺失引入的梯度偏差对缺失比例向量p的依赖关系（主项为O(||p||)）。
2. 提出Richardson外推去偏法：通过在原始不完整数据基础上，进一步人为引入受控的缺失，结合原始和人为增加缺失后的梯度，抵消一阶偏差项。
3. 推广至多步Richardson过程：当缺失指标独立时，利用梯度偏差的多线性结构递归消除更高阶偏差项。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法可将偏差阶数显著降低，且适用于任何梯度可计算的参数化损失函数。实验表明该方法有效提升了广义线性模型（GLM）的优化效果，并证明了“主动增加缺失”反而能提高学习准确性的反直觉结论。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种基于Richardson外推法的去偏SGD算法，通过“人为增加缺失比例”来抵消原有数据缺失带来的梯度偏差。理论证明该方法能将梯度偏差从O(||p||)降低至O(||p||^2)，并支持多步递归去偏。实验证明该方法在广义线性模型中有效，且能与MICE等现有填补方法结合。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient methods are central to modern large-scale learning, but their use with incomplete covariates remains delicate since imputation schemes generally introduce systematic gradient biases, as shown for linear models. In this work, we prove that all parametric models exhibit similar gradient bias for various imputation procedures and characterize exactly the dependence on the missingness ratio vector $p$, with $O(\|p\|)$ as the leading term. We exploit this analysis to propose a simple debiasing procedure for stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with missing values based on Richardson extrapolation, which leverages the exact expression of the gradient bias. The key idea is to \emph{deliberately add missingness}: from an already incomplete observation, we generate a further-thinned version at a higher, controlled missingness level, and combine the two resulting stochastic gradients to cancel the leading bias term. We prove that one Richardson step reduces the gradient bias from $O(\|p\|)$ to $O(\|p\|^2)$ under several missingness scenarios. Our proposed method is computationally efficient, model-agnostic and applies to any parametric loss whose stochastic gradient can be computed after imputation. Furthermore, when missing indicators are independent, the population gradient bias is a multilinear polynomial in $p$ and depends only on population gradient errors induced by declaring a single coordinate missing. In this case, our method generalizes to a multi-step Richardson procedure which recursively cancels higher-order terms. Empirically, Richardson debiasing improves optimization and estimation across several generalized linear models and combines positively with widely used imputation procedures such as MICE. These results suggest that, somewhat counter-intuitively, adding controlled missingness on top of existing missing data can make stochastic learning from incomplete data more accurate.

</details>


### [212] [Tail Annealing for Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20068)
*Jean Pachebat*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Log-FM的简单数据转换方法，通过在流匹配模型训练前后应用软对数变换，有效解决了生成模型难以拟合重尾数据及数值不稳定的问题，无需修改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Lipschitz生成模型难以从高斯噪声中生成具有重尾特性的数据（即长尾数据），且现有处理重尾分布的方法存在训练不稳定性等问题。

Method: 提出了一种软对数变换 (soft-log transform) $\phi(x) = \mathrm{sign}(x) \cdot \log(1 + |x|)$，并配合Hill诊断方法按坐标决定是否进行变换，预处理数据后使用标准流匹配 (Flow Matching) 模型，采样后再进行反变换（指数映射）。

Result: 在包含144种配置的多变量基准测试中，Log-FM在$W_1$、CVaR$_{99}$和极端分位数指标上优于专用基线模型，并且在2,880次运行中保持了零严重发散的极高稳定性。

Conclusion: Log-FM方法通过简单有效的软对数变换，成功解决了生成模型处理重尾分布的难题，在保持标准生成架构的同时，显著提升了对极端值的建模能力与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Standard generative models struggle with heavy-tailed data: Lipschitz architectures cannot produce power-law tails from Gaussian noise, and interpolating between heavy-tailed data and Gaussians is ill-posed. We propose a simple fix: apply the soft-log transform $φ(x) = \mathrm{sign}(x) \cdot \log(1 + |x|)$ coordinate-wise to data before training, then exponentiate samples after generation. A Hill diagnostic decides per-coordinate whether to transform, leaving light-tailed margins untouched at no added complexity. This compresses heavy tails into a range where standard flow matching succeeds, without heavy-tailed base distributions or architectural modifications. We provide theoretical intuition for why this works: the log-transform maps Pareto tails to exponentials, and the induced dynamics implement a form of tail annealing via power transformations. On a 144-configuration multivariate benchmark (3 copulas, $d$ up to 100, 4 tail indices), Log-FM dominates specialized baselines on $W_1$, CVaR$_{99}$, and extreme-quantile metrics, and is the only method with zero severe divergences across 2{,}880 runs.

</details>


### [213] [Goal-Oriented Lower-Tail Calibration of Gaussian Processes for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20145)
*Aurélien Pion,Emmanuel Vazquez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯优化中高斯过程预测分布在低置信区间（低水平集）校准不足的问题，提出了 tcGP 事后校准方法，通过改进低尾部预测精度，有效提升了贝叶斯优化的寻优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化中，高斯过程的核选择及超参数不当会导致预测分布误校准，进而歪曲探索-利用平衡。特别是对于最小化任务，EI 准则高度依赖于当前最优值下方的尾部预测，因此低尾部的不准确性会直接损害优化决策。

Method: 1. 引入了两个空间校准概念：设计空间上的发生校准（occurrence calibration）和水平集上的阈值μ-校准（thresholded μ-calibration）。 2. 提出了 tcGP（threshold-calibrated GP），一种用于在阈值 t 以下校准 GP 预测分布的事后方案。 3. 在此基础上改进了 EI（期望提升）优化算法。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，tcGP 方法显示出相对于标准 GP 模型及全局校准 GP 模型更好的低尾部校准效果和更高的贝叶斯优化性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 tcGP 方法能够有效校准高斯过程在低阈值处的预测分布，从而改善贝叶斯优化的探索-利用平衡，在保证全局搜索密度的同时提升了优化性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) selects evaluation points for expensive black-box objectives using Gaussian process (GP) predictive distributions. Kernel choice and hyperparameter selection can lead to miscalibrated predictive distributions and an inappropriate exploration-exploitation trade-off. For minimization, sampling criteria such as expected improvement (EI) depend on the predictive distribution below the current best value, so lower-tail miscalibration directly affects the sampling decision. This article studies goal-oriented calibration of GP predictive distributions below a low threshold $t$ in the noiseless setting, for standard GP models with hyperparameters selected by maximum likelihood. A framework for predictive reliability below $t$ is introduced, based on two notions of spatial calibration: occurrence calibration over the design space and thresholded $μ$-calibration on sublevel sets of the form $\{x\in\mathbb{X}, f(x)\le t\}$. Building on this framework, we propose tcGP, a post-hoc method that calibrates GP predictive distributions below~$t$, and we show that the resulting EI-based global optimization algorithm remains dense in the design space. Experiments on standard benchmarks show improved lower-tail calibration and BO performance relative to standard GP models and globally calibrated GP models.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [DOTRAG: Retrieval-Time Reasoning Along Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18760)
*Larnell Moore,Naihao Deng,Rada Mihalcea,Farnaz Jahanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DotRAG 是一种无需训练的 GraphRAG 框架，通过引入“思维拆分”（DOT）机制，将检索过程重构为受查询引导的路径搜索，有效解决了传统 RAG 在复杂多跳任务中检索精度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的“先检索后推理”的 GraphRAG 模式在处理复杂多跳任务时，往往因依赖启发式规则而导致检索到无关上下文或遗漏关键关系路径，难以适应特定的查询逻辑。

Method: 提出 DotRAG 框架：1. 将检索重构为基于路径的推理过程；2. 引入“思维拆分”（Division of Thought, DOT）机制，将检索分解为局部的搜索空间，并根据查询动态调整搜索策略；3. 生成查询条件约束以引导图探索并裁剪无关区域，无需显式的逐步推理链。

Result: DotRAG 在 MetaQA 和 UltraDomain 数据集上达到了 SOTA（最先进）性能，并在多跳任务中表现出持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: DotRAG 通过引入“思维拆分”（DOT）将检索重构为一种推理过程，证明了推理引导的检索在处理复杂多跳查询时的优越性，为无需训练的 GraphRAG 范式提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) is dominated by a retrieve-then-reason paradigm, where context is retrieved using heuristics and then reasoned over. Such methods struggle to adapt to the query-specific logic required for complex multi-hop tasks, often accumulating irrelevant context or missing correct relational paths. We propose DotRAG, a training-free GraphRAG framework that reformulates retrieval as a reasoning process over paths. Our approach generates query-conditioned constraints that guide graph exploration, prune irrelevant regions, and iteratively discover relational paths without relying on explicit step-by-step reasoning chains. We introduce Division of Thought (DOT), an abstraction that decomposes retrieval into localized search spaces and adapts the search strategy to each query. DotRAG achieves SOTA performance on MetaQA and UltraDomain, with consistent gains on multi-hop tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-guided retrieval.

</details>


### [215] [ALDEN: Boosting Private Data Extraction from Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Active Learning and Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18762)
*Xingyu Lyu,Jianfeng He,Ning Wang,Yidan Hu,Tao Li,Danjue Chen,Shixiong Li,Yimin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ALDEN 是一种新型、高效的 RAG 系统数据提取攻击技术，它通过主动学习和利用知识库的主题分布信息，大幅提升了从 RAG 检索知识库中窃取私有数据的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对 RAG 系统的私有数据提取攻击在实际应用中效果有限，表现为数据提取率低且实用性不足。本研究旨在开发一种更高效、具备更强攻击能力的攻击方法。

Method: 1. 采用主动学习（Active Learning）策略，通过多样化的恶意查询来提升数据提取成功率；2. 提出一种基于衰减的动态算法（Decay-based Dynamic Algorithm），利用 RAG 知识库的数据分布特征来指导恶意查询的生成和优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，ALDEN 在数据提取率和实际执行效果上显著超过了当前最先进（SOTA）的攻击方法。

Conclusion: ALDEN 是一种高效的针对 RAG 系统的私有数据提取攻击框架，通过结合主动学习和知识库分布估计，在提取效率和成功率上显著优于现有方法，暴露了 RAG 系统的潜在隐私安全风险。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to augment large language models with external knowledge retrieval to improve reliability and generalization. However, recent studies have shown that RAG systems remain vulnerable to data extraction attacks, where adversaries can extract private data by embedding malicious commands into user queries. Despite their feasibility, existing attacks typically suffer from low data extraction rates and limited practical effectiveness. Here, we propose ALDEN, a novel attack that effectively and efficiently extracts private data from RAGs. First, we employ active learning to diversify malicious queries and improve data extraction rates. Second, we observe that the data distribution of the underlying knowledge base provides valuable guidance for query generation and introduce a decay-based dynamic algorithm to estimate the corresponding topic distribution. By combining them together, we demonstrate that ALDEN substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods through comprehensive evaluations.

</details>


### [216] [Query-Conditioned Graph Retrieval for Contextualized LLM Reasoning in Personalized Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18763)
*Zhenyu Lu,Mahyar Abbasian,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对穿戴式传感数据分析中上下文获取效率与质量的平衡问题，本文提出了 WAG 框架，通过图结构与查询自适应检索，实现了比传统 RAG 更精准、高效的数据推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在分析穿戴式传感数据时面临上下文选择的困境：上下文不足导致推理局限，上下文过多则造成模型效率下降及生成质量退化。

Method: 提出了一种基于图的上下文检索框架 WAG（Wearable As Graph）：1. 将穿戴式数据构建为个性化知识图谱；2. 利用分层贝叶斯建模整合全局（群体/个体模式）与局部（短期偏差）信息；3. 引入查询开放度信号控制检索范围。

Result: 在超过 10,000 个真实世界数据查询的评估中，WAG 相比基线方法和标准 RAG 方法取得了约 70% 的胜率。

Conclusion: WAG 通过将穿戴式设备数据结构化为图并引入查询自适应检索机制，显著提升了 LLM 在分析个性化、多模态穿戴式数据时的推理准确性与效率，优于传统的 RAG 方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to analyzing wearable sensing data, which are long-term, multimodal, and highly personalized. A key challenge is context selection: providing insufficient context limits reasoning, while including all available data leads to inefficiency and degraded generation quality. We propose Wearable As Graph (WAG), a graph-based context retrieval framework that enables query-adaptive reasoning over wearable data with LLMs. WAG organizes wearable metrics and user-specific signals into a personalized knowledge graph, and retrieves a query-conditioned subgraph to support downstream generation. The retrieval process integrates global relationships, capturing prior knowledge and population- and individual-level patterns via hierarchical Bayesian modeling, with local relationships that reflect short-term signal deviations. A query openness signal further controls retrieval breadth. We evaluate WAG on over 10,000 data-grounded queries from real-world wearable datasets. Across LLM-based and human evaluations, WAG achieves an approximately 70% win rate over baseline and standard RAG methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, query-adaptive context retrieval for LLM-driven analysis of wearable data.

</details>


### [217] [STAR: Semantic-Tuned and Tail-Adaptive Retriever for Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18765)
*Shuai Li,Chen Huang,Duanyu Feng,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对GraphRAG中存在的语义捷径和长尾路径偏置问题，本文提出STAR检索器，通过词级交互和路径加权对比学习优化信息检索，有效提升了多跳问答的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GraphRAG方法在多跳问答中存在稀疏语义信息挑战，导致严重的“语义捷径偏置”（Semantic Shortcut Bias）和“长尾路径偏置”（Long-Tail Path Bias），限制了检索与后期问答的效果。

Method: 提出了STAR（Semantic-Tuned and Tail-Adaptive Retriever），包含两个核心学习范式：
1. 基于交叉注意力和难路径挖掘的“词级交互学习”，以解决语义捷径偏置。
2. 基于尾部自适应路径加权的“路径加权对比学习”，以解决长尾路径偏置。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，STAR相较于现有基线方法，平均检索性能提升了1.8%，LLM问答性能提升了2.2%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为STAR的检索器，通过双重学习范式有效缓解了GraphRAG中的语义捷径偏置和长尾路径偏置，显著提升了多跳问答任务的效果。

Abstract: To augment Large Language Models (LLMs) for multi-hop question answering, a mainstream solution within Graph Retrieval Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) leverages lightweight retrievers to efficiently extract information from a given Knowledge Graph (KG). However, existing methods often overlook the inherent challenge of sparse semantic information in graphs.
