Specifically, our experiments reveal that these methods produce biased retrieval Semantic Shortcut Bias and Long-Tail Path Bias, leading to inadequate semantic modeling and limited GraphRAG effectiveness. To address these issues, we propose STAR, a semantic-tuned and tail-adaptive retriever for GraphRAG. STAR integrates two key learning paradigms: token-level interaction learning and path-weighted contrastive learning. The former employs a cross-attention architecture and a hard path mining mechanism to jointly model the query and path, thereby mitigating the Semantic Shortcut Bias. The latter introduces a tailored contrastive learning objective that utilizes tail-adaptive path weighting, designed to optimize the training process and ease the Long-Tail Path Bias. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STAR consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average retrieval performance gains of 1.8\% and LLM QA performance improvements of 2.2\% across all benchmark datasets. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/STAR-C583.

</details>


### [218] [Retrieve Only Relevant Tables Whether Few or Many: Adaptive Table Retrieval Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18766)
*Taehee Kim,Seungbin Yang,Jihwan Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对Text-to-SQL中表格检索数量难以预判的问题，本研究提出了一种自适应动态检索方法，通过阈值控制与高效重排序，实现了更精准的证据召回，显著提升了检索及SQL生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Text-to-SQL中的表格检索通常使用固定的Top-k策略，但这导致检索数量固定，无法适配不同查询对表格数量的需求，进而产生检索不全或引入无关噪音的问题。

Method: 提出了一种自适应表格检索方法，包含：1. 自适应阈值机制（用于动态决定检索数量）；2. 滑动窗口重排序算法（用于在大规模数据库中高效筛选相关表格）。

Result: 在Spider、BIRD和Spider 2.0等基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于固定Top-k策略，有效提升了检索准确率及下游任务的表现。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入自适应阈值机制和滑动窗口重排序算法，成功解决了传统Top-k表格检索策略中无法确定最优检索数量的问题，在多个基准测试中显著提升了检索质量和下游文本到SQL（Text-to-SQL）任务的性能。

Abstract: Retrieving relevant tables from extensive databases for a given natural language query is essential for accurately answering questions in tasks such as text-to-SQL. Existing table retrieval approaches select a pre-determined set of k tables with the highest similarity to the query. However, the number of required tables varies across queries and cannot be known in advance. Enforcing a fixed number of retrieved tables regardless of the query may either retrieve an undersized set, failing to obtain all necessary evidence, or retrieve an oversized pool, including irrelevant tables. To address this issue, we propose an adaptive table retrieval method that adjusts the number of tables retrieved according to the requirements of each query. Specifically, we utilize an adaptive thresholding mechanism to selectively retrieve tables and integrate a sliding-window reranking algorithm to efficiently process a large table corpus. Extensive experiments on Spider, BIRD, and Spider 2.0 demonstrate that our method effectively addresses the limitations of the top-k retrieval strategy, improving performance in retrieval and downstream tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/sbY99/Adaptive-Table-Retrieval.

</details>


### [219] [DualView: Adaptive Local-Global Fusion for Multi-Hop Document Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18767)
*Litong Zhang,Jiaxin Li,Kuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的双视图级联文档重排序框架，通过融合局部相关性与全局语义依赖，在多跳问答中以极低的延迟超越了大型重排序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要从多个文档中聚合信息，现有方法在难以平衡“最小相关文档集识别”与“高召回率”之间的矛盾，且大型交叉编码器（Cross-encoders）存在计算延迟过高的问题。

Method: 提出一种双视图级联重排序架构：(1) 局部评分器（Local Scorer）：使用堆叠自注意力机制实现查询-文档的细粒度相关性评估；(2) 全局评分器（Global Scorer）：利用基于Transformer的上下文聚合模型捕捉文档间的依赖关系；(3) 自适应门控（Adaptive Gate）：基于查询语义对两种视图进行动态融合。作为E5-base-v2的轻量级后置处理阶段运行。

Result: 在MuSiQue数据集上实现99.4%的Top-4召回率和97.8%的Full Hit准确率，延迟仅为4.0ms，性能显著优于参数量大得多的BGE-Large和Jina-v3等模型（召回率提升明显，延迟降低5-6倍）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双视图级联重排序框架通过结合局部细粒度相关性与全局文档间依赖关系，在保持极低延迟的同时，显著提升了多跳问答任务中的文档召回能力，证明了在有限计算预算下实现高效信息聚合的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires aggregating information from multiple documents, a critical capability for knowledge-intensive applications. A fundamental challenge lies in efficiently identifying the minimal relevant document set from retrieved candidates while maintaining high recall.
  We present an efficient dual-view cascaded reranking framework for multi-hop document reranking. Operating as a lightweight post-retrieval stage over E5-base-v2 candidates, our architecture comprises: (1) a Local Scorer employing stacked cross-attention for fine-grained query-document relevance; and (2) a Global Scorer modeling inter-document dependencies via Transformer-based context aggregation. These views are dynamically fused through an Adaptive Gate conditioned on query semantics.
  Under the fixed candidate set reranking setting with offline cached embeddings, our model achieves competitive results, particularly outstanding on MuSiQue with 99.4% Top-4 Recall and 97.8% Full Hit accuracy at 4.0 ms latency (249 QPS). It substantially outperforms 600M-parameter cross-encoders (BGE-Large: 92.0% Recall, Jina-v3: 90.1% Recall) while maintaining 5 to 6 times lower latency. Ablation studies validate that both Local and Global views contribute substantially to multi-hop performance.

</details>


### [220] [ClusterRAG: Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18769)
*Gibson Nkhata,Uttamasha Anjally Oyshi,Quan Mai,Susan Gauch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ClusterRAG 引入了基于聚类的协同过滤机制，通过整合相似用户的背景信息来克服传统 RAG 检索低效与个性化不足的问题，并在多种实验设置下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统存在检索成本高的问题，且未能挖掘相似用户之间的协同信号，导致个性化生成效果受限。

Method: 提出了一种基于聚类的协同过滤方法（ClusterRAG）：1. 通过用户个人资料文档构建用户向量表示；2. 利用密度聚类算法将相似用户归入语义簇；3. 执行簇级匹配与细粒度排序的层级化检索策略，整合目标用户及相似用户群体的背景信息。

Result: 在 LaMP 基准测试中表现优异，能够与多种密集检索器、排序器及大模型（微调或零样本）无缝集成，显著提升了任务完成度。

Conclusion: ClusterRAG 通过利用群体协作信号提升了个性化 RAG 的性能，具有良好的通用性和扩展性，能有效兼顾检索效率与生成质量。

Abstract: Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on accurately selecting user-relevant documents. In practice, existing RAG approaches often suffer from high retrieval costs and overlook that collaborative signals from similar users can enhance personalized generation for the current user. We propose ClusterRAG, a Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation. ClusterRAG represents users through their profile documents, organizes users into semantically coherent clusters using density-based clustering, and performs retrieval at both the cluster and document levels via cluster-level similarity and fine-grained ranking. Extensive experiments on the LaMP benchmark demonstrate that jointly leveraging the target user's profile and profiles from top similar users consistently yields the best performance across diverse tasks. Further analysis shows that ClusterRAG integrates seamlessly with different dense retrievers and rankers, and remains effective when paired with both fine-tuned and zero-shot language models.

</details>


### [221] [LWGR: Lagrangian-Constrained Personalized World Knowledge for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18771)
*Lingyu Mu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Kaican Lin,Zhitong Zhu,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Zhengxiao Liu,Zheng Lin,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中知识提取不灵活及融合不可控的问题，LWGR 框架引入拉格朗日约束，实现了个性化的知识提取与有界、可控的知识融合，显著提升了推荐准确度与商业收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）方法依赖固定的手动指令，无法捕捉用户兴趣的多维异质性，且盲目的知识融合可能与行为信号冲突，损害推荐效果。

Method: 提出了 LWGR 框架，主要包含：1) 基于软提示（Soft Instructions）的个性化知识提取方法；2) 将知识融合建模为具有显式性能退化限制的约束优化问题，并利用拉格朗日原始-对偶方法求解；3) 结合近线预计算与轻量化在线服务的部署方案。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上表现优于 8 个 SOTA 基线，性能提升高达 11.23%；在工业广告平台实测中实现了 1.35% 的收益增长。

Conclusion: LWGR 通过拉格朗日约束实现了个性化知识提取与受控融合，不仅有效提升了推荐性能，还具有优秀的工业落地实用性，为大模型驱动的生成式推荐提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in large language model (LLM) based generative recommendation (GR) shows that leveraging LLM world knowledge can substantially improve performance. However, existing methods rely on fixed, manually designed instructions to generate semantic knowledge and directly incorporate it into GR, which has two limitations. First, fixed instructions cannot capture the multidimensional heterogeneity of user interests. Second, uncontrollable knowledge fusion may conflict with behavioral signals and harm recommendations. To address these limitations, we propose LWGR, a framework that leverages Lagrangian constraints to transfer users' personalized world knowledge from LLMs into generative recommendation. LWGR enhances GR along two axes: knowledge extraction and fusion. It builds personalized soft instructions to extract behavior-relevant LLM world knowledge, and formulates knowledge fusion as an optimization problem with explicitly bounded performance degradation, which is solved by a Lagrangian primal-dual method to selectively incorporate beneficial knowledge. We further design two training strategies for different LLM scales and a deployment scheme that combines nearline precomputation with lightweight online serving. Experiments on multiple public datasets and one industrial dataset show that LWGR outperforms eight state-of-the-art baselines by up to 11.23% and brings a 1.35% revenue lift on a large-scale advertising platform, demonstrating its effectiveness and practicality.

</details>


### [222] [Improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation without Taxonomy-based Error Categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18772)
*Gongbo Zhang,Yifan Peng,Chunhua Weng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RePAIR 是一种新型的 Agentic RAG 纠错范式，通过绕过复杂的错误分类，直接将错误输出映射到纠错行动，提升了 RAG 系统的鲁棒性和生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Agentic RAG 系统过度依赖批评者反馈，但忽略了纠错过程本身的鲁棒性，尤其是错误分类不准确或纠错指令无效的问题，限制了系统的整体效果。

Method: 提出了一种名为 RePAIR（Response-Action learning paradigm）的范式。该方法摒弃了传统的细粒度错误分类，利用响应-动作学习，直接建立从有缺陷的 RAG 输出到纠错行动计划的映射关系。

Result: RePAIR 在多个基准测试中均展现出稳健的性能提升，有效增强了 Agentic RAG 系统的输出准确性及自我修正能力。

Conclusion: RePAIR 通过直接从错误输出学习缓解策略，摆脱了对显式错误分类和批评监督的依赖，证明了简化错误修正流程并在 Agentic RAG 系统中实现性能提升的可行性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factual accuracy of large language model (LLM) outputs by grounding generation in external knowledge. Recent agentic RAG systems extend this paradigm with critical agents to evaluate model responses and iteratively refine outputs. However, most prior work implicitly assumes reliable critic feedback and focuses on planning strategies, while paying limited attention to the robustness of the error-correction process itself, which can be impacted by misaligned error categories and ineffective or incorrect corrections. Here, we hypothesize that RAG performance can be improved without explicit error categorization. We propose RePAIR, a response-action learning paradigm that directly maps flawed RAG outputs to error-mitigating action plans without relying on fine-grained error taxonomies and explicit critic supervision. Across multiple benchmarks, RePAIR consistently improves agentic RAG performance.

</details>


### [223] [M3DocDep: Multi-modal, Multi-page, Multi-document Dependency Chunking with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18774)
*Joongmin Shin,Jeongbae Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 M3DocDep，一种基于 LVLM 的多模态文档解析流水线。它通过显式恢复文档中的块级依赖关系（树结构）来指导分块，从而构建出语义完整的检索单元，显著提升了复杂长文档中的 RAG 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本导向型分块器及生成式解析器往往忽视多页文档中的跨页层级关系、图表与标题的绑定关系以及边界提示，导致产生碎片化或冗余的分块，严重影响了后续 RAG 阶段的检索精度和问答质量。

Method: 提出了 M3DocDep 流水线：1. 使用 SharedDet 进行预处理；2. 通过具有边界感知 SoftROI 池化的多模态模型提取块嵌入；3. 使用 biaffine head 评分亲子依赖边；4. 采用 MST（最小生成树）约束解码全局有效的文档依赖树；5. 基于树结构构建包含层级路径和页码范围的文档块。

Result: 在 DHP 基准测试中，STEDS 指标提升了 28.5% 至 39.6%；在语料级 RAG 基准测试中，检索 nDCG 提升了 1.1% 至 15.3%，QA ANLS 提升了 4.5% 至 15.3%。

Conclusion: 在长文档RAG任务中，在分块前恢复文档的层级依赖关系（即利用结构化信息）对于提升检索质量和问答效果至关重要。M3DocDep 证明了构建基于文档树的语义一致性分块是解决多页复杂文档解析难题的有效途径。

Abstract: In long, multi-page industrial documents, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) depends heavily on whether chunk boundaries follow the document's true structure. Existing text-centric chunkers and generative hierarchy parsers often miss cross-page parent-child relations, figure/table-caption bindings, and boundary cues, which leads to fragmented or redundant chunks and degrades both retrieval and answer quality. We propose M3DocDep, an LVLM-based pipeline that first recovers block-level dependencies and then constructs chunks along the recovered document tree. The pipeline uses SharedDet as a common DP+OCR preprocessing layer, extracts multimodal block embeddings with boundary-aware SoftROI pooling, scores candidate parent-child edges with a biaffine head, decodes a globally valid dependency tree with MST constraints, and builds tree-guided chunks annotated with section paths and page ranges. Under a shared-block evaluation protocol, M3DocDep improves STEDS by +28.5 to +39.6 percent on DHP benchmarks, retrieval nDCG by +1.1 to +15.3 percent, and QA ANLS by +4.5 to +15.3 percent on corpus-level RAG benchmarks. These results show that recovering document dependencies before chunking yields more coherent retrieval units for long, multi-page multimodal documents.

</details>


### [224] [Query-Aware Flow Diffusion for Graph-Based RAG with Retrieval Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18775)
*Zhuoping Zhou,Davoud Ataee Tarzanagh,Sima Didari,Wenjun Hu,Baruch Gutow,Oxana Verkholyak,Masoud Faraki,Heng Hao,Hankyu Moon,Seungjai Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QAFD-RAG 是一种无需训练的图检索框架，通过查询感知的动态流扩散实现语义对齐的子图导航，提供理论保证并实现了高效的查询意图响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图 RAG 方法存在启发式设计缺乏理论保证及静态探索策略无法捕捉查询意图的局限性。

Method: 提出 Query-Aware Flow Diffusion RAG (QAFD-RAG) 框架：通过查询嵌入动态加权图边，在图遍历过程中引导信息流向语义相关区域，结合训练无关的流扩散机制，仅需在局部子图上进行计算。

Result: 该方法在问答（QA）和 Text-to-SQL 任务上表现优于现有 SOTA 方法，且具备指数级收敛速度，其检索复杂度与子图规模相关，而非全图规模。

Conclusion: QAFD-RAG 提供了一种基于查询感知流扩散的高效检索机制，在保持较低计算复杂度的同时，实现了理论保证的子图检索，显著优于现有的图 RAG 方法。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems leverage interconnected knowledge structures to capture complex relationships that flat retrieval struggles with, enabling multi-hop reasoning. Yet most existing graph-based methods suffer from (i) heuristic designs lacking theoretical guarantees for subgraph quality or relevance and/or (ii) the use of static exploration strategies that ignore the query's holistic meaning, retrieving neighborhoods or communities regardless of intent. We propose Query-Aware Flow Diffusion RAG (QAFD-RAG), a training-free framework that dynamically adapts graph traversal to each query's holistic semantics. The central innovation is query-aware traversal: during graph exploration, edges are dynamically weighted by how well their endpoints align with the query's embedding, guiding flow along semantically relevant paths while avoiding structurally connected but irrelevant regions. These query-specific reasoning subgraphs enable the first statistical guarantees for query-aware graph retrieval, showing that QAFD-RAG recovers relevant subgraphs with high probability under mild signal-to-noise conditions. The algorithm converges exponentially fast, with complexity scaling with the retrieved subgraph size rather than the full graph. Experiments on question answering and text-to-SQL tasks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art graph-based RAG methods.

</details>


### [225] [Mask-to-Correct$^+$: Leveraging Retriever Diversity for Masking-guided Faithful Fact Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18776)
*Payel Santra,Lavisha Sharma,Madhusudan Ghosh,Partha Basuchowdhuri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为解决事实修正中训练数据匮乏与语义忠实度问题，本文提出了无需训练的M$_2$C及多检索器集成框架M$_2$C$^+$，在无需人工标注的情况下显著提升了修正的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实修正方法依赖稀缺的人工标注数据，存在跨领域泛化差及忽略修正过程语义忠实度的问题，且检索增强生成的效果受单一检索器限制。

Method: 提出Mask-to-Correct (M$_2$C)：一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的训练无关、仅推理的框架，利用多样性感知遮蔽（diversity-aware masking）识别错误片段并验证修正的忠实度；在此基础上引入M$_2$C$^+$：一种集成多检索器的框架，以缓解检索偏见。

Result: 在基准数据集上超越现有基线，在不使用金标准证据的情况下，SARI得分最高提升了14%。

Conclusion: M$_2$C与M$_2$C$^+$框架无需训练，能够有效提升自动化事实修正的性能与鲁棒性，在不依赖人工标注标签的情况下优于现有基线。

Abstract: The rapid spread of misinformation on social media highlights the need for robust, automated fact correction frameworks. However, existing works rely on supervised learning from manually annotated claim-evidence pairs, which are scarce and prone to biases, limiting their generalization across domains. Moreover, these methods overlook semantic faithfulness in their correction process. To address these challenges, we propose Mask-to-Correct (M$_2$C), a training-free, inference-only Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) based framework that leverages diversity-aware masking to identify erroneous spans of claims and evaluate the faithfulness of corrections using retrieved evidence. However, the effectiveness of RAG heavily depends on the choice of retriever, which may vary across queries. To mitigate this, we further introduce M$_2$C$^+$, an ensemble-based framework that combines corrections across multiple rankers to reduce retrieval bias and improve robustness. Extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed frameworks consistently outperform all baselines, achieving up to 14% improvement in SARI scores, without using gold evidence.

</details>


### [226] [A Reproducibility Analysis of PO4ISR: Diagnosing and Mitigating Semantic Drift in LLM-Based Session Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18780)
*Aditya Tiwari,Konduri Naga Lakshmi Rekha,Rajesh Kumar Mundotiya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对 PO4ISR 会话推荐模型进行了可重复性研究，指出其在长会话中存在“上下文漂移”问题；为此提出增强版 PO4ISR++，通过引入反思性提示和动态调整机制，显著提升了模型在复杂场景下的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的大语言模型（LLM）在会话推荐任务中的推理能力在不同语义领域的泛化性和可重复性尚未得到充分验证，且原始模型在复杂数据集上存在显著的性能衰减。

Method: 1. 对 PO4ISR 进行可重复性实验与压力测试；2. 识别出长会话中的“上下文漂移”（contextual drift）失效模式；3. 提出改进框架 PO4ISR++，整合了反思性提示（reflexive prompting）和一致性排序检测（consistent rank detection）技术。

Result: 改进后的 PO4ISR++ 在处理复杂语义数据集时表现出极强的鲁棒性与稳定性，在 Games 数据集上性能提升了 54%，在 Bundle 数据集上提升了 96%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现有基于推理的会话推荐模型（PO4ISR）在跨领域及长会话中的脆弱性，并通过引入 PO4ISR++ 有效解决了上下文漂移问题，证明了动态提示策略在提升模型跨领域鲁棒性方面的必要性。

Abstract: Reasoning-based Large Language Models (LLMs) like PO4ISR have set new benchmarks in session-based recommendation. However, the reproducibility of their reasoning capabilities across diverse semantic domains remains unexplored. In this work, we conduct a rigorous reproducibility study of PO4ISR to assess its generalization limits. Our analysis reveals a critical failure mode: standard reasoning prompts suffer from severe contextual drift in long sessions, leading to performance degradation on semantically complex datasets like Games and Bundle. To quantify and resolve this stability gap, we introduce PO4ISR++, a robustness-enhanced implementation that integrates reflexive prompting and consistent rank detection. Unlike the original static prompting strategy, our approach dynamically adapts to cross-domain cues. We benchmark both the original implementation and our robust variant on ML-1M, Games, and Bundle. Our results confirm that while the original model struggles in new domains, our reproducible extension restores performance, yielding a stabilized gain of up to 54% on Games and 96% on Bundle. We release open-source artifacts, including the reproduced baseline and our enhanced framework, to facilitate reliable future research in LLM-based recommendation.

</details>


### [227] [Trust or Abstain? A Self-Aware RAG Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18792)
*Xi Zhu,Ziqi Wang,Kai Mei,Wujiang Xu,Minghao Guo,Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 RAG 系统中检索知识与模型自身知识冲突的问题，本文提出了 SABER 框架，通过多轨迹推理自我评估知识可靠性，无需微调即可显著提升 RAG 的准确性与忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 系统中存在知识冲突（即检索到的外部知识与模型原有参数知识不一致或均不可靠），现有方法主要关注知识来源的选择，缺乏对推理路径正确性的自我反思（Self-awareness）。作者认为实现高忠实度（Faithful）的 RAG 需要模型具备识别自身知识边界和推理局限的能力。

Method: 1. 构建模型特定的基准测试：基于约 69K 个查询-上下文实例，评估 LLM 在 PK（参数知识）和 CK（上下文知识）路径下的表现。
2. 提出 SABER (Self-Aware Belief Estimator)：结合自我先验（self-prior）与多轨迹推理（multi-trace inference）产生的 PK 和 CK 条件表征，利用两个轻量级预测器估计可靠性。
3. 决策策略：通过 4 单元决策过程（信任 PK、信任 CK、信任两者或弃权）实现知识协调。

Result: 在四种主流 LLM 上进行了测试，SABER 在端到端准确率和应对冲突的忠实度上均超越了 10 个显现时推理及微调基准；在弃权设置下，其风险-覆盖曲线（Risk-coverage curve）优于所有基于提示（prompt-based）的弃权策略。

Conclusion: SABER 框架通过引入自我感知信念估计，无需微调即可有效解决 RAG 中的知识冲突问题，在提高准确性与忠实度方面表现优异，并提供了可调的风险-覆盖平衡机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves large language models (LLMs) by incorporating external evidence, but it also introduces knowledge conflicts when retrieved contextual knowledge (CK) and parametric knowledge (PK) disagree or are both unreliable. Existing approaches mainly coordinate which source to use, without explicitly asking whether each answer path is correct. We argue that faithful RAG requires LLM self-awareness, namely the ability to recognize the limits of its own knowledge and reasoning. To ground this problem, we construct a model-specific, ground-truth-aligned knowledge-conflict benchmark by evaluating LLM backbones on PK-only and CK-conditioned answer paths over approximately 69K query-context instances per backbone, drawn from five conflict-QA datasets. We then introduce SABER, a Self-Aware Belief Estimator for RAG that requires no LLM fine-tuning. SABER combines a self-prior with PK-side and CK-side conditional reasoning representations from multi-trace inference, then estimates reliability beliefs with two lightweight predictors to drive a 4-cell decision over trust PK, trust CK, trust either, or abstain. Across four LLM backbones, SABER improves end-to-end accuracy and conflict-specific faithfulness over ten inference-time and fine-tuning baselines, with the largest gains on conflict-heavy datasets. Under abstention, SABER's risk-coverage curve Pareto-dominates every prompt-based abstainer, providing a tunable balance between coverage and answer risk. Our code is available at https://github.com/xizhu1022/SABER.

</details>


### [228] [Towards FairRAG: Preventing Representational Harm in Retrieval-Augmented Generation by Enforcing Fair Exposure at Retrieval Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18806)
*Riddhi Tikoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了RAG系统中检索阶段引入的偏见问题，提出了一种名为“Representative Stochastic”的排名策略，通过该策略有效降低了检索和生成阶段的代表性偏见，实现了更好的公平性与效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG系统中，检索模块可能引入并放大代表性偏见，导致LLM在生成回答时出现偏差；需研究如何通过优化检索排名策略来缓解这种偏见。

Method: 对比了两种已有的排名策略（Standard 和 Stochastic）与两种提出的暴露感知策略（Forced-Exposure 和 Representative Stochastic）；利用 TREC 2022 公平排名数据集进行实验，评估不同策略在四个场景问答提示下的 exposure bias（暴露偏见）和 utility（效用）。

Result: Representative Stochastic 排名器实现了统计学上的近乎平等的平均暴露；实验发现生成阶段的人口统计学平价与检索阶段的暴露平价密切相关，证实了偏差的传播链条。

Conclusion: 检索排名是缓解下游偏见的关键环节；提出的“Representative Stochastic”排名策略能有效平衡相关性与公平性；RAG系统中的生成偏见主要源于检索阶段的代表性偏见并随之传播。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) integration has accelerated in high-stakes domains, model hallucination is a critical issue. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a technique for addressing hallucination; however, RAG's multi-component pipeline introduces vulnerabilities where biases can be introduced. This study considers two previously developed utility-focused ranking strategies (Standard and Stochastic) alongside two proposed exposure-aware approaches (Forced-Exposure and Representative Stochastic). Using the TREC 2022 Fair Ranking Dataset, which contains Wikipedia articles annotated as protected or non-protected, the LLM was asked to identify relevant articles with citations for four scenario-based Q&A prompts. The retrieval rankings and the generated outputs were evaluated for exposure bias and utility across all ranking methods. Overall, the Representative Stochastic ranker resulted in a statistically significant near-parity average exposure, acknowledging that relevance scores initially produced during retrieval are already shaped by representational bias, whereas the other rankers assume those scores are unbiased. Across all the methods of document ranking, generation demographic parity closely mirrored the exposure parity, reinforcing that representational bias in RAG systems is driven by retrieval and propagates to generation. These findings highlight that retrieval ranking is a critical point for mitigating downstream bias and propose a Representative Stochastic ranker that reintroduces fairness in RAG systems.

</details>


### [229] [The 99% Success Paradox: When Near-Perfect Retrieval Equals Random Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18857)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Harshvardhan Singh,Tony Joseph,Cien Zhang,Akash Vishwakarma,Svetlana Karslioglu,Michael Wyatt Thot,Ameya Gawde*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BoR 指标，揭示了传统 IR 检索在高深度下往往表现为“随机选择”，证明了盲目增加 $K$ 值在 RAG 和 Agent 应用中不仅无效，还会因引入噪声导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 IR 评估侧重于召回率，假设人类会进行过滤；但在 LLM/RAG 自动化处理中，这种过滤缺失，导致高召回率可能掩盖系统性能实为“随机级别”的事实。现有评估指标无法识别检索何时变得多余或无效。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Bits-over-Random (BoR) 的度量指标，通过观测概率（P_obs）与超几何分布基准概率（P_rand）的比值的对数，衡量检索系统的选择性。在 20 Newsgroups、BEIR/SciFact、MS MARCO 以及 LLM Agent 工具选择场景中进行了验证。

Result: BoR 揭示了在特定深度（如 $K=100$）下，BM25 和 SPLADE 的高成功率本质上接近随机选择；LLM 在此类深度下的性能退化与 BoR 指标的低值吻合；MS MARCO 数据上显示现有检索系统多已触及该指标的理论上限。

Conclusion: BoR 能够揭示传统 IR 指标（如 Recall）在特定深度下的随机性掩盖问题。研究建议在评估 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) 和 Agent 工具检索时，应将 BoR 作为补充指标，并重新评估检索深度以避免在无效的“高召回”上浪费计算资源。

Abstract: For most of the history of information retrieval (IR), search results were designed for human consumers who could scan, filter, and discard irrelevant information on their own. This shaped retrieval systems to optimize for finding and ranking more relevant documents, but not keeping results clean and minimal, as the human was the final filter. However, LLMs have changed that by lacking this filtering ability.
  To address this, we introduce Bits-over-Random (BoR), a chance-corrected measure of retrieval selectivity that reveals when high success rates mask random-level performance. We measure selectivity as $BoR = \log_{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}_{obs}}{\mathrm{P}_{rand}}\right)$, where $\mathrm{P}_{rand}$ is the hypergeometric baseline for the chosen success rule (here, coverage: $ \geq1 $ relevant in top-$K$).
  On the 20 Newsgroups dataset, BM25 and SPLADE both report $>99$% success at $K=100$ (coverage), yet $BoR \approx 0$, indicating random-level selectivity at that depth. When the expected coverage ratio $\left(\frac{K \cdot \bar{R}_{q}}{N}\right)$ exceeds 3-5, the baseline dominates and selectivity collapses. Downstream retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) evaluation confirms this pattern: LLM accuracy can degrade substantially at $K=100$, consistent with the near-zero BoR ceiling. In contrast, BoR remains positive on BEIR/SciFact and on MS MARCO (where 41 systems cluster within 0.2 bits of the theoretical ceiling despite a 13-point recall gap), confirming baseline predictions across sparse and large-scale settings. We further show that the collapse boundary applies to LLM agent tool selection, where small catalog sizes cause selectivity to vanish even with perfect selectors. These findings suggest reporting BoR alongside traditional metrics and reconsidering depth choices when additional retrieval provides negligible selectivity gains while inflating computational costs.

</details>


### [230] [SynGR: Unleashing the Potential of Cross-Modal Synergy for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18920)
*Wei Chen,Xingyu Guo,Shuang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Jing Fan,Zhao Zhang,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SynGR 是一种新型生成式推荐框架，通过显式挖掘模态间的协同效应和突现语义，解决了现有方法在多模态融合中对单模态依赖过强的问题，从而提升了推荐准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态生成式推荐方法主要侧重于模态间的简单对齐，忽视了模态间产生的“突现属性”（emergent properties），即无法从单一模态推断出的深层语义关联。

Method: 提出一种协同生成推荐框架 SynGR，通过约束对主导模态的过度依赖，显式地促进生成过程中对跨模态依赖关系和突现语义的挖掘。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，SynGR 相比现有基线方法取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: SynGR 通过显式建模跨模态协同信息，克服了生成式推荐中对单一模态的过度依赖，显著提升了推荐系统的性能。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm by formulating item recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence generation task over item identifiers. Recent studies have incorporated multimodal signals to provide richer token-level evidence for generation. However, existing approaches largely rely on alignment-centric fusion and underexplore synergistic information across modalities. In practice, synergistic information plays a critical role in capturing emergent item properties that cannot be inferred from any single modality alone. Such properties encode intrinsic item semantics and guide user preferences, enabling models to move beyond surface-level feature matching. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{SynGR}, a synergistic generative recommendation framework that explicitly encourages the exploitation of cross-modal dependencies during generation. By constraining overreliance on dominant modalities, SynGR enables the model to capture emergent item semantics beyond shared or modality-specific signals. Extensive experiments across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that SynGR achieves superior performance.

</details>


### [231] [KadiAssistant: A conversational AI Agent for information retrieval in Kadi4Mat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18850)
*Adrian Cierpka,Mohammad Shafiqul Islam,Johannes Steinhülb,Eric Dietriche Sesso Domtchoueng,Michael Selzer,Arnd Koeppe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KadiAssistant 是一款集成了隐私保护语义搜索与自托管 LLM 的 AI 助手，旨在解决跨学科科研数据访问障碍，通过检索增强生成技术在严格权限控制下实现异构数据的智能检索与整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨学科（如电池研究）领域，数据格式异构、标准各异、访问权限复杂且多为敏感数据，导致科研人员难以高效检索、整合和利用分散的科研数据。

Method: 采用“隐私设计”（privacy-by-design）理念，结合自托管大语言模型（Self-hosted LLM）与基于检索增强生成（RAG）的隐私保护语义搜索技术，直接集成于 Kadi 生态系统，并严格遵守现有的细粒度访问控制权限。

Result: 构建了一个能够跨异构数据源进行信息筛选、聚合与结构化输出的AI助手，在确保数据安全和隐私的前提下，打破了学科间的壁垒，简化了数据交互过程。

Conclusion: KadiAssistant 有效解决了跨学科研究数据访问的复杂性、敏感性和异构性难题，降低了科研门槛，并显著增强了科研数据的可发现性（FAIR原则），为隐私敏感型研究数据的智能处理提供了可扩展的框架。

Abstract: We introduce KadiAssistant, a privacy-by-design AI assistant integrated into the Kadi research data ecosystem, enabling researchers to efficiently access, aggregate, and synthesize information from heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive research data. Interdisciplinary fields such as materials science bring together disciplines with their own terminology and standards. While this convergence fuels innovation, it also makes it increasingly difficult to connect and access knowledge, as data are distributed across disciplines, organizations, and individuals. For example, battery research combines electrochemical measurements, materials characterization data, physics-based simulations, and manufacturing parameters, each using different formats, vocabularies, and standards. Efficiently storing and sharing such heterogeneous data via research data platforms, such as Kadi4Mat, demands domain knowledge, technical expertise, and familiarity with metadata schemas and interfaces. Research data also vary in sensitivity: newly generated 'warm' data are often private, whereas published 'cold' data are usually openly accessible. The Kadi ecosystem offers fine-grained access control needed for sensitive data. A solution for efficient information retrieval in Kadi must therefore respect the fine-grained access permissions. To address these intertwined challenges of information retrieval, strong data privacy, and complex access control, KadiAssistant combines a self-hosted large language model (LLM) with a privacy-preserving semantic search, inspired by retrieval-augmented generation, that can access files and record metadata on Kadi. This allows the assistant to screen, aggregate, and structure information into a highly informative answer. KadiAssistant therefore bridges terminology and standards, lowers access barriers for researchers, and strengthens the Findable pillar of FAIR data principles.

</details>


### [232] [Code-Guided Reasoning for Small Language Models: Evaluating Executable MCQA Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18827)
*Prateek Biswas,Dhaval Patel,Vedant Khandelwal,Shuxin Lin,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 作者提出了Code-Guided Reasoning (CGR) 协议，通过标准化代码脚手架评估，证明了辅助工具能显著提升小型语言模型在MCQA任务中的表现，并为分析这些辅助推理过程提供了系统化的追踪与评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多项选择题（MCQA）基准测试主要评估小型语言模型（SLMs）直接回答问题的能力，忽略了实际部署中模型常依赖代码、外部工具等脚手架进行推理的现实情况。

Method: 提出Code-Guided Reasoning (CGR) 评估协议，将评估过程标准化为六个组件（接口、直接求解器提示、生成器提示、Python脚手架、辅助提取器、三通道结果记录），并对包含20,498条结果数据进行了实证评估。

Result: 在非零基线分区中，使用工具辅助的宏观准确率达到66.21%，远高于直接回答的38.11%（提升28.10个百分点）。在更严格的情境下（直接准确率高于30%），准确率仍有14.11个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: CGR框架证明了可执行推理工具的使用能显著提升小型语言模型（SLMs）在多项选择题（MCQA）任务中的表现，同时CGR提供的追踪资源为分析复杂推理过程中的性能增益、失败模式及潜在风险提供了必要工具。

Abstract: Multiple-choice QA benchmarks usually evaluate small language models (SLMs) as direct answerers, but deployed language-model systems increasingly rely on external scaffolds such as tools, code, and repeated model calls. We introduce Code-Guided Reasoning (CGR), an evaluation protocol and generated-program resource for measuring when executable reasoning scaffolds improve SLM performance on MCQA tasks. CGR standardizes six components: a normalized item interface, a direct solver prompt, a generator prompt, a Python scaffold, solver-call and extraction helpers, and a three-channel result record. On 20,498 retained result rows from a locally prepared MCQA bundle and six metadata-registered solver models, the observed non-zero-baseline partition shows 66.21% macro assisted accuracy versus 38.11% direct accuracy, a +28.10 percentage-point difference with a pair-bootstrap interval of [20.32, 36.43]. Under a stricter Ab > 30% direct-signal gate, the macro difference is +14.11 points. These estimates are descriptive. Assisted inference uses a larger solver-call budget, answer extraction is brittle, Time-MQA contains the observed regressions, and some generated programs violate the no-hard-coding instruction. CGR provides the trace package needed to interpret these results, including direct, assisted, and generator-side answers, partition definitions, generated programs, response metadata, and audits.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [KG-ASG: Collision-Knowledge-Guided Closed-Loop Adversarial Scenario Generation With Primary-Support Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18895)
*Cheng Wang,Chen Xiong,Ziwen Wang,Yuchen Zhou,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有自动驾驶对抗场景生成中语义不明和多车碰撞难以控制的问题，本文提出的 KG-ASG 框架利用碰撞知识图谱引导生成，实现了可控、可解释、高执行性的多车对抗场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性场景生成方法存在三大缺陷：一是样本成因模糊（缺乏语义解释性）；二是缺乏对多车交互的精准控制，易产生无意义的随机碰撞；三是方案往往忽略了复杂闭环反馈下的场景执行可行性。

Method: 提出 KG-ASG 框架：构建结构化碰撞知识库并训练轻量级“碰撞专家（Collision Expert）”以推断碰撞模式、主/从对抗者角色；基于“主-辅”逻辑生成场景，辅以物理与安全硬约束过滤，并集成规划-控制闭环反馈进行场景精修。

Result: 在 WOMD 场景及 MetaDrive 仿真中验证，相比现有方法，KG-ASG 提高了有效的主体攻击率（Valid Primary Attack），降低了非预期的多车碰撞，并在不同自动驾驶控制模型（IDM、Cruise、Expert）下表现出优异的鲁棒性和对抗效果。

Conclusion: KG-ASG 通过引入碰撞知识引导和“主-辅”对抗生成策略，显著提升了自动驾驶安全验证中对抗性场景的有效性、可解释性和执行可行性。

Abstract: Safety validation of autonomous driving systems requires high-risk scenario coverage, clear collision semantics, executable trajectories, and attributable multi-vehicle interactions. Existing safety-critical scenario generation methods often rely on low-level trajectory perturbations, collision-proxy optimization, or single-adversary search, which may produce adversarial samples with ambiguous collision causes or uncontrolled multi-vehicle collisions. This paper proposes KG-ASG, a collision-knowledge-guided closed-loop adversarial scenario generation framework with primary-support attribution. KG-ASG constructs a structured collision knowledge base and trains a lightweight Collision Expert to infer the target collision mode, the unique primary adversary, support vehicles, and their interaction roles. Guided by this semantic prior, multi-vehicle adversarial generation is formulated as a primary-support process, where the primary adversary induces the main conflict and support vehicles shape the surrounding risk structure without becoming additional colliders. Rule, physical, interaction-safety, and single-collider constraints are imposed as hard gates to filter non-executable samples. To handle reactive ego behaviors, planner-controller feedback is further used for failure diagnosis, candidate re-ranking, and terminal refinement. Experiments on WOMD scenarios reconstructed in MetaDrive show that KG-ASG achieves strong adversarial effectiveness while improving Valid Primary Attack, reducing multi-collision, and obtaining closed-loop recovery gains under IDM, Cruise, and Expert controllers. These results demonstrate that collision-knowledge guidance and primary-support single-collider reasoning improve adversarial effectiveness, interpretability, and executability for autonomous driving safety validation.

</details>


### [234] [Geo-Data-Driven HD Map Generation Workflow with Integrated Reference-Free Constraint-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18921)
*Ruidi He,Vaibhav Tiwari,Mohanad Al-Ghobari,Meng Zhang,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需依赖传感器，仅利用公开测绘数据自动生成HD地图的工作流，并通过内置的约束验证机制确保地图的准确性与合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高清(HD)地图生成高度依赖高成本的传感设备与高质量的参考数据，在缺乏专业设备或参考地图的环境下难以实现。因此，亟需一种降低数据依赖、具备自我验证能力的HD地图工程化方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于地理数据的HD地图生成工作流，利用公开地理工程数据集作为输入，通过显式中间表示法将其转化为车道级Lanelet地图。同时，引入基于约束的可执行验证机制，根据道路设计规范在生成过程中直接校验几何、拓扑及高程的一致性。

Result: 通过德国下萨克森州四个城市的真实数据测试，生成的地图满足预设的一致性约束；在缺陷注入测试中，该系统实现了对所选缺陷类型的100%全覆盖检测，且无误报。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于地理数据驱动的HD地图生成与集成式可执行验证相结合，能够作为一种模块化且可审查的替代方案，在减少对高传感依赖和外部参考数据需求的情况下生成高质量地图。

Abstract: High-definition (HD) maps are core artifacts for automated driving systems, but their generation commonly relies on sensor-intensive mobile mapping campaigns, while quality assessment often depends on high-precision reference data. These dependencies make HD map engineering costly and difficult to apply in settings where specialised measurement data or independently measured reference maps are unavailable. This paper presents an engineering-oriented geo-data-driven workflow for HD map generation with integrated representation-level verification. The workflow uses openly available geo-engineering datasets as the primary input source and transforms them into lane-level HD map representations of existing road environments through explicit intermediate representations and processing stages. To assess the generated representations without external reference maps, the workflow integrates executable constraint-based verification into the engineering process. Selected constraints are derived from specifications relevant to automated driving and road-design guidelines. They are evaluated directly on the generated lanelet-based representation to detect geometric, topological, and elevation-related inconsistencies. The workflow is evaluated using real-world shapefile-based road-network data from four cities in Lower Saxony, Germany, and controlled defect-injection scenarios. The real-world evaluation shows that the generated map representations satisfy the selected constraints in the evaluated scenarios, while the defect-injection study demonstrates complete detection of the considered defect types without observed false positives. The results indicate that geo-data-driven HD map generation with integrated executable verification can provide a modular and inspectable complement to sensor-intensive mapping workflows under reduced sensing and reference-data availability.

</details>


### [235] [Distributionally Robust Control via Stein Variational Inference for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19029)
*Hrishikesh Sathyanarayan,Victor Vantilborgh,Harish Ravichandar,Tom Lefebvre,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合分布鲁棒控制与Stein变分推断的模型控制策略，通过更灵活的不确定性建模，解决了机器人接触丰富操作中模型在不确定性下的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法在大规模训练外表现不佳，而经典模型控制方法在处理接触丰富任务中的不确定性时能力有限，难以实现高可靠性操作。

Method: 将操作控制问题建模为分布鲁棒控制优化，利用Stein变分推断（Stein Variational Inference）提出了一种新的确定性控制策略，用于显式建模任务相关的参数不确定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种接触丰富操作任务中展现了比现有模型控制方法高出3倍的鲁棒性，且能有效应对广泛的参数不确定性。

Conclusion: 通过分布鲁棒控制与Stein变分推断的结合，研究证明了在模型控制中显式建模参数不确定性能够显著提升接触丰富任务的鲁棒性，且无需牺牲计算效率。

Abstract: Reliable robotic manipulation requires control policies that can accurately represent and adapt to uncertainty arising from contact-rich interactions. Modern data-driven methods mitigate uncertainty through large-scale training and computation, and degrade significantly in performance with limited number of training samples. By contrast, classical model-based controllers are computationally efficient and reliable, but their limited ability to represent task-relevant uncertainty can hinder performance in contact-rich interactions.
  In this work, we propose to expand the capabilities of model-based manipulation control through more flexible uncertainty modeling that retains performance while exactly adapting to uncertainty. Our approach casts the manipulation problem as a distributionally robust control optimization and proposes a novel deterministic formulation based on Stein variational inference that preserves performance while explicitly modeling task-sensitive parameter uncertainty. As a result, the derived controllers are more aware of task sensitivities to uncertainty, yielding high reliability without compromising performance. Experimental results demonstrate up to 3$\times$ improved robustness across a range of contact-rich manipulation tasks under broad parametric uncertainty, outperforming existing model-based control methods.

</details>


### [236] [RLFTSim: Realistic and Controllable Multi-Agent Traffic Simulation via Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19033)
*Ehsan Ahmadi,Hunter Schofield,Behzad Khamidehi,Fazel Arasteh,Jinjun Shan,Lili Mou,Dongfeng Bai,Kasra Rezaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RLFTSim 是一种新型强化学习微调框架，通过优化奖励函数，不仅提升了交通仿真的真实感，还实现了高效的目标导向场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于监督学习的开环训练方法难以捕捉复杂动态的多智能体交互，导致仿真真实感不足。

Method: 提出 RLFTSim 框架，基于预训练仿真模型，通过设计平衡真实度（fidelity）与可控性（controllability）的奖励函数进行强化学习微调，并引入低方差的密集奖励信号以提升样本效率。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 上实现了最先进（SOTA）的真实感性能，且相比启发式搜索方法，样本效率显著提高，并成功实现了对场景生成的可控性蒸馏。

Conclusion: RLFTSim 通过强化学习微调有效地弥合了仿真模型与真实世界分布之间的鸿沟，能够生成既具备高真实度又可控的驾驶场景，验证了此范式在交通仿真领域超越传统监督学习和启发式搜索方法的潜力。

Abstract: Supervised open-loop training has been widely adopted for training traffic simulation models; however, it fails to capture the inherently dynamic, multi-agent interactions common in complex driving scenarios. We introduce RLFTSim, a reinforcement-learning-based fine-tuning framework that enhances scenario realism by aligning simulator rollouts with real-world data distributions and provides a method for distilling goal-conditioned controllability in scenario generation. We instantiate RLFTSim on top of a pre-trained simulation model, design a reward that balances fidelity and controllability, and perform comprehensive experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset. Our results show improvements in realism, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Compared with other heuristic search-based fine-tuning methods, RLFTSim requires significantly fewer samples due to a proposed low-variance and dense reward signal, and it directly addresses the realism alignment issue by design. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for distilling traffic simulation controllability through goal conditioning. The project page is available at https://ehsan-ami.github.io/rlftsim.

</details>


### [237] [Guiding Neuro-Symbolic Scenario Generation with Spatio-Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19038)
*Lorenzo Bonin,Francesco Giacomarra,Luca Bortolussi,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Francesca Cairoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STRELGen 提出了一种利用时空逻辑约束扩散模型潜在空间的新框架，实现了高真实度、安全关键自动驾驶场景的定向生成，解决了传统穷举测试的效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶测试方法依赖于大规模实地测试，存在成本高昂、效率低下且难以覆盖稀有安全关键场景（edge cases）的核心痛点。

Method: 提出 STRELGen 框架：1. 利用多智能体轨迹生成扩散模型（DM）模拟交通场景；2. 引入时空逻辑（STREL）定义系统的安全与真实性需求；3. 将 STREL 规范的满足度转化为可微函数，通过在 DM 潜在空间进行梯度搜索，定向生成关键边缘场景。

Result: 能够高效生成既符合真实数据分布（高真实性），又具备高度挑战性（安全关键场景）的多智能体交互场景，有效支持自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性评估。

Conclusion: STRELGen 通过结合时空逻辑约束与扩散模型，为自动驾驶系统提供了一种高效、可解释且可扩展的压力测试工具，克服了传统暴力测试方法在成本和效率上的局限性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of autonomous driving (AD) technologies has outpaced the development of robust safety evaluation methods. Conventional testing relies on exposing AD systems to vast numbers of real-world traffic scenes -- a brute-force approach that is prohibitively expensive and statistically ineffective at capturing the rare, safety-critical edge cases essential for validating real-world robustness. To address this fundamental limitation, we introduce STRELGen, a scalable framework for the targeted generation of safety-critical driving scenarios. STRELGen synergistically combines a multi-agent trajectory-generation diffusion model (DM) with Spatio-Temporal Logic (STREL) specifications that encode complex safety and realism properties through a highly interpretable formalism. Crucially, monitoring satisfaction levels of these specifications is differentiable, enabling gradient-based search. At inference time, we optimize directly over the DM latent space to maximize STREL formula satisfaction. The result is efficient generation of highly plausible yet safety-critical multi-agent scenarios that lie within the learned data distribution. STRELGen thus provides a flexible, interpretable, and powerful tool for stress-testing autonomous driving systems, moving beyond the limitations of brute-force data collection.

</details>


### [238] [Neural Operators for Design-Space Surrogate Modeling of Tendon-Actuated Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19104)
*Branden Frieden,James M. Ferguson,Alan Kuntz,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子学习的连续体机器人通用替代建模方法，利用DeepONets和FNOs实现对不同设计参数机器人的快速构型预测，解决了传统方法计算代价高和泛化性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理建模计算昂贵且存在精度限制，现有的学习方法泛化能力较差，难以适应不同设计参数的连续体机器人，亟需一种既高效又具备跨设计泛化能力的建模方法。

Method: 将连续体机器人建模表述为算子学习问题，通过将机器人设计参数和驱动输入映射到构型空间，开发了四种基于DeepONets和FNOs的神经算子架构，并利用仿真数据进行训练。

Result: 所提出的四种神经算子架构在保持高精度的同时，在不同机器人设计之间展现出了快速且精准的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 算子学习（Operator Learning）为连续体机器人力学提供了一种高效、通用且可泛化的替代模型，能够为手术和工业场景下的实时控制、规划及优化提供性能支持。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable dexterous manipulation in constrained environments, but require accurate and efficient models for real-time manipulation and control. Traditional physics-based models can be computationally expensive and may suffer from inaccuracies due to unmodeled effects, while current learning-based methods often generalize poorly beyond the specific robot on which they are trained. We present a formulation of surrogate modeling for tendon-driven continuum robots as an operator learning problem that maps robot design parameters and tendon actuation inputs to resulting configurations. This formulation enables a single trained model to generalize across a large class of robot designs. We develop four novel neural operator architectures--two based on Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets) and two based on Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs)--and train them on simulation data to predict robot configurations. All architectures achieve good accuracy while allowing for fast and accurate generalization across designs. Our results demonstrate that operator learning provides an effective and generalizable surrogate for continuum robot mechanics in the design space, enabling fast modeling for control, planning, and design optimization in surgical and industrial applications.

</details>


### [239] [CosFly: Plan in the Matrix, Fly in the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19120)
*Hanxuan Chen,Xiangyue Wang,Songsheng Cheng,Ruilong Ren,Jie Zheng,Shuai Yuan,Tianle Zeng,Hanzhong Guo,Binbo Li,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于无人机动态目标追踪的CosFly仿真流水线与CosFly-Track大规模多模态数据集，支持多环境下的任务规划、感知及路径优化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决当前无人机动态目标追踪领域缺乏大规模、多样化环境数据集及配套高效仿真流程的问题，以推动学术界在复杂环境下无人机协同导航与感知技术的发展。

Method: 提出了一套模块化的7步构建流程（CosFly），将3D复杂场景转化为结构化障碍物，并生成包含RGB、深度图、语义分割及文本指令的多模态传感器数据；同时对比了“两阶段生成与优化”与“基于梯度的直接路径规划”两种轨迹规划范式。

Result: 发布了CosFly-Track数据集，包含250条经过验证的轨迹、约10万张渲染图片及完整的6-DOF位姿标注，覆盖了城市、公路、森林及海岸等多种复杂场景。

Conclusion: CosFly和CosFly-Track共同构成了一个用于无人机动态目标追踪的可扩展仿真框架与数据集，为无人机导航、多模态感知及空地协同研究提供了有力支持。

Abstract: We present CosFly, a box-structured planning and multimodal simulation pipeline for aerial tracking, together with CosFly-Track, a large-scale UAV dataset for dynamic target tracking across diverse environments including urban centers, highways, rural landscapes, forests, and coastal towns. In our current implementation on CARLA, CosFly provides a modular 7-step construction pipeline that converts complex 3D worlds into structured obstacle representations for planning, then projects the resulting trajectories back into multi-modal sensor data -- including RGB images, high-precision depth maps, and semantic segmentation masks -- paired with natural language navigation instructions. A key feature is the support for configurable fixed-FOV zoom levels (one FOV setting drawn per trajectory and held constant throughout), enabling simulation of various focal lengths through camera-intrinsic adjustments. The pipeline covers the complete workflow from 3D map export through grid simplification, pedestrian and drone trajectory planning, multi-modal rendering with 6-DOF pose annotations, quality inspection, and teacher-student caption generation. We analyze two trajectory-planning paradigms for aerial target tracking: a conventional two-stage pipeline with front-end candidate generation and backend refinement, and a direct gradient-based formulation that optimizes multiple tracking constraints in a single objective. The public CosFly-Track release contains 250 validated trajectories and approximately 100,000 rendered images with complete 6-DOF drone pose annotations (position x, y, z and orientation yaw, pitch, roll). Together, the pipeline and dataset establish a scalable foundation for aerial-ground collaborative research, supporting dynamic target tracking, UAV navigation, and multi-modal perception across diverse environments.

</details>


### [240] [Automatically Improving Simulation Physics for Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19136)
*Anh-Quan Pham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人模拟中物体物理属性缺失的问题，定义了“交互就绪性”指标，并提出了一种基于模拟器反馈的多模态优化方法，能够高效构建高质量、物理一致的铰接物体资产，显著提升机器人模拟学习的稳定性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D数据集虽然包含丰富的几何与运动学信息，但缺乏机器人仿真所需的物理属性，且人工构建高质量可交互仿真物体的工作成本极高。

Method: 1. 定义“交互就绪性”（interaction-readiness）并构建定量的物体质量评估框架；2. 提出一种多模态、模拟器循环（simulator-in-the-loop）的物体生成与优化方法，综合几何、视觉和语义信息，并通过迭代的模拟反馈对物体物理属性进行修正与细化。

Result: 实验表明，经本文方法优化的物体在仿真稳定性、交互动力学表现以及下游强化学习策略的性能上均有显著提升，验证了物理一致性对机器人模拟学习的关键影响。

Conclusion: 物理真实性对于模拟中的铰接物体至关重要。本文提出的多模态模拟器循环反馈框架，能够有效提升物体资产的交互就绪性与物理一致性，从而改善下游具身智能任务的稳定性与学习效果。

Abstract: Simulation is a central tool for scalable robot learning, but its effectiveness depends on the quality of object assets. While modern 3D datasets provide rich geometric and kinematic representations, they typically lack the physical properties required for stable and realistic interaction, requiring significant manual effort to construct simulation-ready articulated objects. In this thesis, we introduce interaction-readiness, which characterizes whether an object can be reliably simulated under manipulation. We propose a quantitative evaluation framework that decomposes interaction-readiness into measurable components, enabling systematic analysis of object quality and revealing failure modes not captured by conventional evaluation. We further present a multi-modal, simulator-in-the-loop approach for generating interaction-ready articulated objects from incomplete 3D assets. The method integrates geometric, visual, and semantic information to infer physical properties and refines them through iterative simulator feedback to improve physical consistency. Experiments across diverse articulated objects and manipulation tasks show that object quality directly impacts simulation stability, interaction behavior, and policy performance. Objects refined by our method exhibit more stable and realistic dynamics, enabling more reliable downstream learning and evaluation. Overall, this thesis demonstrates the importance of physical realism for articulated objects in simulation and introduces a practical multi-modal refinement approach, guided by simulator feedback, for constructing such objects at scale.

</details>


### [241] [COBALT: Crowdsourcing Robot Learning via Cloud-Based Teleoperation with Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19138)
*Ayush Agarwal,Ansh Gandhi,Jeremy A. Collins,Omar Rayyan,Aryan Sarswat,Ranjani Koushik,Masoud Moghani,Ajay Mandlekar,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COBALT 是一个可扩展的机器人远程操作平台，通过支持手机等通用设备的多用户并发采集、低延迟架构及质量控制手段，低成本、大规模地解决了机器人模仿学习中高质量演示数据获取的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操控的模仿学习受到大规模、高质量演示数据稀缺的制约，现有的采集方式成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 开发了基于向量化环境、负载均衡架构的远程操作平台；支持多种大众设备（手机、VR、键盘等）接入；利用内存数据缓存和高效流媒体技术实现低延迟；引入实时指标监控进行自动数据筛选，并实施结构化用户训练课程以提升数据质量。

Result: 该系统实现了单 GPU 支持 8 个并发用户（20Hz，延迟 <100ms），单机 8 GPU 支持 256 个并发模拟客户端；通过全球众包在 5 天内采集了 7500+ 个高质量演示（50+ 小时）；验证了所采集数据集在训练 SOTA 模仿学习模型中的有效性。

Conclusion: COBALT 证明了通过低成本手机等大众设备进行大规模、远程及多用户协作的数据采集是可行的，能够有效解决机器人模仿学习中高质量演示数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: The scarcity of large-scale, high-quality demonstration data remains a bottleneck in scaling imitation learning for robotic manipulation. We present COBALT, a teleoperation platform designed to democratize robot learning at scale both in simulation and in the real world. By leveraging vectorized environments, our scalable, load-balanced infrastructure supports concurrent teleoperation by multiple users on a single GPU, yielding a significant reduction in teleoperation cost. Operators can connect from nearly anywhere on Earth using commonly available devices, including single or dual smartphones, VR headsets, 3D mice, and keyboards. An inmemory data cache and efficient video streaming keep control and rendering synchronous, sustaining dozens of concurrent users at 20 Hz with sub-100 ms end-to-end latency for up to 8 concurrent users per GPU. We also demonstrate stable operation supporting 256 simulated clients across 8 GPUs, underscoring the system's ability to scale across hardware and within individual servers. We perform a comprehensive user study showing that phone-based teleoperation performs comparably to or better than specialized hardware, enabling faster, more ergonomic data collection. To ensure data quality, COBALT logs a suite of real-time metrics to automatically filter suboptimal demonstrations. We further demonstrate that a structured user training curriculum significantly improves data collection quality. Guided by insights from our user study, we crowdsource the collection of a large-scale, high-quality pilot dataset with 7500+ demonstrations (50+ hours) collected with smartphones across nine countries over five days. We validate the dataset's quality by training state-of-the-art imitation learning algorithms. Please visit \href{https://cobalt-teleop.github.io/}{cobalt-teleop.github.io} for more details.

</details>


### [242] [Aerial Inspection Behaviors via RL-based Quadrotor Control for Under-canopy Forest Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19202)
*Fausto Mauricio Lagos Suarez,Akshit Saradagi,Vidya Sumathy,Viswa Narayanan Sankaranarayanan,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层自主四旋翼控制架构，利用深度强化学习处理底层电机控制，配合TSP和RRT*规划算法，实现了林下复杂环境下的高效稳定自动巡检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在林下（under-canopy）复杂环境中，四旋翼无人机在自主巡检任务中缺乏稳健、高效的底层控制与导航支撑的问题。

Method: 提出一种分层控制架构：底层采用基于深度强化学习（RL）的端到端控制器（由状态直接映射到电机转速RPM），实现位姿跟踪；高层采用TSP规划器进行路径排序，并结合RRT*算法生成符合底层RL控制器跟踪限制的无碰撞路径规划。

Result: 通过五个巡检场景验证，该系统成功实现了林下环境中的定点巡检和点对点自主导航。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了基于深度强化学习的底层电机级控制器，结合高层导航引导层（TSP+RRT*），能够有效替代传统控制器，在复杂的森林林下环境中实现可靠的四旋翼自主巡检任务。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of using a deep Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based low-level Quadrotor controller within an autonomous Quadrotor navigation stack for aerial inspection missions in under-canopy forest environments. Specifically, the article presents an end-to-end (mapping states to RPMs) Quadrotor control policy that achieves inspection view-pose tracking (simultaneous position and yaw reference tracking), which is crucial for various target inspection behaviors and point-to-point navigation in forests. To ensure safe and reliable deployment of the end-to-end RL controller in long-range missions, this article utilizes a higher navigation guidance layer comprising of a Traveling Salesman Problem planner (TSP) and a Rapidly-exploring Random Tree Star (RRT*) planner. Over a known map of a forest and a set of user-specified inspection regions, the TSP planner finds the optimal visitation sequence. Between two target regions, collision-free paths that respect the tracking limitations of the lower end-to-end RL policy are generated by an RRT* planner. Through five target inspection scenarios, this article demonstrates that an RL-based motor-level stabilizing controller, supported by a navigation guidance layer, can be used effectively as the low-level inspection execution module for under-canopy forest inspection missions.

</details>


### [243] [DEFLECT: Delay-Robust Execution via Flow-matching Likelihood-Estimated Counterfactual Tuning for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19294)
*Yixiang Zhu,Yonghao Chen,Rui Meng,Jingyu Guo,Jiaxiang Zou,Zijie Yang,Taowen Wang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型在异步推理中的预测-执行失配问题，DEFLECT 通过一种无需人类标注的离线后训练方法，将系统延迟转化为优化信号，显著扩展了 VLA 的延迟容忍度并提升了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在异步推理（机器人执行上一动作块时计算下一动作块）中存在预测-执行失配问题。随着推理延迟增加（如预测滞后 7 个控制步），模型在旧观测值基础上计算的动作在当前物理状态下失效，导致 Kinetix 任务成功率从 89% 骤降至 1% 以下。

Method: 提出 DEFLECT (Delay-Free-Likelihood-Estimation-based Counterfactual Training)。这是一种离线后训练优化方法：利用冻结的参考策略构建反事实的“新鲜/陈旧”动作对，并通过隐式流匹配似然比替代目标（implicit flow-matching likelihood-ratio surrogate）进行打分，无需人工标注、奖励模型或在线训练。

Result: DEFLECT 在高延迟环境下（5-7 个控制步）使成功率提升了 6.4%；在迁移至真实规模 VLA 且存在最大延迟时成功率提升了 4.6%；并在双臂拾取与反应式打地鼠等真实物理任务中表现出一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: DEFLECT 是一种有效的离线后处理优化方案，通过利用“延迟即信号”的机制，显著增强了 VLA 模型在异步推理下的鲁棒性，成功解决了预测-执行失配问题，使其在大延迟环境下仍能保持高成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically deployed with asynchronous inference: the robot executes a previously predicted action chunk while the model computes the next one. This creates a prediction-execution misalignment: the chunk is conditioned on the observation taken before inference began, but executes in a physical state that has already drifted forward by several control steps; naive asynchronous rollover collapses from 89% to under 1% on Kinetix as the inference cycle covers up to seven control steps. We introduce DEFLECT, a fully offline post-training refinement that applies as a near drop-in upgrade to existing async-VLA stacks by converting latency itself into a label-free preference signal: counterfactual fresh/stale action pairs are constructed from a frozen reference policy and scored under the deployment-time conditioning via an implicit flow-matching likelihood-ratio surrogate, with no human labels, reward models, or online rollouts. DEFLECT substantially extends the usable delay envelope of async VLA control, with +6.4 success-rate gain in the high-latency regime (5-7 control steps), +4.6 when transferred to a real-scale VLA at the longest delay, and consistent improvements on two real-robot tasks (a bimanual conveyor pick-and-place and a reactive whack-a-mole).

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Waypoints: Dual-Heatmap Grounding for Cross-Embodiment Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19420)
*Kaijie Yun,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对语义导航中单点回归易失效的问题，本文提出双热图（Dual-Heatmap）表示框架，通过预测导航可供性与朝向热图，实现了物理空间中更安全、更鲁棒的语义指令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语义导航框架多采用确定性的单点回归方法，这忽略了空间不确定性，且容易将目标设置在不可通行区域，导致机器人导航失败。亟需一种能将抽象语义意图映射为物理可行域的方法。

Method: 提出了一种融合视觉-语言的“双热图（Dual-Heatmap）”导航框架，即预测“导航可供性热图（navigation affordance heatmap）”以标识可通行区域，以及“朝向热图（facing heatmap）”以约束机器人姿态；同时构建了基于基础模型（Foundation Model）的自动化合成数据流水线与基准测试。

Result: 在8B参数对比中达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能；在不同机器人形态（Jetbot, H1, Aliengo）的实验中，显著提升了可供性比率（AR），验证了双热图表示在提高导航鲁棒性方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过双热图表示法，将抽象的语义指令转化为可执行的物理空间概率分布，有效克服了传统单点回归带来的不确定性丢失与不可通行目标问题，实现了跨机器人形态的高效、鲁棒语义导航。

Abstract: Grounding open-ended semantic instructions into physically executable local goals is a fundamental challenge in human-robot interaction. While existing navigation frameworks often regress deterministic waypoints, this rigid formulation collapses spatial uncertainty and frequently targets non-traversable object centers, leading to severe execution failures. In this work, we focus on the practical setting of in-FOV semantic navigation, where a robot receives concise, interleaved multimodal (text and image) prompts. To bridge the gap between abstract semantic intent and physical reachability, we propose a unified Vision-Language framework that abandons single-point regression in favor of a Dual-Heatmap representation. Our framework predicts a navigation affordance heatmap that captures continuous reachable regions, coupled with a facing heatmap for orientation constraints. These dense outputs inherently function as a differentiable semantic potential field, integrating seamlessly with downstream local planners. To support this paradigm, we build a fully automated, foundation-model-assisted synthetic data pipeline and establish a comprehensive simulation benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable 8B baselines. Crucially, a feature-fusion study and simulation studies across diverse robot embodiments (Jetbot, H1, Aliengo) reveal that explicit heatmap prediction drastically improves the Affordance Rate (AR). By placing targets reliably in executable free space, our framework effectively mitigates the brittleness of point regression, offering a transferable path toward safe cross-embodiment semantic navigation.

</details>


### [245] [Self-assembling Modular Aerial Robot for Versatile Aerial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19431)
*Junichiro Sugihara,Masaki Kitagawa,Jinjie Li,Yunong Li,Takuzumi Nishio,Kei Okada,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受蚂蚁启发的可在飞行中自组装的模块化机器人系统（LEGION），通过机身间的连接实现了从敏捷独立飞行到集群式物理操纵的形态切换，有效解决了传统小型飞行器在敏捷性与操纵能力上的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型多旋翼飞行器难以在敏捷导航与高负载物理交互之间取得平衡，需要一种能够结合两者优势的技术方案。

Method: 提出了LEGION模块化机器人系统；设计了具备对接接口的单元，通过在飞行中控制接触力与转矩实现零间隙锁闭；开发了能进行形态切换及多种操纵原语（推、拉、转、抓、运）的协同控制策略。

Result: 实现了多单元自主飞行中对接；在户外环境下验证了稳定的零间隙锁定及协同操纵能力；成功执行了多种物理作业任务，证明了系统从敏捷飞行到复杂协作转换的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过受蚂蚁启发的可重构模块化飞行机器人（LEGION），解决了小型无人机在敏捷性与操纵性之间的传统性能权衡，实现了空中自组装与协同作业。

Abstract: Multirotor aerial robots excel at maneuvering in three-dimensional space, and recent advances enable nimble navigation in cluttered and confined environments, especially for small airframes. By contrast, platforms built for high-altitude work tend to be larger to deliver high thrust for stable physical interaction with the environment. However, these conflicting design requirements create a long-standing trade-off between nimble navigation and robust aerial manipulation. Here, we present LEGION units, which are reconfigurable modular aerial robots capable of in-flight self-assembly for cooperative manipulation, drawing inspiration from the self-organized collectives formed by ants. Each unit retains nimble maneuverability while joint-equipped docking interfaces at both ends enable end-to-end self-assembly into a flying manipulator. We show that multiple units autonomously dock in flight; once latched, they maintain a zero-clearance interlock by controlling the contact force and torque, enabling reliable aggregation and articulated motion even outdoors. We further show that self-reconfigurability enables morphological switching between nimble individual flight and collective articulated manipulation, while realizing core in-flight manipulation primitives including pushing, pulling, rotating, grasping, and carrying. LEGION's self-organization enables aerial robots, especially in swarms, to shift from passive observers to active participants in their environment, broadening the scope of aerial physical interaction.

</details>


### [246] [CANINE: Coaching Visually Impaired Users for Interactive Navigation with a Robot Guide Dog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19501)
*Cunjun Yu,Zishuo Wang,Anxing Xiao,Linfeng Li,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CANINE是一种针对导盲机器人操作的自动教练系统，通过知识追踪评估用户水平，并结合基础模型生成个性化语音反馈，显著提升了视障用户的导航技能习得效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用语音指导难以让视障用户掌握使用导盲机器人所需的复杂人机协作与导航技巧，需要一种更高效、个性化的智能训练辅助系统。

Method: 提出CANINE系统，采用分层架构：高层使用知识追踪（Knowledge Tracing）评估用户在不同子技能上的熟练度并动态排序；底层利用基础模型（Foundation Models）分析操作错误原因，生成个性化的自适应语音纠正反馈。

Result: 对照实验证明，相比通用语音指令，CANINE显著提升了用户的学习效率和最终导航水平；保留性实验验证了效果可维持两周；初步案例研究证实了该系统对视障用户的有效性。

Conclusion: CANINE系统能够有效提升视障人士操控导盲机器人的学习效率与导航表现，具备良好的技能保持性，并通过真实用户测试验证了其在实际应用场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Robot guide dogs offer navigation assistance that greatly expands the independent mobility of the visually impaired, but their effective use requires subtle human-robot coordination that is difficult for users to learn from generic verbal instructions. To tackle this challenge, we present CANINE, an automated coaching system that trains users for interactive navigation with a robot guide dog, through personalized, adaptive verbal feedback. CANINE decomposes a complex coordination task into sub-skills and operates at two levels. At the high level, it decides what to train by tracking the learner's proficiency across sub-skills using knowledge tracing and prioritizing training on the weakest areas. At the low level, CANINE decides how to train each sub-skill by observing each human practice episode, using foundation models to infer the underlying causes of errors, and generating targeted verbal corrections adaptively. A controlled study with blindfolded participants, treated as a proxy population for quantitative evaluation, demonstrates that CANINE significantly improves both learning efficiency and final navigation performance compared to generic verbal instructions. We further validate CANINE through a retention study and an exploratory case study. The retention study shows lasting skill improvement after two weeks. The case study confirms CANINE's effectiveness in training a visually impaired user, while revealing additional design considerations for real-world deployment. Both are well aligned with the findings of the controlled study. Project page: https://cunjunyu.github.io/project/canine/

</details>


### [247] [ARC-RL: A Reinforcement Learning Playground Inspired by ARC Raiders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19503)
*Carlo Romeo,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了 ARC-RL，这是一个包含四种独特、风格化游戏生物形态的强化学习基准，旨在解决游戏 NPC 运动控制与传统机器人强化学习的鸿沟，并评估了多种强化学习算法在这一新基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究多关注基于真实硬件的机器人形态，忽视了游戏 NPC 中常见的风格化、非真实生理结构的运动控制需求，缺乏相应的统一评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含四种独特非真实生物形态（Queen, Bastion, Tick, Leaper）的 MuJoCo 环境；定义了统一的观测、动作空间及通用的闭式多组件奖励函数；引入了手工设计的中央模式发生器（CPG）作为演示者，并对比了在线（SAC 等）与离线增强（SACfD 等）强化学习算法。

Result: 通过实证研究揭示了不同强化学习范式在应对高度形态多样性和特定动作风格约束时的适应性差异，提供了一个验证非典型形态运动控制鲁棒性的实验平台。

Conclusion: ARC-RL 提供了一个标准化、多样化的基准，用于解决游戏角色（NPC）与现实机器人运动控制之间的差异，并通过实验展示了不同强化学习范式在该环境下的性能表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for legged locomotion has matured into a stack of multi-component reward functions and physics-engine benchmarks whose morphologies are uniformly derived from real commercial hardware. Game NPCs, however, are bound by stylistic constraints absent from sim-to-real robotics and routinely take the form of creatures with no real-robot counterpart. We introduce ARC-RL, a suite of four MuJoCo continuous-control environments featuring robotic morphologies inspired by the bestiary of ARC Raiders: the 18-DoF tall hexapod Queen, the 12-DoF armoured hexapod Bastion, the 18-DoF compact hexapod Tick, and the 12-DoF quadruped Leaper. All four robots share a unified observation template, action convention, simulation cadence, and a single closed-form multi-component reward function whose only per-morphology variation lives in a small set of weights and parameters. The reward fuses a velocity-tracking tent, a healthy survive bonus, a phase-locked gait-compliance bonus/cost pair, action regularisers, three safety penalties, and a posture anchor; no motion-capture data enters the reward at any point. We additionally provide hand-crafted Central Pattern Generator demonstrators per morphology, which serve both as fixed expert references and as sources of prior data for offline-to-online training. On this playground, we conduct a controlled empirical study comparing standard online algorithms (SAC, SPEQ, SOPE-EO) and methods augmented with prior data (SACfD, SPEQ-O2O, SOPE), and characterise how each paradigm copes with the playground's morphological diversity and animation-style stylistic constraints.

</details>


### [248] [SafeAlign-VLA: A Negative-Enhanced Safe Alignment Framework for Risk-Aware Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19524)
*Kefei Tian,Yuansheng Lian,Kai Yang,Xiangdong Chen,Shen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeAlign-VLA 通过引入反事实负例数据和对比学习策略，弥补了传统 VLA 模型在安全感知上的短板，显著提升了自动驾驶系统在长尾危险场景下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的自动驾驶模型多依赖正向专家演示，缺乏对风险行为和安全边界的理解，难以处理安全关键的长尾场景。

Method: 1. 采用反事实安全配对范式（Counterfactual Safety Pairing）生成负面数据和轨迹。
2. 实施两阶段训练：(1) 负例增强监督微调（Negative-enhanced SFT）；(2) 基于锚点的组相对策略优化（Anchor-based Group Relative Policy Optimization）。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 数据集达到 89.1 PDMS（提升 1.3%）；在 DeepAccident 数据集中将碰撞率降低至 3.36%，同时保持了高水平的语言与风险预测准确率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于负例增强的安全对齐框架 SafeAlign-VLA，通过利用负面数据显著提升了自动驾驶系统在长尾安全场景下的识别与决策能力，有效降低了碰撞风险。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems excel in common scenarios but struggle with safety-critical long-tail cases. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are promising due to their strong reasoning capabilities. However, most VLA-based approaches rely on positive expert demonstrations, rarely exploiting negative samples, leading to insufficient understanding of risky behaviors and safety boundaries. To address this limitation, we propose SafeAlign-VLA, a unified negative-enhanced safe alignment framework that incorporates negative data into supervised learning and reinforcement learning. First, we develop a counterfactual safety pairing paradigm to generate structured safety labels and counterfactual positive trajectories from risky scenarios via counterfactual reasoning. Then, a two-stage training strategy is adopted: negative-enhanced supervised fine-tuning for failure feedback and trajectory correction, followed by anchor-based group relative policy optimization that uses positive and negative trajectories as contrastive anchors to steer sampling and penalize high-risk behaviors via group-relative advantages. Experiments on NAVSIM and DeepAccident validate the proposed framework. SafeAlign-VLA achieves 89.1 PDMS on the NAVSIM v1 testset, improving over the baseline without negative data by 1.3%. On DeepAccident, it reduces the collision rate to 3.36%, while achieving 84.2% language accuracy and 85.8% risk prediction accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed negative-enhanced safe alignment framework for safe and robust autonomous driving.

</details>


### [249] [FlyMirage: A Fully Automated Generation Pipeline for Diverse and Scalable UAV Flight Data via Generative World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19600)
*Jinhan Li,Xijie Huang,Zhaoqi Wang,Yijin Wang,Weiqi Ge,Qiyi He,Mo Zhu,Fei Gao,Yuze Wu,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlyMirage 是一个自动化的空中 VLN 数据生成流水线，利用 LLM 和 3D 高斯溅射技术，高效构建了大规模、高保真且符合动力学约束的无人机导航数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前空中 VLN 领域数据集规模小、多样性不足或真实感欠缺的问题，旨在解决构建高质量训练数据时成本高昂、人工耗时长以及模拟器逼真度有限的瓶颈。

Method: 1. 利用大语言模型（LLM）作为环境设计师以增强场景的多样性；2. 结合生成式世界模型将设计实例化为高保真的 3D 高斯溅射（3DGS）场景；3. 通过自动化场景探索和语义获取技术减少人工参与；4. 集成动态可行路径规划器，确保无人机（UAV）轨迹的物理真实性。

Result: 成功构建了一个大规模、高度多样化且具有真实感（基于 3DGS）的空中 VLN 数据集，其中包含了满足动态物理约束的无人机导航轨迹。

Conclusion: FlyMirage 提供了一个可扩展、自动化且具高度逼真的空中视觉语言导航（VLN）数据生成框架，填补了现有数据集在规模、多样性和真实性方面的空白，为下一代具身导航模型的发展奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: In the field of Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), aerial datasets remain limited in their ability to combine scale, diversity, and realism, often relying on either costly real-world scenes or visually limited simulations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlyMirage, a highly scalable and fully automated data generation pipeline for aerial VLN. Our approach leverages large language models (LLM) as an environment designer to promote scene diversity, paired with a generative world model that instantiates these designs into high-fidelity 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes. To substantially reduce human labor and ensure the feasibility of flight data, FlyMirage automates scene exploration and semantic information acquisition, and further integrates a dynamically feasible planner for uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) trajectory generation. Utilizing this toolchain, we generate a large-scale, diverse, and photorealistic aerial VLN dataset, with dynamically feasible flying trajectories, designed to support the development of next-generation embodied navigation models.

</details>


### [250] [HEAT: Heterogeneous End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Trajectory-Guided World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19631)
*Hoonhee Cho,Giwon Lee,Jae-Young Kang,Hyemin Yang,Heejun Park,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对端到端自动驾驶在跨异构领域训练时表现下降的问题，本文提出轨迹驱动学习范式与世界模型，有效解耦领域特征，实现了多源数据下的高性能模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶模型在跨异构领域（不同城市、传感器配置、交通模式）训练时，由于不同领域间的冲突性学习信号，导致模型倾向于产生在各领域均非最优的折中方案，现有方法在单领域表现良好但多领域泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出了一种“轨迹驱动”学习范式，通过以规划轨迹为核心组织训练，提取驾驶意图的域不变特征；并引入世界模型，通过预测受自我动作影响的未来潜在特征，增强特征一致性并缓解领域偏差。

Result: 在nuScenes、NAVSIM和Waymo三个自动驾驶基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有方法，证明了单个统一模型在多异构领域中保持高性能的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于轨迹驱动的学习范式和世界模型能够有效克服多领域数据异构带来的负面影响，实现单个统一端到端模型在多领域的高性能表现，为可扩展的自动驾驶部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has emerged as a compelling alternative to traditional modular pipelines by directly mapping raw sensor data to driving actions. While recent approaches achieve strong performance on single-domain datasets, their performance degrades significantly when trained jointly across multiple heterogeneous domains. In practice, however, autonomous systems must operate across diverse environments with heterogeneous distributions, including different cities, sensor configurations, and traffic patterns, without domain-specific retraining. This gap highlights a key challenge in multi-domain learning: domain-specific variations across heterogeneous domains introduce conflicting learning signals, driving models toward compromised solutions that are suboptimal across domains. To address this, we propose a trajectory-driven learning paradigm that organizes training around planning trajectories, enabling the model to capture domain-invariant representations of driving intent. Furthermore, we incorporate a world model that predicts future latent features conditioned on ego actions, improving feature consistency and mitigating domain-induced biases. We evaluate our approach on three benchmarks, nuScenes, NAVSIM, and the Waymo end-to-end dataset, and show substantial improvements over existing methods across all domains. Our results demonstrate that a single unified model can be trained on heterogeneous datasets while maintaining strong performance within each domain, highlighting a step toward scalable real-world deployment. We will make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Imitation: Learning Safe End-to-End Autonomous Driving from Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19771)
*Junli Wang,Zhihua Hua,Xueyi Liu,Zebin Xing,Haochen Tian,Kun Ma,Hangjun Ye,Guang Chen,Long Chen,Qichao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondDrive 是一种失败感知模仿学习框架，通过引入负样本生成、多样性采样和排斥距离损失，强制模型学习安全边界，从而提升自动驾驶的安全性及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习大多仅依靠专家轨迹进行训练，忽视了“空间接近不代表行为安全”，导致模型无法有效辨别可能导致碰撞的危险行为（客观的不匹配）。

Method: 1. 引入基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的负样本轨迹生成器，合成具有安全临界性但接近专家的轨迹。
2. 开发多样性感知采样策略，防止模型崩溃并覆盖更多失败模式。
3. 提出排斥距离损失（Repulsive Distance Loss），通过在轨迹空间吸引专家轨迹并排斥硬负样本，构建判别性安全边界。

Result: 在 NAVSIMv1 基准测试中达到 89.7 PDMS，超越现有最优方法；具有良好的模型泛化性以及在 HUGSIM 基准上的零样本迁移表现。

Conclusion: BeyondDrive 通过显式建模安全不对称性，有效解决了端到端自动驾驶中“行为相似性不等于安全性”的问题，为学习安全驾驶策略提供了一种鲁棒且可泛化的新范式。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods for end-to-end autonomous driving predominantly learn from successful demonstrations by minimizing geometric deviations from expert trajectories. This paradigm implicitly assumes that spatial proximity implies behavioral safety, leading to a critical objective mismatch: trajectories with nearly identical imitation losses may exhibit drastically different safety outcomes, where one remains recoverable while the other results in collision. To address this limitation, we propose BeyondDrive, a failure-aware imitation learning framework that jointly learns from successful and failed driving behaviors. First, we introduce a flow matching-based negative trajectory generator that synthesizes safety-critical yet expert-proximate trajectories, enabling explicit modeling of safety asymmetry. Second, we develop a diversity-aware sampling strategy that mitigates mode collapse and improves coverage of diverse failure modes during negative trajectory generation. Third, we propose a Repulsive Distance Loss that simultaneously attracts predictions toward expert demonstrations while repelling them from hard negative trajectories, thereby establishing discriminative safety boundaries in trajectory space. Applied to the uni-modal baseline Latent TransFuser, BeyondDrive achieves 89.7 PDMS on the NAVSIMv1 closed-loop benchmark, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, BeyondDrive generalizes effectively across different autonomous driving architectures, including multi-modal planners, and further demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability on the HUGSIM benchmark.

</details>


### [252] [Justifying bio-inspired robotics research: A taxonomy of strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19840)
*Margaret J. Zhang,Justin Ting,Talia Y. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对仿生机器人领域缺乏统一设计准则的问题，构建了一套仿生设计动机分类法，旨在提升研究设计的系统性、可预测性及评价的透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前仿生设计缺乏系统性方法，导致设计动机与方法不一致，使得仿生机器人研究难以客观评价，常导致设计表面化或效果不及预期的问题。

Method: 提出了一套针对仿生设计（bio-inspired design）的动机分类法（taxonomy），并对不同设计路径对应的预期研究贡献进行了描述与定性分析。

Result: 构建了一个结构化的框架，旨在帮助机器人研究人员明确其设计动机的合理性，同时为资助机构评估不同仿生方案的价值提供依据。

Conclusion: 通过建立生物启发式设计的动机分类法，研究明确了不同设计路径的潜在贡献与预期目标，为机器人领域的研发评估与决策提供了系统性框架，有助于解决当前领域内设计评价标准模糊的问题。

Abstract: For most of human history, we have not thought systematically about how and why we incorporate aspects of the natural world into our designs. The lack of a systematic approach has resulted in inconsistencies in motivations and methods that make it difficult to predict or evaluate the success of bio-inspired design. This mismatch between expectations and results can lead to disappointment when a reader considers a bio-inspired design to be superficial, weak, or incomplete. This is especially true in the field of Robotics, in which similarity to a biological system might be the driving motivation for construction.
