In an effort to assist robotics researchers justify their specific bio-inspired approach and to assist funding program managers with discerning the value of different bio-inspired approaches, here we propose a taxonomy of motivations for bio-inspired design and describe the potential significant contributions that are likely to result from different approaches.

</details>


### [253] [Beyond Action Residuals: Real-World Robot Policy Steering via Bottleneck Latent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19919)
*Dongjie Yu,Kun Lei,Zhennan Jiang,Jia Pan,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZPRL 通过在预训练策略中引入变分信息瓶颈，将强化学习的微调从动作空间转移到潜在空间，实现了更高效、更稳定的机器人策略在线适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轻量级强化学习方法常直接在动作空间施加残差修正，导致探索过程嘈杂且缺乏结构，难以有效解决预训练策略在部署时面临的数据覆盖不足和迁移偏差问题。

Method: 提出 Z-Perturbation Reinforcement Learning (ZPRL)：1. 在离线训练时，通过变分信息瓶颈 (VIB) 模块在策略中加入潜在接口；2. 在线微调时，冻结基策略，仅学习该潜在空间的残差扰动；3. 基于流匹配 (flow-matching) 策略进行实例化。

Result: 在8个仿真任务和4个真实世界任务中均表现优异；真实世界任务中，相较于基准模仿学习策略，平均成功率提升了 33.7%，且探索行为比直接动作残差修正更平滑。

Conclusion: 在紧凑的、任务对齐的潜在空间（latent space）中进行扰动，是实现预训练机器人策略在线强化学习适配的有效接口。该方法在保证样本效率和性能的同时，表现出比动作空间残差方法更平滑的探索行为。

Abstract: Pretrained imitation policies have become a strong foundation for robot manipulation, but they often require online improvement to overcome execution errors, limited dataset coverage, and deployment mismatch. A central question is therefore how reinforcement learning (RL) should adapt policies after offline pretraining. Existing lightweight methods commonly apply residual corrections directly in action space, but this often leads to noisy and poorly structured exploration. In this work, we propose Z-Perturbation Reinforcement Learning (ZPRL), an approach that steers pretrained policies through a compact bottleneck latent rather than through policy weights or output actions. During offline training, we augment the policy with a plug-and-play variational information bottleneck (VIB) module to extract a task-relevant latent interface from observation embeddings. During online finetuning, the base policy is frozen and RL learns only a residual perturbation on this latent, whose decoded representation conditions the frozen action generator. We instantiate ZPRL on flow-matching policies and evaluate it on eight simulation tasks and four real-world tasks. Across diverse manipulation settings, ZPRL improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong post-training baselines. In the real world, ZPRL improves the average success rate on four tasks by 33.7% over imitation base policies while producing smoother exploration behaviors than an action residual counterpart. These results suggest that a compact, task-aligned bottleneck latent provides an effective interface for online RL adaptation. More videos can be found at https://manutdmoon.github.io/ZPRL/.

</details>


### [254] [RoHIL: Robust Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Reinforcement Learning Against Illumination Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19924)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Yixin Xiong,Xiru Gao,Kai Liu,Ke Wang,Xichuan Zhou,Zhe Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoHIL 是一种离线微调框架，通过图像重光照、特殊的重放机制和正则化约束，解决了机器人学习中因光照变化导致的跨环境泛化问题，无需额外的真实机器人交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL 强化学习模型在不同工作站部署时，常因光照等视觉分布的变化导致性能崩溃，而重新收集数据或直接微调则会面临部署成本高昂或灾难性遗忘的问题。

Method: RoHIL 框架由三部分组成：1. 基于世界模型的图像重光照器（Image Relighter），用于将源环境轨迹数据转换为不同虚拟环境光照下的图像；2. 照明保留回放机制（IRR），通过交替回放重光照数据与原始光照数据，防止灾难性遗忘；3. 锚定 Bellman 动作正则化器，用于约束表示和策略偏移。

Result: 在四项真实机器人操作任务中，相比标准 HIL-RL，RoHIL 在光照变化场景下表现显著提升，同时完整保留了模型在原工作站的性能。

Conclusion: RoHIL 框架有效解决了人机回环（HIL）强化学习在跨工作站光照环境变化下的泛化难题。它通过无交互的离线微调技术，在提升目标环境表现的同时实现了对原环境能力的保持，极大地降低了机器人部署成本。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning systems achieve near-perfect success on the workstation where they are trained, but collapse when the same robot is moved to a workstation a few meters away due to shifts in the visual input distribution caused by new lamp positions and window light. Re-collecting demonstrations and re-running HIL on every workstation is incompatible with deployment, and naively fine-tuning on shifted-light data triggers catastrophic forgetting of the source workstation. To close this cross-domain gap, we present RoHIL, an offline fine-tuning framework that uses no extra real-robot interaction. RoHIL combines (i) a world-model-based image relighter that re-synthesises the visual stream of source-workstation trajectories under multiple virtual HDRI environments, leaving actions and rewards real; (ii) Illumination-Retention Replay (IRR), a data-level anti-forgetting mechanism that interleaves relit adaptation transitions with original-light retention transitions to preserve source-workstation Bellman coverage; and (iii) an anchored Bellman-actor regulariser that constrains representation and policy drift from the original source-workstation policy. Across four real-robot manipulation tasks under significant cross-workstation illumination variations, RoHIL substantially improves shifted-light performance where standard HIL-RL collapses, while preserving source-workstation performance, eliminating the need to re-collect data and retrain for every new workstation and environment. Project page: https://anonymous4365.github.io/RoHIL/

</details>


### [255] [CEER: Compliant End-Effector and Root Control as a Unified Interface for Hierarchical Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19981)
*Xinyuan Luo,Xingrui Chen,Xunjian Yin,Hongxuan Wu,Boxi Xia,Zhuoqun Chen,Jinzhou Li,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CEER控制抽象，通过将人形机器人的控制解耦为根节点运动与端点位姿目标，实现了在复杂环境下接触密集型操作的模块化、高效及柔顺控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人 locomotion 表现出色，但在处理接触密集型和长周期操作任务时存在瓶颈。操作任务需要具备柔顺的全身体控制能力，且高层规划与底层控制的解耦（模块化）对于处理多样的操作任务至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种名为CEER的端点-根（EE-root）控制抽象；采用师生学习框架，将全身体控制器蒸馏为低层策略；构建了分层架构，将高层规划器与底层全身体控制解耦，支持即插即用的模块化交互。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验显示：端点跟踪精度达到3.3 cm，姿态控制更加平滑（减少了抖动）；在远程操作下展现了稳定的接触式操作能力；在复杂场景的单物体移动操作任务中成功率达到70%。

Conclusion: CEER通过提供一种兼容性强、可解释的EE-root控制抽象，为解决人形机器人在接触式、长周期移动操作任务中的难点提供了一种通用且模块化的解决方案，证明了其在实际硬件部署和复杂环境中的实用性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved impressive locomotion performance, yet contact-rich and long-horizon manipulation remains a major bottleneck. Manipulation is inherently contact-rich and demands compliant whole-body control for stable interaction, while its diversity and long-horizon nature favor modular, planner-compatible interfaces over joint-space tracking.
  We propose CEER, a compliant end-effector-root (EE-root) control abstraction for modular humanoid loco-manipulation within a hierarchical planning framework. CEER enables compliance-aware whole-body control in an interpretable task space defined by root motion commands and end-effector pose targets, and supports plug-and-play integration with heterogeneous high-level planners. A teacher-student framework is adopted to distill a general motion-tracking controller into a low-level policy that consumes only EE-root commands.
  We further construct a hierarchical system that integrates heterogeneous planners and task modules through the EE-root interface, enabling diverse manipulation tasks without retraining the underlying whole-body policy. Experiments in simulation and on hardware demonstrate 3.3 cm end-effector tracking accuracy with substantially reduced jerk compared to baselines, stable contact-rich manipulation under teleoperation, and up to 70% success in simulated single-object loco-manipulation tasks within a room-scale environment. These results indicate that compliant EE-root control provides a practical abstraction for humanoid loco-manipulation, enabling modular and scalable integration of diverse skills.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Binary Success: A Diagnostic Meta-Evaluation Framework for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19986)
*He-Yang Xu,Pengyuan Zhang,Zongyuan Ge,Xiaoshuai Hao,Serge Belongie,Xin Geng,Yuxin Peng,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MetaFine 框架，通过将评估从“二元成功率”转向“多维度诊断”，揭示了制约具身智能细粒度操作的架构瓶颈在于视觉编码器对空间结构的感知不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的具身智能基准测试过度依赖二元成功率，导致模型能力的虚高并掩盖了细粒度操作中的架构瓶颈，无法真实反映模型在理解、感知和受控行为方面的具体缺陷。

Method: 提出了名为 MetaFine 的诊断性元评估框架，利用组合任务图（compositional task graph）将异构基准测试重构为不同复杂度的诊断场景；引入因果干预分析关键瓶颈；并支持混合虚实验证以校准仿真评估。

Result: 通过 MetaFine 评估发现，当前主流 VLA 模型在细粒度操作中存在维度特定的严重失效；实验证明，提升视觉编码器的空间结构保留能力可直接解锁模型的高精度操作能力。

Conclusion: MetaFine 从单纯的性能排名转向诊断式评估，为识别和修复具身智能的底层物理灵巧性能力提供了可操作的路径，并揭示了视觉编码器在空间结构保留方面的局限性是制约细粒度操作的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation marks a regime where global scene context no longer suffices, and success hinges on the tight coupling of local attribute grounding, high-fidelity spatial perception, and constraint-respecting motor execution. However, current embodied AI benchmarks collapse these capacities into binary success rates, systematically inflating reported capabilities by up to 70% and masking the architectural bottlenecks that impede real-world deployment. We introduce MetaFine, a diagnostic meta-evaluation framework that disentangles manipulation competency along three axes: understanding, perception, and controlled behavior. Built on a compositional task graph, MetaFine absorbs heterogeneous external benchmarks and reconstructs them into diagnostic scenarios of varying complexity under a unified protocol. Evaluating state-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models through this lens exposes severe dimension-specific failures invisible to conventional metrics. Through targeted causal intervention, we identify the visual encoder's ability to preserve local spatial structure as a key bottleneck for fine-grained precision: improving it directly unlocks previously inaccessible manipulation capabilities without modifying downstream policies. MetaFine further supports hybrid real-sim validation, using limited paired real-world rollouts to calibrate scalable simulation-based estimates for more stable physical benchmarking. By shifting evaluation from ranking to diagnosis, MetaFine turns benchmarking into an actionable compass for repairing the layered capacities underlying genuine physical dexterity. The MetaFine framework, benchmarks, and supporting resources will be publicly released at our project page: https://metafine.github.io/.

</details>


### [257] [Hamilton--Jacobi Reachability for Spacecraft Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20138)
*Larry Hui,Jordan Kam,William Su,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析处理两颗卫星的在轨避碰问题，通过零和微分对策建模，计算出避碰的后向可达集，并构建监督控制逻辑以确保卫星在最差干扰下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对轨道上两颗卫星的避碰问题，旨在满足联邦通信委员会（FCC）轨道安全标准的前提下，在存在未知意图的对抗性干扰情况下，提供一种具有数学保证的避碰策略。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) 可达性分析方法，构建基于平面Hill-Clohessy-Wiltshire (HCW) 动力学的零和微分对策模型，计算后向可达集（Backward Reachable Sets）并结合监督混合控制逻辑。

Result: 成功计算出了可表征“在最差干扰下无法避免碰撞”的后向可达集；该集合界定并识别了危险相对状态空间，并能够与混合控制逻辑集成，实现可扩展的安全飞行轨迹规划。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于HJ可达集的避碰框架通过将轨道避碰问题建模为零和微分对策，为卫星在轨运行提供了数学严谨的安全保证，能够有效判断何时启动规避机动。

Abstract: This article presents a Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability framework for a two--satellite collision avoidance problem operating in the same circular orbit, where relative motion is modeled in the radial--tangential--normal (RTN) frame using planar Hill--Clohessy--Wiltshire (HCW) dynamics. We define the target state space as unsafe relative configurations in the orbit plane corresponding to minimum separation requirements consistent with Federal Communications Commission (FCC) orbital standards. The interaction between spacecraft is formulated as a zero--sum differential game, where Player 1 is the controlled satellite and Player 2 is modeled as a bounded adversarial disturbance with unknown intent. We present the HJ formulation and compute backward reachable sets that characterize relative states from which collision cannot be avoided under worst-case disturbances, while states outside this set admit provably collision-free trajectories. These reachable sets are integrated with supervisory hybrid control logic to determine when evasive maneuvers must be initiated, enabling mathematically grounded safety guarantees for scalability.

</details>


### [258] [Implicit Action Chunking for Smooth Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19592)
*Bosun Liang,Shuo Pei,Zirui Chen,Chuanzhi Fan,Chen Sun,Yuankai Wu,Huachun Tan,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DWS 提出一种隐式动作切片框架，通过双窗口机制和时间正则化实现控制平滑，在保持反应式逐步控制的同时消除了 RL 系统的高频振荡，显著优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中高频震荡控制信号导致的物理部署不稳定问题，同时克服显式动作切片法（Action Chunking）导致的动作空间维度爆炸与训练优化困难。

Method: 提出双窗口平滑（DWS）框架，包含：1. 执行窗口（执行确定性调制以实现平滑）；2. 值窗口（校正开放循环执行带来的 Critic 偏差）；3. 基于一阶动作差分的轻量级演员时间正则化。

Result: 在 DeepMind Control Suite、工业能源管理及复杂视觉自动驾驶任务中表现优于 SOTA 方法；在自动驾驶任务中实现了更平稳的控制及 100% 的成功率。

Conclusion: DWS 通过双窗口机制和时间正则化，成功在不改变动作空间维度的前提下，实现了强化学习策略的高效平滑控制，显著提升了物理部署中的安全性和稳定性，在复杂环境展现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning often produces high-frequency oscillatory control signals that undermine the safety and stability required for physical deployment. Explicit action chunking addresses this by predicting fixed-horizon trajectories but scales the policy output dimension proportionally with the horizon length, leading to optimization difficulties and incompatibility with standard step-wise interaction. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes Dual-Window Smoothing (DWS), an implicit action chunking framework for smooth continuous control. Unlike explicit methods, DWS enforces temporal coherence without expanding the action space. It uses a dual-window design: an execution window that ensures physical smoothness through deterministic modulation, and a value window that aligns temporal-difference targets over the horizon to correct critic bias caused by open-loop execution. DWS also includes a lightweight actor-side temporal regularizer based on first-order action differences to promote global continuity. This design effectively bridges the gap between temporal abstraction and reactive step-wise control. Experiments on benchmarks including the DeepMind Control Suite and industrial energy management tasks show that DWS outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines. In complex vision-based autonomous driving tasks, DWS achieves smoother control, safer behavior with reduced jitter, and attains a 100% success rate.

</details>


### [259] [A Heuristic Approach for Performance Tuning in RL-based Quadrotor Control via Reward Design and Termination Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19166)
*Fausto Mauricio Lagos Suarez,Akshit Saradagi,Vidya Sumathy,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过奖励函数设计与启发式参数调整，实现RL控制四旋翼飞行器性能可调（如快速响应与平稳巡检）的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习（RL）控制策略多侧重于速度和敏捷性（如穿越机），但在基础设施巡检等场景中，往往需要具备可调控的精确操作能力，而当前的RL方法在此类任务中缺乏直观的调控手段。

Method: 1. 设计了一种包含双带宽指数项的奖励结构，实现基准临界阻尼响应；2. 结合PPO算法并引入特定的回合截断（episode truncation）条件进行训练；3. 提出了一套启发式规则，通过调整奖励权重和指数系数，在无需重训的情况下微调控制性能。

Result: 该模型在600万步迭代内表现出高效的收敛性；在100次随机初始状态的测试中，三种调控策略（基准、特技、巡检）均能实现高精度的位置和偏航跟踪，稳态误差保持在2%左右。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的启发式方法可在保持临界阻尼响应的前提下，通过奖励机制设计实现四旋翼飞行器控制策略性能的灵活调控（如在巡检和特技飞行之间切换），且具备良好的稳态精度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL)-based quadrotor control policies have achieved impressive performance in tasks such as fast navigation in cluttered environments and drone racing, where the focus is on speed and agility. However, in several applications, such as infrastructure inspection, it is critical to achieve precise, controlled maneuvers with tunable performance. In this article, we present a novel heuristic approach to achieve tunable performance in RL-based Quadrotor control through reward design and termination conditions. We present a novel reward structure containing dual bandwidth exponentials that achieves a baseline critically damped response in setpoint tracking, with low steady-state errors. When trained with a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, in conjunction with episode truncation conditions, the desired performance is achieved in 6 million time steps in a sample-efficient manner. In order to tune the performance about the baseline behavior, we present intuitive heuristic rules to adjust the reward weights and exponential coefficients to achieve faster (acrobatic-like) and slower (inspection-like) settling time performance, while retaining the baseline critically damped response and approximately 2\% steady-state error. We evaluate the three RL policies (baseline, acrobatic, and inspection) across 100 trials and show accurate and tunable performance in position and yaw tracking from random initial conditions, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed heuristic approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用“数据探针”（由随机过程生成的合成序列）来系统化研究数据特性如何影响 LLM 各阶段的表现，旨在从理论上揭开数据对模型影响的本质，而非仅仅依赖经验过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前 LLM 对数据有效性的理解仍依赖于计算密集型的经验法则，缺乏针对不同工作流阶段（训练、微调、对齐等）对数据特征影响的系统性理论理解。

Method: 提出了一种“数据探针”（Data Probes）方法，即通过定义明确的随机过程生成合成序列，以此作为探针观察 LLM 的行为，并将这些序列的统计特性与典型集（Typical Sets）等理论概念相结合进行系统研究。

Result: 构建了一套通过受控合成数据研究 LLM 机制的框架，证明了数据探针能够展示数据特征如何统计性地引导模型行为，从而替代单一的试错式实验。

Conclusion: 该方法为理解大型语言模型（LLM）训练与推理中数据的作用提供了一条超越经验法则的理论路径，通过受控的“数据探针”有助于揭示 LLM 性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性背后的本质规律。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [261] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对文档理解模型难以规模化部署的痛点，论文提出了一套基于微服务的生产级架构，通过优化计算资源调度与异步处理，成功实现了高吞吐量文档处理，并揭示了OCR性能与GPU容量是影响生产效率的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术界过分关注文档理解的新模型研发，导致模型定义与生产规模化应用之间存在巨大差距，缺乏实际部署经验。

Method: 提出了一种微服务架构，将分类、OCR和LLM结构化提取流水线化；核心策略包括混合分类器设计、CPU/GPU处理分离、异步I/O处理以及独立的水平扩展策略。并利用批处理剖析（batch profiling）分析瓶颈。

Result: 发现了两个关键生产洞察：1. OCR过程而非LLM解析是端到端延迟的主要瓶颈；2. 系统并发上限取决于共享的GPU推理容量，而非工作节点数量。

Conclusion: 通过构建微服务架构和实践经验，证明了在生产环境中实现高效文档理解系统的关键在于合理分配计算资源、优化并发策略以及平衡CPU/GPU负载。该研究为从模型开发到生产落地提供了实用的架构范式。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [262] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究证实了向大语言模型提供个人健康记录（PHR）能显著提升其回答患者查询的质量，同时也建立了一套评估框架来监测模型在处理复杂医疗数据时的潜在错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理的个人健康记录（PHRs）通常信息复杂，导致患者难以从中获得有效见解；研究旨在评估大语言模型（LLMs）能否利用 PHR 数据为患者提供更具帮助性的个性化健康解答。

Method: 利用 Gemini 3.0 Flash 模型，结合 2,257 条临床查询和 1,945 份脱敏 PHR 数据；通过三种不同上下文设置（无上下文、基础摘要、完整临床记录）进行实验；使用现有的 SHARP 框架和新开发的错误识别框架，通过自动评分器和临床医生评分（n=95）进行评估。

Result: 引入 PHR 数据后，回答的帮助性显著提高（p < 0.001）；在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面均表现出提升潜力；但也识别出模型在处理复杂医疗信息时的特定错误模式（如时间错位和幻觉）。

Conclusion: 利用个人健康记录（PHRs）作为上下文可以显著提升大模型回答患者健康查询的帮助性，但在理解复杂医疗数据（如时间错位、罕见幻觉）方面仍存在局限，需要持续的监测与评估。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [263] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LBW-Guard，一个置于AdamW优化器之上的治理层，通过动态监控与有界控制，有效解决了大模型在高强度训练下的不稳定性问题，显著提升了训练鲁棒性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型训练在大规模、高学习率和高运行时压力条件下，经常面临训练不稳定、性能下降及计算资源浪费的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为LBW-Guard的治理层。该机制运行在AdamW优化器之上，通过观察训练遥测数据（telemetry），识别不稳定性感知区域，并对优化器的执行应用有界控制（bounded control），在保持训练目标不变的前提下稳定训练过程。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B基准测试中，LBW-Guard将困惑度（Perplexity）从13.21降低至10.74（提升18.7%），端到端训练时间缩短了1.1倍。在高学习率压力测试下（如LR=3e-3），标准AdamW训练崩溃（困惑度高达1885.24），而使用LBW-Guard仍保持在11.57，表现优于传统的梯度裁剪（Gradient Clipping）基线。

Conclusion: LLM训练可以受益于在优化器之上构建治理层（Governance Plane）。LBW-Guard证明了通过有界运行时控制，能够在高压力训练环境下保留计算生产力，且无需替换优化器或仅依赖局部梯度抑制。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [264] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过结合上下文感知的模式增强与集成分层规划、反思及自纠错能力的编排器，显著提升了SQL生成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）取得快速进展，但在将自然语言转化为SQL（NL2SQL）方面仍无法达到人类专家的水平，因此需要更先进的算法来提升执行精度。

Method: 提出了一种多智能体（Multi-Agent）架构，包含：1. 优化的编排器（Orchestrator），利用LLM进行计划、调度、反思与自纠错；2. 模式增强方法（Schema Enrichment），通过生成上下文感知元数据来辅助查询生成；3. 引入业务规则注入，进一步提升SQL生成的准确度。

Result: 在BIRD-SQL基准测试中实现了78.1%的语义准确率，并验证了该方法在不同领域与数据集上的良好泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过多智能体协作与模式增强技术，有效提升了NL2SQL的准确性，在BIRD基准测试中达到了78.1%的语义准确率，证明了其在复杂任务中的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [265] [KAN-MLP-Mixer: A comprehensive investigation of the usage of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) for improving IMU-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19031)
*Mengxi Liu,Sizhen Bian,Vitor Fortes,Francisco Calatrava Nicolas,Daniel Geißler,Maximilian Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,Bo Zhou,Paul Lukowicz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对KAN在噪声环境下的局限性，作者提出了一种结合KAN和MLP优势的混合架构，在人体活动识别任务中实现了比单一模型更高的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN在处理低维干净数据时表现优秀但对噪声敏感，而MLP虽然鲁棒但性能潜力有限，现有的单一模型难以同时兼顾HAR任务中的复杂性、噪声鲁棒性与计算效率。

Method: 提出了一种混合架构：使用KAN层作为输入嵌入层，保留MLP层进行中间特征融合，并引入专门的LarctanKAN模块进行最终的活动分类。通过在八个公共HAR数据集上进行系统性实验，评估了该混合策略的性能。

Result: 该混合KAN-MLP模型在八个数据集上的平均宏F1分数较纯MLP模型提升了5.33%，全面优于单一的KAN或MLP模型，且能够无缝集成到现有SOTA架构中以提升性能。

Conclusion: 结合KAN与MLP的混合架构在人体活动识别（HAR）任务中表现出显著的优势，证明了协同利用两种范式（KAN的精确性与MLP的稳健性/高效性）是提升现实世界传感器数据建模能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have demonstrated an exceptional ability to learn complex functions on clean, low-dimensional data but struggle to maintain performance on noisy and imperfect real-world datasets. In contrast, conventional multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) are far more tolerant to noise and computationally efficient. Replacing all MLP components with KANs in HAR models often degrades accuracy and computation efficiency, highlighting an open challenge: how to combine KANs' precision with MLPs' noise robustness and efficiency. To address this, we systematically explore various placements of KAN modules within deep HAR networks and propose a hybrid architecture that strategically synergizes the strengths of both paradigms, which uses a KAN-based input embedding layer, retains MLP layers for intermediate feature mixing, and introduces a specialized LarctanKAN module for final activity classification. Across eight public HAR datasets, the hybrid KAN-MLP model achieves an average macro F1 score relative improvement of 5.33\% compared pure-MLP model, significantly outperforming standalone KAN and MLP baselines. Furthermore, integrating this hybrid strategy into other state-of-the-art HAR architectures consistently boosts their performance. Our findings demonstrate that a carefully orchestrated combination of KAN, MLP, or other conventional neural components yields more robust and accurate HAR models for real-world wearable sensing environments.

</details>


### [266] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分了波动的环境状态（驱动探索）与噪声观测（抑制探索），并提出了CAUSE算法以实现更优的动态决策，同时从计算神经科学角度为理解精神疾病的探索异常提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策研究通常将不确定性的不同来源混为一谈。研究旨在区分奖励状态随时间变化的“波动性（volatility）”和通过噪声观测到的“随机性（stochasticity）”，并探究二者对探索行为的不同影响。

Method: 通过扩展Gittins指数框架至高斯状态空间多臂老虎机模型，利用控制即推断（control-as-inference）方法推导出了CAUSE（Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration）算法。

Result: 研究发现波动性增强探索，而随机性抑制探索。CAUSE算法在处理异质噪声结构的环境中表现优于传统探索策略，且在非定常（restless）环境中比标准的Gittins指数策略更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了波动性和随机性在诱导探索行为上的本质不对称性，并提出CAUSE算法在异质噪声环境中优于传统探索策略。同时，该框架为理解由于噪声推断机制异常导致的探索行为偏差（如精神病理学表现）提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [267] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过分析LLM在异构环境下的卸载与抢占行为，揭示了性能降级的关键因素，并为下一代多模型LLM调度器提供了设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM部署环境常需在共享的异构硬件上同时运行多个架构、规模各异的模型，现有的单模型吞吐量优化方案无法解决资源受限下的多模型调度挑战。

Method: 通过实验研究（Empirical Study）分析不同LLM在不同硬件平台下的行为，重点考察层级卸载（layer offloading）和抢占（preemption）对性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现：卸载会导致解码吞吐量出现强非线性且与模型相关的性能下降（小模型更敏感）；抢占带来的开销主要源于模型状态重载而非KV缓存传输，且该成本具有显著的平台和模型依赖性；序列长度和互连带宽会加剧数据移动和执行效率低下的问题。

Conclusion: 针对异构GPU环境下的多模型服务，未来的调度器设计必须考虑模型特定的卸载敏感度、工作负载特性以及抢占与数据传输的实际代价，以实现高效的资源管理。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [268] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个基于LLM的五阶段调查研究框架，通过引入PMT知识图谱约束的A-TLM模型，在灾难背景下的数据插补与分析任务中展现出比传统统计方法更优的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究目前面临响应率下降、样本偏差、缺失数据及AI欺诈回答等结构性挑战，现有研究缺乏对LLM在全调查流程中、特别是高风险灾难语境下的严谨评估。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据插补及分析的五阶段LLM集成框架。引入了PMT约束下的共现知识图谱，开发了包括零样本推理、RAG及创新的Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM)等七种配置，并通过2024年飓风米尔顿佛罗里达州受访者数据进行了实测验证。

Result: A-TLM模型在灾难相关块状MNAR条件下实现了最低的RMSE（1.439）和近乎零的偏差（-0.121）；相较于标准RAG，基于PMT因果结构的检索显著提升了准确性（MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097）；证明了通过约束检索架构可有效管理模型幻觉。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过结合保护动机理论（PMT）的知识图谱和结构化检索（A-TLM模型），可以将大语言模型有效且可靠地应用于调查研究全流程。特别是在灾难背景下的数据不完整（MNAR）处理中，该方法优于传统的插补基准，并可通过分层审计解决潜在的偏差掩盖问题。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [269] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果分析揭示了MLLM中产生模态冲突幻觉的机制是注意力头的不平衡分布，并据此提出MACI干预手段，成功在推理时有效抑制了幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在文本假设与视觉证据冲突时常产生“模态冲突幻觉”，目前尚不清楚该现象背后的模型内部机理（哪些组件导致或抵抗幻觉）。

Method: 使用基于路径修补（path patching）的头级因果分析技术，量化分析MLLM内部注意力头的因果作用；提出MACI（模态冲突感知因果干预）策略，在检测到冲突时动态抑制驱动头。

Result: 研究发现“幻觉驱动头”作用分布广泛且权重叠加占优，“幻觉抵抗头”高度集中且数量稀少。MACI方法在MMMC基准测试中显著降低了幻觉，表现优于现有的推理时基线，且具备零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: MLLMs中存在“模态冲突”导致幻觉的机制，即“幻觉驱动头”与“幻觉抵抗头”之间的不平衡路由结构。通过MACI进行条件干预，能在不显著影响模型原语义能力的前提下，有效抑制冲突引发的幻觉。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [270] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AquaUI是一种无需额外训练的GUI智能体令牌压缩方案，通过适应性四叉树利用GUI画面的空间冗余，并在多步交互中利用条件策略保持时间一致性，在提升效率的同时兼顾了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LMM的GUI智能体在处理高分辨率截图时面临挑战，因为GUI界面具有极高空间冗余（大部分区域信息极少，而关键组件需要高保真），而现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么忽视了GUI固有的结构化布局和空间特性。

Method: 提出了一种训练无关（training-free）的推理时令牌缩减方法：1. 适应性四叉树（Adaptive Quadtree）：根据像素信息密度动态划分截图，并为每个叶节点保留一个代表性令牌，同时保持位置编码一致性；2. 条件四叉树（Conditional Quadtree）：利用多步交互中的时间连续性，通过参考上一帧结构来优化当前帧的划分，以保持跨帧局部信息的稳定性。

Result: 在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct模型上的实验表明，AquaUI在保持99.06%原始性能的前提下，实现了最高13.22%的速度提升及29.52%的视觉令牌缩减。

Conclusion: AquaUI 证明了GUI截图中的空间冗余可以在无需重新训练的情况下，通过非均匀信息密度的处理策略在推理阶段得到有效利用，在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [271] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过计算社会选择理论和 Penrose-Banzhaf 指数分析了 PoS 区块链权益加权治理的权力结构，揭示了权力与权益分配之间存在难以彻底消除的扭曲，并量化了真实治理环境下的失衡状况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨权益证明（PoS）区块链中“基于权益加权投票”的治理范式所带来的权力扭曲问题，特别是少数大户控制决策而非拥有全部权益时的治理公平性问题。

Method: 采用计算社会选择理论（Computational Social Choice），利用 Penrose-Banzhaf 权力指数定量评估权力分配；结合数学分析与 Project Catalyst 的真实链上治理数据进行实证研究。

Result: 研究发现，权益加权投票机制难以完全避免权力失衡；通过对 Project Catalyst 数据的分析，量化了当前治理系统中存在的具体权力偏差程度。

Conclusion: 在权益加权投票机制中，权力与权益份额之间存在本质上的不对称，且很难实现完美对齐。不过，在特定条件下，这种不对称性在期望意义上是可以被近似平衡的。实际治理数据表明，现有的权益加权系统存在明显的权力失衡。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [272] [Agentic Trading: When LLM Agents Meet Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19337)
*Yihan Xia,Panpan You,Taotao Wang,Fang Liu,Han Qi,Xiaoxiao Wu,Shengli Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对77项LLM交易智能体研究的审计发现，该领域虽架构演进迅速，但因缺乏标准化评估协议、成本模型及可复现机制，导致研究成果难以横向比较，严重制约了领域成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在审视将大语言模型（LLM）作为交易智能体（感知、决策、执行、反馈）的研究现状，识别当前领域在评估协议与实证研究上的规范性问题。

Method: 该研究采用系统性综述方法，通过制定协议对截止至2026年3月9日的77项研究进行筛选与编码。重点针对含有“行动输出”与“闭环评估”的19项核心实证研究进行了审计与可复现性评估。

Result: 研究发现严重的“协议不可比性”：19项核心研究中，仅2项具备时间一致性分裂协议，仅1项具备交易成本模型，无人达到R3级可复现性。大多数研究在执行时间、幸存者偏差处理等方面存在严重缺失。

Conclusion: LLM 交易智能体领域虽架构实验活跃，但缺乏统一的评估协议、执行语义及可复现性（Reproducibility），这些是当前制约该领域发展的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: A growing body of work explores how Large Language Models (LLMs) can be embedded in trading systems as agents that perceive market information, retrieve context, reason about decisions, emit tradable actions, and adapt under market feedback. This paper reframes LLM-based trading agents as expert-system decision pipelines and presents an audit-oriented evidence map of 77 included studies in a protocol-coded snapshot screened through 2026-03-09. A primary empirical subset (n=19) satisfies the minimum boundary of Action Output plus Closed-Loop Evaluation; the remaining 58 included studies are retained as background and design context. The central empirical finding is protocol incomparability: within the primary subset, only 2/19 studies report extractable time-consistent split protocols, 1/19 reports an explicit transaction-cost model, 1/19 documents universe or survivorship handling, 11/19 report execution timing or semantics, 15/19 are coded as R0, and no study reaches R3 reproducibility. We therefore use Architecture-Capability-Adaptation as a working analytical lens rather than a validated taxonomy, and we foreground the evidence ledger, reproducibility audit, and reporting checklist as the main contributions. The resulting survey shows that architectural experimentation is expanding rapidly, while comparable evaluation protocols, execution semantics, and reproducible artifacts remain the field's immediate bottlenecks.

</details>


### [273] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRAM 是一种全新的概率化递归推理框架，通过引入随机潜在轨迹，实现了推理任务的多假设探索与生成，在复杂推理与约束满足任务中均优于传统确定性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，只能收敛到单一预测，难以处理需要多假设、多种解决方案或生成式任务的复杂人工智能问题。

Method: 提出了生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将递归潜在推理建模为随机轨迹。采用摊销变分推断（Amortized Variational Inference）进行训练，支持通过递归深度和多轨迹采样进行推理时扩展。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务中，GRAM 表现优于确定性递归和循环基线模型，并成功展示了在无条件生成任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRAM 通过将递归推理概率化，实现了比确定性递归模型更强的跨任务泛化能力、多假设推理能力以及生成能力，证明了随机潜在轨迹在提升推理深度和灵活性方面的优势。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [274] [What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19447)
*Xiaozhe Li,Tianyi Lyu,Yang Li,Yichuan Ma,Peiji Li,Linyang Li,Qipeng Guo,Dahua Lin,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SERL框架，通过结合任务奖励与精细化的环境反馈，解决了LLM智能体在长程复杂任务中的信用分配问题，在大规模测试中展现了卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大模型（LLM）智能体在稀疏任务奖励下的长程信用分配难题（Long-horizon credit assignment），克服了现有方法过度依赖轨迹级奖励或低效辅助信号的局限性。

Method: 提出SERL（Selective Environment-reweighted Learning）框架。该方法以任务奖励确定更新方向，同时利用五种不同反馈源（如错误信息、页面变化等）和两种插入粒度，通过环境反馈动态调整更新的权重与位置，从而精准强化关键动作。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop数据集上分别达到了90.0%和80.1%的成功率，在性能上超越了现有的强化学习与蒸馏基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过选择性地利用任务奖励与环境反馈，SERL能够有效地进行长程信用分配，证明了在关键决策点引入与动作相关反馈的必要性，显著优于传统的奖励机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train LLM agents from sparse task rewards, but long-horizon credit assignment remains challenging: a single success-or-failure signal must be distributed across many actions. Existing methods rely on trajectory-level rewards or proxy signals, without fully leveraging per-step environmental feedback. Multi-turn agent settings are underexplored, where feedback can include error messages, page changes, observations, or reference trajectories. We systematically study five feedback sources and two insertion granularities and introduce SERL, a selective environment-reweighted learning framework. SERL uses the task reward to determine update direction, while environment feedback adjusts placement and magnitude, focusing on critical actions. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SERL achieves 90.0% and 80.1% success, outperforming strong RL and distillation baselines. Analysis shows that grounded, action-relevant feedback at meaningful points consistently outperforms indiscriminate use of longer or richer context.

</details>


### [275] [Minimax Optimal Variance-Aware Regret Bounds for Multinomial Logistic MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19768)
*Pierre Boudart,Pierre Gaillard,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对MNL混合MDP的强化学习算法，通过引入问题相关方差常数 $\bar{\sigma}_T$，实现了更精细的 $\tilde{O}(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾界，并在理论上证明了其极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MNL混合MDP算法遗憾界为 $\tilde{O}(dH^2\sqrt{T})$，未能针对结构化MDP场景进行优化。研究旨在引入问题相关参数，改善结构化MDP下的遗憾界并寻求极小极大最优性。

Method: 借鉴逻辑斯谛强盗（logistic bandit）文献，引入了表征最优下游价值函数归一化平均方差的问题相关常数 $\bar{\sigma}_T \leq 1/2$。基于此设计了新的强化学习算法，实现了 $\tilde{O}(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$ 的遗憾界。

Result: 算法在最坏情况下能恢复现有最优边界，在结构化MDP（如KL约束的鲁棒MDP）中通过将视界依赖降低 $H$ 倍，显著提升了性能；同时给出了与上界匹配的下界。

Conclusion: 本文首次推导出了MNL混合MDP的遗憾下界 $\Omega(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$，证明了所提算法在对数因子内是极小极大最优的，并完整刻画了该类MDP的遗憾复杂度。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning for episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) whose transitions are modelled by a multinomial logistic (MNL) model. Existing algorithms for MNL mixture MDPs yield a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\sqrt{T})}$ (Li et al., 2024), where $d$ is the feature dimension, $H$ the episode length, and $T$ the number of episodes. Inspired by the logistic bandit literature (Abeille et al., 2021; Faury et al., 2022; Boudart et al., 2026), we introduce a problem-dependent constant $\barσ\_T \leq 1/2$, measuring the normalised average variance of the optimal downstream value function along the learner's trajectory. We propose an algorithm achieving a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$, which recovers the existing bound in the worst case and improves upon it for structured MDPs. For instance, for KL-constrained robust MDPs, $\barσ\_T = O(H^{-1})$, reducing the horizon dependence by a factor $H$. We further establish a matching $\smash{Ω(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$ lower bound, proving minimax optimality (up to logarithmic factors) and fully characterising the regret complexity of MNL mixture MDPs for the first time.

</details>


### [276] [Generative Auto-Bidding with Unified Modeling and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19457)
*Mingming Zhang,Feiqing Zhuang,Na Li,Shengjie Sun,Xiaowei Chen,Junxiong Zhu,Fei Xiao,Keping Yang,Lixin Zou,Chenliang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自动出价中探索与安全难以平衡的难题，本文提出了GUIDE框架，通过决策Transformer、Q值引导和逆动力学回退机制，实现了性能提升与风险控制的统一，并在真实广告平台场景中取得了显著的业务指标增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期的规则与强化学习方法在处理长短期奖励依赖、平衡探索与安全方面存在不足。现有生成式出价模型缺乏显式的安全撤退机制，导致探索过程低效且存在财务超支风险。

Method: 提出GUIDE框架（Generative Auto-Bidding with Unified Modeling and Exploration），核心包括：（1）采用决策Transformer（DT）建模历史行为与状态转移；（2）引入Q值模块进行正则化引导探索；（3）利用逆动力学模块（IDM）构建安全回退策略；（4）通过“探索-防护-选择”流水线动态平衡效率与合规性。

Result: 在公共数据集与Taobao真实场景测试中表现优于SOTA。在线部署效果显著：广告GMV提升4.10%，点击量提升1.40%，成本提升1.66%，广告投资回报率（ROI）提升3.52%。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过融合生成式建模、策略引导与安全回退机制，解决了自动出价中探索效率与财务风险的博弈难题。大规模在线部署验证了该方法在提升广告GMV、点击率及投资回报率方面的优越性与工业落地价值。

Abstract: Automated bidding is central to modern digital advertising. Early rule-based methods lacked adaptability, while subsequent Reinforcement Learning approaches modeled bidding as a Markov Decision Process but struggled with long-term dependencies. Recent generative models show promise, yet they lack explicit mechanisms to balance exploration and safety, relying solely on action perturbations or trajectory guidance without a safety fallback. This results in inefficient exploration and elevated financial risk for advertising platforms.
  To address this gap, we propose GUIDE (Generative Auto-Bidding with Unified Modeling and Exploration), a framework that synergistically integrates directed exploration with a safe fallback mechanism. GUIDE employs a Decision Transformer (DT) to jointly model historical bidding actions and environmental state transitions. A Q-value module guides the DT's exploration via regularization constraints, while an Inverse Dynamics Module (IDM) leverages DT-predicted future states to infer robust, behaviorally consistent actions as a safe policy fallback. The Q-value module then adaptively selects the final action between these two options, balancing exploration and safety. Together, these components form an integrated "explore-safeguard-select" pipeline that unifies efficiency and safety.
  We conduct extensive experiments on public datasets, in simulated auction environments, and through large-scale online deployment on Taobao, a leading Chinese advertising platform. Results show GUIDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scenarios. In real-world deployment, GUIDE achieves notable gains: +4.10% ad GMV, +1.40% ad clicks, +1.66% ad cost, and +3.52% ad ROI, demonstrating its effectiveness and strong industrial applicability.

</details>


### [277] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对在线强化学习中常见的模式崩溃问题，提出了DMPO算法，通过从反向KL转向前向KL分布匹配，有效保持了策略的多样性，在组合优化和复杂推理任务中取得了明显的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线强化学习方法（如GRPO）基于反向KL最小化，具有显著的模式寻求（mode-seeking）倾向，导致训练中出现模式崩溃，限制了策略的多样性与探索能力。

Method: 提出了分布式匹配策略优化（DMPO），通过构建基于奖励加权的样本群组目标分布，并将策略分布与该目标对齐，以实现模式覆盖（mode-covering）。

Result: DMPO在文本和视觉驱动的NP-Bench基准测试中，分别取得了43.9%和43.1%的质量比（高于GRPO的40.1%和38.4%），并在数学推理及域外任务中表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: DMPO通过分布匹配实现了原则性的前向KL最小化，有效解决了策略梯度算法中的模式崩溃问题，显著增强了模型在NP-hard组合优化及复杂推理任务中的探索能力与泛化性能。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [278] [Attention-Guided Reward for Reinforcement Learning-based Jailbreak against Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19485)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Yuzhe Huang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型推理模型（LRM）的注意力模式与越狱风险之间的相关性，并提出一种结合强化学习与注意力引导的越狱新方法，显著提升了攻击的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）虽然推理能力强大，但其展示出的思维链（CoT）推理过程反而使其面临比传统LLM更高的越狱风险，缺乏对LRM特定推理机制安全性的深入研究。

Method: 1. 深入分析LRM的注意力机制，发现攻击者通过分散对恶意prompt的注意力、聚焦推理内容的注意力可提高成功率。2. 提出一种基于强化学习（RL）的越狱攻击方法，将注意力信号集成至奖励函数中。3. 引入多样的说服策略以扩充RL的动作空间。

Result: 在五个主流闭源和开源LRM上进行了实验，结果显示该方法在越狱攻击成功率（ASR）、攻击效率和迁移性方面均显著优于现有主流的越狱方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了大型推理模型（LRM）的推理过程存在特定的脆弱性，即其注意力机制与越狱攻击成功率高度相关。通过利用这种注意力模式并结合强化学习及多样化说服策略，研究者成功提升了越狱攻击的有效性，强调了在LRM安全防御中保护推理过程的重要性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex problems by generating structured, step-by-step reasoning content. However, exposing a model's internal reasoning process introduces additional safety risks; for example, recent studies show that LRMs are more vulnerable to jailbreak attacks than standard LLMs. In this paper, we investigate jailbreak attacks on LRMs and reveal that the attack success rate (ASR) is closely correlated with LRMs' attention patterns. Specifically, successful jailbreaks tend to assign lower attention to harmful tokens in the input prompt, while allocating higher attention to those tokens in the reasoning content. Motivated by this finding, we propose a novel jailbreak method for LRMs that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to enhance attack effectiveness, explicitly incorporating attention signals into the reward function design. In addition, we introduce diverse persuasion strategies to enrich the RL action space, which consistently improves the ASR. Extensive experiments on five open-source and closed-source LRMs across three benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves substantially higher ASR, outperforming existing approaches in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability.

</details>


### [279] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出目前的Transformer图灵完备性研究存在误区，明确区分了固定系统与可伸缩模型族的区别，强调了上下文管理机制对LLM实际计算能力的关键限制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对学术界和工业界普遍将“可伸缩模型族”中的图灵完备性结论误用于“固定系统”设定的现状，旨在纠正这一认知偏差，明确真实LLM的计算能力边界。

Method: 通过形式化定义“固定系统（fixed-system）”与“可伸缩模型族（scaling-family）”两种设定进行辨析，对比分析现有的图灵完备性证明及其适用性，并通过理论论证强调上下文管理对计算能力的决定性作用。

Result: 指出了现有研究在证明图灵完备性时的概念混淆；明确界定了实际LLM部署受限的计算能力范围，并证明了不同的上下文管理策略会导致显著的计算能力差异。

Conclusion: 现有关于Transformer图灵完备性的文献存在严重的认识误区：在“可伸缩模型族”设定下证明的图灵完备性不能等同于“固定系统”设定下的通用计算能力；上下文管理机制是决定真实LLM计算能力的核心组件。

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [280] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对知识图谱构建中架构映射的人工成本高问题，本文提出了 BLINKG 基准测试来量化评估 LLM 的自动化映射性能，实验结论指出 LLM 虽有潜力，但在复杂任务中仍需进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱构建面临繁重的手动映射工作，现有自动化方案（如 RML）仍需大量人工干预；尽管大语言模型（LLM）初显潜力，但目前仍缺乏评估其在异构数据源到本体映射性能的标准化框架。

Method: 提出了 BLINKG 基准测试框架，该框架包含基于真实业务案例、复杂度递增的一系列评估场景，并利用当前主流大语言模型进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，主流 LLM 在简单的映射任务中表现出色，但在复杂场景下仍存在性能局限，无法完全替代人类专家。

Conclusion: BLIN KG 为评估大模型在知识图谱构建中的映射能力提供了基准，通过实验揭示了大模型在复杂场景下的现有局限，并为实现（半）自动化知识图谱构建设定了研究要求。

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [281] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对成对比较在分析投票分歧结构上的局限性，本文引入“多数矩阵”框架，证明了多数投票分歧度量需要至少3个选项的比较才能准确定义，并设计了平衡认知负荷的偏好采集协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好调查方法主要分为成对比较和全排序。成对比较简单但无法区分结构性分歧与噪声；而全排序认知负荷过重。本文旨在寻找一种既能识别结构性分歧，又在认知负荷与所需信息间达成平衡的最小化偏好表达方式。

Method: 1. 构建分层框架（stratified framework），基于“多数矩阵”（plurality matrix）记录每个子集S中各选项排名第一的概率。2. 定义度量水平（level）：即表达该度量所需的最小子集大小。3. 设计两种采集协议（elicitation protocols）用以估计多数矩阵，权衡参与者数量与认知负荷。

Result: 确定了多数分歧度量处于“3-水平”；提出了“多数矩阵”作为一种比成对比较更高级的结构化数据表示方法，并证明了其在分析复杂分歧机制中的有效性及可行性。

Conclusion: 现有的成对比较（2-水平）不足以捕捉广泛的分歧度量，研究证明许多度量（如排序方差和分歧性）必须达到3-水平及以上。通过调查研究，超越3-水平在理论和实验上均具有重要价值。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$. We define the level of a disagreement measure as the smallest subset size needed to express it, showing that many existing notions, including rank-variance and divisiveness, sit at level $3$, proving that pairwise comparisons are not enough. In addition, we demonstrate the interest of going beyond level $3$ both theoretically and experimentally. To make these results actionable, we design two elicitation protocols to estimate the plurality matrix, exploring the trade-off between the number of required participants and the cognitive load requested to each of them.

</details>


### [282] [Generative-Evaluative Agreement: A Necessary Validity Criterion for LLM-Enabled Adaptive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19529)
*Grandee Lee,Yue Wang,Che Yee Lye,Luke Peh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出GEA指标衡量LLM自我评估的有效性，发现模型在处理复杂设计技能时表现欠佳，并建议通过细分技能评分量表来提高评估的准确性和客观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前LLM在生成试题、模拟学生回答并进行评分的“闭环”中存在严重的自指验证问题（self-referential validation），需要量化其一致性与有效性。

Method: 引入生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为评估指标，通过在大规模语言模型生成的两阶段自适应评估中，测量评分函数是否能还原生成函数预设的学生能力水平。

Result: GEA测量结果显示，模型仅能恢复约一半的预期方差 (r = 0.698)，且存在系统性的正向偏差；语法验证类技能GEA较高 (r > 0.7)，而设计类技能几乎为零，且低技能被高估现象会干扰自适应评估的选择。

Conclusion: LLM在进行自我评估时的有效性受到限制，尤其在抽象设计类技能上。通过使用细粒度、基于技能分解的评分量表（rubrics）是提升生成与其评估一致性（GEA）的主要手段。

Abstract: When the same LLM generates assessment items, simulates student responses, and scores them, the validation loop is self-referential. We introduce Generative-Evaluative Agreement (GEA), a validity criterion measuring whether an LLM's scoring function recovers the skill levels its generative function was instructed to produce. In the first direct measurement of GEA on a two-stage adaptive assessment, the model recovers roughly half the intended variance r = 0.698 with systematic positive bias. GEA is strong r > 0.7 for syntactically verifiable skills but near zero for design-level skills, and low-skill overestimation inflates scores near the routing threshold. We argue that granular, skill-decomposed rubrics are the principal proposed mechanism for strengthening GEA and outline complementary mitigations.

</details>


### [283] [Library Drift: Diagnosing and Fixing a Silent Failure Mode in Self-Evolving LLM Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19576)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自演化技能库因过度累积导致性能停滞的“库漂移”问题，本文提出了诊断日志系统及一套治理“配方”，经实验证实可显著提升智能体任务解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化技能库存在“库漂移”（Library Drift）失效现象，即无限制的技能累积导致检索质量下降、错误正例注入及性能停滞，且目前缺乏对该机制的深入理解和有效管理。

Method: 1. 触发机制：通过消融实验隔离漂移诱因；2. 诊断工具：开发追踪级诊断系统（证据日志、技能贡献度评分、路由参与指标）；3. 治理方案：提出一套最小化治理策略（基于结果的退休机制、有界容量上限、元技能优先级）。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100基准测试中，通过提出的治理策略，将模型保持集的pass@1从0.258提升至0.584（增长0.328）。同时，通过八项消融实验明确了各治理组件的有效贡献。

Conclusion: 库漂移现象严重制约了自演化技能库的性能。通过实施基于结果导向的治理策略（包含显式的退休机制、容量限制和元技能构建优先级），可以有效解决数据退化和检索冗余问题，显著提升智能体在复杂任务（如MBPP+）上的性能表现。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries face a silent failure mode we term \emph{library drift}: unbounded skill accumulation without outcome-driven lifecycle management causes retrieval degradation, false-positive injections, and performance stagnation. Recent evaluation confirms the symptom--LLM-authored skills deliver +0.0pp gain while human-curated ones deliver +16.2pp (SkillsBench)--yet the underlying mechanism has not been isolated. We provide (1) a reproducible trigger: ablations that isolate drift--one disables skill injection (flat floor, +0.002), one imposes premature retirement (active harm, $-$0.019); (2) trace-level diagnostics: an append-only evidence log with per-skill contribution scores, attribution verdicts, and router engagement metrics that make the failure visible before it reaches end-task scores; and (3) a verified fix: a minimal governance recipe (outcome-driven retirement + bounded active-cap + meta-skill authoring prior) that lifts held-out pass@1 from a 0.258 baseline to a late-window mean of 0.584 (rolling gain $+$0.328) on MBPP+ hard-100 over 100 rounds. Eight ablations decompose which governance mechanisms are load-bearing and which are subsumed, providing a concrete playbook for diagnosing library drift in any self-evolving agent.

</details>


### [284] [EMO-BOOST: Emotion-Augmented Audio-Visual Features for Improved Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19630)
*Aritra Marik,Marcel Klemt,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决深度伪造检测泛化性差的问题，本文提出了Emo-Boost框架，通过引入情绪这一高层语义线索，结合底层的音视频检测技术，显著提升了模型对未知伪造类型的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI快速迭代使得针对每种特定伪造类型收集数据并训练检测器变得不切实际，因此提升深度伪造检测器对未知伪造技术的泛化能力至关重要。作者提出利用高层语义线索（如情绪）来辅助底层特征检测。

Method: 提出Emo-Boost框架，将现有的RGB/声学底层伪造检测器与自研的EmoForensics检测器融合；EmoForensics通过视听情绪识别模块，对音视频流中的情绪表征建模，分析其帧内及跨模态的时间一致性。

Result: 在FakeAVCeleb数据集上，结合了底层检测与情绪检测的Emo-Boost框架，使跨伪造类型的泛化性能（AUC）平均提升了2.1%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，情绪等高层语义线索与底层的取证检测器具有互补性，引入EmoForensics框架能够有效提升深度伪造检测器在处理未知伪造类型时的泛化能力。

Abstract: With every advancement in generative AI models, forensics is under increasing pressure. The constant emergence of new generation techniques makes it impossible to collect data for each manipulation to train a deepfake detection model. Thus, generalizing to deepfakes unseen during training is one of the major challenges in current deepfake detection research. To tackle this challenge, we employ high-level semantic cues and argue that these cues can support low-level focused approaches in generalizing to unseen types of manipulations. In this work, we study emotions as a high-level semantic cue. We propose Emo-Boost, a multimodal deepfake detection framework that fuses an off-the-shelf RGB- and acoustic-focused deepfake detector with our emotion-based deepfake detector EmoForensics. EmoForensics utilises vision and audio emotion recognition modules and models intra- and inter-modal temporal consistency in emotion representations from an audio-visual stream. We found that EmoForensics and the low-level focused method capture complementary signals. Consequently, combining both signals in EmoBoost enhances the average cross-manipulation generalization AUC by 2.1% on FakeAVCeleb.

</details>


### [285] [Pseudocode-Guided Structured Reasoning for Automating Reliable Inference in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19663)
*Weicong Ni,Tianbao Jiang,Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对VLM在机器人自动化应用中的幻觉难题，该论文提出了PStar框架，通过结构化伪代码和任务难度感知机制，实现了更具鲁棒性和可解释性的分步推理，有效提升了决策的准确性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在机器人自动化中应用广泛，但其存在的“幻觉”现象会导致决策失效，在现实物理部署中面临严峻的安全与可靠性挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为 PStar（Pseudocode-guided Structured Reasoning）的框架，包含：1. 设计一组抽象推理函数和结构化伪代码库以实现模块化推理；2. 设计“难度特征向量”（DFV）来评估任务复杂度并自适应选择推理策略。

Result: PStar 在 POPE 测试集上达到 87.1% 的得分，在 MMStar 测试集上达到 68.0%，表现优于 GPT-4V，显著降低了幻觉率。

Conclusion: PStar 提供了一种通过结构化伪代码引导和自适应难度评估来减少VLM幻觉的有效方案，对于提升机器人自动化任务的可靠性和确定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are becoming the cornerstone of high-level reasoning for robotic automation, enabling robots to parse natural language commands and perceive their environments. However, their susceptibility to hallucinations introduces critical failures in decision-making, posing significant safety and reliability risks in physical deployments. This challenge is exacerbated by the open-ended nature of real-world tasks, where questions vary vastly in difficulty and modality, demanding robust and adaptable reasoning strategies. To tackle this, we propose the Pseudocode-guided Structured Reasoning framework (PStar), which adaptively selects structured pseudocode reasoning paths to help VLMs perform flexible and step-by-step reasoning. We first design a set of abstract reasoning functions and formulate a structured pseudocode library to represent modular reasoning strategies. Crucially, we design a Difficulty Feature Vector (DFV) that allows the model to assess question complexity and adaptively choose appropriate reasoning strategies-enhancing robustness and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PStar significantly reduces hallucination rates, achieving state-of-the-art scores of 87.1% on POPE and 68.0% on MMStar, outperforming even GPT-4V. By providing a validated mechanism to reduce visual-language errors, PStar offers a critical step toward deploying more trustworthy and deterministic VLMs for real-world automated systems, where such errors can lead to catastrophic outcomes.

</details>


### [286] [Transforming Constraint Programs to Input for Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19671)
*Jo Devriendt,Patrick De Causmaecker,Marc Denecker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用约束优化问题对称性自动生成局部搜索邻域的方法，减少了现有方法对复杂人工定义的依赖，并在多种经典问题中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部搜索算法在组合优化中的应用通常需要繁琐的人工介入以适配算法输入，为了降低开发复杂度，需要一种自动化的邻域生成方法。

Method: 建立约束优化问题的对称性与局部搜索邻域之间的联系，并在IDP系统框架内实现从约束规范中自动生成邻域。

Result: 在六个经典优化问题上进行了评估，实验结果证明了该自动生成技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用约束优化问题的对称性，可以自动生成高效的局部搜索邻域，从而减少人工介入，验证了该方法在多种经典优化问题上的有效性和可行性。

Abstract: Applying local search algorithms to combinatorial optimization problems is not an easy feat. Typically, human intervention is required to compile the constraints to input data for some metaheuristic algorithm. In this paper, we establish a link between symmetry properties of constraint optimization problems and local search neighborhoods, and we use this link to automatically generate neighborhoods from a constraint specification in the context of the IDP system. We evaluate the obtained neighborhoods for six classical optimization problems. The resulting observations support the viability of this technique.

</details>


### [287] [Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent for CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19748)
*Yin Xiaolong,Liu Yu,Shen Jiahang,Lu Xingyu,Ni Jingzhe,Fan Fengxiao,Sang Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于复杂CAD模型生成的记忆增强强化学习框架，通过引入闭环纠错机制和动态记忆管理，解决了大模型在处理复杂几何约束时的推理失效问题，实现了高效、稳定的自动建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的CAD生成方法在处理长序列、复杂几何约束任务时，常因推理链断裂和缺乏有效纠错机制而效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种记忆增强强化学习框架，包含由几何内核封装的结构化工具链，闭环系统（设计意图理解、全局规划、执行、多维验证），以及双轨记忆模块（案例库+技能库）和动态效用检索算法。利用强化学习进行检索与策略优化以避免检索陷阱。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提升了复杂CAD模型生成任务中的成功率与几何一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的记忆增强强化学习框架有效解决了CAD生成中长序列推理断裂和几何约束难以满足的问题，通过自我修正机制实现了无需大规模标注数据的持续进化。

Abstract: Automatic generation of computer-aided design (CAD) models is a core technology for enabling intelligence in advanced manufacturing. Existing generation methods based on large language models (LLMs) often fall short when handling complex CAD models characterized by long operation sequences, diverse operation types, and strong geometric constraints, primarily because reasoning chains break and effective error-correction mechanisms are lacking. To address this problem, this paper proposes a memory-augmented reinforcement learning framework for CAD generation agents. The framework encapsulates the underlying geometric kernel into a structured toolchain callable by the agent and builds a closed-loop mechanism of design intent understanding, global planning, execution, and multi-dimensional verification. It also designs a dual-track memory module consisting of a case library and a skill library, and proposes a dynamic utility retrieval algorithm. By introducing reinforcement learning into retrieval and policy optimization, the agent can effectively avoid retrieval traps in which examples are semantically similar but geometrically infeasible, enabling online self-correction and continual evolution without additional large-scale annotated data. Experiments show that the proposed method significantly improves both the success rate and geometric consistency on complex CAD model generation tasks.

</details>


### [288] [What Really Improves Mathematical Reasoning: Structured Reasoning Signals Beyond Pure Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19762)
*Yuze Zhao,Junpeng Fang,Lu Yu,Zhenya Huang,Kai Zhang,Qing Cui,Qi Liu,Jun Zhou,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 代码并非通用的推理催化剂；模型的推理能力提升更多源于结构化思维链（如数学逻辑），而非单纯的代码执行；通过精准的数据配比（例如提高结构化数学样本密度），可以实现任务间性能的协调与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型训练普遍包含代码数据，但代码对于程序设计以外的通用推理能力的实际贡献并不明确，且相关领域存在关于代码对推理提升效果的争议。

Method: 在10T token语料库上进行细粒度领域拆分的受控预训练实验；通过“路径分析”（routing analyses）评估模型在不同数据组合下的专家激活模式，从而从机制层面解析数据间的交互作用。

Result: 1. 仅包含可执行代码时，代码主要提升编程能力，对通用推理无明显增益，且与知识密集型任务（尤其是复杂数学推理）存在竞争关系；2. 推理能力的提升主要归功于代码-文本、数学-文本等混合类结构化推理轨迹；3. 增加结构化数学样本密度能有效提升数学推理性能，且不损害编程能力。

Conclusion: 代码并非普适的“推理增强剂”。研究表明，推理能力的提升更多源于特定领域（如数学）的结构化思维链（CoT），而非单纯的可执行代码。通过策略性地优化数据组合（增加特定领域的结构化样本密度），可以在保持编程能力的同时显著提升数学推理能力。

Abstract: Code has become a standard component of modern foundation language model (LM) training, yet its role beyond programming remains unclear. We revisit the claim that code improves reasoning through controlled pretraining experiments on a 10T-token corpus with fine-grained domain separation. Our findings are threefold. First, when code is restricted to standalone executable programs and Code-NL data are controlled for, code substantially improves programming ability but does not act as a general reasoning enhancer; instead, it competes with knowledge-intensive tasks, especially complex mathematical reasoning. Second, the reasoning gains often attributed to code are better explained by cross-domain structured reasoning traces, such as code-text and math-text mixtures, rather than by executable code alone. Third, increasing the density of structured math-domain samples within a fixed math budget yields substantial gains on difficult mathematical reasoning while largely preserving programming performance, suggesting that cognitive scaffolds offer a targeted way to mitigate cross-domain trade-offs.
