Finally, routing analyses show that data-composition effects are reflected in expert-activation patterns, providing mechanism-level evidence for competitive and synergistic interactions across domains. Our results clarify which data characteristics transfer across capability dimensions and point to more precise data-centric optimization strategies.

</details>


### [289] [GroupAffect-4: A Multimodal Dataset of Four-Person Collaborative Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19765)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Alice Modica,Anna Obara,Shan Ahmed Shaffi,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Tanya Ignatenko,Ted Vucurevich,Karim Haddad,Daniel Barratt,Daniel Overholt,Jesper Bunsow Boldt,Paolo Burelli,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GroupAffect-4 是一个开放获取的多模态语料库，旨在支持协同场景下多尺度（个体、人际、群体）情感计算研究；它整合了生理、眼动、音频等多维度信号，并提供了完整的基准测试脚本与数据集规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的情感计算与社会信号处理语料库在分析协同办公或共同活动场景时，往往缺乏对“个体-人际-群体”这一耦合过程的全面覆盖，且所需的生理、行为和心理信号通常分散在不同的数据集传统中，难以进行整合研究。

Method: 构建了包含 40 名参与者（10 个四人小组）的多模态语料库。收集了生理信号（腕带）、眼动追踪、音频记录、自我报告、性格测试及任务绩效。所有数据在时间轴上对齐，并采用 BIDS 启发式结构进行归档，同时定义了涵盖个体状态、人际特质及群体动力学三个维度的 15 个基准分析任务。

Result: 数据集数据完整度高（生理数据覆盖率 >91%，眼动数据 >98%），且经过情感操纵检测证明具有良好的任务效度。通过留一小组评估法（leave-one-group-out）确立了 15 项基准测试任务的可行性。

Conclusion: GroupAffect-4 通过提供高质量、多层级的协同情感交互数据，弥补了现有数据集在个体、人际与群体情感分析上的断层，为情感计算领域提供了标准化的基准数据集。

Abstract: Existing affective-computing, social-signal-processing, and meeting corpora capture important parts of human interaction, but they rarely support analysis of affect in co-located groups as a coupled individual, interpersonal, and group-level process. The required signals (per-participant physiology, eye movement, audio, self-report, task outcomes, and personality) are usually fragmented across separate dataset traditions. We introduce GroupAffect-4, a multimodal corpus of 40 participants in 10 four-person groups, each completing four ecologically varied collaborative tasks spanning information pooling, negotiation, idea generation, and a public-goods game. Each participant is instrumented with a wrist-worn physiology sensor, eye-tracking glasses, and a close-talk microphone; sessions include continuous affect self-reports, post-task questionnaires, task outcomes, and Big-Five personality scores, all time-aligned to a shared clock. The dataset covers over 91% of expected physiology windows and 98% of eye-tracking windows, with strong task validity confirmed by a clear affective manipulation check across the negotiation block. We define fifteen benchmarkable targets spanning three analysis levels -- within-person state, between-person traits, and group dynamics -- and report leave-one-group-out feasibility baselines establishing the dataset's evaluative scope. GroupAffect-4 is released with a BIDS-inspired structure, Croissant metadata, a datasheet, per-session quality reports, and open processing scripts. Code and processing scripts are available at https://github.com/meisamjam/GroupAffect-4; the dataset is publicly archived at https://zenodo.org/records/20037847.

</details>


### [290] [OpenComputer: Verifiable Software Worlds for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19769)
*Jinbiao Wei,Qianran Ma,Yilun Zhao,Xiao Zhou,Kangqi Ni,Guo Gan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenComputer 是一个全新的计算 Agent 评估框架，通过引入可审计的状态验证器和自动化任务合成，解决了传统 LLM 评估方案在桌面自动化任务中鲁棒性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机 Agent 评估方法（如 LLM-as-judge）在需要细粒度应用状态检查的任务中往往不够准确，且缺乏可审计、可验证的自动化桌面场景评估框架。

Method: 提出了 OpenComputer 框架，包含四个模块：(1) 应用专用状态验证器；(2) 基于执行反馈的自进化验证层；(3) 任务生成流水线；(4) 记录轨迹及提供部分奖励计算的评估控制台。涵盖 33 款桌面应用及 1000 个可机检任务。

Result: 实验表明，在细粒度任务成功判定上，OpenComputer 的验证器比 LLM-as-judge 与人类判断更一致；高性能 Agent 在端到端任务上表现不佳，且开源模型在在该框架下的表现较原有基准（OSWorld-Verified）有明显下降，暴露了自动化的局限性。

Conclusion: OpenComputer 揭示了当前主流大模型在复杂桌面自动化任务上的鲁棒性差距，证明了基于客观验证的评估方法比 LLM-as-judge 更准确，并为构建可靠的计算机 Agent 评估基准提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present OpenComputer, a verifier-grounded framework for constructing verifiable software worlds for computer-use agents. OpenComputer integrates four components: (1) app-specific state verifiers that expose structured inspection endpoints over real applications, (2) a self-evolving verification layer that improves verifier reliability using execution-grounded feedback, (3) a task-generation pipeline that synthesizes realistic and machine-checkable desktop tasks, and (4) an evaluation harness that records full trajectories and computes auditable partial-credit rewards. In its current form, OpenComputer covers 33 desktop applications and 1,000 finalized tasks spanning browsers, office tools, creative software, development environments, file managers, and communication applications. Experiments show that OpenComputer's hard-coded verifiers align more closely with human adjudication than LLM-as-judge evaluation, especially when success depends on fine-grained application state. Frontier agents struggle with end-to-end completion despite partial progress, and open-source models exhibit sharp drops from their OSWorld-Verified scores, exposing a persistent gap in robust computer automation.

</details>


### [291] [Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Continuous AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19779)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将保形预测应用于连续AI智能体评估，实现了分布无关的覆盖保障，并解决了多阶段流水线、排序不确定性及大规模排行榜评估中的统计稳健性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决连续式AI智能体评估中，对于预测质量分数缺乏严格、分布无关的覆盖保证问题。

Method: 应用了分割保形预测（Split Conformal Prediction）与自适应保形推理（ACI）；开发了多智能体流水线的组合不确定性界限、用于成对排序的保形弃权规则，以及针对排行榜规模多重检验的FDR校正弃权机制。

Result: 保形区间在24小时跨度内标定误差低于0.02；ACI能有效处理智能体发布后的波动；实证显示跨源情感分歧与排序不稳定性相关（r=0.64），且框架能有效捕获超越现有基准的独立评估信号。

Conclusion: 该框架为连续式AI智能体评估提供了有效的统计保障，展示了保形预测在处理动态、多阶段评估任务中的鲁棒性与实用价值。

Abstract: We adapt split conformal prediction and adaptive conformal inference (ACI) to continuous AI agent evaluation, providing distribution-free coverage guarantees for forecasted quality scores. Conformal intervals achieve calibration error below 0.02 across all nominal levels at the 24h horizon, while ACI correctly widens intervals by 35% following agent releases then reconverges. We further develop compositional uncertainty bounds for multi-agent pipelines (validated via simulation across inter-stage correlations rho in [-0.5, 0.9]), a conformal abstention rule for pairwise rankings with controlled false-ranking rate, and FDR-corrected abstention for leaderboard-scale multiple testing. Evaluating 50 agents via 18 real-time signals collected hourly, we show that per-agent conditional coverage is well-concentrated around the nominal level (mean 80.4%, 90% of agents within [72%, 90%]), and that cross-source sentiment divergence predicts ranking instability (r=0.64, p<0.01). A circularity-controlled validation confirms the framework captures signal beyond benchmarks (rho_s=0.52, p<0.01, n=35). Code and data are released under CC BY 4.0.

</details>


### [292] [From Prompts to Pavement Through Time: Temporal Grounding in Agentic Scene-to-Plan Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19824)
*Ahmed Y. Gado,Omar Y. Goba,Alaa Hassanein,Catherine M. Elias,Ahmed Hussein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在自动驾驶多智能体通信中引入时间调节对推理一致性的影响，发现其虽未显著提高NLP指标，但提升了场景推理的预测能力和行为稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前自动驾驶领域使用LLM/LMM集合进行场景理解与规划时，普遍忽视时间维度，导致连续动作推理不一致，从而影响安全性和可解释性。

Method: 设计了三种具有不同时间集成力度的规划器架构，并在BDD-X数据集的精选子集上，通过语义、句法及逻辑度量进行评估。

Result: 时间调节虽然改变了推理风格，但在标准的NLP正确性度量上并未带来统计学意义上的显著提升；然而，在定性分析中观察到了预测性危险推理、稳定的纠正行为及战略性分歧。

Conclusion: 基于提示工程（prompt-based）的时间接地（temporal grounding）在提升量化指标方面存在局限性，但在提升自动驾驶场景下的预测性危险推理和行为稳定性方面具有显著的定性价值；本文确立了时间场景到规划推理（temporal scene-to-plan reasoning）的首个实证基准。

Abstract: Recent attempts to support high-level scene interpretation and planning in Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) using ensembles of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) continue to treat time as a secondary property. This lack of temporal grounding leads to inconsistencies in reasoning about continuous actions, undermining both safety and interpretability. This work explores whether temporal conditioning within inter-agent communication can preserve or enhance coherence without introducing degradation in semantic or logical consistency. To investigate this, we introduce three planner architectures with progressively increasing temporal integration and evaluate them on curated subsets of the BDD-X dataset using semantic, syntactic, and logical metrics. Results show that while temporal conditioning reshapes reasoning style, it yields no statistically significant improvements in standard NLP-based correctness metrics. However, qualitative analysis reveals predictive hazard reasoning, stable corrective behavior, and strategic divergence in the Sentinel. These findings clarify the limits of prompt-based temporal grounding and establish the first empirical benchmark for temporal scene-to-plan reasoning.

</details>


### [293] [Robotics-Inspired Guardrails for Foundation Models in Socially Sensitive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19940)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Drazen Brscic,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出一种名为“Grounded Observer”的新型防护框架，通过将交互轨迹视为闭环控制系统，而非单纯的输出过滤，从而在复杂的社会交互应用中提供了更具适应性和安全约束的实时监控与干预机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型防护技术主要侧重于单次输出的风险降低，无法解决社会敏感领域中交互路径及累积性、上下文相关的安全失效问题。

Method: 将基础模型的防护栏（Guardrails）建模为不确定性系统中的闭环行为控制问题，借鉴机器人学中的形式化约束强化方法，提出“Grounded Observer”框架，对交互轨迹进行实时监控与干预。

Result: 在小对话、居家自闭症治疗及学校行为降级干预三个实际部署场景中，该框架成功实现了对交互漂移的实时纠偏，并适应了多样化的社会语境。

Conclusion: 通过引入“带约束的行为控制”范式，研究证明了在大型模型交互中引入针对轨迹的闭环监控机制，能够有效提升复杂社交场景下的安全性与稳定性。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive domains such as education, mental health, and caregiving, where failures are often cumulative and context-dependent. Existing guardrail approaches -- ranging from training-time alignment to prompting, decoding constraints, and post-hoc moderation -- primarily provide empirical risk reduction rather than enforceable behavioral guarantees, and largely treat safety as a property of individual outputs rather than interaction trajectories. We reframe guardrails as a problem of runtime behavioral control over interaction trajectories, drawing on robotics to introduce formal constructs for constraint enforcement in uncertain, closed-loop systems. We instantiate these ideas in the Grounded Observer framework and apply it across three real-world deployments: small talk, in-home autism therapy, and behavioral de-escalation in schools. Across settings, the framework enables runtime interventions that mitigate drift into undesirable interaction regimes while adapting to diverse social contexts. We discuss extensions to the framework and propose research directions toward stronger guarantees.

</details>


### [294] [Probabilistic Tiny Recursive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19943)
*Amin Sghaier,Ali Parviz,Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PTRM为Tiny Recursive Models引入了随机化探索机制，通过注入噪声并行生成多条推理路径并筛选最优结果，实现了在极低计算成本下对复杂推理任务性能的显著突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tiny Recursive Models (TRM) 虽然参数量极小且推理高效，但其确定性递归机制容易导致推理陷入局部最优解且缺乏逃逸机制，限制了其在复杂逻辑任务中的性能上限。

Method: 提出Probabilistic TRM (PTRM)框架：在TRM的深度递归过程中注入高斯噪声，生成多条并行的推理轨迹以探索不同的解空间；利用模型原有的Q-head（价值头）对多条轨迹的结果进行评估与筛选（Best-of-N策略）。该方法具有任务无关性，无需重新训练或定制化数据增强。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中显著提升了准确率：Sudoku-Extreme任务从87.4%提升至98.75%；Pencil Puzzle Bench任务从62.6%提升至91.2%。在后者中，PTRM以7M参数的规模实现了前沿LLM（55.1%准确率）近两倍的精度，且推理成本仅为后者的0.0001倍。

Conclusion: PTRM通过引入随机探索机制，成功克服了Tiny Recursive Models（TRM）在确定性递归中陷入局部最优的缺陷。该框架无需重训练即可提升复杂推理任务的性能，并在极小的模型参数规模下展现出超越前沿大语言模型的推理精度，验证了测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）在小模型中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Tiny Recursive Models (TRM) solve complex reasoning tasks with a fraction of the parameters of modern large language models (LLMs) by iteratively refining a latent state and final answer. While powerful, their deterministic recursion can lead to convergence at suboptimal solutions, without escape mechanism. A common workaround relies on task-specific input perturbations at test time combined with answer aggregation via voting. We introduce Probabilistic TRM (PTRM), a task-agnostic framework for test-time compute scaling that addresses this limitation through stochastic exploration. PTRM injects Gaussian noise at each deep recursion step, enabling parallel trajectories to explore diverse solution basins, and selects among them using the model's existing Q head (used for early stopping in the original TRM). Without requiring retraining or task-specific augmentations, PTRM enables substantial accuracy gains across benchmarks, including Sudoku-Extreme (87.4% to 98.75%) and on various puzzles from Pencil Puzzle Bench (62.6% to 91.2%). On the latter, PTRM achieves nearly double the accuracy of frontier LLMs (91.2% vs. 55.1%) at less than 0.0001x the cost, using only 7M parameters.

</details>


### [295] [GeoX: Mastering Geospatial Reasoning Through Self-Play and Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20006)
*Kyeongjin Ahn,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoX 是一个无需大规模人工标注的自博弈框架，通过生成并执行空间逻辑程序进行强化学习，显著提升了多模态模型在地理空间推理任务上的能力，并配套发布了相关基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间推理需要处理复杂的场景空间结构，但获取大规模人工标注数据的成本极高，限制了该领域的发展。作者旨在寻找一种无需大规模标注数据也能获取空间推理能力的方法。

Method: 提出了名为 GeoX 的自博弈框架。该框架利用单一多模态策略，通过溯因（abduction）、演绎（deduction）和归纳（induction）三种推理模式，生成空间问题相关的可执行程序，并由验证器（verifier）执行程序以提供奖励信号，进而利用强化学习同步优化策略与任务解决能力。

Result: GeoX 将基础 VLM 的性能平均提升了高达 5.5 个百分点，其效果达到或超过了使用数百万条人工标注数据训练的基准模型。此外，项目还发布了一个通过自博弈生成的地理空间理解基准数据集。

Conclusion: GeoX 证明了通过自博弈（self-play）产生可执行程序并获得反馈奖励，可以有效提升多模态大模型在地理空间推理任务中的表现，且无需依赖大规模人工标注数据。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning requires solving image-grounded problems over the complex spatial structure of a scene. However, developing this capability is hindered by the cost of annotating a vast and combinatorial question space. We propose GeoX, a self-play framework that acquires spatial logic through executable programs that yield verifiable rewards, without relying on large-scale human-curated data Given a satellite or aerial image, our framework employs a single multimodal policy that proposes spatial problems as executable programs and solves them under three reasoning modes-abduction, deduction, and induction-over spatial primitives and an image understanding tool. A verifier executes each program to covert a reward signal that jointly optimizes the two roles via reinforcement learning. GeoX consistently improves its base VLMs by up to 5.5 points on average, matching or exceeding conventional baselines trained on millions of curated data. Along-side the proposed method, we release a benchmark for geospatial understanding accumulated through self-play.

</details>


### [296] [AutoResearchClaw: Self-Reinforcing Autonomous Research with Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20025)
*Jiaqi Liu,Shi Qiu,Mairui Li,Bingzhou Li,Haonian Ji,Siwei Han,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zihan Dong,Congyu Zhang,Letian Zhang,Guiming Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xinyu Yang,Lu Feng,Xujiang Zhao,Haifeng Chen,Jiawei Zhou,Xiao Wang,Weitong Zhang,Hongtu Zhu,Yun Li,Jieru Mei,Hongliang Fei,Jiaheng Zhang,Linjie Li,Linjun Zhang,Yuyin Zhou,Sheng Wang,Caiming Xiong,James Zou,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoResearchClaw 是一款通过多智能体辩论、自动纠错和灵活人机协作来提升科研自动化水平的系统，在实验中表现显著优于现有方案，旨在成为人类科研的有力辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科研自动化系统通常采用单智能体线性管线，缺乏迭代优化能力，且不具备跨任务学习经验或处理实验失败的能力，难以模仿真实的科研流程。

Method: 提出由五大核心机制驱动的多智能体自动化科研管线：1. 多智能体辩论（假设生成与分析）；2. 具备 Pivot/Refine 决策循环的自愈执行器（将失败转化为反馈）；3. 可验证的结果报告机制；4. 具备七种干预模式的人机协作接口；5. 跨运行的经验演进系统。

Result: 在 ARC-Bench 的 25 个主题实验评估中，AutoResearchClaw 的性能优于 AI Scientist v2 54.7%；实验证明，在关键高杠杆决策点进行针对性的人机协作，比全自动或完全人工监督更有效。

Conclusion: AutoResearchClaw 作为一个研究辅助工具，通过多智能体协作、故障自我纠正和人机协作机制，显著提升了科研自动化的效率与可靠性，强调了人工智能在科学研究中“增强”而非“替代”人类判断的定位。

Abstract: Automating scientific discovery requires more than generating papers from ideas. Real research is iterative: hypotheses are challenged from multiple perspectives, experiments fail and inform the next attempt, and lessons accumulate across cycles. Existing autonomous research systems often model this process as a linear pipeline: they rely on single-agent reasoning, stop when execution fails, and do not carry experience across runs. We present AutoResearchClaw, a multi-agent autonomous research pipeline built on five mechanisms: structured multi-agent debate for hypothesis generation and result analysis, a self-healing executor with a \textsc{Pivot}/\textsc{Refine} decision loop that transforms failures into information, verifiable result reporting that prevents fabricated numbers and hallucinated citations, human-in-the-loop collaboration with seven intervention modes spanning full autonomy to step-by-step oversight, and cross-run evolution that converts past mistakes into future safeguards. On ARC-Bench, a 25-topic experiment-stage benchmark, AutoResearchClaw outperforms AI Scientist v2 by 54.7%. A human-in-the-loop ablation across seven intervention modes reveals that precise, targeted collaboration at high-leverage decision points consistently outperforms both full autonomy and exhaustive step-by-step oversight. We position AutoResearchClaw as a research amplifier that augments rather than replaces human scientific judgment. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw.

</details>


### [297] [Neurosymbolic Learning for Inference-Time Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20098)
*Gabriel Freedman,Adam Dejl,Adam Gould,Mansi,Lihu Chen,Jianqi Jiang,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ITA的神经符号框架，将形式化论证语义集成至大语言模型中，实现了对声明的可信三元验证（真/假/不确定），确保了结论与其论证过程的忠实性与透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、金融等高风险领域，声明验证往往面临信息不全或冲突的情况，二元分类不足以覆盖复杂场景。此外，现有模型通常缺乏决策过程的忠实解释（Faithful explanation），难以保证预测与其理由的一致性。

Method: 提出“推理时论证（Inference-time Argumentation, ITA）”神经符号框架。该方法使用形式化论证语义作为归纳偏置，指导大语言模型（LLM）生成论证并赋予基础分值，通过论证语义计算最终的三元判决结果（True/False/Uncertain）。训练过程中，模型根据论证产生的预测质量进行优化。

Result: 在两个三元声明验证数据集上实验表明，ITA相比现有的论证方法表现更优，并能与非论证的直接预测基准竞争，同时提供了可解释、确定的论证结构。

Conclusion: ITA框架通过引入形式化论证语义，实现了在三元声明验证任务中预测结果与论证逻辑的内在一致性（Faithful），克服了传统模型事后解释（Post-hoc）可能存在的不可靠性，同时在性能上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Claim verification is an important problem in high-stakes settings, including health and finance. When information underpinning claims is incomplete or conflicting, uncertain answers may be more appropriate than binary true or false classifications. In all cases, faithful explanations of the considerations determining the final verdict are crucial. We introduce inference-time argumentation (ITA), a trainable neurosymbolic framework for ternary claim verification in which a formal argumentation semantics giving the strength of claims is used both (i) to guide LLM training as models learn to generate arguments and assign them base scores (representing intrinsic strengths) and (ii) to compute ternary (true/false/uncertain) predictions from generated, scored arguments. As a result, at training time, argument generation and scoring can be optimised according to the quality of the induced argumentative predictions. Moreover, at inference time, the final prediction is faithful, by construction, to the arguments and scores determining the verdict, rather than being justified by a potentially unfaithful post-hoc reasoning trace as in conventional reasoning models. We finally show that, on two datasets for ternary claim verification, ITA improves upon argumentative baselines and can perform competitively against non-argumentative direct-prediction baselines, while providing verdicts that are computed deterministically from explicit, inspectable argumentative structures.

</details>


### [298] [Using Aristotle API for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean 4: A Formalisation Case Study of the Grasshopper Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20120)
*Gabriel Rongyang Lau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究深入分析了一个由AI生成的Lean 4形式化证明（Grasshopper问题），发现AI能有效处理局部引理的验证，但在处理需要全局组合逻辑推导的奥数题时，仍面临难以完成逻辑闭环的核心局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在奥林匹克数学竞赛题目上已取得进展，但其证明产出的可信度受到实际验证情况的限制。研究旨在探究AI证明在处理复杂全局逻辑时的实际能力与局限性。

Method: 选取2009年IMO第6题（Grasshopper问题）作为研究对象，对Aristotle API生成的Lean 4证明代码进行详细的技术审计与形式化评估，分析其中已验证的引理及未解决的“sorry”部分。

Result: AI生成的证明虽验证了4个关键辅助引理（涉及最大化与交换策略），但未能完成主定理的全局计数推导，导致主定理遗留一个未解决的“sorry”，未能实现逻辑闭环。

Conclusion: 本文通过对具体的AI生成的数学证明进行案例分析（Grasshopper问题），揭示了AI辅助定理证明在局部推理中表现良好但难以完成全局组合逻辑的局限性。证明了仅依赖局部证明搜索无法保证复杂数学命题的完整验证。

Abstract: AI-assisted theorem proving can now generate substantial Lean developments for olympiad-level mathematics, but the evidential status of such developments depends on which declarations are actually verified. This paper reports a Lean 4 formalization case study of an Aristotle API proof attempt for the Grasshopper problem, originally posed as IMO 2009 Problem 6. The generated artifact states a generalized Lean version of the theorem, contains four verified helper lemmas for local components of a maximality and adjacent-swap exchange strategy, and leaves the main theorem grasshopper closed directly by one unresolved sorry. The verified components establish that the final partial sum equals the total sum, that an adjacent transposition can affect only the relevant intermediate partial sum, that the changed partial sum has the expected form, and that maximality at a position admitting an adjacent successor swap forces a corresponding forbidden-set membership fact. The Aristotle output summary identifies the intended remaining mathematical step as the global counting step needed to show that these membership facts produce at least n distinct forbidden values, contradicting the cardinality assumption |M| < n; the Lean source itself does not reduce the main theorem to a separately encoded counting lemma. This case study gives an inspectable example of a central limitation in AI-assisted formalization, namely that local proof search can succeed while the global combinatorial bookkeeping required for a theorem remains unresolved. The paper contributes a reproducible Lean artifact and a precise analysis of its verified and unverified proof content.

</details>


### [299] [Not Every Rubric Teaches Equally: Policy-Aware Rubric Rewards for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20164)
*Utkarsh Tyagi,Xingang Guo,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Daniel George,Anas Mahmoud,Jackson Lee,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POW3R 是一种新型评分奖励框架，通过动态调整优化权重而不是固定原始分值，显著提高了模型在多准则 RL 任务中的训练效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于评分准则（Rubric-based）的强化学习方法通常将多个评价标准简单聚合，忽略了不同标准在训练过程中的“可学习性”（有些已饱和，有些尚不可达）。这种静态权重导致优化信号无效，降低了训练效率。

Method: 提出了“策略感知评分奖励框架”（Policy-aware Rubric Reward framework, POW3R）。它通过对比学习（Rollout-level contrast）动态调整每个准则的权重，优先优化那些当前能够区分模型输出的评价维度，同时保持原始的人类设定的评分准则权重平衡。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个基准模型的对比实验中，POW3R 在 30 项指标比较中胜出 24 项。不仅提升了平均得分和“严格完成率”（Strict Completion），还在达到相同性能水平时将训练速度提高了 2.5 到 4 倍。

Conclusion: POW3R 通过区分“衡量最终质量的标准（Human Weights）”与“当前训练所需的优化信号（Contrastive Weights）”，有效解决了传统评分准则（Rubric-based rewards）在强化学习中的局限性。该方法证明了优化信号的动态调整能显著提升训练效率和最终模型性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has made post-training highly effective when correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important model behaviors require satisfying several qualitative criteria at once. Rubric-based rewards address this setting by grading prompt-specific criteria and aggregating them into a scalar reward. Yet standard static aggregations conflate a criterion's human-assigned importance with its current usefulness as an optimization signal. We show that this assumption breaks down in rubric RL: many important criteria are already saturated or currently unreachable, while criteria that distinguish rollouts are not necessarily those with the largest human weights. We introduce POW3R, a policy-aware rubric reward framework that preserves human weights and category balance as the rubric objective while adapting criterion-level reward weights during training. POW3R uses rollout-level contrast to emphasize criteria that currently separate the policy's outputs, making the GRPO reward more informative without changing the underlying evaluation target. Across three base policies on two datasets spanning multimodal and text-only settings, POW3R wins $24$ of $30$ base-policy/metric comparisons, improving both mean rubric reward and strict completion (the fraction of prompts whose response satisfies every required rubric criterion) over vanilla GRPO with rubric rewards, and reaches the same plateau in $2.5$--$4\times$ fewer training steps. Rubric rewards should therefore distinguish what should matter in the final answer from what can teach the current policy.

</details>


### [300] [EngiAI: A Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark Suite for LLM-Driven Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19743)
*Gioele Molinari,Florian Felten,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了面向工程设计领域的EngiBench基准测试与EngiAI系统架构，通过多维度测试评估了主流LLM在复杂工程工作流中的表现，指出条件分支任务与长流程指令遵循是模型急需提升的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估框架难以适配结合了仿真、检索与制造准备的复杂工程任务，缺乏专门针对工程设计领域多智能体系统（MAS）的标准化评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含工作流、RAG检索及HPC高性能计算编排三个维度的多智能体基准测试集；开发了基于LangGraph的EngiAI多智能体系统（MAS），通过监督者架构协调7个专业Agent执行工程任务。

Result: 在Beams2D任务上，闭源模型可达96-97%完成率，开源4B模型为55-78%；条件分支任务极具挑战性（完成率跌至20-53%）；RAG门控测试验证了检索对参数选择的关键作用；HPC编排测试表明模型的指令遵循能力会随任务链长度增加而明显退化。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一套针对工程设计任务的多智能体基准测试（EngiBench）及参考实现架构（EngiAI），验证了当前大模型在复杂工程任务中的能力边界，特别是揭示了长流程指令遵循和条件分支任务是当前LLM应用中的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to engineering design tasks, yet existing evaluation frameworks do not adequately address multi-agent systems that combine simulation, retrieval, and manufacturing preparation. We introduce a benchmark suite with three evaluation dimensions: (1) a workflow benchmark with seven prompt styles targeting distinct cognitive demands-including direct tool use, semantic disambiguation, conditional branching, and working-memory tasks; (2) a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) benchmark with gated scoring isolating retrieval contributions to parameter selection; and (3) an High Performance Computing (HPC) benchmark evaluating end-to-end ML training orchestration on a SLURM cluster. Alongside the benchmark we present EngiAI, a Multi-Agent System (MAS) reference implementation built on LangGraph that operationalizes the benchmark by coordinating seven specialized agents through a supervisor architecture, unifying topology optimization, document retrieval, HPC job orchestration, and 3D printer control. Across four LLM backends and two EngiBench problems, proprietary models achieve 96-97% average task completion on Beams2D, while open-source 4B-parameter models reach 55-78%, with clear generational improvement. Conditional branching proves most challenging, with task completion dropping to 20-53% for the conditional style on Photonics2D. RAG gating confirms near-perfect retrieval-augmented scores ($\approx 1.0$) versus near-zero without retrieval, validating the evaluation design. On HPC orchestration, one model completes all pipeline steps in 100% of runs while another drops to 50%, revealing that multi-step instruction following degrades over long-running workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Operational Memory Architecture for Kubernetes:Preserving Causal Context Across the Evidence Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18755)
*Shamsher Khan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对Kubernetes原生机制会导致Pods重启后丢失故障证据（证据边界问题）的痛点，论文提出了操作内存架构（OMA），通过捕获事件并自动重建因果链，在极低资源开销下实现了高效的故障证据保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes的原生事件保留模型存在“证据边界”问题，即Pod在重启后，用于诊断故障的关键字段（LastTerminationState）会迅速被覆盖，导致高频CrashLoop故障的诊断证据丢失。

Method: 提出并实现了一种操作内存架构（OMA）：
1. 采用Go编写的Kubernetes Watcher实时捕获操作事件。
2. 使用SQLite作为后台存储，实现操作内存记录。
3. 定义了三种模式（P001, P002, P003）来识别和重建常见的因果故障链。
4. 提供简洁的查询接口供用户分析故障上下文。

Result: 1. 能够有效捕获事件并构建因果链，防止关键证据丢失。
2. 实验表明，构建因果边的平均延迟低于1ms。
3. 系统运行开销极小（事件处理速度约2.8个/秒，内存占用低于10MB）。

Conclusion: OMA通过将操作事件存储并构建为因果链，成功解决了Kubernetes中因LastTerminationState被覆盖而导致的故障证据丢失问题，具有极低的资源开销和微秒级的延迟。

Abstract: Kubernetes clusters generate rich operational events during pod lifecycle transitions, yet the platform's native event retention model discards the most diagnostically valuable context. The LastTerminationState field, which records a container's last failure, is overwritten shortly after a pod restart. We define this as the evidence horizon. During high-frequency crash loops, this horizon may be crossed multiple times before inspection, permanently losing critical evidence.
  This paper introduces the Operational Memory Architecture (OMA) to preserve causal failure evidence before event rotation. OMA encodes evidence retention and causal reconstruction as explicit architectural requirements. It captures operational events into causal chains using three patterns: P001 (OOMKill chain), P002 (ConfigMap variable misconfiguration), and P003 (ConfigMap volume mount propagation).
  We implement OMA as an open-source system with a Go-based Kubernetes watcher, SQLite operational memory store, and a simple query interface. Experiments on Minikube and AKS include a 30-run latency analysis and stress tests with up to 20 crash-looping pods. Causal edges are built with mean latency below 1 ms. The collector processes ~2.8 events/sec while using under 10 MB memory, showing minimal overhead and effective evidence preservation.

</details>


### [302] [Unleashing the Power of Tree-of-Thoughts for Edge-Enabled AIGC Service Provisioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19108)
*Zhang Liu,Shanhao Zhan,Shaowei Shen,Lianfen Huang,Qiao Xiang,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对移动边缘计算场景下的 ToT 生成任务，本文提出了基于 DAG 的任务分配模型与 DSAC 算法，在满足质量约束的同时，显著降低了 AI 生成内容的计算时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ToT 虽能提升 AIGC 质量，但因需多次调用计算密集型模型，对资源受限的移动设备造成了巨大压力；亟需一种边缘计算协同下的高效任务调度策略。

Method: 1. 通过实验量化 GenAI 模型的计算资源（以 Token 为准）、延迟与质量的关系；2. 构建基于有向无环图（DAG）的 ToT 推理流程模型；3. 提出 DSAC（扩散模型辅助的 Soft Actor-Critic）算法，用于优化思路由任务分配。

Result: DSAC 算法在减少总生成延迟方面表现优异：相比 PPO 降低 8.32%，相比 SAC 降低 11.57%，相比 DDQN 降低 36.09%；与本地生成相比，延迟降低超过 80%。

Conclusion: 通过引入扩散模型辅助的 DSAC 算法优化 ToT 思维任务分配，能在保证 AIGC 服务质量的前提下，显著降低计算延迟，优于传统强化学习算法及完全本地化方案。

Abstract: Delivering AI-generated content (AIGC) services fundamentally relies on the reasoning capabilities of generative AI (GenAI) models. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) enhances such reasoning by guiding models through intermediate steps, while Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) further extends CoT by exploring multiple candidate reasoning paths simultaneously, thereby greatly improving AIGC service quality. However, generating diverse reasoning paths requires separate calls to computationally intensive GenAI models, posing significant challenges for resource constrained user devices. In this paper, we investigate mobile edge computing-enabled AIGC service provisioning with ToT prompting. Specifically, using creative writing AIGC tasks as a case study, we first characterize the number of output tokens as a measure of computational resources in GenAI models and establish its relationship with generation delay and quality through experiments with Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct. Afterward, we introduce a directed acyclic graph (DAG) model to accurately characterize the reasoning process of ToT prompting, where each vertex represents a thought and each directed edge denotes a transition between consecutive thoughts. We then formulate a DAG-based thought assignment problem aimed at minimizing generation delay subject to a user-adjustable quality constraint. To address this problem, we propose a diffusion-based soft actor-critic (DSAC) algorithm that innovatively integrates diffusion models to determine optimal thought assignment decisions. Through extensive simulations, we demonstrate that the proposed DSAC achieves total generation delay reductions of up to 8.32% over PPO, 11.57% over SAC, and 36.09% over DDQN across various simulation settings, while reducing latency by over 80% compared to the fully local generation baseline even under stringent quality requirements.

</details>


### [303] [Meta-Theorems for Cuttable Distributed Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19157)
*Marthe Bonamy,Avinandan Das,Cyril Gavoille,Timothé Picavet,Jukka Suomela,Alexandra Wesolek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一套元定理，证明了平面图上的常数轮局部近似算法可转化为亏格为$g$的图或“局部友好”图上的近似算法；针对MDS问题，将近似比改进为常数级$34+\varepsilon$，优于现有的依赖于亏格$g$的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有界亏格（genus-$g$）图上的分布式近似算法（如MDS问题）近似比依赖于亏格系数$g$，性能待优化。研究旨在开发通用的元定理，将平面图上的有效近似算法自动推广至更复杂的图类。

Method: 1. 利用平面图到有界亏格曲面图的转换技术；2. 建立针对“可切割最小化问题”的元定理（meta-theorems），利用局部子图的解与全局解之间的比例关系进行分析；3. 基于渐进维度（asymptotic dimension）将结果扩展至更广泛的“局部友好”图类。

Result: 1. 将MDS的近似比从旧有的$24g+O(1)$提升至常数级$34+\varepsilon$（针对任意亏格$g$）；2. 将成果推广至最小值$k$-元组支配集（Minimum $k$-Tuple Dominating Set）；3. 将适用范围从有界亏格图拓展至“局部友好”图类。

Conclusion: 通过推广“可切割最小化问题（cuttable minimization problems）”的元定理，作者成功将平面图上的局部算法成果迁移至有界亏格图及“局部友好（locally nice）”图类，显著提升了最小支配集（MDS）及相关问题的分布式近似算法性能。

Abstract: We prove that given any $α$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for Minimum Dominating Set (MDS) on planar graphs, we can construct an $f(g)$-round $(3α+1)$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for MDS on graphs embeddable in a given Euler genus-$g$ surface. Heydt et al. [European Journal of Combinatorics (2025)] gave an algorithm with $α=11+\varepsilon$, from which we derive a $(34 +\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for graphs of genus $g$, therefore improving upon the current state of the art of $24g+O(1)$ due to Amiri et al. [ACM Transactions on Algorithms (2019)]. It also improves the approximation ratio of $91+\varepsilon$ due to Czygrinow et al. [Theoretical Computer Science (2019)] in the particular case of orientable surfaces.
  We generalize this result into two directions: (1) by considering other graph problems studied in Distributed Computing such as Minimum $k$-Tuple Dominating Set, for which constant-round approximation algorithms were known for planar graphs, but not for graphs of bounded genus; and (2) by considering graph classes beyond bounded genus graphs, called locally nice, and relying on the asymptotic dimension of the class. We prove these results by a series of meta-theorems about cuttable minimization problems with constant-round approximation LOCAL algorithms. Roughly speaking, in cuttable problems, one can systematically extract small subgraphs whose solutions are in proportion to the global solution restricted to the neighbourhood of the subgraph.

</details>


### [304] [Generalized Compare-and-Swap and Space-Efficient Universal Constructions for the Infinite-Arrival Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19237)
*Vassos Hadzilacos,Myles Thiessen,Sam Toueg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GCAS（广义比较并交换）原语，并利用它在无限到达模型下实现了两种空间高效的无等待通用构造，其中一种创新的内存回收方案成功将空间复杂度降至与点争用线性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CAS 仅限于相等性测试，限制了在某些复杂分布式场景下的适用性；同时，在参与进程数未知或无限的情况下，现有的无等待通用构造往往难以同时满足高效的空间复杂度和并发需求。

Method: 提出了一种广义比较并交换（GCAS）原语，通过参数化比较器（<, =, >）扩展了传统 CAS；设计了两种针对无限到达模型的无等待通用构造算法，其中第二种算法引入了一种创新的内存回收方案以实现点争用相关的线性空间复杂度。

Result: 成功实现了两种在无限到达模型下的无等待通用构造：第一种空间复杂度与已参与进程数呈线性关系；第二种在有界并发假设下，空间复杂度与点争用呈线性关系，且该实现采用了新颖的内存回收机制。

Conclusion: GCAS 提供了一种更灵活的原子操作原语，并展示了其在处理无限到达模型（infinite-arrival model）下的优越性，特别是通过创新的内存回收机制实现了空间复杂度与点争用（point contention）线性相关的无等待通用构造。

Abstract: We introduce GCAS, a natural generalization of the well-known compare-and-swap (CAS) object. Intuitively, GCAS just replaces the fixed equality test of CAS with a parametrized comparator chosen from $\{<, =, >\}$. To showcase the utility of GCAS, we present two space-efficient wait-free universal constructions for systems where the number of participating processes is unknown and may be infinite (the infinite-arrival model). The first has space-complexity linear in the number of processes that have participated so far, while the second has space-complexity linear in the point contention but assumes bounded concurrency. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first wait-free universal constructions that achieve this space complexity in the infinite-arrival model. To achieve space complexity linear in the point contention, our second universal construction uses a novel memory recycling scheme that works in the infinite-arrival model with bounded concurrency. The ideas behind this recycling scheme could be of more general use.

</details>


### [305] [Understanding Inference Scaling for LLMs: Bottlenecks, Trade-offs, and Performance Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19775)
*Moiz Arif,Avinash Maurya,Sudharshan Vazhkudai,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 随着思维链（CoT）推理任务的普及，推理架构面临从“计算密集”向“内存容量/带宽密集”的转变；本文通过大规模实验揭示了不同参数规模与模型类型（稠密/MoE）下的瓶颈现状，并提出了相应的并行策略选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI正向推理中心化架构演进（如长思维链CoT），导致推理负载特征发生根本性变化（KV缓存占用大、推理时间延伸），原有的针对计算密集型预填充阶段的优化策略已无法满足需求。

Method: 通过在GPU集群上对参数规模从8B至671B的模型进行全面评估，系统化地对比分析了数据并行（Data Parallelism）、张量并行（Tensor Parallelism）和流水线并行（Pipeline Parallelism）的相互作用。

Result: 1. 数据并行在推理负载下易受KV缓存碎片化导致容量陷阱；2. 32B参数规模是张量并行收益的转折点；3. 稠密模型偏好高程度张量并行以应对带宽限制；4. 稀疏MoE模型受限于路由与同步延迟，需采用混合策略。

Conclusion: 推理负载（Reasoning-centric workloads）引发了从“计算受限”到“容量受限”的架构范式转移。传统的并行化启发式方法已不再适用，必须根据模型规模（稠密 vs 稀疏MoE）采用差异化的并行策略，以克服内存带宽、KV缓存碎片化及同步延迟等瓶颈。

Abstract: The transition from standard generative AI to \emph{reasoning-centric architectures}, exemplified by models capable of extensive Chain-of-Thought~(CoT) processing, marks a fundamental paradigm shift in system requirements. Unlike traditional workloads dominated by compute-bound prefill, reasoning workloads generate long chains of reasoning tokens that shift inference into a \emph{Capacity-Bound regime}. This paper presents a comprehensive system characterization, evaluating models ranging from 8B to 671B parameters on GPUs clusters. By systematically exploring the interplay between Data, Tensor, and Pipeline parallelism, we identify critical bottlenecks that defy standard scaling heuristics. Our analysis reveals that data parallelism is throughput efficient for small models but hits a capacity trap on reasoning workloads as KV-cache fragmentation forces early throttling resulting in sub-optimal compute utilization. Tensor parallelism unlocks stranded memory and delivers sublinear gains near the 32B crossover. At frontier scale, dense models (e.g., Llama-405B) are interconnect and memory-bandwidth bound and favor high-degree TP, while sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models (e.g., DeepSeek-R1) are limited by routing and synchronization latency and benefit from hybrid strategies. These insights provide a rigorous decision framework for navigating the reasoning cliff, establishing new architectural imperatives for the next generation of inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [306] [LatentBox: An Efficient Latent-First Storage System for AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19385)
*Zirui Wang,Yunjia Zheng,Tingfeng Lan,Zhaoyuan Su,Haoran Ni,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对AI生成图像的存储臃肿问题，LatentBox通过存储模型潜在张量替代全像素图像，并配合动态缓存优化与GPU按需重建，在大幅节省存储空间的同时确保了高效的访问性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像数量激增，传统存储全像素格式导致严重的存储冗余与成本压力，且AI图像具有可从“潜在空间（latent space）”确定性重建的特性。

Method: 1. 基于35个月的生产追踪数据进行大规模分析；2. 架构设计：采用“潜在优先”存储策略，将潜在张量作为持久化对象；3. 优化策略：结合按需GPU重建技术，动态平衡高频访问图像（像素格式）与低频图像（潜在格式）的存储比例。

Result: LatentBox实现了78.7%的持久化存储空间节省，并在保持与传统存储系统相当甚至更优的平均及尾部延迟下完成了请求响应。

Conclusion: LatentBox通过将AI生成图像存储为紧凑的潜在张量而非全分辨率像素，成功在保持较低访问延迟的同时显著降低了存储开销，是针对AI生成内容存储挑战的高效解决方案。

Abstract: The explosive growth of AI-generated images has created a sustainability challenge for storage infrastructure. Platforms like Midjourney and Adobe Firefly already host billions of generative images, yet conventional object stores persist them as blobs with full-resolution pixels, consuming huge amounts of storage capacity and bandwidth. Unlike natural photos, however, AI-generated images can be deterministically reconstructed from compact, model-native latent tensors, making persistent image storage fundamentally redundant.
  This paper presents LatentBox, a latent-first storage system for AI-generated images. LatentBox treats compressed latents as durable storage objects and uses on-demand GPU reconstruction on the read path to trade inexpensive compute for large persistent storage savings. Our design is guided by the first large-scale analysis of AI-generated image access we are aware of, based on a 35-month, 2-billion-request production trace from a major generative-content platform. Motivated by the trace analysis, LatentBox keeps frequently accessed images in decoded pixel format for fast hits, stores less-active objects as compressed latents to expand effective cache capacity, and continuously adjusts the splits between the image and latent cache to optimize user-perceived access latency.We build a LatentBox prototype and evaluate it with the production trace. LatentBox reduces persistent storage by 78.7% with competitive or even lower mean and tail latency over a pure image-based storage.

</details>


### [307] [A parallel wakeup problem and multi-room light switch strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19488)
*John Haslegrave,Paul A. Russell,Mark Walters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式系统中的“唤醒问题”，解决了 Kane 和 Kominers 在处理多房间、多开关通信模型时提出的关于最少状态数的悬而未决的问题，并界定了对称协议可行性的确切参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中的“唤醒问题”（wakeup problem）要求在对称协议下实现处理器间的协作。针对 Kane 和 Kominers 提出的在多个并行且不可区分房间中使用二进制开关进行通信的变体，研究其解的存在性及所需最少开关状态。

Method: 通过分布式计算模型中的对称与非对称协议分析，研究了基于共享寄存器（无全局时钟）的处理器协作机制，推导了状态转换的最优限制条件。

Result: 得出了在非对称情形下解决该问题所需的最少开关状态数，并给出了对称“唤醒”情形下处理器与寄存器数量的精确可行性条件。

Conclusion: 研究确定了在不同处理器数量和寄存器数量下，对称“唤醒问题”是否有解的精确界限，并回答了关于Kane和Kominers提出的非对称模型中所需最少开关状态的未决问题。

Abstract: The wakeup problem in distributed computing asks for a symmetric protocol that enables one of several processors to eventually guarantee that all (or, in a more general setting, enough) other processors have acted, using a shared register but no global clock. Dropping the symmetry requirement gives a well-known exercise often phrased in terms of prisoners entering, in an unknown sequence, a room equipped with a single binary switch, and using it to communicate. Kane and Kominers recently analysed a more general version of the latter with multiple parallel and indistinguishable rooms. We answer some open questions of Kane and Kominers regarding the minimum number of switch states needed for the prisoners to solve the problem. We also consider the symmetric ``wakeup'' version of this scenario, and establish exactly for which numbers of processors and registers a solution is possible.

</details>


### [308] [GEM: GPU-Variability-Aware Expert to GPU Mapping for MoE Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19945)
*Sourish Wawdhane,Avinash Kumar,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型推理中因GPU性能不一致导致的同步瓶颈问题，本文提出了GEM框架，通过综合考量GPU性能特征与专家模型的使用模式，动态优化专家在GPU上的映射策略，从而消除计算堆积，显著降低推理时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在推理时存在严重的“同步短板”问题，即计算延迟受限于最慢的GPU（straggler）。现有的专家放置策略仅关注负载均衡，忽略了不同GPU之间的硬件处理能力差异，导致经常将高负载任务错误地分配给慢速GPU。

Method: 提出了GPU感知（GPU-variability-aware）的专家映射框架GEM：1. 分析专家模型的类型（Consistent专家与Temporal专家）；2. 根据GPU的实际处理性能，对各GPU分配非均匀的计算负载，以实现计算同步；3. 避免将高频使用的专家放置在慢速GPU上，并对同时活跃的专家进行负载均衡分散。

Result: 实验表明，与基准测试相比，GEM框架平均提升了7.9%的端到端延迟，最高提升幅度可达16.5%。

Conclusion: GEM框架通过感知GPU性能差异并优化专家模型（Expert）的分配策略，有效解决了MoE模型推理中的“短板效应”（straggler problem），显著提升了端到端推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Expert (MoE) models enable efficient inference by employing smaller experts and activating only a subset of them per token. MoE serving engines distribute experts across multiple GPUs and route tokens to appropriate GPUs at inference time based on experts activated. They process tokens in lock-step fashion, where tokens within a batch must finish processing before proceeding to the next layer. This synchronization barrier acts as a critical bottleneck because the performance of MoE models is limited by the straggler GPU that finishes last. Stragglers emerge when too many heavily used experts are placed on the same GPU or the slowest GPU. While prior works place experts that balance token loads across GPUs, they all overlook GPU variability and often place highly used experts on the slowest GPUs.
  We propose GEM, GPU-variability-aware Expert Mapping, a framework for GPU variability-aware expert to GPU mapping for MoE models. GEM exploits two insights. First, we must place experts such that each GPU receives non-uniform token loads based on their variability and they all finish processing a layer at about the same time. Our studies show that there are two types of experts: consistent that are used most of the time and temporal that are often used together for the remaining time. Our second insight is that we must place simultaneously used consistent and temporal experts on different GPUs and avoid placing them on slower GPUs to reduce slowdown. GEM gathers the variability profile of GPUs for each model and task and uses the token load distributions per task to map experts to GPUs. Our experiments show that GEM improves end-to-end latency by 7.9% on average and by up to 16.5% compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [309] [Resilient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19452)
*Julien Dallot,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijevic,Stefan Schmid,Patrik Welters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用“可疑节点预测器”来提升拜占庭协议韧性的方法，通过量化预测器准确性与系统容错能力之间的权衡（一致性 vs 鲁棒性），为动态环境下的分布式系统提供了更优的容错理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拜占庭协议在容错能力上往往有固定的上限（如 $n/3$），该文旨在研究当系统能够利用预测器辅助识别可疑节点时，如何根据预测器的准确度动态优化算法的韧性（即能承受的最大故障节点数）。

Method: 提出了基于预测器（Predictor）的拜占庭协议模型；通过构建算法在预测正确（一致性）和预测错误（鲁棒性）两种情形下的容错边界；利用理论推导验证了权衡的紧致性，并分析了预测误差对系统韧性的量化降级过程。

Result: 1. 给出了一致性与鲁棒性之间的权衡公式：在非认证环境下，一致性容错为 $\alpha n$，鲁棒性容错为 $\frac{1-\alpha}{2}n - 1$；在认证环境下，鲁棒性提升至 $(1-\alpha) n - 1$。2. 定义了“平滑性”：在非认证场景下，每多一个错误预测损失1个单位韧性；在认证场景下，需两个错误预测才损失1个单位韧性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入信任度预测器，实现了拜占庭容错与性能之间的平衡；证明了在非认证和认证环境下，算法的韧性（Resilience）与预测器的准确性存在精确的权衡（Trade-offs），且该边际是紧的。此外，研究揭示了韧性随预测器误差增加的量化衰减规律（平滑性）。

Abstract: This paper studies the Byzantine Agreement problem where the nodes have access to a predictor that flags nodes for suspicion of faulty (Byzantine) behavior. We focus on algorithmic resilience -- the maximum number of faulty nodes an algorithm can tolerate -- and present algorithms and impossibility results whose resilience depend on the accuracy of the predictor. As our first main result, we bring a complete characterization of the consistency--robustness trade-offs in both the non-authenticated and authenticated settings: for $n$ nodes and a parameter $α\in [0, 1]$, we present algorithms that tolerate up to $α\cdot n$ faulty nodes when the predictor is correct (consistency), and up to $\frac{1-α}{2} \cdot n - 1$ faulty nodes when the predictor is arbitrarily wrong (robustness); in the authenticated setting the robustness bound improves to $(1-α) \cdot n - 1$. These trade-offs are exactly tight as we show that one additional faulty node renders the problem impossible. Our second main result characterizes smoothness: the rate at which resilience degrades as the predictor becomes less accurate. We show that resilience linearly decreases in the number of wrong predictions as long as that number stays within a constant fraction of $n$. Concretely, in the non-authenticated setting each additional wrong prediction loses one unit of resilience, whereas in the authenticated setting the decline is halved since two wrong predictions are needed to lose one unit of resilience.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [Hardness and Approximation for Coloring Digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19654)
*Parinya Chalermsook,Harmender Gahlawat,Felix Klingelhoefer,Alantha Newman,Chaoliang Tang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文探讨了有向图双色数（dichromatic number）和无环数（acyclic number）的计算复杂性，证明了即便是限制在锦标赛图中，这两个参数也极难近似，并为特殊类别的有向图提供了改进的着色算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有向图的两个基本参数（双色数 $\vecχ$ 和无环数 $\vecα$）的计算复杂度和近似算法问题。这些参数是对无向图对应参数的推广，但其在近似算法研究方面尚有缺口。

Method: 1. 复杂度理论分析：通过规约证明锦标赛图（tournament）上的近似难度；2. 算法设计：提出了针对 $\ell$-双色有向图的 $O(n^2\ell)$ 时间着色算法；3. 组合分析：针对特殊结构（$\vecχ(D) \le 2$ 或无有向三角形）优化了边界算法。

Result: 1. 锦标赛图中，对于任意 $\epsilon > 0$，将 $\vecα$ 和 $\vecχ$ 近似到 $n^{1-\epsilon}$ 之内是困难的；2. 提出了 $\ell$-双色有向图的 $\ell \cdot n^{1-1/\ell}$ 近似着色算法；3. 改进了低双色数和无有向三角形图的结构分析与算法工具。

Conclusion: 该文证明了有向图的双色数（dichromatic number）和无环数（acyclic number）在锦标赛图中仍然存在极强的不可近似性，并给出了一些特殊情况下的多项式时间近似着色算法和界限分析结论。

Abstract: The dichromatic number $\vecχ(D)$ of a digraph is the minimum number $k$ such that $V(D)$ can be partitioned into $k$ subsets, each inducing an acyclic digraph. The acyclic number $\vecα(D)$ is the cardinality of a largest induced acyclic subdigraph of $D$.
  We study these problems from an approximation point of view. We begin with establishing that even when restricted to tournaments, approximating $\vecχ$ and $\vecα$ remain as challenging as their undirected counterparts on general graphs. Specifically, we establish that for every $ε>0$, it is hard to approximate both $\vecα$ and $\vecχ$ up to a factor of $n^{1-ε}$ even when restricted to tournaments.
  We next consider approximate coloring of digraphs in special cases. We begin with establishing that we can color $\ell$-dicolorable digraphs using at most $\ell \cdot n^{1-\frac{1}{\ell}}$ colors in time $O(n^{2\ell})$; in particular, we can color $2$-dicolorable digraphs with $2\sqrt{n}$ colors in polynomial time. We then focus on bounding the dichromatic number of dense digraphs as a function of the independence number $α$ of the underlying graph. We consider two special cases in this regard: digraphs with $\vecχ(D)\leq 2$ and digraphs that do not contain any directed triangle. For these cases, we present algorithms which generalize and improve existing tools and results.

</details>


### [311] [Deterministic Volume Estimation of Truncated Hypercubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19809)
*Kyra Gunluk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种针对特定凸约束（如背包问题、范数约束）的超立方体截面体积近似算法，在保持高精度的前提下实现了多项式时间计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算高维几何体（如被线性约束截断的超立方体）的体积是一个经典的计算几何难题，且通常被证明是 #P-hard 的。作者试图在某些特定类型的约束下，寻找能够高效计算体积近似值的多项式时间算法。

Method: 提出了一种确定性多项式时间算法，该算法能够处理由多个非负、单调且凸的单变量函数之和构成的约束条件（如背包约束和范数球约束），并实现 $(1 + \varepsilon)$-乘法近似。

Result: 算法的时间复杂度为 poly_k(n, 1/ε, L, L_o)，能够在多项式时间内针对给定的 $k$ 个约束条件，实现对单位超立方体截面体积的 $(1 + \varepsilon)$ 近似。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了即便在一般情况下该问题是#P-hard的，但在特定约束条件（和形式）下，可以设计出多项式时间算法来对高维超立方体截面的体积进行近似计算。

Abstract: We present a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm for estimating the volume of a hypercube intersected by a fixed number of constraints of the type $f(x) \leq b$, where $f$ is the sum of univariate functions that are each nonnegative, monotone, and convex. Such constraints include knapsack and norm-ball constraints. The case of the unit hypercube truncated by a single linear constraint (halfspace) is already #P-hard. Given $k$ such constraints in dimension $n$, with total input length of at most $L$ bits, total output length of at most $L_o$ bits, and an error parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, our algorithm computes a $(1 + \varepsilon)$-multiplicative approximation of the volume of their intersection with the unit hypercube $[0,1]^n$ in time poly$_k(n, 1/\varepsilon, L,L_o)$.

</details>


### [312] [Linear Kernels for $l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19853)
*Yuxi Liu,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为 $l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity 问题设计了 $O(kl)$ 大小的线性核，涵盖了从通用 $l \geq 3$ 情况到 $l=1, 2$ 的特定改进，显著优化了该问题的核化复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 $l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity 问题的参数化复杂性，寻求更小规模的核（Kernel），以提高该问题在固定参数易解性方面的表现。

Method: 基于扩展皇冠分解（Extended Crown Decomposition）、线性规划（Linear Programming）及其他组合优化技术。

Result: 1. 提出了 $O(kl)$ 大小的核，计算时间复杂度为 $|V(G)|^{O(l)}$；2. 对 $l=1$ 情况匹配了最著名的 $2k$ 核；3. 对 $l=2$ 情况（即 Deletion to Induced Matching）提供了 $3k+1$ 的核，优于之前的 $6k$ 结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为 $l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity 问题提供了首个针对固定 $l \geq 3$ 的线性核，并在特定情形（$l=1, 2$）下改进或匹配了已知最优结果，证明了该类问题在核化方面的优秀性质。

Abstract: The \textsc{$l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity} problem asks whether, given an input graph $G$ and an integer $k$, there exists a vertex subset $S\subseteq V(G)$ of size at most $k$ such that every connected component in $G - S$ has exactly $l$ vertices. In this paper, we present an $O(kl)$-vertex kernel for this problem, computable in $|V(G)|^{O(l)}$ time. This is the first known linear kernel for each fixed $l\geq 3$. For $l=1$, this problem reduces to the classical \textsc{Vertex Cover}, and our result matches the best-known $2k$-vertex kernel. For $l=2$ (known as \textsc{Deletion to Induced Matching}), we can get a $(3k + 1)$-vertex kernel, improving the previously known result of $6k$ vertices. Our kernelization algorithm is built upon on an extended crown decomposition combined with linear programming and other techniques.

</details>


### [313] [Deterministic Single Exponential Time Algorithms for Co-Path Packing and Co-Path Set Parameterized by Treewidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19870)
*Yuxi Liu,Kangyi Tian,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了 Co-Path Packing 和 Co-Path Set 问题在树宽参数下缺乏确定性单指数时间算法的理论缺口，提供了新的确定性算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“Cut & Count”随机算法无法去随机化，导致这两个问题在树宽参数下是否存在确定性单指数时间算法一直是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 文章未描述具体算法细节，但明确指出了目标是提供针对树宽参数的确定性单指数时间算法，以替代现有的基于“Cut & Count”技术的随机算法。

Result: 提出了针对 Co-Path Packing 和 Co-Path Set 问题的确定性单指数时间算法，参数化为树宽（treewidth）。

Conclusion: 本文成功消除了 Co-Path Packing 和 Co-Path Set 问题在树宽参数下缺乏确定性单指数时间算法的缺口，证明了此类算法的存在性。

Abstract: The \textsc{Co-Path Packing} (resp., \textsc{Co-Path Set}) problem asks whether a given graph can be edited to a collection of induced paths by deleting at most $k$ vertices (resp., $k$ edges). Both are fundamental problems with significant applications in bioinformatics and have been extensively studied within the framework of exact and parameterized algorithms. Currently, the state-of-the-art approach utilizes the randomized ``Cut \& Count'' technique, which solves \textsc{Co-Path Set} in $O^*(4^{\mathbf{tw}})$ time and \textsc{Co-Path Packing} in $O^*(5^{\mathbf{pw}})$ time, where $\mathbf{tw}$ is treewidth and $\mathbf{pw}$ is pathwidth. However, as there is no known method to derandomize the ``Cut \& Count'' technique, the existence of deterministic single exponential time algorithms for these problems parameterized by treewidth has remained an open question. In this paper, we resolve this gap by providing deterministic single exponential time algorithms for both problems when parameterized by treewidth.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [C2CServe: Leveraging NVLink-C2C for Elastic Serverless LLM Serving on MIG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19481)
*Shutian Luo,Ali Zafar Sadiq,Rui Yang,Mingye Zhang,Haiying Shen,Wei Wang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: C2CServe 提出了一种利用高速 CPU-GPU 互联将模型权重存放在 CPU 内存而非 GPU HBM 的架构，结合混合内存计算内核和细粒度调度，成功解决了无服务器 LLM 服务的冷启动延迟和 HBM 容量瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器 LLM 服务面临权衡：专用 GPU 浪费内存，而 GPU 时间共享导致模型加载带来的冷启动延迟过高；空间共享（如 MIG）则受限于 GPU HBM 容量不足以容纳现代大模型权重。

Method: 1. 利用 CPU 内存存储模型权重，按需流式传输至 MIG 切片；
2. 开发 HybridGEMM 内核，动态平衡 HBM 与 C2C 带宽的数据访问；
3. 采用层次化调度器协调模型放置、输入分块和内核选择，并配合在线反馈控制以缓解 C2C 总线竞争。

Result: 在 GH200 平台上，相比现有最先进系统，密集模型冷启动延迟降低达 7.1 倍，MoE 模型降低 4.6 倍，且在 C2C 竞争下保持了 95% 以上的首字延迟（TTFT）和每 Token 延迟（TPOT）性能。

Conclusion: C2CServe 利用先进的 CPU-GPU 互联（如 NVLink-C2C）打破了传统 GPU HBM 容量对模型部署的限制，实现了高效的请求级细粒度服务，显著降低了冷启动延迟并提升了资源利用率。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving is increasingly serverless in shape: large model catalogs, long-tail invocations, and multi-tenant demand. Existing GPU serving systems face a tradeoff: dedicated-GPU allocation wastes scarce HBM under sparse traffic, while GPU time sharing places model initialization and weight loading on the cold-start path. Spatial GPU sharing such as multi-instance GPU (MIG) provides isolation and accounting, but each slice has too little HBM for modern LLM weights.
  We observe that high-bandwidth CPU--GPU interconnects, such as NVLink-C2C (C2C) in NVIDIA GH200 and GB200 Superchips, change the memory constraint: model weights can reside in CPU memory and be streamed on demand to MIG instances, shifting model residency from scarce HBM to abundant host memory. Leveraging this capability, we present C2CServe, a request-granularity serverless LLM serving system that allows MIG instances to switch models across requests without reloading weights into HBM. C2CServe introduces HybridGEMM, a heterogeneous-memory-aware GEMM kernel that adapts data access patterns to balance HBM and C2C bandwidth across MIG partitions using a single tuning knob. To mitigate shared-C2C contention, C2CServe further uses a hierarchical scheduler that coordinates model placement, input chunking, and kernel selection with online feedback control. On GH200, C2CServe reduces cold-start latency by up to 7.1x for dense models and 4.6x for MoE models compared with state-of-the-art serverless LLM serving systems, while maintaining over 95\% TTFT and TPOT attainment under C2C contention.

</details>


### [315] [SpecSA: Bridging Speculative Decoding and Sparse Attention for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19893)
*Zhibin Wang,Ziyu Zhong,Nuo Shen,Yuhang Zhou,Rong Gu,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SpecSA 是一个将动态稀疏注意力转化为验证导向任务的框架，通过优化跨查询重用和内核执行策略，解决了长上下文 LLM 推理中投机解码与稀疏注意力结合时的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码和动态稀疏注意力（NSA）虽均可加速长上下文 LLM 推理，但两者结合时存在“投机验证依赖跨查询通用性”与“NSA 使用查询特有稀疏布局”之间的结构失配问题，导致 KV 块重用率低、NSA 开销增大及验证策略难以优化。

Method: 提出了 SpecSA 框架，核心手段包括：1. 重叠感知的组查询执行（overlap-aware grouped-query execution）；2. 基于刷新/重用机制的 NSA 内核融合（kernel fusion）；3. 基于性能分析的提示感知编排（profile-guided prompt-adaptive orchestration）。

Result: 在 NVIDIA H100 GPU 上的实验表明，SpecSA 相比传统的自回归 NSA 解码，端到端吞吐量提升最高达 3.49 倍，稀疏投机验证的内核加速最高达 6.86 倍。

Conclusion: SpecSA 成功解决了动态稀疏注意力（NSA）与投机解码在长上下文推理中并行使用时的结构性失配问题，在大规模推理任务中显著提升了吞吐量和内核执行效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding and dynamic sparse attention are two complementary approaches for accelerating long-context LLM inference: the former amortizes target-model execution across multiple verifier queries, while the latter reduces each query's KV-cache working set. Directly combining them, however, exposes a structural mismatch: speculative verification relies on cross-query commonality, whereas dynamic sparse attention assigns query-specific sparse layouts. This mismatch limits KV-block reuse, amplifies NSA's branch-wise overheads, and makes verification strategy selection input- and regime-dependent. We present SpecSA, a sparse speculative-verification framework that turns dynamic sparse attention into a verification-oriented workload. SpecSA combines overlap-aware grouped-query execution, refresh/reuse-based NSA kernel fusion, and profile-guided prompt-adaptive orchestration to improve cross-query reuse, reduce selected-index and branch-fusion overheads, and select effective draft-verification strategies under user-specified precision classes. Experiments on NVIDIA H100 GPUs show that SpecSA achieves up to 3.49x end-to-end throughput over autoregressive NSA decoding and up to 6.86x kernel speedups for sparse speculative verification.

</details>
